### [1] [When Does Memory Help? A Cost-Aware Evaluation of Long-Term Memory in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05441)
*Shweta Mishra,Shashank Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MERIT是一个评估LLM代理长期记忆边际效用的基准与测试框架，包含23,440个带全成本核算的片段式工具任务。研究发现嵌入检索在更新事实召回上表现不稳定，结构化事实存储和LLM摘要等"更新即写"存储更可靠，且全重放策略在经济上永远不划算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评估基准（如LoCoMo、LongMemEval）仅通过对话历史问答来衡量记忆，而没有评估记忆是否真正改变了工具使用代理的实际行为。作者旨在设计一个度量记忆对任务执行代理的边际效用的评测体系，并纳入明确的成本核算。

Method: 构建MERIT基准与测试框架：在三个领域设计依赖早期片段事实的工具使用任务，通过自动化泄漏检查验证任务确实依赖先前信息；提供难度梯度并包含更新事实召回任务；实施受控记忆损坏机制；对所有记忆操作进行完整的token和美元计量。通过两代试点及预先注册的多模型多随机种子实验，对比嵌入检索、结构化事实存储、LLM摘要等不同记忆实现，并设置混合方案及经济性分析。

Result: 在23,440个评分片段中，记忆将依赖任务的成功率从泄漏验证的0.00基线提升至0.55-1.00。更新事实场景中，嵌入检索表现不稳定（0.30-0.95，最大随机种子差距0.45），代理仅55%时间按检索到的正确值行动；而更新即写存储保持在0.70-1.00，混合方案反而劣于单独的事实存储。换用不同记忆实现可改变任务成功率达60个百分点；最优条件每美元的边际效用为2.7-3.9倍，全重放策略在经济上从不划算。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的选择对工具使用代理的任务成功有重大影响；嵌入检索在事实更新场景下可靠性不足，而结构化存储和摘要式更新更稳健；记忆评估应超越问答式基准，关注实际任务效用与成本权衡。作者开源了全部基准、框架和trace。

Abstract: Long-term memory for LLM agents is evaluated today by conversational recall benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval), which measure question answering over dialogue history, not whether remembered facts change what a tool-using agent does. We present MERIT (Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks), a benchmark and harness that measures the marginal utility of memory for task-executing agents under explicit cost accounting. MERIT provides episodic tool-use tasks in three domains whose dependence on earlier-episode facts is verified by an automated leak check; a difficulty ladder ending in updated-fact recall; controlled memory corruption; and full token and dollar metering of every memory operation. Across 23,440 scored episodes ($42.57), a two-generation pilot on gpt-4.1-mini and a preregistered 3-model x 3-seed grid (GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5; memory side held fixed), memory lifts dependent-task success from a leak-verified floor of 0.00 to 0.55-1.00. On updated facts, embedding retrieval collapses unpredictably (0.30-0.95 across models; max seed gap 0.45), and agents act on a correctly retrieved value only 55% of the time, while update-on-write stores (a structured fact store and, notably, LLM summarization) remain at 0.70-1.00; the hybrid is worse than the fact store alone. A latest-generation spot-check (Claude Sonnet 5, gated on a clean full-replay control) reproduces the pattern. Swapping a memory's implementation moves task success by up to 60 points, and full replay is never economical: the best condition per domain delivers 2.7-3.9x its marginal utility per dollar. We release the benchmark, harness, and all traces.

</details>


### [2] [SciLitBench: Benchmark and Design Principles for LLM-Powered Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05505)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciLitBench，一个覆盖标题/摘要筛选、全文筛选和模式引导数据提取的多阶段系统综述基准，包含42,981条检索记录、1,012篇全文和888篇纳入论文的标注。在22个开源大语言模型中测试发现，明确的纳入/排除标准可将标题和摘要筛选的F2提升28.8%，研究者撰写的理由可将全文筛选提升15%；但数据提取性能差异巨大，准确率从出版年份的0.97降至计算方法Jaccard重叠的0.37，最强模型仅能恢复30%的评价证据和25%的局限性。该基准揭示了高召回筛选与证据完整提取之间的实用边界，为评估LLM辅助证据综合提供了可复现资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述需要对数千条记录进行持续的人类判断，但现有对大语言模型（LLM）的评估通常只孤立地考察综述的某个阶段，缺乏端到端的多阶段统一评估基准，无法全面反映LLM在真实证据综合流程中的实际表现。

Method: 构建SciLitBench多阶段基准，涵盖三个核心阶段：标题与摘要筛选、全文筛选以及模式引导的数据提取。数据集包含42,981条检索记录、1,012篇全文及888篇纳入论文的标注。在22个来自六个模型系列的开源LLM上进行系统评估，并测试显式纳入/排除标准对筛选性能的影响，以及研究者撰写的理由对全文筛选的辅助效果。

Result: 显式纳入/排除标准将标题和摘要筛选的F2提升28.8%，研究者撰写的理由将全文筛选提升15%。数据提取性能差异显著：出版年份准确率达0.97，而计算方法的Jaccard重叠仅为0.37；最强模型仅能恢复30%的标注评价证据和25%的局限性，表明提取任务的可靠性远低于筛选任务。

Conclusion: SciLitBench识别出LLM在高召回筛选与证据完整提取之间存在的实际性能边界，为评估和推进LLM辅助的证据综合提供了多阶段、可复现的基准资源，促使研究者正视目前LLM在精细证据提取环节的局限。

Abstract: Systematic reviews require sustained human judgment across thousands of records, yet existing evaluations of large language models (LLMs) typically examine review stages in isolation. We introduce SciLitBench, a multi-stage benchmark spanning title and abstract screening, full-text screening, and schema-guided data extraction, with 42,981 retrieved records, 1,012 full texts, and annotations for 888 included papers. Across 22 open-weight LLMs from six model families, explicit inclusion and exclusion criteria improve title and abstract screening $F_2$ by 28.8\%, while researcher-authored rationales improve full-text screening by 15\%. Data extraction reveals a different reliability regime: performance declines from 0.97 accuracy for publication year to 0.37 Jaccard overlap for computational approach, while the strongest models recover only 30\% of annotated evaluation evidence and 25\% of limitations. SciLitBench identifies a practical boundary between high-recall screening and evidence-complete extraction and provides a reproducible resource for evaluating LLM-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [3] [What LLM Trading Agents Actually Do in Production: A Six-Month, Population-Scale Record from Two Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05663)
*T. J. Barton,Chris Constantakis,Patti Hauseman,Annie Mous,Alaska Hoffman,Brian Bergeron,Hunter Goodreau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对两个生产环境下的自主语言模型交易代理系统（DX Terminal Pro 与 DXAP）进行了连续、全规模的实证测量，跨度约六个月，涵盖750万次单模型调用和约30万次链上操作。研究发现：（1）平台操作层（风险滑块、固定效应、排行榜边界）对行为的塑造远大于策略文本本身；（2）仓位规模设定对波动率盲视，杠杆中位数恒定在5倍，且11%的账户集中了62%的爆仓；（3）代理几乎捕获不到其触及的浮盈，49.3%到达+300bps浮盈的头寸最终以亏损平仓；（4）两个舰队均无方向性优势，DXAP整体不盈利且落后于匹配的散户基准。论文以17条方法论准则收尾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一个连续、真实生产环境、全规模的自主LLM交易代理观测记录，以回答"策略文本与运行系统究竟谁在决定行为、代理是否真正拥有交易能力"这一核心问题，弥补以往仅基于模拟或单系统研究的空白。

Method: 构建两套同源设计的生产系统（DX Terminal Pro在Base memecoin市场、DXAP在Hyperliquid永续市场），记录约6个月、750万次单模型调用和231,638个多工具turn；采用回归不连续设计（排行榜top-3边界1.75x）、方差分解（代理固定效应吸收60%）、波动率六分位对照、配对回放联赛（416个生产场景）等统计手段，并通过日聚类推断、置换检验和共同费用重述进行稳健性检验。

Result: 四项核心发现：操作层（风险滑块+0.425杠杆/级、固定效应60%方差、排行榜RD 1.75x）主导行为；仓位对波动率盲视（各六分位杠杆中位数均为5.0x，单一仓位设置群体11%仓位占62%爆仓）；代理无法捕获浮盈（43.2%头寸触及+300bps，其中49.3%仍以亏损收盘，机械括号策略可收回+39.0bps/仓位）；两舰队无方向性优势（DXAP回合胜率41% vs 50%散户基准，前沿模型决策质量在该时间尺度上统计不可区分）。

Conclusion: 结论是：在当前生产观测尺度下，自主LLM交易代理的实际收益率由运行的接口与机制层（而非策略内容或模型智能）决定，它们缺乏方向性边际优势且系统性错过自身触达的浮盈。作者最后以自身撤回经历为代价，提出17条方法论准则以供后续真实世界代理研究借鉴。

Abstract: We present a continuous, population-scale measurement record of autonomous language-model trading agents operating in production across two systems with one design lineage: DX Terminal Pro (3,505 user-funded vaults trading real ETH in Base memecoin markets for 21 days, February to March 2026) and the DXAP live alpha fleet (500 to 599 user-created agents all-history, 91 to 117 concurrently active, trading Hyperliquid perpetuals, June to August 2026). The record spans roughly six months, 7.5M single-model invocations with about 300K onchain actions, and a further 231,638 multi-tool turns producing 14,596 fills. Four findings carry the paper. First, the operating layer determines behavior more than anything written in strategy text: a risk slider explains leverage (+0.425 per level), agent fixed effects absorb 60% of variance, and a leaderboard render boundary causally routes selection (regression discontinuity 1.75x at the top-3 cut). Second, sizing is volatility-blind: median leverage is 5.0x in every volatility sextile, and one posture-slider cell (11% of the book) holds 62% of liquidations. Third, agents capture almost none of the upside they reach: 43.2% of positions saw at least +300 bps of favorable excursion within 24h, yet 49.3% of those closed with a negative trade return; a mechanical bracket recovers +39.0 bps per position. Fourth, neither fleet shows a directional edge. The DXAP fleet is not profitable and trails a matched Hyperliquid retail benchmark (41% vs. 50% roundtrip win rate). A paired-replay league of frontier models on 416 captured production scenarios finds decision quality statistically indistinguishable at this horizon, while choice stability differs sharply across model families. Every headline survives day-clustered inference, permutation nulls, and a common-fee restatement; the paper closes with a 17-rule methodology canon bought with our own retractions.

</details>


### [4] [Reasoning-Aware Compression: Identifying and Protecting Vulnerable Reasoning Circuits for Energy-Efficient LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05512)
*Leonard Twagirayezu,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"推理感知"的大语言模型压缩框架。该框架在五个推理基准（GSM8K、FOLIO、MATH-500、ProofWriter、MuSiQue）上、结合GPU硬件级能耗测量来评估量化条件，并通过在保留校准集上的扰动扫描，对所有196-224个(层,投影)组合的INT4脆弱性进行逐模块分析，最后选择性地将最敏感回路恢复为FP16。实验发现：INT4量化可因延长推理链而增加能耗；量化脆弱性具有任务依赖性；选择性压缩可实现统一方法无法达到的Pareto最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）在部署时能耗巨大，但现有压缩方法对所有组件采用统一量化策略，可能损害关键的推理电路（reasoning circuits），导致推理质量或能耗产生意料之外的负面影响。

Method: 提出一个推理感知的压缩框架：(1) 在五个推理基准上同时进行基准量化条件评估与GPU硬件级能耗测量；(2) 通过保留的校准集上的扰动扫描（perturbation sweep），对所有196-224个(layer, projection)组合进行逐模块INT4脆弱性画像；(3) 选择性地将最脆弱的敏感电路恢复至FP16精度，形成混合精度压缩方案。

Result: 三项主要发现：(1) INT4量化可以通过延长推理链来增加能耗——尽管功耗降低25%，但在GSM8K上净能耗反而增加；(2) 脆弱性具有任务依赖性：注意力投影对数学推理更为关键，而逻辑推理中的敏感模式因架构而异；(3) 选择性压缩达到了统一方法无法触及的Pareto最优：R1-Qwen-7B在ProofWriter上Top-10%选择获得比FP16高12个百分点（+12 pp）且能耗降低9.7%，并在五个推理基准的保留数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 推理感知的、任务特定的选择性压缩能够克服统一量化的缺陷，实现同时提升推理性能与降低能耗的Pareto最优。该工作强调了能耗评估应关注"净能耗"（推理链长度与功耗的乘积）而不仅是瞬时功耗，并为LRM部署中的混合精度压缩提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) impose substantial energy costs during deployment, yet current compression methods apply uniform quantization across all components, risking damage to critical reasoning circuits. We present a reasoning-aware compression framework that benchmarks quantization conditions across five reasoning benchmarks, GSM8K, FOLIO, MATH-500, ProofWriter, and MuSiQue, with hardware-level GPU energy measurement; profiles per-module INT4 vulnerability across all 196-224 (layer, projection) pairs via a perturbation sweep on a held-out calibration split, then selectively restores the most sensitive circuits to FP16. Three findings emerge. First, INT4 quantization can increase energy by extending reasoning chains; a 25% power reduction becomes a net energy increase on GSM8K. Second, vulnerability is task-dependent: attention projections are more critical for mathematical reasoning, and sensitivity patterns differ by architecture in logical inference. Third, selective compression achieves Pareto-optimal points inaccessible to uniform methods: R1-Qwen-7B Top-10% on ProofWriter gains +12 pp over FP16 at -9.7% energy, validated on held-out data across five reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [5] [From Event Logs to Governed Action: A BlueSky Agenda for Agentic Process Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07984)
*Yiyuan Yang,Zheshun Wu,Yong Chu,Zhenghua Chen,Zenglin Xu,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"事件到行动的过程挖掘"（event-to-action process mining）新议程，旨在将异构运营事件数据转化为受治理的行动建议，而非仅提供回顾性洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI改变了过程挖掘的目标：过程感知型智能体将不再只询问"发生了什么"，而会询问"在给定证据、隐私预算、组织权限和下游风险下，某个拟议行动是否应当执行"。现有过程挖掘聚焦于回顾性洞察，无法支撑这种行动导向的决策需求。

Method: 提出四个可挖掘工件作为核心议程：（1）事件-对象表示（event-object representations）；（2）行动证据包（action evidence packages）；（3）治理契约（governance contracts）；（4）将执行（act）、推迟（defer）、询问（ask）和拒绝（refuse）均视为有效输出的基准测试。同时强调整合代理BPM、LLM辅助过程挖掘、对象中心事件标准、因果过程监控和隐私保护学习等正在成熟的技术。

Result: 论文作为BlueSky立场论文，提出并论证了过程挖掘领域的新研究方向与数据挖掘目标框架，而非给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 过程挖掘应向"为可问责行动挖掘组织行为"转型，而非仅提供回顾性洞察。该议程恰逢其时，可将多个独立成熟的技术线汇聚为过程挖掘内部新数据挖掘目标。

Abstract: Process mining has long turned event logs into process knowledge: discovered models, conformance evidence, bottleneck diagnoses, and runtime predictions. Agentic AI changes the target. Process-aware agents will not only ask what happened. They will ask whether a proposed action should be taken, given the available evidence, privacy budget, organizational authority, and downstream risk. This BlueSky paper proposes event-to-action process mining: a process-mining agenda for transforming heterogeneous operational event data into governed action. The goal is not another dashboard, a generic enterprise simulator, or a language interface over logs. We argue that the community needs four mineable artifacts: event-object representations, action evidence packages, governance contracts, and benchmarks where act, defer, ask, and refuse are all valid outputs. This agenda is timely because agentic business process management (BPM), LLM-assisted process mining, object-centric event standards, causal process monitoring, and privacy-preserving learning are maturing separately. Bringing them together defines a data-mining target inside process mining: mining logged organizational behavior for accountable action, not only retrospective insight.

</details>


### [6] [The Failure Happens Before the Drift: The Social Dynamics of Values in LLM Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05514)
*Farah Atif,Sougata Saha,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于世界价值观调查（WVS）的仿真框架，用于评估LLM智能体能否忠实模拟人类多样化且冲突的价值观系统。研究发现超过50%的人格无法在初始对话中表达其分配的WVS特征，2-7%在多次对话后发生价值观漂移，且模拟对话在风格一致性与语义多样性之间呈现出与人类不同的权衡，表明当前LLM智能体作为人类价值观代理仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地被用作社会科学研究中人类参与者的替代品，但其能否忠实模拟人类多样且相互冲突的价值观系统仍不清楚。该研究旨在系统性地检验这一问题。

Method: 构建WVSgrounded仿真框架，包含具有不同文化背景和沟通风格的多样化智能体，进行纵向、价值观密集的讨论。实验涉及约4000次对话、1200个人格、15个话题和三个模型（GPT-4o、Gemini-2.5-Flash和Gemma-4-E4B），评估价值观忠实性、漂移和对话真实性，并通过消融实验（移除人口统计学细节）检验影响因素。

Result: 超过50%的人格从一开始就未能表达其分配的WVS特征；2-7%在重复对话后发生漂移。移除人口统计学细节可改善部分模型的忠实性，但总体趋势不变：模拟的价值观分布仍系统性偏离分配的WVS特征。与人类讨论相比，模拟对话在风格一致性与语义多样性之间表现出不同的权衡——内容多样化但风格重复。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体能够生成看似合理的对话，但在代表和维持人类多样化价值观特征方面仍是有限的代理，尚不能完全替代人类参与者进行社会科学研究。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used as proxies for human participants in social science research, yet it remains unclear whether they can faithfully simulate diverse and conflicting human value systems. We present a World Values Survey (WVS)-grounded simulation framework where culturally diverse agents with different communication styles engage in longitudinal, value-laden discussions. Across approximately 4,000 conversations involving 1,200 personas, 15 topics, and three models (GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Gemma-4-E4B), we evaluate value faithfulness, value drift, and conversational realism. We find that more than 50\% of personas fail to express their assigned WVS profiles from the outset, while 2-7\% drift after repeated conversations. Ablations removing demographic details improve faithfulness for some models but do not change the broader trend: simulated value distributions still systematically deviate from the assigned WVS profiles. Compared to human discussions, simulated dialogues show a different trade-off between stylistic consistency and semantic diversity, often producing content-wise varied but stylistically repetitive exchanges. These findings suggest that current LLM agents can generate plausible conversations, but remain limited proxies for representing and preserving diverse human value profiles over time.

</details>


### [7] [EdgeMem: LLM-Free Agent Memory Construction and Retrieval via Evidence-Preserving Multi-Anchor Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05553)
*Zeyang Cui,Jiannong Cao,Zhiyuan Wen,Bo Yuan,Junlan Feng,Shengyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EdgeMem是一种基于"保留原始对话轮次并通过互补的内容、时间和情节线索组织它们"原则的智能体记忆方法，使用轻量级局部处理构建多锚点超图，检索直接返回源证据，仅在最终答案生成时使用LLM。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆方法通常将交互历史压缩为摘要或其他LLM生成的表示，重复生成会增加成本，且可能在系统得知未来查询需求之前就丢弃包含答案的关键细节。

Method: EdgeMem围绕一个核心原则设计：保留原始交互轮次，通过互补的内容（content）、时间（temporal）和情节（episodic）线索来组织它们。具体实现采用轻量级局部处理构建一个多锚点超图（multi-anchor hypergraph），检索直接返回源证据，仅在最终答案生成阶段调用LLM，从而将多会话历史的结构化访问与原始对话的忠实保留相结合。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上展现出强检索和基于记忆的问答能力；在LoCoMo上，EdgeMem在共享提示下的七个复现系统中取得了最高的严格评判得分（61.01 vs 58.70），且构建和检索过程无需任何生成式LLM调用。

Conclusion: EdgeMem表明，保留并组织源证据（而非进行生成式记忆管理）可以为智能体记忆提供一个既有效又高效的坚实基础。

Abstract: Agent memory allows LLM agents to use earlier interactions when answering new queries. Existing methods often compress interaction histories into summaries or other LLM-generated representations. Repeated generation adds cost and can discard answer-bearing details before the system knows what a future query will require. We propose EdgeMem, an agent-memory method built around a simple principle: preserve original interaction turns and organize them through complementary content, temporal, and episodic cues. EdgeMem realizes this principle with a multi-anchor hypergraph constructed by lightweight local processing. Retrieval directly returns source evidence and reserves LLM use for final answer generation, combining structured access to multi-session histories with faithful retention of the original conversation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show strong retrieval and memory-grounded question answering; on LoCoMo, EdgeMem achieves the highest strict-judge score among seven reproduced systems under a shared prompt (61.01 versus 58.70), while construction and retrieval require no generative-LLM calls. Overall, EdgeMem shows that preserving and organizing source evidence provides an effective and efficient foundation for agent memory without generative memory management.

</details>


### [8] [Agents Trust Tools Too Much: Measuring Reliance on Unreliable Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05587)
*Hoyeol Yang,Woojung Song,Taewon Kim,Jonghyun Song,Seoyeon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了工具使用型AI智能体对不可靠工具返回结果的过度信任问题。通过对14个LLM在三种工具（网络搜索、LLM子代理委派、代码执行）下的评估发现，智能体普遍存在过度信任现象，平均采纳不可靠返回内容的比例超过三分之一，网络搜索场景下甚至高达68%。更严重的是，推理轨迹分析显示智能体有时能识别冲突并内部恢复正确答案，却仍只呈现被破坏的内容而不警告用户。研究者尝试了用户提示、工具元数据和后训练三种干预手段，但没有一种能在所有工具上持续缓解过度信任问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用智能体的评估通常假设工具返回的信息是可靠的，但现实世界中工具可能返回看似合理实则错误的结果。作者希望通过系统性评估揭示智能体在工具返回不可靠信息时的行为模式，并探索有效的缓解干预措施。

Method: 对14个LLM使用三种工具（网络搜索、LLM子代理委派、代码执行）进行测试。通过人工破坏每种工具的返回结果，测量智能体在最终答案中采纳被破坏内容的比率。随后分析推理轨迹，并在三个层面进行干预实验：用户提示层面、工具提供者元数据层面、智能体构建者后训练层面。

Result: 所有工具场景下智能体均表现出高水平的过度信任：每种工具的平均采纳率均超过三分之一，网络搜索场景高达68.0%。推理轨迹显示智能体常能识别冲突甚至内部恢复正确回答，但最终只输出被破坏的答案而无任何警告。三种干预措施（用户提示、工具元数据、后训练）仅对特定模型或特定工具有效，没有一种能在所有工具上一致缓解过度信任问题。

Conclusion: 对不可靠工具的过度信任是严重且持久的失效模式。评估和干预不应仅仅关注智能体能否成功完成任务，还需要使智能体具备验证工具输出的能力，并对未解决的信息冲突进行透明沟通。现有干预手段覆盖面不足，需要更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of tool-using agents primarily measure whether an agent can successfully complete diverse tasks with tools. These evaluations generally assume that tools return reliable information. However, tool returns in real-world systems can be plausible yet incorrect. We investigate how agents respond to unreliable tool returns by evaluating fourteen LLMs using three tools-web search, LLM sub-agent delegation, and code execution. For each tool, we corrupt its returns and measure whether agents adopt the corrupted content in their final answers. Agents exhibit high levels of overtrust across all three settings: the mean adoption rate exceeds one third for every tool and reaches 68.0% for web search. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals a particularly concerning failure mode: agents often recognize conflicts and even recover the correct answer internally, yet present only the corrupted answer without warning the user. To mitigate agents' overtrust in tool returns, we intervene at three levels: prompting by the user, metadata from the tool provider, and post-training by the agent builder. Although some interventions help for particular models or tools, none consistently mitigates overtrust across tools. These findings identify overtrust in unreliable tools as a serious and persistent failure mode, motivating evaluations and interventions that enable agents to validate tool outputs and transparently communicate unresolved conflicts.

</details>


### [9] [The convergent laboratory: when AI reasoning, autonomous experiments, high performance and quantum computing reshape chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05643)
*Eliu Huerta,Xiaoyun Wang,Geetika Gupta,Edward H. Sargent,Cameron J. Owen,Victor Fung,Abhishek Mitra,Austin Cheng,Emma Bouchard,Shams Mehdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是TPC26会议催生的评论文章，讨论AI、自主智能体、自动驾驶实验室和高性能/量子计算在材料科学发现领域的融合，认为该领域正处于变革性突破的关键转折点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汇聚学术界、国家实验室和工业界领袖，探讨AI、自主智能体、自动驾驶实验室、高性能计算与量子计算等新兴技术如何协同放大各自在材料科学发现中的影响力，为化学科学领域的转型性进展提供参考和指引。

Method: 基于TPC26会议中来自这些前沿领域研究者的第一手经验，综合讨论AI驱动推理、自主智能体框架、自动驾驶实验室以及容错量子处理器等技术的交汇与协同效应。

Result: 指出了材料科学发现正处于多重技术同步成熟的"临界点"，预期将出现变革性进展和生产性颠覆。

Conclusion: 该评论作为所处转折点的参考文献，表明AI、自主实验室和量子计算等技术并行成熟将重塑化学科学发现流程。

Abstract: This Comment emerges from TPC26 (https://tpc26.org), a conference convening leaders from academia, national laboratories, and industry who are reshaping materials science discovery. The meeting explored how AI, autonomous agents, self-driving labs, higher performance and quantum computing converge to amplify their individual impact on materials science discovery. The perspectives here reflect the firsthand experiences of researchers at these frontiers and capture the essence of this global endeavor. As AI-driven reasoning, autonomous agentic frameworks, self-driving laboratories, and fault-tolerant quantum processors mature simultaneously, we offer this Comment as a reference at what we believe is a tipping point of transformative advances and productive disruption in the chemical sciences.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond Prompts: Measuring and Optimizing LLM Tool-Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05736)
*Cen,Zhao,Haibo Ruan,Wenjie Chen,Pei-fen Tu,Usman Abbasi,Joel Hesch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究固定模型LLM工具智能体的运行时环境优化（资源受限的harness选择），提出了一个与优化器无关的评估协议，并推出了PRISM优化器——通过故障表面聚类将修复路由到提示词、工具边界中间件或联合编辑表面进行Pareto搜索。在三个基准上PRISM取得10-15个百分点的平均held-out提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工具智能体可以通过修改运行时环境（提示、工具接口、中间件、状态处理、恢复逻辑）来改进，而无需重新训练模型。当前缺乏在这一固定模型设置下系统地搜索和评估harness（运行时环境配置）的框架。

Method: 提出优化器无关的评估协议，报告平均held-out提升、最差条件提升、可重复性、成本诊断和RelLift95(B)（保守的held-out增益估计）。搜索空间限定为提示词和工具边界中间件的编辑（受保护的拦截，非任意重写）。实例化协议包括仅提示词和提示词加中间件两类优化器，其中PRISM通过聚类故障并将修复路由至不同编辑表面（提示/中间件/联合）进行Pareto搜索。通过组件消融实验归因性能提升来源。

Result: 在BFCL multi-round、tau2-Retail和tau2-Telecom三个基准上，PRISM分别取得14.2、14.9和10.1个百分点的平均held-out提升，且在所有三个基准上都获得正的实证RelLift95。消融实验表明性能提升主要来自故障表面路由和编辑模式约束。跨优化器的比较发现，某些搜索过程偶尔能找到大收益但选择的更新脆弱，因此可靠性与平均held-out提升需同时报告。

Conclusion: 固定模型的harness选择是一个可行且有效的优化方向。评估不应只看平均held-out提升，还需报告所选harness的可靠性（如通过RelLift95）。PRISM验证了故障表面路由和受限编辑模式（工具边界中间件）作为搜索空间约束的有效性。

Abstract: LLM tool agents can be improved without retraining by modifying the runtime harness around a fixed model: prompts, tool interfaces, middleware, state handling, and recovery logic. We study this setting as resource-bounded harness selection for fixed-model multi-turn tool agents, with the search surface scoped to prompts and tool-boundary middleware: edits are guarded intercepts at the tool boundary, not arbitrary rewriting of agent execution logic. Our optimizer-agnostic protocol reports mean held-out lift, worst-condition lift, repeatability, logged cost diagnostics, and RelLift95(B), a conservative estimate of the held-out gain of the harness selected under budget B. We instantiate the protocol with prompt-only and prompt-plus-middleware optimizers, including PRISM, which clusters failures and routes repairs to prompt, tool-boundary middleware, or joint edit surfaces within a Pareto search. On BFCL multi-round, tau2-Retail, and tau2-Telecom, PRISM obtains mean held-out lifts of 14.2, 14.9, and 10.1 percentage points and positive empirical RelLift95 on all three benchmarks, and a component ablation attributes the margin chiefly to failure-surface routing and the edit-pattern constraint. Across optimizers, the results show that some search procedures can occasionally find large gains but still choose brittle updates, so the reliability of the chosen harness should be reported alongside average held-out lift.

</details>


### [11] [Inference-Time Graph Engineering for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05774)
*Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReActNet，一个无需训练的框架，将查询和角色专业化智能体编译为一系列有向通信图（时序工作流图），通过结构化消息传递执行，在多项任务上优于固定拓扑和可学习拓扑基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM系统多依赖静态或固定拓扑的图结构通信，缺乏对任务条件化协调的显式建模。作者认为应从根本上重新审视编排方式：不仅需要决定哪些智能体通信，还需工程化可执行的工作流图来编码推理过程中信息的何时、为何及如何流动。

Method: ReActNet将图编译与图执行分离。首先，将查询和一组角色专业化智能体编译为时序有向通信图序列；每个图快照对应一个推理阶段，每条边携带自然语言指令指定源智能体应向目标智能体提供的消息。然后通过结构化消息传递执行：智能体通过整合先前状态与掌控者分配的邻居消息来更新推理状态，最终由聚合器综合各状态产出答案。该方法无需强化学习或梯度型拓扑优化。

Result: 在知识推理、数学问题求解、代码生成和GAIA式助手任务中，ReActNet一致优于固定拓扑和可学习拓扑基线，同时保持有竞争力的推理成本。

Conclusion: 有效的多智能体编排不仅取决于哪些智能体进行通信，还取决于工程化可执行的工作流图——这些图编码了推理过程中信息应在何时、为何以及如何流动。

Abstract: Recent multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on graph-structured communication to coordinate specialized agents. We revisit multi-agent orchestration from a graph-engineering perspective: rather than optimizing a static topology, we synthesize a task-conditioned temporal workflow graph that jointly specifies agent connectivity and edge-level communication semantics. We introduce ReActNet, a training-free framework that compiles a query and a set of role-specialized agents into a sequence of directed communication graphs. Each graph snapshot corresponds to one reasoning stage, and each edge carries a natural-language instruction specifying the message that a source agent should provide to a target agent. The compiled temporal graph is then executed through structured message passing: agents update their reasoning states by integrating their previous states with messages from controller-assigned neighbors, and a final aggregator synthesizes the resulting states into the answer. This design separates graph compilation from graph execution, making multi-agent coordination explicit, inspectable, and task-conditioned without requiring reinforcement learning or gradient-based topology optimization. Across knowledge reasoning, mathematical problem solving, code generation, and GAIA-style assistant tasks, ReActNet consistently improves over fixed-topology and learned-topology baselines while maintaining competitive inference cost. These results suggest that effective multi-agent orchestration depends not only on which agents communicate, but also on engineering executable workflow graphs that encode when, why, and how information should flow during reasoning.

</details>


### [12] [DI-Bench: Systematically Generating In-Domain Data Intelligence Benchmarks for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05776)
*Jiangyun Zhang,Kristen Surrao,Torpong Nitayanont,Yupei Zhang,Roopali Singh,Zhiyu Chen,Julia Huang,Zhou Tang,Shayan Ali Akbar,Omar Alonso,Erwin Cornejo,Yuan Li,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DI-Bench，一个用于生成数据智能（DI）领域基准测试的自动化流水线，通过构件链接图将数据表、维度、指标和文档关联起来，生成需要同时具备业务知识检索与分析计算能力的任务，在公开数据集上生成731个任务并评估了4个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共基准测试很少评估企业智能体是否能将业务知识与分析计算相结合，而手工构建此类基准成本高昂，因此需要自动化生成真实场景DI基准的流水线。

Method: 构建构件链接图（连接数据表、维度、指标与文档），基于此生成涉及结构化数据和相关知识的问题；通过查询执行生成基准答案，并利用LLM进行问题生成与验证。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上生成了包含731个任务的基准，涵盖知识检索、分析计算和基于规则推理；对4个模型评估显示平均准确率仅32%，尤其在业务规则修改计算的任务中表现大幅下降。

Conclusion: DI-Bench能够生成具有区分度的高难度DI基准，揭示出现有模型在需要将业务规则与计算逻辑结合的任务中存在显著短板。

Abstract: Evaluating enterprise agents on domain-specific benchmarks is critical, yet public benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can integrate business knowledge with analytical computation, and constructing such benchmarks manually is costly. We present DI-Bench, a pipeline for generating realistic benchmarks for data intelligence (DI), the practice of extracting insights from large volumes of enterprise data. To emulate realistic DI tasks that require both computation and knowledge retrieval, DI-Bench builds an artifact linkage graph over data tables, dimensions, metrics, and documents to form questions involving structured data and associated knowledge. Ground truth answers are derived via query execution, followed by LLM question generation and validation. Applied to two public datasets, the pipeline produces a 731-task benchmark covering knowledge retrieval, analytical computation, and rule-grounded reasoning. To show the discriminatory capability and difficulty of the benchmark, we evaluate four models, revealing a substantial finding: models achieve only 32% accuracy when doing computational tasks where retrieved business rules modify the computation.

</details>


### [13] [More Than Mimicking Reviewers: Evaluating LLMs for Pre-Submission Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05788)
*Pouya Parsa,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了一种面向作者的LLM审稿系统，该系统在投稿前生成并压缩原子化关注点报告，以提前模拟同行评审压力测试。基于3,398篇ICLR 2026投稿的实验表明，独立采样仅覆盖44.9%的历史评审问题，而去重与"回填"策略可将覆盖提升至78.7%（严格）/84.9%（按严重性加权），但代价是3.6倍请求和5.2倍token消耗。压缩是主要瓶颈：隐藏的Top-32"神谕"选择器能保留完整覆盖，而仅用论文的选择器只能保留40–44%。消融实验表明代表性选择和匹配器灵敏度是压缩损失的主因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审反馈往往来得太晚，作者无法进行有意义的修改。作者希望将部分评审压力测试提前到投稿之前，因此需要一个系统能够提前生成类似评审的关注点，但现有系统生成大量关注点后如何在保留覆盖率的同时有效压缩成简短报告，是一个未被充分研究的问题。

Method: 构建一个作者面向的LLM系统，分两步：(1) 生成大规模原子化关注点池；(2) 将其压缩为简短报告。从10,000篇ICLR 2026投稿中筛选出3,398篇有评审前可访问版本的手稿，评估系统覆盖的历史评审问题比例以及被遗漏关注点的潜在有效性。对比独立采样、去重+回填策略，并设置隐藏Top-32神谕及多种仅基于论文的选择器进行消融分析。

Result: 在10篇论文的诊断集上，独立采样覆盖44.9%的历史问题；去重和回填达到78.7%严格和84.9%严重性加权覆盖率，但需要3.6倍请求和5.2倍token。隐藏Top-32神谕保留完整256候选池的79.3%加权覆盖率，而仅依赖论文内容的选择器仅保留40–44%。大型候选池能提供广泛覆盖但压缩效果差。

Conclusion: LLM预审稿系统能够产生高覆盖率的大规模关注点池，但有效压缩为短报告是该范式的核心挑战。代表性选择（从海量候选中选择最具代表性的关注点）和匹配器敏感性（将生成内容与真实评审问题对齐的判断能力）是压缩质量差距的主要来源，为后续改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Peer-review feedback often arrives too late for authors to make meaningful revisions. We study an author-facing LLM system that moves part of this stress test before submission: it generates a broad pool of atomic concerns and compresses them into a short report. We evaluate agreement with historical reviews and, separately, the possible validity of concerns they omit.
  From 10,000 ICLR 2026 submissions, we use 3,398 manuscripts with accessible versions that predate review. On a ten-paper diagnostic, independent sampling covers 44.9% of historical issues; deduplication and refill reaches 78.7% strict and 84.9% seriousness-weighted coverage, at 3.6$\times$ more requests and 5.2$\times$ more tokens. A hidden Top-32 Oracle preserves the full 79.3% weighted coverage of a 256-candidate pool, but paper-only selectors retain only 40--44%. LLM review therefore provides broad coverage with a large candidate pool but compresses poorly; ablations identify representative selection and matcher sensitivity as the main sources of this gap.

</details>


### [14] [Spillover-Aware Multi-Value Steering for Pluralistic LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05800)
*Weici Pan,Xander Barron,Jiawei Zhou,Zhenhua Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种零成本的激活引导校正方法，解决多元对齐（pluralistic alignment）中多概念同时引导时产生的"溢出效应"（spillover）问题。作者将问题类比为因果推断中的"处理-溢出"分解，发现溢出源于引导方向间的几何纠缠（可通过Gram矩阵刻画），并从激活范数惩罚目标中推导出精确解耦各方向贡献的校正方案。整个流程无需微调、奖励模型或人工提示工程，仅需领域问题即可自动发现价值维度、提取方向、诊断纠缠并应用校正引导。在气候话语任务上，净引导效果从+5.9%提升至+14.0%，经10万对成对判断验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导（activation steering）方法只能一次处理一个概念，而多元对齐需要同时引导多个价值维度。朴素的多维引导会产生显著的溢出效应——本意为某价值引导的效果会泄漏到其他价值维度上，这种泄漏在表达方需要不同价值强调的实际场景中是有害的，因此需要一种能同时精准控制多维价值方向的方法。

Method: 论文将溢出效应与因果推断中的处理-溢出分解进行类比，将溢出归因于引导方向间的几何纠缠，并以引导方向的Gram矩阵进行刻画。作者从含激活范数惩罚的目标函数中推导出一种零成本的校正方法，能够精确解耦每个方向对隐藏状态贡献。所构建的端到端流水线仅需领域问题输入，即可自动发现价值维度、提取引导方向、诊断纠缠程度并应用校正后的引导，全程无需微调、奖励模型或人工提示工程。

Result: 在气候话语（climate discourse）场景中，该校正将净引导效果从+5.9%提升至+14.0%，显著改善了多价值维度同时引导时的精度和目标的独立实现程度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了多元对齐中多维激活引导的溢出问题可以用几何手段有效解决，零成本校正方法兼具理论简洁性和实际有效性。该方法提供了一条无需复杂训练或人工提示即可实现多利益相关方价值对齐的轻量级路径。

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behavior at inference time by adding learned directions to hidden states, but existing methods handle one concept at a time. Pluralistic alignment, where different stakeholders need different value emphases, requires steering multiple dimensions simultaneously. We show that naive steering produces substantial spillover: the effect intended for one value leaks into others. This parallels the treatment-versus-spillover decomposition in causal inference. We trace spillover to geometric entanglement of steering directions, captured by their Gram matrix, and derive a zero-cost correction from an activation-norm-penalized objective that decouples each direction's contribution exactly. Our end-to-end pipeline requires no fine-tuning, no reward model, and no manual prompt engineering: given only domain questions, it automatically discovers value dimensions, extracts directions, diagnoses entanglement, and applies corrected steering. On climate discourse, the correction improves the net steering effect from +5.9% to +14.0%, validated over 100,000 pairwise judgments.

</details>


### [15] [Exposing Weaknesses in Emotion Recognition in Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05806)
*Amir Ben Khalifa,Fanny Bezancon,Amine Trabelsi,Bessam Abdulrazak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑对话情感识别（ERC）中"每句话可被赋予单一明确情感标签"的核心假设，通过零样本LLM实验和多标注者重标注研究，证明聚合指标掩盖了系统性失败，大量模型"错误"实为标注歧义，并提出了基于LLM-as-Judge的独立情感评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ERC方法依赖任务特定微调，可能利用数据集特有线索；更关键的是，ERC领域一个核心但鲜被质疑的假设是每条话语可被赋予单一明确的情感标签。作者旨在检验这一假设的有效性，并揭示标准单标签评估范式的局限性。

Method: 1) 使用LLM在零样本设置下研究ERC，并加入前置对话轮次作为上下文；2) 进行聚合指标与错误模式细粒度分析，聚焦否定句、感叹句和插入语；3) 开展涉及四名人工标注者的受控重标注研究，测量标注一致性；4) 提出LLM-as-Judge框架，独立评估每种情感在对话语境中的合理性，而非强制执行单标签决策。

Result: 错误在含否定、感叹和插入语的话语周围集中出现，且该模式在所有评估模型中一致，表明属于基准限制而非模型缺陷。重标注研究显示，仅有35%的情况出现强标注者一致性，中性话语在高中一致性子集占主导，而许多情感类别落入低一致性区间。

Conclusion: 许多看似模型失误的案例实际反映的是真实的标注歧义，而非情感理解能力不足。标准单标签评估不足以衡量ERC能力，需采用独立评估每种情感合理性的LLM-as-Judge框架作为替代方案。

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversations (ERC) aims to identify speakers' emotions in multi-turn dialogue. Accurate emotion recognition can support a wide range of applications, including empathetic conversational agents, mental health support, and educational technologies. While many recent approaches rely on task-specific fine-tuning, such models may exploit dataset-specific cues. A central yet rarely questioned assumption in ERC is that each utterance can be assigned a single unambiguous emotion label. To investigate this assumption, we study ERC using Large Language Models (LLMs) in a zero-shot setting while incorporating preceding conversational turns as context. We show that aggregate metrics mask systematic failures. Errors concentrate around utterances containing negations, exclamations, and interjections. This pattern is consistent across all evaluated models, suggesting limitations in the benchmarks rather than model-specific weaknesses. A controlled re-annotation study involving four human annotators supports this finding: strong agreement is observed in only 35 percent of cases, with neutral utterances dominating high-agreement instances, while many emotional categories fall into low-agreement regimes. These findings suggest that many apparent model errors reflect genuine annotation ambiguity rather than poor emotion understanding. Standard single-label evaluation is therefore insufficient. To address this limitation, we introduce an LLM-as-Judge framework that evaluates each emotion independently according to its plausibility in the conversational context rather than enforcing a single-label decision.

</details>


### [16] [Agentic BAIM-LLM Evaluation (ABLE): Benchmarking LLM Use of Protein Design Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05818)
*Bryce Cai,Geetha Jeyapragasan,Samira Nedungadi,Jake Yukich,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ABLE基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在双用途蛋白质设计工作流中使用生物AI模型（如ProteinMPNN和AlphaFold3）的能力，涵盖结构检索、序列生成和设计验证等任务，并对15个前沿模型进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生物AI模型在蛋白质设计中的应用日益广泛，LLM智能体作为这些模型的交互界面可能大幅降低蛋白质设计的准入门槛，但同时也带来双用途（dual-use）安全风险。目前缺乏系统的基准来评估LLM智能体在蛋白质设计中调用生物AI模型的能力和安全性。

Method: 构建了ABLE基准，包含结构检索、序列生成、设计验证等任务集合；评估了15个前沿LLM模型在信息检索、工具选择、工具使用等方面的表现，并将部分任务结果与领域专家人类基线进行比较。

Result: 15个模型中7个拒绝执行所有任务，其余模型表现差异显著。Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 3 Pro在信息检索、工具选择和工具使用方面得分最高。模型与专家人类基线相比仍有差距，尤其在规划、策略生成以及将生物学知识与工具使用相整合方面表现不稳定。

Conclusion: 当前LLM确实能够显著降低蛋白质设计的准入门槛，但在规划能力、策略生成和跨学科知识整合方面仍不稳定，需要进一步提升；ABLE基准为评估LLM智能体在双用途蛋白质设计中的表现提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: We introduce ABLE, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents' ability to use biological AI models (BAIMs), such as ProteinMPNN and AlphaFold3, in dual-use protein design workflows. ABLE assesses agent performance through a set of tasks spanning structure retrieval, sequence generation, and design validation. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that seven refuse all tasks, while the remaining models exhibit substantial performance differences. Claude Sonnet 4 and Gemini 3 Pro achieve the highest scores across information retrieval, tool selection, and tool use. We further compare model performance on a subset of tasks against an expert human baseline. Our results suggest that current LLMs can substantially lower barriers to protein design, but remain inconsistent in planning, strategy generation, and integrating biological knowledge with tool use.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Top-$k$ Skill Retrieval: Diversity-Aware Skill Routing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05824)
*Wang Wei,Tiankai Yang,Samyadeep Basu,Hongjie Chen,Yue Zhao,Zhengzhong Tu,Xiyang Hu,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSR（多样技能路由）框架，采用行列式点过程（DPP）在技能路由中平衡相关性与非冗余性，通过查询残差多样性核函数处理冗余技能重叠并减少共享查询相关性带来的惩罚，在SkillRouter基准上相较强点级重排基线提升了召回率和全覆盖率，尤其在多技能查询上增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部技能库，但在大规模技能注册表上进行用户请求路由非常困难，因为许多技能在功能上冗余，而复杂任务往往需要互补的技能集合。现有的技能路由器通常基于查询相关性独立地对候选技能进行排序，这可能在冗余技能上浪费上下文预算，导致关键互补技能被遗漏。

Method: 提出Diverse Skill Routing (DSR)，一种多样性感知的重排序框架，使用行列式点过程（DPP）来平衡相关性和非冗余性。DSR引入了查询残差多样性核函数（query-residual diversity kernel），该核函数惩罚冗余技能间的重叠，同时减少仅由共享查询相关性造成的惩罚，从而在保持相关性的前提下最大化技能集合的互补性。

Result: 在SkillRouter基准测试上，DSR相比强点级重排基线提升了召回率和全覆盖率，且在多技能查询上获得了更大的性能增益。实验结果表明多样性感知的路由策略在处理多技能复杂查询时尤为有效。

Conclusion: 技能路由不应仅被视为相关性排序问题，还应被看作互补集合的选择问题。DSR证明引入多样性感知机制可以有效改善技能路由质量，减少上下文浪费，提升系统在多技能场景下的整体表现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external skills, but routing user requests over large skill registries is difficult because many skills are functionally redundant while complex tasks often require complementary skill sets. Existing skill routers typically rank candidates independently by query relevance, which can waste context budget on redundant skills. We propose Diverse Skill Routing (DSR), a diversity-aware reranking framework that uses a Determinantal Point Process to balance relevance and non-redundancy. DSR introduces a query-residual diversity kernel that penalizes redundant skill overlap while reducing penalties caused only by shared query relevance. On the SkillRouter benchmark, DSR improves recall and full coverage over a strong pointwise reranking baseline, with larger gains on multi-skill queries. These results suggest that skill routing should be treated not only as relevance ranking, but also as complementary set selection.

</details>


### [18] [AgentBrew: Offline Tool-Use Agent Learning from Raw Real-World Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05837)
*Zhiyi Lyu,Yewen Li,Longtao Zheng,Shengtian Yang,Lang Feng,Lei Feng,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentBrew是一个离线训练框架，仅需单批原始交互轨迹即可学习有效的工具使用策略，无需任务验证器或在线策略回放。通过回顾式任务推断和PMI-based信用分配从噪声轨迹中提取训练信号，在GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL三个真实MCP应用中显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Agent在真实应用中日益普及，但针对特定环境的训练面临根本困难：真实应用缺乏预定义任务和验证器、没有忠实模拟器、且环境交互预算有限。现有方法依赖大规模在线交互或严格过滤，难以在真实场景中落地。

Method: AgentBrew包含两个核心组件：(1) 回顾式任务推断（Retrospective Task Inference）——根据轨迹的实际结果逆推对齐的指令，不依赖预定义任务；(2) 基于PMI的信用分配（PMI-Based Credit Assignment）——利用点互信息将轨迹关于推断指令的总信息分解为逐动作的加性信用，用于加权策略训练目标，放大信息量高的动作、抑制无效动作。

Result: 在三个真实MCP应用（GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL）上，AgentBrew使Qwen3-32B平均提升+8.7 Acc/+9.7 Score，超越Qwen3-235B（+2.3/+4.4），并优于拒绝采样（+5.9/+10.3）。

Conclusion: 精细的离线学习可以从原始轨迹中恢复基于过滤方法会丢弃的有用监督信号，证明了无需任务定义和验证器的离线训练范式在真实工具使用场景中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in real-world applications through tool-use APIs, yet training them for specific environments remains fundamentally difficult: real-world applications provide no pre-defined tasks or verifiers, no faithful simulators, and limited budget for large-scale environment interaction. In this paper, we propose \textbf{AgentBrew}, an offline training framework that learns effective tool-use policies from a single batch of raw interaction trajectories, without task verifiers or iterative on-policy rollouts. The agent first explores the target environment to collect a raw trajectory corpus without quality filtering. To extract training signal from this noisy corpus, \emph{retrospective task inference} reconstructs an aligned instruction for each trajectory based on its actual outcome, and \emph{PMI-Based credit assignment} decomposes the trajectory's total information about the inferred instruction into additive per-action credits via pointwise mutual information (PMI). These credits weight the policy training objective, amplifying informative actions while suppressing ineffective ones. On three real-world MCP applications (GitHub, Notion, PostgreSQL), AgentBrew improves Qwen3-32B by +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score on average, surpassing Qwen3-235B (+2.3 / +4.4) and outperforming rejection sampling (+5.9 / +10.3). These results demonstrate that fine-grained offline learning can recover useful supervision from raw trajectories that filtering-based approaches would discard. The code is available at https://github.com/alphatogo/AgentBrew

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Final Decisions: A Process-Centric Benchmark for Transparent AI-Assisted Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05947)
*Siming Yuan,Xueyi Zhang,Wangze Ni,Tianfang Xiao,Shimin Di,Jia Zhu,Zhuoren Jiang,Rong Tan,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向AI辅助同行评审的过程中心化诊断基准，通过将论文内容、摘要、批评、建议和决策建模为过程变量，评估AI评审过程各阶段的可靠性，而非仅关注最终评审质量或决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对AI辅助同行评审的评估主要集中于生成评审的整体质量或最终决策的准确性，无法提供充分证据证明模型决策是否得到了充分且可靠的评审证据支持。研究者旨在弥补这一空白，通过过程中心的评估方法来诊断AI评审过程中的问题。

Method: 该基准将评审过程表示为(x, z_s, z_c, z_r, y)，分别对应论文内容、摘要、批评、建议和决策。研究将PeerRead、NLPeer ARR-22和OpenReview-ICLR的异构评审记录转换为过程对齐数据，使用直接从论文内容进行决策预测（Direct）作为基线，比较金标准过程变量与预测过程变量的决策价值，并开展阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析。

Result: 在三个数据集和六个模型上的实验表明，金标准过程变量通常具有更高的决策价值。主分析模型中，金标准与预测变量之间的差距在数据集和随机种子间保持稳定，且该差距在大多数模型-数据集组合中被复现。尽管模型生成的中间评审文本在相邻阶段间表现出较高的局部一致性，但最终决策并未被前序评审证据一致地支持。

Conclusion: 该基准旨在评估用于辅助而非替代人类评审员的AI系统，提供了一个透明且可审计的诊断工具，用于评估AI辅助同行评审过程各阶段的可靠性，揭示了模型中间步骤与实际决策支持之间的不一致问题。

Abstract: Peer review is central to quality control in science. However, existing evaluations of AI-assisted peer review mainly focus on the overall quality of generated reviews or the accuracy of final decisions. They therefore provide limited evidence about whether model decisions are supported by sufficient and reliable review evidence. We introduce a process-centric diagnostic benchmark for AI-assisted peer review. It uses (x,$z_s$,$z_c$,$z_r$,y)
  to represent the paper content, summary, critique, suggestion, and decision. We convert heterogeneous review records from PeerRead, NLPeer ARR-22, and OpenReview-ICLR into process-aligned data. Our benchmark uses direct decision prediction from the paper content (Direct) as its baseline. It compares the decision value of Gold-process variables and Predicted-process variables, and conducts stage-level evaluation, chain-consistency evaluation, and interventional sensitivity analysis. Experiments across three datasets and six models show that Gold-process variables generally have higher decision value. For the main analysis model, the Gold--Predicted gap remains stable across datasets and random seeds. This gap is also reproduced in most model--dataset combinations. Although model-generated intermediate review texts show relatively high local consistency across adjacent stages, the final decisions are not consistently supported by the preceding review evidence. Our benchmark targets AI systems designed to assist rather than replace human reviewers. It provides a transparent and auditable diagnostic tool for evaluating the reliability of their review processes.

</details>


### [20] [MOAE: Multi-Objective Agent Evolution with Pareto-Preserving Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05992)
*Hengle Jiang,Qijun Cai,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多目标智能体进化（MOAE）方法，将迭代式上下文优化组织为保留帕累托前沿的进化搜索，在保持安全性的同时同时提升任务性能和轨迹质量，避免将所有目标压缩为固定标量分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体评估日益多维化：能力强的智能体不仅需要任务完成准确率高，还须在交互质量、安全和效率方面表现出色。现有方法虽考虑多目标，但多将异构测量值压扁为固定标量分数，这种做法依赖于指标归一化和偏好权重，可能丢弃代表有用部署权衡的候选方案。

Method: MOAE将迭代式上下文优化组织为保留帕累托的进化搜索，作用于完整智能体轨迹之上。在有限的过程预算内，维护非支配候选的经验档案，使用目标特定的诊断指导子代生成，并仅在部署阶段采用约束感知的选择，从而将搜索期间的候选保留与最终返回解所依据的偏好相分离。该方法无需参数更新，且每个目标可替换为任意可测量属性（文中实例化为任务性能、轨迹质量和安全）。

Result: 在TravelPlanner和AgentDojo上的实验表明，在匹配的过程预算下，MOAE持续提升任务性能和轨迹质量，同时保持强安全性。搜索行为分析进一步显示，帕累托保留扩展了可达目标区域，并提高了联合改善的频率。

Conclusion: MOAE证明了无需在搜索过程中承诺固定标量化，即可通过保留帕累托的上下文进化实现对多个智能体属性的优化，展示了该方法在多目标智能体优化中的潜力。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents continue to advance, their evaluation has become increasingly multifaceted: a capable agent must not only achieve high task completion accuracy but also perform well in interaction quality, safety, and efficiency, raising a central question: can these objectives be optimized simultaneously? Existing methods have considered multiple objectives, but many collapse heterogeneous measurements into a fixed scalar score. Such scalarization depends on metric normalization and preference weights and may discard candidates that represent useful deployment trade-offs. We introduce Multi-Objective Agent Evolution (MOAE), which organizes iterative in-context refinement as a Pareto-preserving evolutionary search over complete agent rollouts. Given a limited rollout budget, MOAE maintains an empirical archive of non-dominated candidates, uses objective-specific diagnostics to guide offspring generation, and applies constraint-aware selection only at deployment. This separates candidate preservation during search from the preference used to return a final solution. The procedure requires no parameter updates and allows each objective to be replaced by any measurable property, which we instantiate as task performance, trajectory quality, and safety. Experiments on TravelPlanner and AgentDojo show that MOAE consistently improves task performance and trajectory quality while maintaining strong safety under matched rollout budgets. Search-behavior analysis further shows that Pareto preservation expands the attainable objective region and increases the frequency of joint improvement. These results demonstrate the potential of Pareto-preserving in-context evolution for optimizing multiple agent properties without committing to a fixed scalarization during search.

</details>


### [21] [Explaining AI Agents Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06063)
*Vittoria Vineis,Fabiano Veglianti,Lorenzo Antonelli,Claudia Di Carlo,Matteo Silvestri,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个事后可解释AI（XAI）框架，将智能体（AI Agent）冗长的执行轨迹转化为结构化报告和忠实于可观察行为的自然语言解释，仅依赖执行轨迹即可跨架构、环境和任务适用，能够在人工与自动化评估中优于普通LLM生成的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在真实环境中与外部工具交互并进行顺序决策，缺少充分的人类监督，因此需要可靠且可审计的过程级透明性解释。然而传统XAI方法无法为这类交互式、多步骤系统提供过程级透明度，亟需针对AI智能体设计的可解释性框架。

Method: 提出一个事后(post-hoc)XAI框架，将智能体的执行轨迹转化为结构化报告和自然语言解释；解释显式地基于智能体的可观察行为(execution trace)进行接地(grounding)，不依赖内部模型状态，因此可适用于不同智能体架构、环境和任务。

Result: 在多个基准和架构上的人工及自动化评估表明，该框架能生成高质量、忠实于轨迹的解释，并可靠地识别不支持的主张、不合理的行为和证据缺失，优于朴素LLM生成的解释。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了智能体过程级可解释性的空白，通过仅基于执行轨迹的忠实解释，实现了跨架构的可迁移性，为智能体的审计与信任提供了有效支撑。

Abstract: AI Agents are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where they interact with external tools and make sequential decisions with limited human oversight. This creates a pressing need for reliable and auditable explanations of what an agent did and why. However, traditional Explainable AI (XAI) methods fall short of providing the process-level transparency required for such interactive, multi-step systems, motivating a paradigm shift toward approaches specifically designed for AI Agents. To address this gap, we present a post-hoc XAI framework that transforms a lengthy agent's execution trace into a structured report and a faithful natural-language explanation explicitly grounded in its observable behavior. Because it relies solely on execution traces, the framework applies across different agent architectures, environments, and tasks. Human and automated evaluations across multiple benchmarks and architectures show that our framework produces high-quality, trace-faithful explanations while reliably identifying unsupported claims, unjustified actions, and evidence gaps, outperforming naive LLM-generated explanations.

</details>


### [22] [Substrate-Portable Execution for Production LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06128)
*Tarun Gopinath,Atul Kulkarni,Vijay Rajakumar,Shrikar Katti,Parthasarathy Govindarajen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种绑定自适应的Agent执行平台，将工作流定义与执行基质（substrate）解耦，使同一工作流可无代码修改地编译为实时流式、异步SWF编排或分布式批处理推理三种执行模式，在不损失输出质量的前提下实现约50%的推理成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的LLM Agent在实时在线服务、异步后台任务和高吞吐批处理等多种模式下执行工具调用循环、检索链和组合工作流，但执行语义通常与单一运行时绑定。作者在Amazon Rufus助手（拥有大规模工具目录，服务数百万客户）中遇到可移植性问题：复用流式编排会导致异步和批处理工作负载被阻塞，且无法利用提供50%折扣的批处理推理API。

Method: 提出binding-adaptive Agent执行平台，核心思想是将工作流定义与执行基质分离。开发者仅需将工作流定义为类型化数据流图（typed dataflow graph）；平台将该图分别编译为三种执行基质：进程内流式处理（面向实时在线服务）、持久化AWS SWF编排（面向异步执行）、分布式Apache Flink流处理（面向批推理）。LLM推理被建模为可挂起的图节点，其行为随执行基质自适应：在线模式流式递送，异步模式持久化重试，离线模式批量提交。

Result: 作者在五种编排模式（单推理RAG、迭代ReAct、组合PreAct、条件路由、多Agent深度研究）下验证了数十个生产Agent配置。三种执行绑定之间的输出质量无可检测差异；批处理执行将单查询推理成本降低至与已发布批处理API定价一致的水平，并与流式路径并行运行于生产规模。

Conclusion: 工作流定义与执行基质的解耦是解决LLM Agent跨模式可移植性的有效途径。该平台在不要求任何工作流代码变更的情况下支持多种执行模式，实现了成本优化与输出质量保持的统一，为大规模生产Agent系统提供了实用架构方案。

Abstract: Production LLM agents execute tool-calling loops, retrieval chains, and compositional workflows in multiple modes, yet execution semantics are often coupled to one runtime. We encountered this portability problem in Rufus, a conversational AI assistant with a large tool catalog that serves millions of Amazon customers. Rufus supports real-time serving, asynchronous background tasks, and high-volume batch workloads such as evaluation and content pregeneration. Each mode has distinct service-level objectives and typically uses a separate runtime. Reusing streaming orchestration makes asynchronous and batch workloads blocking and prevents use of batch inference APIs, which offer a 50 percent discount at published prices. We present a binding-adaptive agent execution platform that separates workflow definition from execution substrate. Developers define a workflow once as a typed dataflow graph. The platform compiles the graph to in-process streaming for real-time serving, durable AWS SWF orchestration for asynchronous execution, or distributed Apache Flink stream processing for batch inference. No workflow code changes are required. LLM inference is represented as a suspendable graph node whose behavior depends on the substrate: streaming delivery online, durable retry asynchronously, and batched submission offline. We validated dozens of production agent configurations across five orchestration patterns: single-inference RAG, iterative ReAct, compositional PreAct, conditional routing, and multi-agent deep research. Across all three bindings, we found no detectable difference in output quality. Batch execution reduced per-query inference cost in line with published batch API pricing while operating alongside the streaming path at production scale.

</details>


### [23] [MVFA: A Multi-View Text-Guided Multimodal Fusion LLM Adapter for Sentiment Analysis and Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06188)
*Pengfei Shao,Jisheng Dang,Jiawen Fang,Ning Liu,Wencan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Jianhuang Lai,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MVFA，一种多视图文本引导的多模态融合适配器，通过参数高效方式增强冻结的大语言模型（如ChatGLM3-6B、LLaMA2-7B、Qwen3-8B）在多模态情感分析和对话情绪识别中的能力，在CH-SIMS V2.0、MELD和CHERMA三个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析需要有效建模文本、音频、视觉模态间的异构交互。尽管LLM具备强大的语言理解能力，但将其适配到多模态情感计算面临挑战：全参数微调计算成本过高，而现有轻量适配器在跨模态融合时往往丢失丰富的文本语义线索。

Method: MVFA框架包含两个核心模块：（1）多视图文本引导融合模块——通过最大池化、均值池化和注意力池化构建互补的文本视图，引导与音频和视觉特征的跨模态交互；（2）增强的Q-Former融合模块——将融合后的多模态表示压缩为紧凑的可学习伪token，输入到冻结的LLM中。以ChatGLM3-6B-base为主骨干，并在LLaMA2-7B和Qwen3-8B上验证可移植性。

Result: 在CH-SIMS V2.0上获得84.62% Acc2和84.59% F1，在MELD上获得67.36% Acc和66.03% WF1，在CHERMA上获得74.66% Acc，均达到SOTA性能，且仅更新少量参数。

Conclusion: 多视图文本引导的融合范式是参数高效多模态LLM适配在情感计算领域的一种有效且可扩展的方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis and emotion recognition in conversations demand effective modeling of heterogeneous interactions across textual, acoustic, and visual modalities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer powerful language understanding, adapting them to multimodal affective computing remains challenging: full-model fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive, while many existing lightweight adapters fail to preserve rich textual cues during cross-modal fusion. To address these limitations, we propose the multi-view text-guided multimodal fusion adapter (MVFA), a parameter-efficient framework that augments frozen LLMs with strong multimodal reasoning capability. MVFA first constructs complementary text views via max pooling, mean pooling, and attention pooling; these views then guide cross-modal interactions with audio and visual features. The fused multimodal representations are subsequently compressed into a compact set of learnable pseudo-tokens through an Enhanced Q-Former Fusion Module. Using ChatGLM3-6B-base as the primary backbone, we further validate MVFA on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B to examine its portability across multiple frozen LLM backbones. MVFA is evaluated on three challenging datasets: CH-SIMS V2.0, MELD, and CHERMA. Experimental results demonstrate that MVFA achieves state-of-the-art performance on key metrics while updating only a small fraction of parameters. Specifically, it attains 84.62\% Acc2 and 84.59\% F1 on CH-SIMS V2.0, 67.36\% Acc and 66.03\% WF1 on MELD, and 74.66\% Acc on CHERMA. These findings establish multi-view text-guided fusion as an effective and scalable paradigm for parameter-efficient multimodal LLM adaptation in affective computing. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Overwhelm1208/MVFA.

</details>


### [24] [AutoKD: Autonomous Knowledge Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06366)
*Qinwen Ge,Bo Ni,Haowei Fu,Ngoc N. Tran,Erik Blasch,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoKD是一个多智能体框架，用于自动化知识发现，其特点是计算性的"发现-验证-累积"循环，通过持久化洞察图存储已验证的发现并引导后续探索。六个协调的LLM智能体在开放式发现循环中协作，评估表明其在覆盖已知发现和补充人类研究方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据丰富领域的科学发现受限于人类带宽——数据量增长远超研究人员阅读、推理和综合的能力。现有基于LLM的多智能体系统只能进行一次性（one-shot）假设生成，且缺乏自动验证机制，也无从让发现累积并指导后续研究。

Method: AutoKD由六个协调的LLM智能体组成，运行在开放式发现循环中。验证通过的发现存储在持久化"洞察图"（insight graph）中，该图同时充当长期记忆和探索引导机制。框架同时支持自动计算验证（computational）与知识累积（cumulative）两个特性。

Result: 在三个不同数据集上从两个视角进行评估：开放式质量（对照已发表发现）和条件式质量（通过文献派生查询）。结果显示AutoKD能覆盖已知发现并产生实质性新发现，可补充人类驱动的研究。

Conclusion: AutoKD证明了LLM多智能体系统不仅能自动化假设生成，还能在可自动验证的领域实现累积性、闭环式的自主知识发现，为数据丰富领域的研究提供规模化扩展能力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery in data-rich domains is currently constrained by human bandwidth: the growth in the volume and complexity of real-world data far outpaces the rate at which researchers can read, reason, and synthesize. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have begun to automate portions of the research cycle, but they target hypothesis generation in settings where validation cannot itself be automated, and each run is one-shot, with no mechanism for findings to accumulate or steer subsequent inquiry. This paper introduces AutoKD, a multi-agent framework for autonomous knowledge discovery that is both computational and cumulative, allowing validated findings to persist and inform subsequent inquiry. Six coordinated LLM agents collaborate in an open-ended discovery loop, where accepted findings are stored in a persistent insight graph that serves as both long-term memory and an exploration-steering mechanism. We evaluate AutoKD on three diverse datasets from two perspectives: Open-ended Quality against published findings, and Conditioned Quality via literature-derived queries. Across both evaluation perspectives, AutoKD covers known findings and surfaces substantive discoveries that complement human-driven research. Our code is available at https://github.com/GeQinwen/AutoKD.

</details>


### [25] [MARBO: Relational Belief Grounding for LLM Agents in Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06563)
*Hwang Yechan,Bae Sangjun,Kim Jeongmo,Bang Sangwoo,Han Seungyul*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MARBO框架，通过关系信念引导偏好优化，使小型LLM智能体在社交推理游戏中行为更一致、策略更优，并超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交推理游戏要求智能体在部分可观测条件下维护关于隐藏角色和团队阵营的关系信念，但现有LLM智能体方法在优化动作和对话时未将其显式锚定于信念，导致策略不一致，尤其在紧凑型LLM智能体上表现明显。

Method: 提出多智能体关系信念优化（MARBO），一种信念引导的偏好优化框架，利用关系信念来指导战略决策和游戏内对话。MARBO仅在行为得到可靠关系信念支持且导致战略上有利的社会结果时提供偏好反馈，从而在不确定性下鼓励更一致的学习。

Result: 在代表性社交推理游戏上的实验表明，MARBO使紧凑型LLM智能体能够持续优于现有基线。

Conclusion: MARBO验证了将行为决策显式锚定于关系信念能够有效提升小型LLM智能体在社交推理游戏中的策略一致性与表现。

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) require agents to reason under partial observability by maintaining relational beliefs about hidden roles and team alignments. While recent LLM-agent approaches improve gameplay through prompting and preference optimization, they often optimize actions and in-game speech without explicitly grounding them in such beliefs. This frequently leads to strategically inconsistent behavior, especially for compact LLM agents. We introduce Multi-Agent Relational Belief Optimization (MARBO), a belief-grounded preference optimization framework that leverages relational beliefs to guide strategic decisions and in-game speech. MARBO provides preference feedback only when behaviors are supported by reliable relational beliefs and lead to strategically favorable social outcomes, encouraging more consistent learning under uncertainty. Experiments on representative SDGs show that MARBO enables compact LLM agents to consistently outperform existing baselines. The Code is available on https://github.com/PleaseTakemeAway/MARBO.

</details>


### [26] [Monte Carlo-Based Ex-Ante Assessment of the Green Benefits of an AI-Driven Smart Agriculture Platform in Hainan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06737)
*Zhaoyang Li,Ruijie Zhang,Zhaoji Sun,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对智慧农业平台绿色价值评估框架缺失的问题，以海南热带农业AI决策平台为对象，构建了从摇篮到农场大门的农业碳核算模型，通过蒙特卡洛模拟传播参数不确定性。在全采纳情景下，农药使用中位数减少23.5%、化肥减少21.0%、灌溉水减少16.5%、碳强度下降21.5%。Sobol敏感性分析表明测土推荐和有机替代共同解释了约83%的碳强度减排方差。该框架提供了可复现、可校准的预期绿色价值评估和试点观测设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧农业平台被视为实施中国农药化肥减量、节水降碳战略的关键载体，但缺乏统一的定量框架来评估其绿色价值。

Method: 以集成了大语言模型问答、多模态病虫害诊断、物联网感知、卫星遥感和闭环田间记录系统的热带农业AI决策平台为对象，构建从摇篮到农场大门（cradle-to-farm-gate）的农业碳核算模型，覆盖农药化肥生产、田间N2O、灌溉用电和水稻CH4。将平台干预转化为可量化的传递参数，并在三个海南场景（芒果、冬菜、水稻/南繁，面积权重40%:30%:30%）下通过蒙特卡洛模拟（10,000次迭代）传播参数不确定性，辅以Sobol一阶敏感性分析和收敛性检验。

Result: 全采纳情景下中位数减排：农药使用23.5%（90%区间15.0%-33.2%）、化肥21.0%（13.8%-28.9%）、灌溉水16.5%（10.9%-23.5%）、碳强度21.5%（16.1%-27.2%）。化肥减量≥15%的实现概率高达90.6%，碳强度显著下降概率98.1%，但总节水量≥20%概率仅约20%。Sobol分析显示测土配方推荐与有机替代共同解释约83%的碳强度减排方差。10,000次迭代使所有统计量稳定收敛，并给出保守/基线/乐观情景边界。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可复现、可校准的预期绿色价值评估与试点观测设计方法论，支持面向不同作物场景给出差异化结论，为智慧农业平台的绿色绩效量化评价提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Smart agriculture platforms are widely regarded as key carriers for implementing China's pesticide and fertilizer reduction, water-saving and carbon-reduction agendas, yet a unified quantitative framework for assessing their green value is still lacking. Taking an AI-driven decision platform for tropical agriculture as the object (integrating large-language-model question answering, multimodal pest diagnosis, IoT sensing, satellite remote sensing, and a closed-loop field record system), this study builds a cradle-to-farm-gate agricultural carbon accounting model covering pesticide and fertilizer production, field N2O, irrigation electricity and paddy CH4, translates platform interventions into quantifiable transmission parameters, and propagates parameter uncertainty by Monte Carlo simulation over three Hainan scenarios (mango, winter vegetable, rice/nanfan, area-weighted 40%:30%:30%). Under full adoption, median reductions are 23.5% (90% interval 15.0%-33.2%) for pesticide use, 21.0% (13.8%-28.9%) for fertilizer, 16.5% (10.9%-23.5%) for irrigation water, and 21.5% (16.1%-27.2%) for carbon intensity. Attainment probabilities are high for fertilizer reduction >=15% (90.6%) and clear carbon decline (98.1%), but only about 20% for aggregate water saving >=20%, favoring scenario-specific statements. Sobol first-order indices show soil-test recommendation and organic substitution jointly explain about 83% of the variance of aggregate carbon-intensity reduction. Convergence tests show 10,000 iterations stabilize all statistics; conservative/baseline/optimistic scenario bounds are reported. The framework offers a reproducible, calibration-ready methodology for ex-ante green-value assessment and pilot observation design.

</details>


### [27] [Learning transferable human physiology from two million hours of sleep with SleepFM-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06849)
*Rahul Thapa,Christopher Sun,William Theodor Lehn-Schioler,Sophia Claire Kivelson,Umaer Hanif,Hyatt Moore,Harrison G. Zhang,Hafsa Ahmed,Marcus Dige,Niels R. Lorenzen,Elisabeth Roxane M. Heremans,Adrien Specht,Ulysse Gimenez,Robin Guillard,Andreas Brink-Kjaer,James Zou,Emmanuel Mignot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SleepFM-2，一个基于282,511份多导睡眠监测记录（超过两百万小时的多模态生理数据）训练的睡眠基础模型。相比前代SleepFM，它在疾病预测和睡眠分期上有提升，支持多种事件检测，可迁移到可穿戴设备和主观睡眠表型；结合人口统计信息后，在外部医疗系统中对215种后来记录的EHR表型达到预设的判别标准，其中155种表型的PSG表征在人口统计特征之外贡献了可复现信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠为健康监测提供了夜间窗口，涵盖脑、心、肌肉和呼吸系统的协同活动。先前已有SleepFM基础模型，但需要在更大规模数据上提升疾病预测能力、支持更多睡眠事件类型，并检验模型向可穿戴设备及主观睡眠体验的迁移能力，以验证多模态睡眠生理数据能否作为人类健康的可迁移表征。

Method: 在282,511份PSG记录上训练和评估SleepFM-2，其中235,865份用于预训练。模型采用多模态生理信号（脑电、心电、肌电、呼吸等），编码器冻结后用于多种下游任务；评估涵盖疾病预测、睡眠评分、觉醒/肢体运动/呼吸事件检测，以及向可穿戴传感器（手腕PPG与加速度计、头戴式与耳内EEG）和主观睡眠表型的迁移。

Result: SleepFM-2在疾病预测和睡眠评分上优于SleepFM；结合年龄、性别和BMI后，在两个独立队列（包括预训练中未见的医疗系统）中对215种EHR表型达到了预设判别与显著性标准，其中155种表型超出人口统计信息的增量。冻结编码器在睡眠事件检测上与专家评分者一致，跨六个加速度计队列改善睡眠分期，在UK Biobank中达到与直接在加速度计上预训练模型相当的疾病预测性能；同时还捕捉到传统PSG摘要未能恢复的主观睡眠信息。

Conclusion: 多模态睡眠生理数据能够提供跨疾病、临床任务、传感器类型和主观体验的可迁移人类健康表征。SleepFM-2验证了睡眠基础模型在大规模多中心数据上的实用价值，其与人口统计特征的组合可复现地预测多种后续临床表型。

Abstract: Sleep provides a nightly window into health by capturing coordinated activity across the brain, heart, muscles and respiratory system. We introduce SleepFM-2, a sleep foundation model developed and evaluated on 282,511 polysomnography recordings from 26 cohorts, including 235,865 used for pretraining. These data span more than two million hours of multimodal physiology. Compared with SleepFM, SleepFM-2 improves disease prediction and sleep scoring, supports arousal, limb movement and respiratory event detection, and transfers to wearable sensing and subjective sleep phenotypes. A model combining its PSG representation with age, sex and BMI met a prespecified discrimination and significance criterion for 215 subsequently recorded EHR phenotypes in two held-out cohorts, including one health system unseen during pretraining. For 155 phenotypes, the PSG representation added reproducible information beyond demographics. SleepFM-2 also outperformed a 480-feature baseline derived from the same recordings. Its disease scores revealed a reproducible principal component associated with reduced sigma-band spatial coupling and increased hypnodensity entropy. The frozen encoder performed within the observed range of expert scorers for sleep events and transferred to wakeful EEG, headband and in-ear EEG, wrist PPG and wrist accelerometry. It improved sleep staging across six accelerometry cohorts and achieved disease-prediction performance in UK Biobank similar to models pretrained directly on accelerometry. Finally, SleepFM-2 captured aspects of subjective sleep not recovered by conventional PSG summaries, particularly reports of the recorded night. These results show that multimodal sleep physiology can provide a transferable representation of human health across diseases, clinical tasks, sensors and subjective experience.

</details>


### [28] [A visual large language foundational model for medical image recognition using clinician-oriented social media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06914)
*Lingxuan Hou,Yuhua Xie,Yue Hu,Yan Zhuang,Junqi Li,Chengzhi Xia,Binh Phu Nguyen,Abubakar Siddique,Minh Nguyen,Yao Hou,Yanju Bao,Kexin Liu,Ke Chen,Jianjun Sun,Zeqi Li,Trung Nguyen,Jiangli Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用去标识化的医学图像和临床专家在社交媒体上的评论，通过先进LLM与临床医生介入验证的流程构建了ThoughtMed-1M长格式医学VQA数据集（含超100万对问答），并基于此训练了FOLTMed模型，在42个医学VQA基准上取得SOTA表现（宏准确率85.4%），事实性和相似度指标上超越现有模型3-5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医学领域显示出潜力，但受限于缺乏能够捕捉临床推理和显式图像-文本对齐的医学视觉问答（VQA）数据集，阻碍了其在医疗场景中的应用。

Method: 利用面向临床医生的社交媒体上分享的去标识化医学图像和专家评论，结合先进LLM与临床医生在环（clinician-in-the-loop）验证，构建了严格的流水线来生成ThoughtMed-1M数据集——包含超过100万VQA对，捕捉结构化临床逻辑和医学图像-文本对齐；在此基础上训练FOundational LLM Trained on ThoughtMed-1M（FOLTMed）模型。

Result: FOLTMed在42个医学VQA基准数据集上取得最佳性能，宏准确率达85.4%，并在ThoughtMed-1M测试集上生成更具临床连贯性的回复；在事实性和相似度指标上超越现有最先进模型3-5%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种可扩展的数据构建范式，为推进临床扎根的多模态大语言模型研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across diverse domains, showing considerable potential in medicine. However, their application in medical settings remains limited by the scarcity of visual question answering (VQA) datasets that capture clinical reasoning and explicit image-text alignment. Here, we leverage de-identified medical images and expert commentaries shared on clinician-oriented social media. By combining an advanced LLM with clinician-in-the-loop verification, we established a rigorous pipeline to construct ThoughtMed-1M, a long-form medical VQA dataset containing over one million VQA pairs and designed to capture structured clinical logic and medical image-text alignment. To demonstrate its utility, we developed a FOundational LLM Trained on ThoughtMed-1M (FOLTMed). FOLTMed achieved state-of-the-art performance across 42 medical VQA benchmark datasets, with a macro accuracy of 85.4%, and generated more clinically coherent responses on the ThoughtMed-1M test set. It outperformed state-of-the-art models by 3--5% across factuality and similarity metrics, highlighting a scalable paradigm for advancing research on clinically grounded multimodal LLMs.

</details>


### [29] [When and Why LLM Causal Priors Help: Closed-Loop Prior Selection for Amortized Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06941)
*Haohao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环先验选择框架，将LLM蒸馏因果图注入先验数据拟合网络（PFN）作为一个预算约束优化问题，在7.34M参数的Do-PFN上实现了2.75倍的形式显著性能提升，并通过机制实验证明增益依赖于蒸馏图的语义内容而非结构多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN（先验数据拟合网络）amortize因果效应估计任务，但其合成训练先验目前依赖手工设计，这是Do-PFN和CausalPFN共同承认的瓶颈。LLM能够为给定领域"绘制"合理的因果图，可作为先验材料，但缺乏系统性方法判断是否注入、增益来源以及何时注入，实践依赖手动试错。

Method: 提出闭环先验选择框架：将先验注入形式化为候选先验池上的预算约束优化问题；候选先验经过廉价后训练，用以真实域泛化为主导的复合指标打分；胜出者接受完整训练并进行成对统计验证。

Result: 在7.34M参数的Do-PFN上，框架胜出的先验在主评估域上取得2.75倍的正式显著增益，误差低于未注入的官方基线；相邻监测域的泛化显著改善，且无监测能力退化。机制实验显示增益依赖蒸馏图的语义内容而非仅结构多样性（方向性证据）。

Conclusion: 该框架使LLM因果先验的使用从手动试错转变为经验可验证的选择问题。语义性先验注入的规律性也得到了确认，为后续研究者提供了可复用的方法论。

Abstract: Causal effect estimation asks how an outcome would change under an intervention, and medicine, economics, and public policy all treat it as a foundational task. Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) amortize the task: a model trained on large numbers of programmatically generated synthetic causal tasks reads a new problem's observational data into context and returns an interventional-effect estimate in a single forward pass. The capability of such models is largely determined by the synthetic training prior, which is currently designed by hand, a bottleneck acknowledged by both Do-PFN and CausalPFN. Large language models (LLMs) can now ``draw'' plausible causal graphs for a given domain, suggesting that LLM-distilled graphs could serve as prior material. Whether injecting such graphs helps at all, where any gain comes from, and when injection helps. Practice has so far relied on manual trial and error. We propose a \emph{closed-loop prior selection framework} that casts prior injection as a budget-constrained optimization over a candidate prior pool. Candidates undergo cheap post-training and are scored by a composite metric dominated by real-domain generalization; the winner then receives full training and paired statistical validation. On a 7.34M-parameter Do-PFN, the framework's winner attains a formally significant $2.75\times$ gain on the primary evaluation domain, and its error falls below that of the uninjected official base. Generalization on an adjacent monitoring domain improves significantly, and no monitored capability degrades. Mechanism experiments show that the gain depends on the semantic content of the distilled graph rather than its structural diversity alone does not produce it (directional evidence). With this framework and this regularity in hand, the use of LLM causal priors stops being manual trial and error and becomes an empirically verifiable selection problem.

</details>


### [30] [iBrain: A Unified Foundation Model Reading the Brain from Surface to Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06960)
*Ying Chen,Tiou Wang,Zhifeng Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出iBrain，一个统一的神经基础模型，能够从颅内脑电（iEEG）和神经元峰电位（spiking activity）两种异质侵入性神经记录信号中联合学习，使用信号特异性编码器和共享时空Transformer骨干，在7000多小时数据上预训练，并在多个基准上超越单信号基线，展现跨记录设置的迁移性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经基础模型大多针对单一侵入性记录范式独立开发，缺乏对不同侵入性神经信号（如iEEG和峰电位）的联合预训练探索。由于这些信号在空间和时间尺度上存在显著差异，如何统一建模异质侵入性记录信号成为一个未被充分研究的关键问题。

Method: iBrain采用信号特异性编码器以适应不同信号（iEEG和峰电位）的独特特征，并构建共享的时空Transformer骨干来建模跨通道和跨时间的依赖关系。模型在超过7000小时的异质神经记录数据上，通过掩码信号重建（masked signal reconstruction）和通道视图对齐（channel-view alignment）两种预训练任务进行联合学习。

Result: iBrain在多个基准测试中一致性地优于单信号预训练基线，实现了最先进的性能。进一步的实验表明iBrain在多样化的记录设置下展现出良好的迁移性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 在异质侵入性神经记录上进行联合预训练具有巨大潜力，能够支撑可扩展的神经建模，并在不同记录设置和下游任务间获得可迁移的表示。

Abstract: Invasive neural recordings provide high-fidelity measurements of brain activity, with signals such as intracranial EEG (iEEG) and intracortical spiking activity capturing neural dynamics at different spatial and temporal scales. Yet existing neural foundation models have largely been developed independently for different invasive recording paradigms, leaving joint pretraining across heterogeneous invasive signals underexplored. In this work, we introduce iBrain, a unified foundation model that jointly learns from iEEG and spiking activity. iBrain employs signal-specific encoders to accommodate their distinct signal characteristics and a shared spatiotemporal Transformer backbone to model dependencies across recording channels and time. We pretrain iBrain on over 7,000 hours of heterogeneous neural recordings using masked signal reconstruction and channel-view alignment, promoting contextual modeling of neural dynamics and robustness across different channels. iBrain consistently outperforms single-signal pretraining baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks. Further experiments demonstrate that iBrain exhibits transferability and data efficiency across diverse recording settings. These results highlight the potential of joint pretraining on heterogeneous invasive neural recordings to support scalable neural modeling and transferable representations across recording settings and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [31] [VST: Verifiable Structured Transport for Auditable Agent-to-Agent Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07065)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对Agent-to-Agent(Alpha挖掘中采矿与评估智能体间因自由文本通信缺乏稳定契约、无法回放而导致的反馈循环低效问题，提出将通信重构为"类型化、可因果寻址、单播记录"的结构化协议，形成因果轨迹，并在其上部署多类型控制器与事务性验证-跳跃控制器。结果表明结构是核心贡献而非精度：同等信息量的自由文本通道可达到相同预测命中率，但类型化带来可模式校验、可确定性回放和可构造性防泄漏等可审计性优势。在CSI 1000样本外测试中，单一运行是八种方法中唯一在因子层面保持正的中位年化收益和Sharpe的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当代LLM多智能体系统中，Alpha挖掘涉及采矿与评估智能体间的反复反馈循环，而智能体之间的交接采用无稳定契约、不可重放的自然语言消息，这种自由形式的通信模式严重拖慢了A2A的Alpha发现过程。

Method: 首先将智能体间通信重构为类型化、可因果寻址、单播记录的结构化A2A协议，使提交流形成因果轨迹。在该轨迹上部署一个带四个类型化头的单一预测器，用来预测若干周期后两名采矿者将收到的累积指导；随后引入事务性验证-跳跃控制器，仅在通过四级门控时才提交多周期投机性结果，否则回滚到确切的前序状态。

Result: 在CSI 1000样本外留出集上，单一运行是八种方法（七个基线加本方法）中唯一一个在因子层面保持正的中位年化收益和Sharpe的方法，尽管超越基准的中位超额收益对所有方法（含本方法）均为负。其开发期选出的前20组合在优化区间内划分的样本上达到0.71的中位留出Sharpe。对照消融表明，同等信息量的自由文本通道能够达到相同的预测器命中率。所有结果均为描述性报告，不计成本。

Conclusion: 本工作的核心贡献在于结构化本身而非预测精度：类型化所提供的核心价值是一种可构造性（by construction）的可审计状态——可进行模式校验、确定性回放、并从构造上防止预测结果泄漏给评估器，而非经验压力测试的保证。同时作者坦承单次运行结果的局限性，未将跳跃机制的效果与其继承的搜索基座相分离，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Agent-to-agent (A2A) alpha discovery is slowed by repeated feedback cycles between mining and evaluation agents, whose hand-offs, in contemporary LLM multi-agent systems, are free-form natural-language messages that carry no stable contract and cannot be replayed. We first restructure this communication as a structured agent-to-agent protocol of \emph{typed, causally addressable, unicast records}, so that the committed stream forms a causal trajectory. On that trajectory a single predictor with four typed heads forecasts the accumulated guidance the two miners would receive several cycles ahead; a transactional verify--leap controller then commits a multi-cycle speculative outcome only when it passes a four-level gate, and otherwise rolls back to the exact prior state. Structure is the enabling contribution, and its value is not accuracy. A controlled ablation shows an equal-information free-text channel reaches the same predictor hit rate. What typing provides is a state that can be schema-checked, replayed deterministically, and prevented by construction from leaking a forecast to an evaluator: auditability by construction, not an empirically stress-tested guarantee. On a CSI~1000 out-of-sample holdout, our single run is the only one among eight methods (seven baselines and ours) to hold a positive median annualized return and Sharpe at the factor level, though the median return \emph{in excess} of the benchmark stays negative for every method including ours; its development-selected top-20 portfolios reach a $0.71$ median holdout Sharpe, selected on a split inside the optimization horizon. We report these single-run results descriptively, gross of costs, and are explicit about their limits throughout; in particular we do not isolate the effect of the leap machinery from the inherited search substrate, which we leave to future work.

</details>


### [32] [Risk Is Not Review Value: Wrong-Answer Exposure Under Bounded Review Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07095)
*SangJin Park,Myungsub Choi,Jineok Kim,Minseung Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM助手在有限审查预算下如何优先审查生成答案的问题，提出将审查优先级建模为暴露度降低（exposure reduction）问题，结合错误概率、可修复性、影响和成本四个维度进行排序，并提出WAER和PRRE两个新评估指标。在720项TAT-QA/SciFact基准上，该方法在20%预算下保持WAER几乎不变（0.605 vs 0.600），同时将PRRE从0.881降至0.716。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助手产出的答案数量远超人类在用户看到之前可以审查的量，而现有评估大多只判断答案是否正确、有无依据或置信度高低。在有界审查预算下，需要确定哪些答案应优先被检查，但仅凭风险不够——高风险答案可能难以修复，而中等风险答案反而可能从现有证据中直接纠正。因此需要设计结合风险、可修复性和影响的优先级排序方法。

Method: 将审查优先级问题建模为暴露降低（exposure reduction）问题。每个候选答案的审查价值由四个因子加权组合：估计的错误概率（wrongness）、干预可操作性（intervention affordance，即该答案是否可通过基准证据被修复）、影响（impact）和修复成本（cost）。评估采用两个指标：WAER（Wrong-Answer Exposure Ratio，错误答案未被审查的比例）和PRRE（Post-Repair Residual Exposure，确定性基准支持修复后仍暴露的错误比例）。PRRE使用独立的可修复性规则进行评估，不重复利用排序用的可操作性分数，避免评估偏差。

Result: 在包含720个条目的TAT-QA/SciFact压力基准上，基于审查价值（review-value）的排序在20%审查预算下使答案级WAER几乎不变（0.605 vs 0.600），但将PRRE从0.881显著降至0.716，表明该方法能够在不算错答案漏检的情况下大幅降低经过修复后仍暴露给用户的错误。

Conclusion: 值得信赖的LLM评估不应仅关注错误检测率，还应衡量在有限审查能力下如何有效地减少暴露给用户的错误答案。将可修复性纳入审查优先级排序能够在同等预算下显著降低修复后仍暴露的错误，为LLM产出的可信保障提供了新的评估范式。

Abstract: LLM assistants often produce more answers than humans can review before users see them. Most evaluations ask whether an answer is wrong, unsupported, or low-confidence. Bounded review budgets instead ask which answers should be checked first under a fixed review budget. Risk alone is not enough: a high-risk answer may be hard to repair, while a moderately risky answer may be directly correctable from available evidence. For generated-answer evaluation, we model review prioritization as exposure reduction, where review value combines estimated wrongness, intervention affordance, impact, and cost. We evaluate review queues with Wrong-Answer Exposure Ratio (WAER), the fraction of wrong answers left unreviewed, and post-repair residual exposure (PRRE), the fraction still exposed after deterministic benchmark-supported repairs. PRRE uses repairability rules that do not numerically reuse the affordance scores used for ranking. On a 720-item TAT-QA/SciFact stress benchmark, review-value ranking keeps answer-level WAER nearly unchanged at 20% budget (0.605 vs. 0.600) but lowers PRRE from 0.881 to 0.716. These results show that trustworthy LLM evaluation should measure not only error detection, but also how limited review capacity reduces exposed wrong answers.

</details>


### [33] [EmoMed: An Emotionally-Aware Agent for Multimodal Medical Support with Real-Time Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07194)
*Ivan Nasonov,Nikita Glazkov,Ivan Makovetskiy,Mikhail Mozikov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Andrey Savchenko,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EmoMed，一个多模态医疗咨询智能体，能够根据用户的情绪状态（焦虑、困惑、紧迫感）自适应调整回复语气、结构和详细程度，同时通过双重检索机制（网络事实核查与API连接的知识库）保持临床准确性。在七个SOTA语言模型上评测，结果表明情感自适应响应在多维度上优于中性基线且不牺牲临床准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗咨询场景中，患者常处于焦虑、困惑等情绪状态，传统医疗AI以中立、统一的语气回复，缺乏对患者情绪状态的理解和回应，可能在降低患者体验和沟通效果的同时影响医患信任。现有医疗代理多侧重医学事实准确性，忽略了情绪适应性在医患沟通中的关键作用。

Method: EmoMed系统包含三个核心组件：(1) 多模态输入处理，同时处理文本和医学图像；(2) 情绪/情感识别模块，从用户输入中检测焦虑、困惑、紧迫性等情感指标；根据情感状态调节回复的语气、结构和细节层次。(3) 双重检索机制保障事实可靠性，包括网络事实核查和通过API连接的持续更新的医学知识库。评估使用LLM-as-judge、MedQA风格准确性测试、BERT Score、安全性/有用性评分及多模态医学基准，覆盖GPT-4/5、Qwen3、Llama 4、Gemini 2.5、Grok4、Claude3七个模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，情感自适应响应在所有评估维度上持续优于中性基线，且不损害临床准确率。受控用户研究证实了这一发现，参与者报告了更高的感知共情度和沟通清晰度，同时保持了对事实准确性的信任。

Conclusion: 将情感感知整合到医疗咨询代理中可以同时提升共情体验和维持临床准确性，为下一代人性化医疗AI系统提供了可行方案与开源实现。

Abstract: We present EmoMed - a multimodal medical consultation agent that adapts its responses based on users' emotional states while maintaining clinical accuracy. The system processes text and medical images, detects affect indicators (anxiety, confusion, urgency) from user input, and adjusts response tone, structure, and detail level accordingly. To ensure factual reliability, the agent grounds clinical information through a dual retrieval mechanism: web-based fact-checking and an API-connected, continuously updated medical knowledge base. We evaluate our approach across seven state-of-the-art language models (GPT-4/5, Qwen3, Llama 4, Gemini 2.5, Grok4, Claude3) using comprehensive metrics including LLM-as-judge assessments, MedQA style accuracy tests, BERT Score, safety/helpfulness ratings, and multimodal medical benchmarks. The results demonstrate that emotionally adaptive responses consistently outperform neutral baseline across evaluation dimensions, without compromising clinical accuracy. A controlled user study validated these findings, with participants reporting improved perceived empathy and communication clarity, while maintaining trust in factual accuracy. Source code: https://github.com/NasonovIvan/EmoMed-Agent

</details>


### [34] [Agentic Algorithm Engineering: Improving Shared-Memory Exact Minimum Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07204)
*David A. Bader,Adil Chhabra,Ernestine Großmann,Monika Henzinger,Alexander Noe,Christian Schulz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"智能体算法工程"(AAE)的新方法论，利用自主大语言模型智能体在现有代码库上循环执行算法工程流程（提出假设→实现→基准测试→保留或丢弃改动）。作者将该方法应用于最小割问题的VieCut求解器上，在已手工调优的基础上进一步获得显著加速：DIMACS核心实例上顺序版提升1.28倍、32线程并行版提升1.63倍，真实世界k-core上分别提升6.26倍和127倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
