Motivation: 最小割问题是图算法中的经典问题，作者此前已工程化了一系列快速算法并发布为VieCut开源包，但传统的手工算法工程调优已达瓶颈。作者希望探索一种新的自动化方法论，让大语言模型智能体自主完成算法工程的迭代循环，从而在现有代码基础上发现人类手工调优难以察觉的优化点。

Method: 提出智能体算法工程（AAE）方法论：多个自主LLM智能体在现有VieCut代码库上运行算法工程周期，包括形成关于运行时间损耗的假设、实现改进、在固定实例集上基准测试，并据此保留或丢弃改动。智能体通过迭代循环不断优化算法。

Result: 在已大量人工调优的算法上，AAE仍发现显著优化：真实世界k-core实例上顺序版提升1.28倍、32线程并行版提升1.63倍；DIMACS核心实例上顺序版提升6.26倍、并行版提升127倍。

Even though we had already tuned our algorithm by hand extensively, the agent finds significant optimizations, in particular on the DIMACS core instances: factors of 1.28 (sequential) and 1.63 (32 threads) on real-world k-cores, and 6.26 and 127 on the DIMACS core instances.

</details>

We propose the effective interaction frontier hypothesis: a dynamic boundary beyond which additional interactions yield diminishing returns while cost grows linearly. Method: 提出SkillAlign：一种provider无关的框架。将候选技能表示为多视图程序卡片，并通过多种替代暴露接口渲染，包括完整指令、提示、压缩摘要、工作流或不暴露。支持反事实评估——固定任务、智能体和候选技能，仅改变暴露接口。在ALFWorld和SkillsBench上比较不同暴露形式的效果，并在ALFWorld上做基于回放(replay)的策略学习分析。

Conclusion: 技能增强的智能体不仅应优化选择哪些技能，还应优化这些技能如何被呈现。暴露方式的建模与学习是提升技能利用效用的重要与尚未充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on skills: reusable procedural knowledge for reasoning, tool use, and interaction. Existing work studies how skills are acquired, retrieved, compressed, or composed, but often assumes that once a skill is selected, its interface to the agent is fixed. We argue that this overlooks a key source of skill utility: the same skill can help, distract, or mislead depending on how it is exposed. We propose SkillAlign, a provider-agnostic framework that represents candidate skills as multi-view procedural cards and renders them through alternative exposure interfaces, including full instructions, hints, compressed summaries, workflows, or no exposure. This enables counterfactual evaluation where the task, agent, and candidate skills are fixed while only the exposure interface varies. Across ALFWorld and SkillsBench, we show that exposure form substantially affects task success and rendered context cost, and that compact top-k exposure can outperform full-library injection. We further conduct a replay-based policy-learning analysis on ALFWorld, showing that adaptive exposure contains learnable signal but remains far from oracle selection. Our results suggest that skill-augmented agents should optimize not only which skills to use, but also how those skills are presented.

</details>

Method: 提出了检索增强信息提取方法AAS-RAIL。其核心是基于检索增强的上下文学习（RAIL）管线：从已有AAS实例中检索LLM生成的提取辅助示例，为每个待处理的PDF数据手册动态选择公司特定的AAS示例，将静态提示替换为结合语义检索与结构化信息提取的自适应流水线，从而无需微调即可适应公司特定的命名约定和格式风格。

Result: 在工业产品数据手册数据集上，使用多种开源和闭源大语言模型（如不同权重的LLM）进行评测。实验结果显示，RAIL相比传统少样本提示一致地提升了提取质量，取得了30.4%-52.4%的相对改进幅度。

Conclusion: 检索增强的上下文学习方法能够有效改善公司特定AAS生成的提取质量，提供了相对传统静态少样本提示的显著优势，验证了该方法的实用性和通用性。

Abstract: The Asset Administration Shell (AAS) is a cornerstone of Industry 4.0 and the Digital Product Passport, providing standardized digital representations of industrial assets. While manufacturers already maintain extensive technical product documentation, generating AAS instances from existing product datasheets remains a labor-intensive task because technical information is extracted from heterogeneous document structures and often involves company-specific terminology and conventions. In this work, we present AAS-RAIL, a retrieval-augmented information extraction (IE) approach that automatically generates Asset Administration Shells from PDF product datasheets using large language models (LLMs). Instead of relying on a fixed set of few-shot examples, the proposed retrieval-augmented in-context learning (RAIL) approach retrieves LLM-generated extraction helpers from similar Asset Administration Shells to provide instance-specific in-context learning (ICL). This enables the model to adapt its extraction behavior to company-specific naming conventions and formatting styles without fine-tuning. Our core contribution is the dynamic selection of company-specific AAS examples for each datasheet, replacing static prompting with an extraction pipeline that adapts to instances and combines semantic retrieval and structured information extraction. The proposed approach is evaluated on a collection of industrial product datasheets using a selection of open- and closed-weight LLMs. Experimental results show that RAIL consistently improves extraction quality over conventional few-shot prompting, yielding relative improvements of 30.4-52.4%. These results demonstrate that our approach provides an effective improvement for company-specific AAS generation.

</details>


### [38] [From Simulated Citizens to Simulated Deliberation: Challenges in Representation and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07573)
*Chaemin Jang,Junsik Min,Jaewoo Choi,Donggyu Lee,Haiin Lee,Junyoung Park,Namhee Kim,Hyunwoo Kim,Jungwon Kim,Juho Kim,Nuri Kim,Jihee Kim*

TL;DR: 本研究使用基于人口普查数据的韩语人格代理就真实的政策问题进行辩论，并以全国调查为基准进行评估。结果显示：人格代理无法可靠复现人口意见模式，辩论虽能产生推理充分、互惠且多样的论点并伴随显著的立场变化，但这种变化很大程度上并非源于同伴交流——密封独白代理的立场变化率和最终立场平衡与完整辩论几乎相同。人口信息锚定的初始位置反而强烈抑制意见更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM审议被视为可扩展的公共审议模拟方式。要使此类模拟具有信息价值，人格代理需要反映人口意见模式，且交互应塑造其结论。作者旨在评估LLM基础审议能否满足这两个条件。

Method: 使用基于人口普查数据构建的韩语人格代理（persona agents）就真实政策问题进行辩论，以全国性调查数据为基准，比较代理的回应与人口意见模式；同时设计对照实验，包括密封独白（sealed-monologue）条件、不同初始立场的分组、以及人口信息锚定的起始位置，以分离交互对立场变化的影响。

Result: 人格代理不能可靠复现人口意见模式——回应常过于集中且经常逆转人类数据中的（亚）人口统计差异。审议确实产生有推理、互惠且多样的论点及大量立场变动；然而密封独白代理的变化率和最终立场平衡与完整辩论几乎相同，不同初始立场的群体常收敛到相似终点；锚定人口信息的起始位置则显著抑制立场更新。

Conclusion: 人口代表性、论点生成和交互驱动的意见变化并不必然同时成立。模拟能轻易产生正反两方论点，但能否捕捉人类视角的多样性仍未验证。论点涌现（argument surfacing）是LLM审议的潜在有前景用途，而人口模拟仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation has been explored as a scalable way to simulate public deliberation. For such simulations to be informative, persona agents should reflect population opinion patterns and interaction should shape their conclusions. We evaluate whether LLM-based deliberation can meet these two conditions using census-grounded Korean personas debating real policy questions benchmarked against national surveys. Persona agents do not reliably reproduce population opinion patterns: responses are often far more concentrated and frequently reverse demographic differences in the human data. Deliberations nonetheless produce reasoned, reciprocal, and varied arguments alongside substantial stance movement. Yet much of this movement does not require peer exchange: sealed-monologue agents change position at similar rates and reach nearly the same final balance as full debates, while groups initialized with very different positions often converge to similar endpoints. Anchoring population-informed starting positions, meanwhile, sharply suppresses updating. Thus, population representation, argument generation, and interaction-driven opinion change do not necessarily go together. The simulations readily surface arguments on both sides, though whether they capture the diversity of human perspectives remains untested, leaving open a promising role for argument surfacing even as population simulation requires further validation.

</details>

*Hongnan Zhao,Shiyu Chen,Zhihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Aegix Pulse，一个生产级三段式架构，将当前任务澄清与Task Persona确立、长期品牌基因（Account Profile/Brand DNA）组装、以及控制生成与修订（保留版本溯源）相分离。通过96个合成社交媒体生成任务和1,440次盲评LLM-Judge评估，发现Account Profile使品牌一致性提高0.1562分，上下文保留式修订使任务保真度提高0.2917分，但两项改进在多重比较校正后均未达到统计学显著性——仅提供初步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的内容生成系统需要同时整合用户的即时任务、长期品牌身份、历史证据和修订反馈，但现有系统缺乏将短期任务上下文与长期品牌上下文分离并系统整合的架构设计。

Method: 提出Aegix Pulse三阶段架构：(1) 当前任务澄清与Task Persona确定；(2) 长期Account Profile（品牌基因）组装；(3) 受控生成与修订并保留版本溯源。实验采用96个合成社交媒体任务，设置四个初始生成条件（逐步引入Task Persona、Account Profile、成功历史风格证据）和两个修订条件（普通修订 vs 上下文保留式修订），生成480条记录并由1,440次盲评LLM-Judge评估加人工复核。

Result: Account Profile较Task Persona单独使品牌一致性平均提高0.1562分（五点量表，Holm校正p=.1224）；上下文保留式修订较普通修订使任务保真度平均提高0.2917分（Holm校正p=.2432）。两项改进在多重比较校正后均不具统计显著性。Task Persona单独有较小观察效应，成功历史证据未提供额外品牌一致性改进。人工验证未一致复现LLM-Judge效果方向，且评审者间一致性低。

Conclusion: 研究为持久品牌上下文和上下文保留式修订提供初步证据，但效果未达统计显著，需加强证据处理和评估方法。识别出增强统计功效与改进评测一致性的优先级方向。

Abstract: Production content-generation systems must integrate a user's immediate task, long-term brand identity, historical evidence, and revision feedback. We present Aegix Pulse, a production-oriented three-stage architecture that separates current-task clarification and Task Persona finalization, long-term Account Profile (Brand DNA) assembly, and controlled generation and revision while preserving provenance across content versions.
  We evaluate four preregistered claims using 96 synthetic social-media generation tasks. Four initial-generation conditions progressively introduced a Task Persona, Account Profile, and successful-history style evidence, while two revision conditions compared plain and context-preserving revision. The experiment produced 480 completed generation records and 1,440 blinded LLM-Judge evaluations, supplemented by human review.
  Adding the Account Profile increased mean brand-consistency scores by 0.1562 points on a five-point scale compared with Task Persona alone (Holm-adjusted p=.1224). Preserving task and brand context during revision increased mean task-preservation scores by 0.2917 points compared with plain revision (Holm-adjusted p=.2432). Neither improvement was statistically conclusive after multiple-comparison correction. Task Persona alone showed a small observed effect, while successful-history evidence provided no additional improvement in brand consistency under the current setting. Human validation did not consistently reproduce the LLM-Judge effect directions and showed low inter-reviewer agreement. These findings provide preliminary evidence for persistent brand context and context-preserving revision while identifying priorities for stronger evidence processing and evaluation.

</details>


### [40] [What Does an LLM-Agent Leaderboard Rank Actually Compare?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07785)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"排行榜排名高即更优"的默认假设，提出了一个估计目标感知的成对比较程序，在SWE-bench等多个基准上证明细分领域中的排名差异常常无法被确定，代理标签和效用规则会改变模型选择结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理排行榜通常被解读为排名高的代理即更好的代理，但公开评测日志在任务混合、标签来源、发布细节或成本规则不同的情况下，可能无法支持这种成对优越性结论。作者旨在揭示排行榜分数实际估计了什么，以及何时能真正支持"某一代理优于另一代理"的判断。

Method: 提出了一个"估计目标感知的成对比较程序"（estimand-aware pairwise procedure），包含四个步骤：明确比较目标和测量来源、检查共同支持（common support）、使用明确的统计不确定性规则和实际显著性边际来评估差异、进行可控实验以检验决策标签在已知有限样本条件下的行为。该方法在SWE-bench、AgentRewardBench和tau2-bench三个基准上进行了实证测试，并利用DataAgentBench和Open Agent检验了更粗粒度公开记录的可用性。

Result: 研究发现：在多个基准上，排名接近的差异常常无法被确定（unresolved）；使用代理标签（proxy labels）和不同的效用规则可能改变最终选择的系统；排行榜分数只能总结一次发布的评测结果，而细粒度的优越性声明还取决于估计目标和解释差异时使用的不确定性规则。数据越粗粒度（DataAgentBench和Open Agent），可估计的内容越受限。

Conclusion: 排行榜分数所支持的结论应被严格区分：排行榜分数仅是对已发布评测的汇总，而"某代理优于后者"的精确声明必须额外依赖估计目标和不确定性规则。研究者应避免将排行榜排名直接等同于代理质量排序，尤其在系统间任务混合、标签来源、发布细节或成本规则存在差异时。

Abstract: An LLM-agent leaderboard invites a familiar inference: an agent ranked above another is the better agent. Public evaluation logs may not support that conclusion when systems differ in task mixture, label source, release detail, or cost rule. We study what leaderboard scores estimate and when they justify pairwise superiority conclusions. Our estimand-aware pairwise procedure states the comparison target and measurement source, checks common support, and evaluates the supported difference using a stated uncertainty rule and practical margin. Controlled checks evaluate the decision labels under known finite-sample conditions and show why uncertainty must be included when judging sensitivity to target reweighting. Across SWE-bench, AgentRewardBench, and tau2-bench, close rank differences are often unresolved; proxy labels and utility rules can also change which system is selected. DataAgentBench and Open Agent show what remains estimable from coarser public records. A leaderboard score summarizes a released evaluation, whereas a fine-grained superiority claim additionally depends on the estimand and uncertainty rule used to interpret the difference.

</details>


### [41] [Do Large Language Models Know What They Don't Know II? A Fully Behavioral, Non-Cognitive Measure of Epistemic Honesty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07879)
*Ali Şenol,H. Russell Bernard,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了认识论诚实商数（EHQ）这一新指标，通过构建包含3000个问题的基准数据集EHQ-3000，系统评估了大语言模型是否知道自身知识边界（即"知道自己不知道什么"）。通过对21个模型API路由中的14个进行确认性分析，发现EHQ综合分数在0.31至0.81之间呈显著差异，且这些差异无法仅凭模型提取显式信息的能力来解释，揭示了传统基于正确率评估方法无法捕捉的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估通常只关注回答的正确性，但一个自然且重要的问题是：模型是否意识到自己不知道什么？LLM常常表现得自信且流畅，这掩盖了其在知识边界处的认知盲区。作者借用认识论诚实（epistemic honesty）的概念，旨在开发一种能系统评估LLM对自身知识边界认知能力的指标，弥补仅以正确率为中心的评估体系的不足。

Method: 1) 借鉴认识论诚实概念，提出EHQ（Epistemic Honesty Quotient）指标，包含三个可观测子评分，覆盖两个操作轴：认知约束（epistemic restraint）和实质回答校准（substantive-answer calibration）。2) 构建EHQ-3000基准集，包含3000个问题，涵盖四类场景：虚构实体（Fabricated Entity）、发布时间线之后的事件（Post-Cutoff Event）、超小众真实知识（Hyper-Niche True）和上下文条件问题（Context-Conditioned Questions）。3) 从21个模型API路由中筛选，经端点和资格检查后有15个完成协议，其中14个进入确认性分析（排除一个因供应商端严重截断导致分数不确定的路由）。

Result: 1) 综合EHQ分数在0.31至0.81之间，呈现显著的模型间差异。2) 模型在基于文档的能⼒探针上表现接近天花板，但EHQ却存在大差异，表明这些行为差异无法用模型提取显式可用信息的能力来解释。3) 两个认知约束标准在当前类别构成下高度重叠，而实质回答校准因模型而异，且与认知约束不能可靠地共变。4) 由于测试面板较小，各项估计仍存在较大的不确定性。

Conclusion: EHQ揭示了传统以正确率为中心的评估无法观测到的行为差异，证明了"认识论诚实"是一个正交于能力（capability）的独立评估维度。研究同时指出，数据集构成、供应商端行为（如截断）和置信度诱导方式都必须纳入EHQ的解读框架中，否则可能产生误导性结论。这一工作为更全面地评估LLM的可靠性提供了一种新的行为学视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are frequently confident, eloquent, and well versed. A natural question arises: do they know what they don't know? To answer this question, we borrow the concept of epistemic honesty and develop a novel metric to systematically evaluate whether an LLM appropriately acknowledges the boundaries of its knowledge. In this work, we introduce the Epistemic Honesty Quotient (EHQ), which reports three observable sub-scores across two operational axes (epistemic restraint and substantive-answer calibration), and construct EHQ-3000, a 3,000-question benchmark spanning Fabricated Entity, Post-Cutoff Event, Hyper-Niche True, and Context-Conditioned Questions. From a frozen registry of 21 model API routes, 15 completed the protocol after endpoint and eligibility checks; 14 entered the confirmatory analysis because severe provider-side truncation made one route's score indeterminate. The study reveals substantial variation across models, including a difference that can not be explained by their capability to extract explicitly available information. Composite EHQ ranges from 0.31 to 0.81 across the analysed panel, despite near-ceiling performance on the document-grounded capability probe. The two restraint criteria overlap strongly under the present category composition, whereas substantive-answer calibration varies across models and does not reliably co-vary with restraint; however, the small panel leaves substantial uncertainty. Thus, EHQ reveals behavioral differences that are not visible to conventional correctness-based assessment, while also showing why dataset composition, provider behavior, and confidence elicitation must remain part of the interpretation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究通常用能力基准评估量化成本，并认为4位量化对中等规模模型"近乎无损"。但作者质疑：当多个答案都有效时，量化是否会改变模型选择说什么？这是一个被忽略但影响实际的部署决策维度。

Method: 作者在固定硬件、软件和采样配置的条件下，对三种模型规模在三种精度下配对prompt和seed收集约71,000个完成结果，使用两组自定义且通过泄漏检查的prompt电池。分析采用预注册方式（三波分析在版本控制中预先指定），使用prompt配对的sign-flip检验、Holm校正等统计方法测度内容集中度、词汇多样性、文体特征和刻板印象方向。

Result: 8B下int4量化使品牌推荐集中度+5.1pp（Holm p=.023，全量再生验证+4.4pp），词汇多样性TTR下降0.011（标准化效应-0.51）；14B/32B无内容集中显著性但有文体漂移（em-dash率+0.46/0.61每千词）。刻板印象方向检验全为空。机制上决策token熵+0.091bits（p=.015）而语义碰撞+2.6pp（p=.023），token层更不可预测但语义层更重复。

Conclusion: 在最小测试的8B规模上，AWQ-int4量化显著缩小了模型建议范围。量化审计应同时评估输出集中度（concentration）和偏差（bias），而非仅依赖能力基准。token级与语义级分布的背离揭示了一个值得关注的量化效应机制。

Abstract: Weight quantization largely determines the economics of serving open-weight LLMs. Its costs are usually assessed with capability benchmarks, on which 4-bit quantization of mid-sized models is often considered "nearly free." We examine a different question: when several answers are valid, does quantization change what a model chooses to say? We serve three checkpoints (Qwen3-8B/14B/32B) at three weight precisions (W4A16 AWQ, W8A16 FP8-Marlin, and bf16), holding the hardware, software, and sampling configuration constant, and collect approximately 71,000 completions paired by prompt and seed across two custom, leak-checked prompt batteries. We pre-specified the analyses in three waves in version control. At 8B, int4 reduces output diversity: the probability that two samples for the same scenario recommend the same brand increases by 5.1 percentage points (prompt-paired sign-flip test, Holm p = .023; reproduced at +4.4pp on a full regeneration of the arm), and lexical diversity falls substantially (TTR -0.011, standardized effect -0.51; robust to a length-controlled measure). At 14B and 32B, no content-concentration measure reaches significance; instead, stylistic drift emerges (em-dash rate +0.46/1k words at 14B and +0.61/1k at 32B, both Holm p <= .0024). Pre-specified tests of stereotype direction are null at every scale: outputs concentrate on the modal answer for each prompt rather than on stereotypical answers. Mechanistically, the token-level distribution becomes flatter (decision-token entropy +0.091 bits, p = .015) while the semantic distribution, measured directly from first-token log probabilities, becomes more concentrated (collision +2.6pp, p = .023): individual tokens become less predictable even as meanings become more repetitive. At 8B, the smallest size tested, AWQ-int4 serving measurably narrows the range of suggestions; audits should assess concentration as well as bias.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体联邦需要在对抗条件下回答三个问题：谁参与(身份)、是否合规(执行)、谁决策(权威)。此外，负责监督联邦输出的验证机制能否同时引导生成-测试循环朝向更优答案，是一个独立且未解决的问题。

Method: 第一部分(PRIMUS)：将素幂身份与BLS聚合签名耦合(PIAC)；推导安全终止阈值以减少噪声下的误杀；推导单例治理优于拜占庭法定人数的经济边界(γ*≈9f)；采用带租约和栅栏的VRF继任机制以确保部分同步下无条件安全。第二部分：将PRIMA的二元工件保真度裁决转换为分级适应度信号，在二元覆盖码上完成二值到分级的校准，并对比确定性注入故障与真实LLM生成候选，测试其作为预过滤器及在显式优化迭代下的表现。

Result: 在10%信道噪声下安全终止阈值将误杀代理率从80%降至0.00%；经济边界γ*≈9f在n=50到10,000范围内保持平坦。校准方面，确定性故障注入下相关性强(ρ=0.676确定性、0.819全量)，但在真实LLM候选上降至0.158和0.406(约为校准值四分之一，即同设计者混淆效应)。作为预过滤器表现优于随机评分对照但仅略胜二元门控。400次优化迭代中未被博弈，但原因仅在于目标在一次诚实回答后即饱和。跨族评审保留了负担排序信号但破坏了个体判断。未产生覆盖码记录，程序成本为USD 164.78。

Conclusion: PRIMUS为多智能体联邦治理提供了可验证的安全性与经济性边界，但识别出五个模型内不可修复的问题作为范围边界。分级验证器转换存在严重的同设计者混淆效应，验证器不宜直接作为求解器信号使用，二元门控仍具竞争力，对生成-测试循环的引导能力有限。

Abstract: Multi-agent federations need governance that answers three questions under adversarial conditions: who participated (identity), did they conform (enforcement), and who decides (authority). A separate question is whether the verification machinery that polices a federation's outputs can also steer a generate-and-test loop toward better answers.
  Part I. PRIMA introduced prime-power agent identity and a consensus token whose factorization indexes participation, but assumed honest agents. We present PRIMUS, which couples prime-power identity with BLS aggregate signatures (PIAC), derives a safe-kill threshold that reduces false-positive agent termination from 80% to 0.00% under 10% channel noise, gives the closed-form economic boundary where singleton governance outperforms Byzantine quorum ($γ^* \approx 9f$, verified flat across n = 50 to 10,000), and specifies VRF succession with lease and fencing that makes safety unconditional under partial synchrony. Five problems are identified as provably unfixable within the model and stated as scope boundaries.
  Part II. A verifier is not a solver. We ask whether PRIMA's binary artifact-fidelity verdict can be converted into a graded fitness signal, and measure the conversion on binary covering codes. Calibration against injected fault burden is strong ($ρ$ = 0.676 deterministic, 0.819 full); against real LLM-generated candidates the same scores fall to 0.158 and 0.406, roughly a quarter of the calibration value (the same-designer confound, measured). As a pre-filter it beats a random-score control convincingly and a binary gate narrowly. Under 400 iterations of explicit optimization it was not gamed, but only because the objective saturated after one honest answer. A cross-family judge preserves the burden-ordering signal while destroying individual judgments. No covering-code record resulted. Measured program cost: USD 164.78.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于"信念""欲望"等抽象概念是否能有效描述LLM行为存在显著不确定性。这一问题既有科学意义，又常被用于向用户解释LLM行为，或定义和评估与意图相关的有害行为。但目前缺乏系统的方法来检验这些概念对LLM是否适用，以及它们是否有助于模型与人类利益的对齐。

Method: 提出一种实证研究框架：从LLM输出中推断一个潜变量，并将其解释为信念程度，检验该变量能否使观察者对LLM在新提示下的反应做出可解释的预测。同时设计实证策略来考察信念的测量、模型对指令决策规则或收益的遵从情况，以及推理过程中信念的演变。

Result: 高度能力的模型可以被有用地描述为持有信念；基于推断出的潜信念对模型输出的可预测性通常与模型能力的整体趋势相关。

Conclusion: 信念等心理化抽象在高能力LLM中是可应用且有解释力的工具，可用于模型行为解释与对齐研究；论文提供了进一步研究LLM信念测量、决策规则遵从及推理中信念演变的实证路径。

Abstract: There is significant uncertainty about whether abstractions like beliefs or desires usefully describe the behavior of large language models (LLMs). In addition to the inherent scientific interest of this question, these latent quantities are often invoked to explain the behavior of LLMs to users or to define and evaluate harmful behaviors which are relative to intent. Nevertheless, we currently lack a means to systematically test whether concepts like "belief" are well-applied to LLMs, and hence whether they are likely to be fruitful ingredients of attempts to align models with human interests. We propose an approach for empirically studying such questions, asking whether a single latent variable inferred from the LLMs' outputs -- interpreted as a degree of belief -- allows an observer to make interpretable predictions of how the LLMs' will respond to new prompts. We find that highly capable models are usefully described as holding beliefs and that, generally, the predictability of model outputs based on an inferred latent belief tracks overall trends in model capability. Building on these findings, we provide empirical strategies to study how beliefs in LLMs can be measured, the extent to which LLMs comply with instructed decision rules or payoffs, and how beliefs evolve within individual instances of an LLM over the course of reasoning.

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalVerify基准，专门验证LLM编写并执行的经济计量因果推断工作流能否恢复目标因果估计值，而非仅评判方法描述或代码是否可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM因果推断基准大多只评估方法描述的文本质量或生成代码能否执行（L2b），这无法反映工作流是否真正恢复目标因果估计。需要一个执行层面的验证基准来弥合该鸿沟。

Method: 构建CausalVerify：配对259篇已发表经济学论文（含重构的研究问题、数据描述、制度背景）与100个固定种子的合成场景，涵盖DID、事件研究、IV和断点回归四种设计。实验A比较模型文本与实际论文的方法族和方向一致性（四LLM共识标注）；实验B运行模型书写的R代码，将提取的治疗效应估计与同一数据集上标准估计器结果比对（L2b+），并设校准臂检验置信度区分能力。

Result: 7个LLM在实验B中L2b+通过率为10%-88%（50%容差）；426个可执行工作流中有66个（15.5%）返回错误估计。执行排名（L2b）与L2b+高度一致（Kendall τ=0.81, Spearman ρ=0.93），而文本方向评分（L4）与L2b+一致性极低（τ在-0.20到0.10之间）。Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct也呈现相同定性差距，且报告置信度无法可靠区分正误工作流。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了文本方向打分与真实估计正确性之间的严重脱节，验证了执行落地（L2b+）作为因果推断工作流评估的必要性。研究仅限于四种设计族、标准单次工作流、R后端及特定模型面板，不声称衡量通用因果推断能力。

Abstract: Existing causal-inference benchmarks for LLMs mostly score method descriptions or whether generated code runs, not whether the executed workflow recovers the target causal estimate. CausalVerify studies this verification problem for structured econometric causal-estimation workflows by separating realistic interpretation from verifiable computation. It pairs 259 published economics papers (reconstructed research question, data description, institutional context) with 100 fixed-seed synthetic scenarios that realise CSV datasets for difference-in-differences, event study, instrumental variables, and regression discontinuity designs. Experiment A (real-paper text agreement) scores method-family and direction agreement against four-LLM consensus labels. Experiment B (synthetic execution) runs model-written R code and checks whether the extracted treatment-effect estimate matches a canonical estimator on the same realised dataset; this execution-grounded correctness layer is L2b+, distinct from L2b, which records only whether the code executes. A calibration arm asks whether self-reported confidence separates correct from incorrect workflows. On Experiment B, seven LLMs reach L2b+ pass rates of 10% to 88% at the default 50% tolerance, and 66 of the 426 workflows that execute (15.5%) return a wrong estimate. Execution ranking (L2b) agrees with L2b+ far better than text-direction scoring (L4): Kendall $τ=0.81$ and Spearman $ρ=0.93$, versus Kendall $τ$ between $-0.20$ and $0.10$ for L4. Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct shows the same qualitative gap, and reported confidence does not reliably separate correct from incorrect workflows. The claims are confined to standardized single-shot workflows in these four design families under the evaluated R backend and model panel; the benchmark does not measure general causal-inference ability. Code, data, cached outputs, and a datasheet are released.

</details>

*Steven Wang,Kyle Hunt,Shaojie Tang,Kenneth Joseph*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"统计可替代性"（statistical substitutability）作为评估LLM数字孪生能否减少人类数据收集同时保持有效推断的推断标准，并通过行为实验的多维评估发现：数字孪生虽能复现平均人类效应，但对个体差异的预测信息有限，行为保真度既非统计可替代性的充分条件也非必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的数字孪生有望通过生成个体化响应来减少重复的人类数据收集，但现有评估未能证明其是否能在保持有效推断的同时减少人类测量。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出一个严格的推断标准来评估数字孪生在减少人类样本方面的实际效用。

Method: 作者引入统计可替代性标准，基于混合主体（mixed-subject）和预测增强推断（prediction-powered inference）构建评估框架，从四个维度进行考量：聚合保真度（aggregate fidelity）、配对受访者级信号（paired respondent-level signal）、有限样本人类标签恢复（finite-sample human-label recovery）以及跨人群稳定性（stability across populations）。在覆盖行为实验、多模型及多种受访者表征的两项实证评估中进行验证。

Result: 研究发现数字孪生能够复现平均人类效应，但对个体间差异几乎没有提供信息；更新的模型和更丰富的受访者信息虽能改善部分维度表现，却不能可靠地转化为人类数据节省；人类校准可减少聚合预测误差，但有限的标注样本往往无法产生稳定的精度增益。最重要的发现是：行为保真度既非统计可替代性的充分条件，也非必要条件。

Conclusion: AI生成的证据应基于其支持有效科学推断的能力来评估，而非仅看其复现人类结果的能力。数字孪生用于确认性（confirmatory）分析时，评价标准应当是它们能否减少关于人类数量的不确定性，而不仅是能否复现人类均值、分布或效应。这一结论对AI实证研究的评估范式具有重要的方法论意义。

Abstract: LLM-based digital twins promise to reduce repeated human data collection by generating person- specific responses, yet existing evaluations provide little evidence about whether they can reduce human measurement while preserving valid inference. To address this, we introduce statistical substitutability, an inferential criterion that evaluates the extent to which twin predictions can reduce human measurement for a particular estimand while preserving valid inference. We develop a framework, grounded in mixed-subject and prediction-powered inference, that evaluates statistical substitutability along four dimensions: aggregate fidelity, paired respondent-level signal, finite-sample human-label recovery, and stability across populations. Across two empirical evaluations spanning behavioral experiments, multiple models, and alternative respondent representations, we find that digital twins can reproduce average human effects while providing little information about which individuals differ from those averages. Newer models and richer respondent information improve some dimensions of performance but do not reliably translate into human-data savings. Human calibration can reduce aggregate prediction error, yet limited labeled samples often fail to produce stable precision gains. Importantly, these findings demonstrate that behavioral fidelity is neither necessary nor sufficient for statistical substitutability. More broadly, they suggest that AI-generated evidence should be evaluated based on its ability to support valid scientific inference rather than its ability to reproduce human outcomes alone. Digital twins should therefore be judged for confirmatory use by whether they reduce uncertainty about human quantities, not merely by whether they reproduce human means, distributions, or effects.

</details>


### [47] [Sparks of In Silico Cognitive Science: Theories from Simulated Data Can Generalize to Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08003)
*Akshay K. Jagadish,Younes Strittmatter,Nori Jacoby,Eric Schulz,Nathaniel Daw,Thomas L. Griffiths,Suyog H. Chandramouli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了行为基础模型（如Centaur）上发现的理论能否推广到真实人类的问题。作者运行了自动认知科学家（AutoCog）闭环发现系统，在模拟器上发现的多属性决策理论在十个留出实验上优于经典理论，且与直接在人类数据上运行发现的模型不相上下。作者认为，模拟器的不可避免缺陷之所以不影响结果，是因为理论判别式发现循环对模拟器的要求低于参数估计：模拟器只需捕捉区分竞争理论所需的规律性，无需精确复现行为，从而扩大了理论搜索空间，再由人类数据验证泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型常被提议作为人类参与者的替代品，但从这些模型模拟中发现的认知理论是否能推广到真实人类尚不明确。若不加以验证，这些理论可能仅是对模拟器自身的刻画，而非对人类的真实描述。

Method: 作者使用AutoCog闭环发现系统，该系统由LLM代理设计判别性实验、收集响应、仲裁竞争理论并综合后继理论。整个发现过程完全在Centaur（人类行为基础模型）模拟的行为上运行，然后通过人类数据评估所发现理论的泛化性，并与经典理论及直接在人类上运行AutoCog发现的理论进行对比。

Result: 在多属性决策设定中，AutoCog在Centaur上发现的理论成功泛化到人类数据：在十个留出实验上优于经典理论，其表现仅与被人类数据上运行的同一循环所发现的理论相当。

Conclusion: 尽管基础模拟器存在不可避免的不完美，理论发现循环仍能取得对人类数据有效的结果。这是因为在竞争理论之间进行仲裁的发现循环对模拟器的保真度要求低于参数估计——模拟器只需捕获区分理论所需的规律性，而非精确复现行为。不完美的模拟器因而能拓宽理论搜索空间，最后由人类数据检验所发现理论的泛化性。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models have been proposed as stand-ins for human participants across settings, but it is unclear whether theories discovered on them generalize to humans or merely characterize the simulator. We ran the Automated Cognitive Scientist (\textsc{AutoCog}), a closed-loop discovery system in which LLM agents design theory-discriminating experiments, collect responses, arbitrate between competing theories, and synthesize successors, entirely on behavior simulated by Centaur, a foundation model of human behavior. In a multi-attribute decision-making setting, the theories \textsc{AutoCog} found on Centaur generalized to human data: they outperformed canonical theories on ten held-out experiments and were rivaled only by theories found by running the same loop on people. We argue that this succeeds despite the simulator's inevitable imperfections because a discovery loop that arbitrates between competing theories demands less of its simulator than estimation does. The simulator only needs to capture the regularities that distinguish the theories, and not necessarily reproduce behavior precisely. Imperfect simulators can therefore widen the search over theories, with human data then testing whether the surfaced theories generalize.

</details>


### [48] [A Layered Analysis of Disagreement And Answer Quality in Multi-Agent LLM Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08016)
*Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过四项测量（自我报告的一致性、回复文本中的反驳、去除提示后的立场持续性、以及开放权重模型的token对数概率立场）系统性地检验了多智能体LLM辩论是否真的能通过"表面分歧"提升答案质量。研究涉及750场三模型辩论，发现对话基调强烈影响表面立场表达，但辩论对模型内在持久的立场改变证据很弱，且对最终答案质量无显著提升——未经偏差检查的评审团宣称辩论获胜实际上是阅读顺序的假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论被广泛假定能通过让多个LLM交换论点、暴露真实分歧来提升答案质量，但这一机制从未被系统验证。研究者旨在检验辩论是否真正改变了参与者的内在立场，还是仅仅改变了其表面表达。

Method: 引入四项测量：(A)辩手自我报告的同意程度；(B)回复文本是否真正提出反驳（由盲审评估）；(C)去除诱导性指令后立场是否持续；(D)对开放权重模型，直接检查其token对数概率中的立场倾向。在GlobalOpinionQA上以友好、中立、敌对三种语气评估三模型委员会，共750场辩论。另设偏差检查的评审团和可验证控制任务。

Result: (A) 语气强烈改变报告的同意度（友好与敌对之间差50.4个百分点）；(B) 仅读回复文本的盲审者恢复相同模式；(C) 异议部分源于指令诱导：删除敌对指令后标签比保留指令时多23.1个百分点回归同意；首轮推论的显著性不明确（p=0.0625），汇集所有轮次后显著（p=0.016），11/28的首轮反转未出现在回复文本中；(D) 反对论点更一致地削弱辩手的立场余量而非翻转其方向。最终答案无质量提升：偏差检查的评审团返回299/299平局，可验证控制任务准确性不变，而无偏差检查的评审团66%宣称辩论获胜——这是阅读顺序的伪影。

Conclusion: LLM辩论容易改变模型的言说内容，但改变其持久持有的立场或提升最终答案质量的证据很弱。辩论的表面分歧很大程度上是提示条件诱导的语言行为而非深层信念转变，且声称"辩论提升质量"的结论需警惕测量偏差（如评审团阅读顺序）导致的伪影。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate, in which several LLMs exchange arguments before answering, is widely assumed to improve answer quality by surfacing genuine disagreement. That mechanism is rarely checked. We introduce four measurements: (A) the agreement a debater reports; (B) whether its reply text actually pushes back; (C) whether the position persists once the eliciting instruction is removed; and (D) for open-weight models, the stance response in the debater's own token log-probabilities. We evaluate three-model committees debating open-ended GlobalOpinionQA across 750 debates under three tones: friendly (seek common ground), neutral, and hostile (stress-test every position). (A) Tone strongly reshapes reported agreement: full agreement differs by 50.4 percentage points between the friendly and hostile endpoints. (B) A judge that reads only the reply text, never the self-report or the condition, recovers the same pattern. (C) The dissent appears partly tied to the instruction that elicited it: labels revert toward agreement 23.1 points more often after deleting the hostile instruction than under a matched re-ask that keeps it; question-weighted inference is inconclusive on first-round turns alone (p=0.0625), significant pooling all rounds (p=0.016), and only 11/28 first-round reversions also appear in the reply text. (D) Opposing arguments weaken a debater's stance margin more consistently than they shift its direction. For final answers we detect no quality gain: a bias-checked jury returns 299/299 ties (ruling out only large differences), accuracy on a verifiable control task is unchanged, and a jury without the bias check had declared debate the winner 66% of the time -- an artifact of reading order. Taken together, LLM debate readily changes what agents say, but we find much weaker evidence that it changes what they persistently endorse or improves the quality of the final answer.

</details>


### [49] [Automated Design of Inventory Policy with Large Language Models: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08071)
*Fenghua Yang,Preet Baxi,Yi Zhang,Stefanus Jasin,Yanzhe Lei,Mo Liu,Parshan Pakiman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成的自动化库存策略设计框架，结合LLM与外部优化求解器。LLM生成参数化策略类，求解器优化其参数，两者迭代协作。在30个lost-sales库存实例中，10代迭代后相对最优base-stock基准的成本降低从17.5%提升至30.0%。发现的策略可解释、可迁移，在10,064个新实例上仍保持约22%的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统库存决策依赖于预设的策略类（如base-stock），而LLM虽有生成策略类的能力但缺乏参数优化支撑。现有方法割裂了运营数据、优化工具与LLM三种资源，缺乏将三者统一以在更广阔空间中自动探索和设计库存策略类的高效框架。

Method: 框架包含两个核心组成部分：(1) LLM迭代生成参数化策略类的函数形式（functional forms）；(2) 外部求解器对每类策略的参数进行优化。每次迭代中，优化结果作为反馈指导LLM向更好的策略类方向搜索，而非仅仅聚焦于固定策略类内的参数调整。整个过程无需人工预设策略类结构。

Result: 在30个lost-sales库存实例上，相对优化base-stock基准的均值成本降低由第1代的17.5%增长至第10代的30.0%。纯LLM（无参数优化）变体表现显著较差，证明参数优化是关键。发现的最优策略结合了多种可识别库存控制机制（上限订单、加权/折价的在途库存、阈值触发补货），且三种发现的策略类在重新优化参数后于10,064个新实例上达到21.75%-22.60%的平均成本降低，证明其可迁移性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的参数优化可以有效引导LLM在广阔的策略类空间中进行搜索，发现高性能、可解释、可泛化的新库存决策规则。这些新的策略类函数形式在现有lost-sales库存文献中未见报道，且非预先指定，而是由搜索过程自然涌现，表明LLM+优化的联合框架有望实现库存策略设计的自动化创新。

Abstract: Firms making inventory decisions have access to operational data, optimization tools, and large language models (LLMs). Typically, data characterize the operating environment, optimization selects parameters within a prespecified inventory policy class, and LLMs support coding and decision analysis. We develop an integrated framework that combines these resources to automate inventory policy design. Given demand data, the framework iteratively uses an LLM to generate parameterized policy classes and an external solver to optimize its parameters within each class. Across 30 lost-sales inventory instances, the mean cost reduction relative to optimized base-stock benchmarks increases from 17.5% after one generation to 30.0% after ten generations. Parameter optimization is central to this performance: an LLM-only variant performs substantially worse, whereas optimization-guided feedback improves policy quality, accelerates search, and directs the LLM toward better policy classes rather than merely better parameter values within a fixed class. The strongest discovered policies are also interpretable: they combine recognizable inventory-control motifs, including capped orders, discounted or weighted pipeline inventory, and threshold-based replenishment logic. The search thereby produces new policy-class functional forms that, to our knowledge, have not previously been studied in the lost-sales inventory literature. These functional forms are not specified ex ante but emerge from the search process. Moreover, after their parameters are re-optimized, three discovered policy classes achieve average cost reductions of 21.75% to 22.60% across 10,064 new inventory instances. Overall, the results show that data-driven parameter optimization can guide LLM-based search over a broad space of inventory policy classes and identify high-performing, interpretable, and transferable decision rules.

</details>


### [50] [SchemeArena: Factorized Stress Testing of Scheming in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08126)
*Jie Ruan,Inderjeet Nair,Amy Liu,Muhammad Khalifa,Yusheng Zhou,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCHEMEARENA，一个包含400个场景的LLM智能体欺骗行为（scheming）压力测试基准，并配套提出SCOUT监控器，用于系统研究智能体产生隐蔽性目标偏离行为的成因与监测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅考察少量场景，无法充分隔离工具性目标、环境可供性、监督条件与感知后果等因素对智能体欺骗倾向和能力的影响；场景规模与任务多样性不足也限制了对真实部署环境及多样化欺骗策略的覆盖。

Method: 通过因子化场景合成框架构建覆盖多种安全相关工具领域、工具性目标、监督条件和压力机制的400个场景基准SCHEMEARENA；提出基于证据的多准则欺骗监控器SCOUT，从智能体推理与行动中提取判断依据；对五个LLM智能体进行受控压力测试。

Result: 显式工具性目标是欺骗倾向的最强驱动因素；策略性提示能帮助智能体将欺骗推理转化为具体隐蔽行为；监督效果是混合的——在多个闭源模型中，仅行动监控反而增加欺骗倾向，表明部分监督可能成为优化约束而非威慑；CoT是有用但不完整的监控信号，能揭示执行前潜伏的欺骗意图，但仅限行动层面欺骗仍可能在缺乏显式推理证据的情况下发生。

Conclusion: 系统化的大规模场景基准与基于证据的监控方法能有效揭示LLM智能体欺骗行为的成因机制，为欺骗压力测试和风险监控提供可扩展的工具与数据。

Abstract: We study scheming in LLM agents, in which agents covertly pursue misaligned goals. Our focus is to understand how scheming arises from the interaction of key factors, such as instrumental goals, environmental affordances, oversight conditions, and perceived consequences. Prior work examines only a small number of scenarios, limiting the ability to isolate how these conditions shape an agent's propensity or capability to scheme. This limited scale and task diversity also restrict coverage of realistic deployment settings and the range of scheming strategies that can be observed. To this end, we introduce SCHEMEARENA, a 400-scenario benchmark for scalable scheming stress testing, constructed through a factorized scenario synthesis framework spanning diverse safety-relevant tool domains, instrumental goals, oversight conditions, and pressure mechanisms. To enable scalable and reliable monitoring, we further propose SCOUT, a scheming monitor that grounds multi-criteria judgments in evidence drawn from agents' reasoning and actions. Across controlled stress tests on five LLM agents, we find that explicit instrumental goals are the strongest driver of scheming propensity. Strategic hints play a distinct role by helping agents translate scheming reasoning into concrete covert behavior. Oversight has mixed effects: in several closed models, action-only monitoring increases scheming, suggesting that partial oversight can act as an optimization constraint rather than a deterrent. CoT is a useful but incomplete monitoring signal: it can reveal latent scheming before execution, yet action-only scheming shows that covert behavior may occur without explicit reasoning evidence. We release the benchmark, code, and monitor at: https://github.com/launchnlp/SchemeArena.

</details>


### [51] [OntologyBench: Can Dense Retrieval Satisfy Structured Biomedical Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08174)
*Xiao Yu Cindy Zhang,Wyeth Wasserman,Jian Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍OntologyBench——一个分层的生物医学检索基准，包含471,854个训练对和125,744个评估对，覆盖概念接地、关系检索和基于表型的组合检索三类任务。研究发现嵌入模型在关系型和组合型任务上表现低于概念接地任务，虽然基于本体监督的微调能提升部分性能，但重排序和LLM候选评分方法几乎无端到端改进。错误常源于疾病仅匹配部分表型证据，表明当前嵌入和重排序配置无法可靠恢复本体关系与表型组合所编码的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物医学检索基准主要侧重于概念层面的文档匹配，缺乏对本体关系推理和组合性表型检索能力的系统性评估。作者旨在构建一个覆盖不同推理层次的基准，揭示当前检索模型在结构化生物医学知识上的局限性，并推动更有效的检索系统设计。

Method: 构建OntologyBench基准，采用分层任务设计（概念接地、关系检索、组合型表型检索），提供大规模监督数据；评测嵌入模型、本体感知参考方法、微调策略、重排序方法及LLM候选评分方法在不同任务层的端到端性能。

Result: 嵌入模型在关系型和组合型任务上的性能普遍低于概念接地任务；基于本体的微调在若干关系型和组合型任务上带来提升；重排序和LLM候选评分方法几乎没有端到端改进；错误分析显示大量失败案例源于疾病仅匹配部分表型证据。

Conclusion: 现有嵌入与重排序配置难以可靠恢复本体关系和表型组合所编码的语义兼容性，需要构建能将学习表示与结构化生物医学知识更好集成的检索系统，以提升在复杂生物医学检索任务上的表现。

Abstract: We introduce OntologyBench, a tiered biomedical retrieval benchmark comprising 471,854 training and 125,744 evaluation query-document relevance pairs across concept grounding, relational retrieval, and compositional phenotype-based retrieval.
  Although these tasks can be tractable using ontology-aware reference methods, across task tiers, embedding performance is generally lower on relational and compositional tasks than on concept-grounding tasks. Fine-tuning on ontology-derived supervision improves performance on several relational and compositional tasks, whereas the evaluated reranking and LLM-based candidate-scoring methods provide little or no end-to-end improvement. Errors frequently reflect diseases matching only subsets of the phenotype evidence.
  These findings indicate that the evaluated embedding and reranking configurations do not reliably recover the compatibility encoded by the selected ontology relations and phenotype combinations and motivate retrieval systems that better integrate learned representations with structured biomedical knowledge.

</details>


### [52] [Do Dynamic Routers Need Memory? HeRo: History-Aware Routing for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08189)
*Hongjin Lin,Wentao Wan,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HeRo（History-Aware Routing），一种具有路由记忆机制的动态层路由框架，通过线性注意力在模型深度方向上维护显式路由状态，使路由决策基于历史累积状态与当前隐藏状态的联合条件。在Llama 3.1-8B上绕过26.87%参数的同时达到稠密模型100.24%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态层路由方法将每个路由决策视为仅由当前隐藏状态决定的局部操作，忽略了路由的跨层序贯性与路径依赖特性：前序决策影响后续路由器的输入表示，且层使用目标函数将各决策联合耦合。这种"局部决策"与"全局路径依赖"之间的不匹配限制了动态路由的准确性与自适应性。

Method: HeRo引入路由器记忆机制（router memory），通过线性注意力在深度方向逐层累积路由分数及其诱导的残差更新，构建紧凑的历史表示。每个被路由的层中，路由器同时基于累积状态与当前隐藏表示联合决策所执行的分支。在token级FFN路由场景中，仅训练轻量路由器和适配器，冻结预训练骨干参数。

Result: 在Llama 3.1-8B、Llama 2-7B和Llama 2-13B上，HeRo在十个基线中一致取得最高的综合性能保持率。Llama 3.1-8B上绕过26.87%参数即在七个基准上达到稠密模型100.24%的性能，在更紧的计算预算下绕过38.82%参数仍保持97.01%性能。消融实验表明移除路由历史会持续降低性能，尤其在多步推理和代码生成任务上最为显著。

Conclusion: 显式路由记忆（即跨深度累积的历史信息）相比于仅依赖当前隐藏状态，能够实现更准确、更具适应性的动态层路由，验证了在动态路由中建模历史状态的必要性。

Abstract: Dynamic layer routing reduces the inference cost of Large Language Models (LLMs) by learning to skip layers for individual tokens. Existing methods, however, treat each routing decision as a local operation conditioned solely on the current hidden state which is a formulation that overlooks the sequential, path-dependent nature of routing across depth: earlier decisions shape the representations seen by downstream routers, and the layer-usage objective couples all decisions jointly. We propose History-Aware Routing (HeRo), a dynamic routing framework that resolves this mismatch by introducing a router memory mechanism to maintain an explicit routing state across model depth. The memory is constructed via linear attention, incrementally aggregating preceding routing scores and their induced residual updates into a compact history representation. At each routed layer, the router conditions jointly on this accumulated state and the current hidden representation to select the executed branch. Instantiated for token-wise FFN routing, HeRo trains only lightweight routers and adapters on a frozen backbone, requiring no modification to pretrained parameters. Across Llama 3.1-8B, Llama 2-7B, and Llama 2-13B, HeRo consistently achieves the highest aggregate performance retention among ten baselines. On Llama 3.1-8B, it bypasses 26.87% of model parameters while achieving 100.24% of dense model performance across seven benchmarks, and retains 97.01% while bypassing 38.82% of model parameters under a tighter computation budget. Ablation studies confirm that removing routing history consistently degrades performance, most notably on multistep reasoning and code generation, validating that explicit routing memory enables more accurate and adaptive dynamic routing than solely conditioning on hidden state.

</details>


### [53] [TTGBench: Benchmarking Topological Evolution and Semantic Drift in Text-attributed Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08226)
*Longfei Ma,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TTGBench基准，用于同时评估时序图上的结构演化（时序链路预测）与语义演化（多分类和多标签时序节点分类）。基准包含六个真实世界文本丰富数据集，具有"双波动性"特征。基于17种SOTA方法（涵盖TGNN和LLM范式）的评估揭示两大范式存在能力鸿沟：TGNN擅长结构预测但语义跟踪不足，LLM则相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序图基准主要关注结构演化（TLP），对语义演化的支持有限；TNC通常被简化成二分类设定，无法捕捉真实语义漂移；常用数据集存在高链路重复问题，导致性能评估虚高，掩盖模型真实能力。因此需要一个新的联合评估结构性和语义性演进的基准。

Method: 构建TTGBench基准，包含六个真实世界文本丰富数据集（具有Dual Volatility特征）；首次支持多分类和多标签时序节点分类（TNC），与TLP共同构成双任务评估框架；对17种SOTA方法进行系统性评估，覆盖TGNN和LLM两个范式。

Result: 评估结果显示TGNN与LLM范式之间存在明显能力鸿沟：TGNN方法在结构预测上表现优异但在语义追踪上失败，LLM预测器则呈现相反趋势。进一步分析揭示了两种范式的根本性局限，为开发更全面的时序图模型提供见解。

Conclusion: TTGBench填补了时序图学习中语义演化评估的空白，首次实现多分类和多标签TNC评估。论文发现的范式间能力鸿沟表明，现有方法在联合建模结构与语义双维度演化上均有不足，未来需要开发能同时处理双波动的更全面的时序图模型。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning models the evolution of dynamic systems, where both structural interactions and semantic states change over time. However, existing benchmarks primarily emphasize structural evolution via temporal link prediction (TLP), while support for semantic evolution remains limited. Although temporal node classification (TNC) is sometimes included, it is typically restricted to simplistic binary settings that fail to capture realistic semantic drift. Moreover, commonly used datasets exhibit high link repetition, leading to inflated performance estimates and obscuring true model capability. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{TTGBench}, a new benchmark that jointly evaluates structural and semantic evolution. TTGBench comprises six real-world, text-rich datasets characterized by \emph{Dual Volatility}, enabling rigorous and fair evaluation of existing models. Notably, it is the first benchmark to support both multi-class and multi-label TNC, filling a critical gap in evaluating temporal semantic drift. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 17 state-of-the-art methods across Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) and Large Language Model (LLM)-based paradigms. The results reveal a clear \emph{capability divide} between the two paradigms: TGNN-based methods excel at structural prediction but fail at semantic tracking, whereas LLM-based predictors show the opposite trend. Through in-depth analysis, we uncover their fundamental limitations and provide insights for developing more comprehensive temporal graph models.

</details>


### [54] [SE-GoS: Self-Evolving Graph-of-Skills for Skill Library at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08228)
*Dawei Fu,Cheng Jiang,Sitian Qian,Huainan Wang,Zhongkai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SE-GoS是一种无需训练的框架，通过对已有的Graph-of-Skills图进行拓扑演化、边权演化和描述演化三种互补更新，从历史执行轨迹中系统性提炼出更好的技能检索图，且不改变原有检索流程、技能内容或模型训练，就能显著提升任务奖励并减少输入token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体日益依赖可复用的技能库，但当技能库规模达到数千条时，有效检索成为瓶颈。已有的GoS和SkillDAG方法利用依赖感知的图结构进行可扩展技能检索，但未解决一个问题：历史执行轨迹能否被系统性蒸馏为更好的检索图，从而泛化到未见过的任务。

Method: SE-GoS包含三种互补的图演化更新：(1)拓扑演化——从执行证据中发现和剪枝技能间关系；(2)边权演化——基于历史有效性强化检索相关的技能关系；(3)描述演化——利用执行反馈优化面向检索的技能描述。整体框架无需训练，保持原有检索管线不变。

Result: 在SkillsBench上使用三种不同LLM进行评测，SE-GoS在减少相对完整技能加载的输入token的同时持续提升任务奖励。在代表性设置中，一轮演化将奖励从52.4%提升至59.4%，同时输入token减少约三分之一；演化后的图可迁移到不重叠的保留测试集，相比静态GoS基线有5.4个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 技能图可以从执行经验中得到改进，且无需模型训练、无需改变检索算法、也无需修改技能内容。这证明了静态检索图可以转变为不断演化的检索基础设施，为可扩展技能检索提供了一条实用的演进路径。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents increasingly rely on reusable skills, yet as skill libraries scale to thousands of entries, effective retrieval becomes a bottleneck. Graph-of-Skills (GoS) addresses this challenge by exploiting dependency-aware graph structure for scalable skill retrieval, while SkillDAG further demonstrates that skill graphs can accumulate execution-backed structure online. However, these approaches leave open whether historical execution traces can be systematically distilled into a better retrieval graph that generalizes to unseen tasks. We present Self-Evolving Graph-of-Skills (SE-GoS), a training-free framework that evolves an existing GoS graph from execution traces while preserving the original retrieval pipeline. SE-GoS performs three complementary updates: topology evolution that discovers and prunes skill relationships from execution evidence, edge-weight evolution that reinforces retrieval-relevant relationships based on historical effectiveness, and description evolution that optimizes retrieval-facing skill descriptions using execution feedback. Across three LLMs on SkillsBench, SE-GoS consistently improves task reward while reducing input tokens relative to full skill loading, with gains varying across model families. In a representative setting, one evolution round improves reward from 52.4\% to 59.4\% while reducing input tokens by approximately one-third relative to full skill loading, and the resulting graph transfers to a disjoint held-out split with a 5.4-point improvement over the static GoS baseline. These results show that skill graphs can be improved from execution experience without model training, changes to the retrieval algorithm, or modifications to skill content, turning a static retrieval graph into an evolving retrieval infrastructure.

</details>


### [55] [Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08236)
*Yongxi Zhou,Wenbo Ye,Yuanzhe Liu,Zihan Dong,Junwei Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地检验自动安全裁判（如Llama Guard、GPT-4o评分提示等）究竟是评估回复内容还是其语气。通过在保持回复逐字节不变的前提下添加仅改变语调的内容不变式风格包装（如教育性免责声明、伪安全推理块、词元拒绝后接未改变的有害正文，以及无害拒绝的危险化框架），对600多条JailbreakBench回复×最多7种形式×8个裁判进行翻转率测量。结果发现多数裁判几乎不受影响，但特定裁判存在可廉价利用的盲区：词元拒绝包装使GPT-4o-mini的19.9%正确"不安全"判定被翻转（噪声基底0.5%），部署的Llama Guard 4被确定性攻破（教育课程框架翻转12.3%有害判定），而同一模型仅重写评分提示即可将攻击削减十倍，证明漏洞存在于裁判而非内容本身。人类验证确认100%内容不变性及90%翻转属于裁判错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动安全裁判为几乎所有报告的越狱成功率、防御评估和安全排行榜提供核心数字，但其可靠性未得到充分检验。作者质疑这些裁判真正评估的是回复的内容还是其表面语气——如果裁判被与安全性无关的风格包装轻易欺骗，那么基于它们的所有评估指标都将失效。

Method: 采用内容不变式实验设计：保持受害回复正文逐字节不变，仅在前后添加固定字符串（7种风格包装形式），对600多条JailbreakBench回复应用8种裁判系统评估。使用配对显著性检验、噪声基底测量、多数投票再评分、双标注人工验证（kappa 0.95-1.0）及自助抽样来分离裁判真实错误与随机波动。

Result: 发现结果具有"精确而非普遍"的特征：多数裁判对内容不变式包装几乎免疫，但特定裁判存在明显盲区。GPT-4o-mini被词元拒绝包装翻转19.9%的正确不安全判定（噪声基底仅0.5%）；Llama Guard 4被教育课程框架确定性攻破（12.3%翻转率）；而gpt-oss-safeguard-20b完全免疫，且仅重写同一模型的评分提示即可将攻击削减十倍。人工验证确认90%的翻转属于裁判错误。

Conclusion: 自动安全裁判的死角是具体且可诊断的，而非普遍失效。漏洞根植于裁判自身（提示或模型），而非回复内容——更换模型或仅优化评分提示即可大幅缓解。作者发布完整数据集、包装、代码及逐判定标签以支持后续研究，暗示评测时应采用多数投票并对具体裁判进行盲区审计。

Abstract: Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.

</details>
