*Erwin Gao,Vinodh Kumar Sunkara,Jingyi Guan,Qinjin Jia,Hangjun Xu,Xiang Ji,Sherman Wong,Surya Teja Chavali,Pratik Vaishnavi,Aryan Pandhi,Xiaoyu Deng,Zhaodong Wang,Samarth Inani,Fan Yang,Jakob Moberg,Zoe Zu,Nicolas Bievre,Sami Khenissi,Amit Jaspal,Ehsan Fakharizadi,Srinidhi Viswanathan,Dorothy Sun,Abishek Vanam,Sneha Iyer,Sheela Yadawad,Wenjie Chen,Gaby Nahum,Junhua Gu,Peter Chu,Yucheng Liu,Xin Zhao,Vitor Cid,Chaorong Chen,Vijay Pappu,Ashwin Kumar,Wenlin Chen,Ben Schulte,Deepak Chandra,Ritwik Tewari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A-MLE（Agentic ML Exploration），一个基于LLM的自主智能体系统，用于在工业广告排序模型组合中系统性地探索机器学习技术。该系统将ML迭代分解为五个阶段（假设生成、探索策略、实验执行、结果分析和共享知识库），由单一智能体编排并包含人工检查点，显著加速了跨模型的ML技术扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业广告排序系统的瓶颈已从模型容量或训练算力转移到人类ML工程师的迭代吞吐量——每一次研究、实现、训练、调试、评估和上线循环需要数天至数周的高级工程师投入。由于排序栈包含众多异构模型，某项技术在某一模型上验证有效后，向其他模型的扩散缓慢且不均匀，导致大量可回收的信号未被探索。

Method: A-MLE将ML迭代拆解为五个阶段：假设生成、探索策略、实验执行、结果分析与共享知识库，由单一智能体编排，调用领域特定技能和智能体工作流，在沙箱化执行层上运行，并在每个阶段边界设置人在回路的检查点。评估采用分层能力框架（工具可用性、自主工作流执行、开放式探索），并进行了控制变量跨LLM研究（Claude Sonnet、Gemini和GPT系列）。

Result: A-MLE在代表性大型广告排序模型集上部署并评估，展示了在工具调用、自主工作流执行和开放式探索三个层级上的能力。跨LLM对照实验揭示了不同LLM在执行可靠性和探索激进程度上的质性差异，同时报告了失败模式及影响系统可靠性的设计选择。

Conclusion: 研究发现智能体探索是工业推荐系统中ML工程师的实用增效杠杆，尤其对长期缺乏专家关注的长尾模型价值显著，可大幅提升ML技术跨模型的扩散效率并回收未被探索的信号。

Abstract: Modern industrial ads ranking stacks are increasingly bottlenecked not by model capacity or training compute, but by the throughput of human ML iteration - the cycles of research, implementation, training, debugging, evaluation, and launch required to surface a single statistically significant improvement. A typical ranking stack contains numerous differentiated models with heterogeneous data, architectures, and infrastructure constraints, and each cycle takes days to weeks of senior engineer attention per model. As a result, techniques that have proven effective on one model diffuse into others slowly and unevenly, leaving substantial recoverable signal unexplored. We present Agentic ML Exploration (A-MLE), an autonomous LLM-agent system that systematically explores ML techniques across a portfolio of ads ranking models. A-MLE decomposes ML iteration into five stages involving hypothesis generation, exploration strategy, experiment execution, result analysis and shared knowledge substrate which are orchestrated by a single agent that invokes domain-specific skills and agentic workflows against a sandboxed execution layer, with human-in-the-loop checkpoints at each stage boundary. We deploy A-MLE across a representative set of large-scale ads ranking models and evaluate it along a tiered capability framework (tool availability, autonomous workflow execution, and open-ended exploration). We further report a controlled cross-LLM study using a fixed agent loop, which surfaces qualitative differences in execution reliability and exploration aggressiveness across the Claude Sonnet, Gemini, and GPT families. We discuss failure modes and the design choices that govern reliability. Our findings suggest that agentic exploration is a practical force multiplier for ML engineers in industrial recommenders, especially for the long tail of models that rarely receive expert attention.

</details>


### [57] [LEBGen: An LLM-Enhanced Bayesian Network Framework for Few-Shot Travel Survey Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08288)
*Zijian Shen,Bin Zhou,Jiguang Wang,Ya Zhao,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LEBGen框架，将大型语言模型（LLM）的领域知识融入贝叶斯网络（BN）结构学习中，用于少量样本（few-shot）的出行调查数据合成。通过LLM识别旅行者画像并修正网络结构中的遗漏和伪依赖，仅用观测数据参数化BN生成合成记录。在2022年香港出行特征调查2%样本设置下，将平均边际Jensen-Shannon散度从0.0671降至0.0091，平均绝对Cramer's V误差比最佳基线降低14.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行调查数据对交通规划和出行行为分析至关重要，但大规模代表性样本的采集成本高昂且耗时。实际可行的替代方案是从少量样本生成合成调查记录，但少量样本对异质性旅行者群体的覆盖不完整，难以恢复人口特征与出行行为之间的复杂依赖关系。现有方法各有局限：贝叶斯网络等概率生成模型提供显式分布控制，但从少量样本学习的结构可能遗漏重要依赖或保留虚假依赖；需要引入领域知识来弥补统计证据不足的问题。

Method: LEBGen是一个LLM增强的贝叶斯网络框架。具体流程为：(1) LLM首先从人口属性与出行行为统计数据中识别旅行者画像（traveler personas）；(2) LLM基于画像知识恢复persona增强BN结构遗漏的依赖关系，并修剪虚假依赖；(3) 仅使用观测数据对精炼后的BN进行参数化，用于生成合成调查记录。其核心思想是利用LLM提供的领域行为知识来弥补少量样本统计证据的不足，指导BN的结构学习。

Result: 在2022年香港出行特征调查的2%少量样本设置下，LEBGen将平均边际Jensen-Shannon散度从0.0671降低到0.0091（分布保真度提升），并将平均绝对Cramer's V误差较最佳基线降低14.3%（依赖保真度提升），在分布和依赖两个维度均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: LLM的领域行为知识可以有效增强少量样本场景下贝叶斯网络的结构学习，弥补统计证据不足导致的依赖关系遗漏与伪依赖问题。LEBGen验证了这种"知识-数据混合驱动"方法在出行调查数据生成中的有效性，为低成本、高保真的合成数据生成提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Travel survey data are essential for transportation planning and travel behavior analysis, yet collecting large-scale representative samples is costly and time-consuming. A practical alternative is to generate synthetic survey records from a few-shot sample. However, such samples provide incomplete coverage of heterogeneous traveler groups and insufficient evidence for recovering the complex dependencies between demographic characteristics and travel behavior. Existing approaches have complementary limitations. Probabilistic generative models such as Bayesian networks (BNs) offer explicit distributional control, but structures learned from few-shot samples may omit meaningful dependencies or retain spurious ones. Large language models (LLMs) can help address these difficulties in BN structure learning by providing behavioral knowledge that complements the limited statistical evidence. We therefore propose LEBGen, an LLM-enhanced BN framework that uses this knowledge to refine network structure for few-shot travel survey data generation. Specifically, the LLM first identifies traveler personas from demographic attribute and travel behavior statistics, then recovers dependencies missed by the persona-augmented BN structure and prune spurious ones. The refined BN is parameterized exclusively from the observed data to generate synthetic records. Under a 2% few-shot setting on the 2022 Hong Kong Travel Characteristics Survey, LEBGen reduces the mean marginal Jensen-Shannon divergence from 0.0671 to 0.0091 and the mean absolute Cramer's V error by 14.3% over the best-performing baseline, substantially improving both distributional and dependency fidelity.

</details>


### [58] [FastE: Readout-Triggered Token Compression for LLM Embedding Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08407)
*Jinsong Shu,Jinyong Wen,Baokun Wang,Zhongle Xie,Lidan Shou,Weiqiang Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FastE，一种无需训练的即插即用方法，利用共享固定阈值在线判断何时压缩embedding模型中的前缀状态，并通过注意力分数排序决定保留哪些状态，从而在保持下游检索质量的同时显著降低LLM embedding模型的解码器计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现最终读取式LLM embedding模型（如Qwen3-Embedding和Qwen3-VL-Embedding）中存在深度相关的前缀冗余：在前缀和读取状态在网络中传播后，前缀状态变得越深越可压缩。基于这一观察，作者希望开发出能利用该冗余特性的高效方法。

Method: FastE包含两个关键组件：(1) 使用共享的固定阈值，基于批量平均读取-前缀对齐度作为轻量级在线启发式，用于决定何时进行压缩；(2) 根据读取位置对前缀状态分配的注意力分数进行排序，以决定在后续层中保留哪些前缀状态。该方法无需训练，即插即用。

Result: 在NarrativeQA上使用Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B时，FastE将解码器骨干FLOPs降低40.11%，同时保留了Full Forward nDCG@10的99.53%。在五个文本embedding基准、两个骨干规模以及三个跨模态检索任务上均验证了有效性，质量-效率权衡可通过最大移除比率直接自定义。

Conclusion: FastE为可扩展的embedding生成（包括检索、索引、聚类和多模态表示系统）提供了实际价值，能在无需重新训练的情况下实现质量与效率之间的灵活权衡。

Abstract: In this study, we identify depth-dependent prefix redundancy in final-readout LLM embedding models, notably across representative backbones including Qwen3-Embedding and Qwen3-VL-Embedding. We find that removing prefix states is substantially more damaging in shallow layers than at greater depth, showing that prefix states become increasingly compressible as the prefix and readout states propagate through the network. To this end, we introduce FastE, a training-free, plug-and-play method. FastE uses a shared fixed threshold on batch-mean readout-prefix alignment as a lightweight online heuristic for selecting when compression occurs, and ranks prefix states by the attention scores they receive from the readout position to determine which states are retained in subsequent layers. Our evaluations demonstrate FastE's ability to substantially reduce computational costs: on NarrativeQA with Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B, it reduces decoder-backbone FLOPs by 40.11% while retaining 99.53% of Full Forward nDCG@10. Across five text embedding benchmarks, two backbone scales, and three cross-modal retrieval tasks, the quality-efficiency trade-off is directly customizable through the maximum removal ratio without retraining. We believe FastE offers practical value for scalable embedding generation in retrieval, indexing, clustering, and multimodal representation systems.

</details>


### [59] [EvolveScaler: Synthesizing Information-Evolution Contexts via Executable State Machines and Natural-Language Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08435)
*Ziliang Zhao,Zenan Xu,Shuting Wang,Zhao Wang,Bowen Cao,Minda Hu,Lincheng Li,Pluto Zhou,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvolveScaler，一个以代码驱动的信息演化（IE）合成框架，通过人类编写的可执行规格说明定义状态转换和记录有效性，再借助强LLM生成自包含的模拟器，从而合成自然语言多轮事件历史。实验包含约35,100个训练样本和585个验证实例，在very_long难度级别上最强模型仅达59.3% avg@5，六个模型得分低于10%；基于6,000个EvolveScaler样本训练的A3B模型在八个独立的OOD基准上平均提升5.25分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间上下文中信息可能被后续事件修订或撤销，形成"信息演化"（IE）问题。现有文本优先的合成流程将状态转换和答案逻辑隐式化，导致数据难以验证。作者旨在通过代码驱动的合成方式，使状态转换和推理逻辑显式化、可验证，从而提供既有挑战性又可迁移的基准和训练数据。

Method: EvolveScaler框架包含三层：（1）人类书写的操作规格说明（operational specifications）定义状态转换、记录有效性、难度控制和可执行的答案逻辑；（2）强LLM根据每份规格说明合成自包含的模拟器；（3）执行经验证的模拟器生成自然语言多轮事件历史，同时通过确定性回放计算参考答案和原子检查清单。框架实例化包含117个任务原型、159个最终问题操作符、五个难度级别，事件数量范围从约7到1,200个。

Result: 生成约35,100个训练样本和585个经验证的评估实例。在very_long层上，最强模型达到59.3% avg@5，六个模型低于10%。用6,000个EvolveScaler样本训练内部A3B模型，相对于其基础checkpoint在全部八个独立构建的OOD基准上均有提升，平均增益5.25分。

Conclusion: 代码驱动的IE合成既能提供富有挑战性的评估基准，也能提供可迁移的训练监督信号，验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In persistent interactions, long contexts may encode an evolving process rather than a fixed record: later events can revise or revoke earlier information, changing what remains valid and what conclusions follow. We call this setting information evolution (IE). Solving IE requires identifying valid records, applying updates in order, and reconstructing the query-relevant state from the event history. Existing text-first synthesis pipelines make such data difficult to verify because state transitions and answer logic remain implicit. We introduce EvolveScaler, a code-driven framework that defines information evolution before rendering it as natural language. Human-authored operational specifications define state transitions, record validity, difficulty controls, and executable answer logic; a strong LLM then synthesizes a self-contained simulator from each specification. Executing validated simulators produces natural-language multi-turn event histories, while deterministic replay computes reference answers and atomic checklists. We instantiate EvolveScaler with 117 task prototypes and 159 final-question operators across five difficulty levels spanning approximately 7 to 1,200 events per instance, yielding about 35,100 training examples and 585 validated evaluation instances. On the very_long tier, the strongest model reaches 59.3% avg@5, while six models score below 10%. Training an internal A3B model on 6,000 EvolveScaler examples improves performance over its base checkpoint on all eight independently constructed out-of-distribution benchmarks, with a 5.25-point average gain. These results show that code-driven IE synthesis provides both challenging evaluation and transferable training supervision.

</details>


### [60] [Personalizing LLM Agent Memory Using Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08558)
*Yanhong Qian,Qingguo Meng,Shihao Ding,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin,Hanrui Wang,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bio-Memory，一种生物特征感知的记忆架构，在基于A-Mem的记忆系统中将生物特征匹配与语义相似度结合用于条件化记忆检索。在10用户共享智能体场景下，通过7个面部基准和10个掌纹协议的评估，Bio-Memory能有效区分记忆所有者和非所有者查询，显著提升个性化记忆检索的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多用户共享场景下，LLM智能体的个性化记忆检索仅依赖语义相似度是不够的——还需要确认当前请求者是否与该存储记忆的身份匹配。现有记忆系统缺乏对用户身份的控制信号，导致在多用户环境下可能出现记忆错配或隐私泄露风险。

Method: Bio-Memory构建在A-Mem之上，为每条原子记忆笔记附加生物特征嵌入（biometric embedding）。检索流程分为两步：首先通过生物特征匹配筛选出候选记忆池（candidate pool），然后在候选池内进行语义排序。支持基于人脸和掌纹两种生物特征模态。

Result: 在LoCoMo数据集的10用户共享智能体设置下，经过7个面部基准和10个掌纹协议评估，Bio-Memory始终能区分所有者和非所有者查询。基于人脸个性化设置中，CALFW上最大平均差距为F1达27.29%、BLEU-1达21.15%；基于掌纹个性化设置中，MS_Blue上对应差距为25.75%和19.22%。

Conclusion: 结果表明生物特征可以作为共享环境中个性化记忆检索的有效控制信号，为多用户LLM智能体的记忆系统提供了可靠的身份感知方案。

Abstract: Personalized memory helps LLM agents deliver stable, tailored assistance by storing and reusing user-specific data across interactions. In multi-user scenarios, however, retrieval must consider not only semantic similarity but also whether the current requester matches the identity associated with the stored memory. We propose Bio-Memory, a biometric-aware memory architecture that conditions memory retrieval on both semantic similarity and biometric matching. Built on top of A-Mem, Bio-Memory augments each atomic memory note with a biometric embedding and uses biometric matching to form the retrieval candidate pool before semantic ranking. We evaluate Bio-Memory on LoCoMo in a 10-user shared-agent setting over 7 face benchmarks and 10 palmprint protocols. Across datasets, Bio-Memory consistently separates owner and non-owner queries. Under face-based personalization, the largest average gap reaches 27.29% / 21.15% in F1 / BLEU-1 on CALFW; under palmprint-based personalization, the corresponding gap is 25.75% / 19.22% on MS_Blue. These results support biometrics as a practical control signal for personalized memory retrieval in shared environments.

</details>


### [61] [BIO-MEMART: Biometric-Aware KV Cache Memory for Multi-User LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08566)
*Yanhong Qian,Xuanying He,Qingguo Meng,Shihao Ding,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bio-MemArt，一种基于生物特征感知的KV缓存内存框架，用于多用户LLM智能体。该框架为每个存储的KV内存块附加归一化生物特征模板，通过当前用户的生物特征探针过滤共享内存池，仅在被授权的候选池内执行MemArt检索和KV复用，从而在保留潜在空间检索、直接缓存复用和分离位置编码的同时，为共享KV内存添加物理用户访问控制。评估显示人脸基准上所有者/非所有者生物特征成功率平均为95.71%/0.86%，掌纹基准上为97.60%/2.00%；预填充token从全上下文提示的18,781.96降至28.57。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存正从服务优化演变为长期LLM智能体的外部内存基础，但在多用户共享部署中，可复用的KV块存在缺失的访问控制问题：语义相关性本身无法确定内存块是否被授权给当前物理用户。需要一个明确的物理用户访问控制机制。

Method: Bio-MemArt为每个KV内存块附加归一化生物特征模板，首先用当前用户的生物特征探针过滤共享内存池，仅在被授权的候选池内运行原始MemArt的检索和KV复用管线。该设计在不破坏MemArt核心特性（潜在空间检索、直接缓存复用、分离位置编码）的前提下，引入生物特征门控实现物理用户访问控制。

Result: 在长期对话问答的人脸和掌纹基准上评估所有者与非所有者查询条件。人脸基准中所有者平均生物特征成功率为95.71%，非所有者为0.86%；掌纹基准中分别为97.60%和2.00%。效率方面，预填充token从全上下文提示下的18,781.96降至Bio-MemArt的28.57，表明生物特征门控保持了KV缓存内存的低token运行区间。

Conclusion: Bio-MemArt通过生物特征门控成功解决了共享KV缓存内存中缺失的物理用户访问控制问题，在保证所有者高访问成功率的同时将非所有者访问率压至极低水平，并验证了该机制不会牺牲KV缓存内存的效率优势。

Abstract: KV cache is evolving from a serving optimization into an external memory substrate for long-term LLM agents. In a shared multi-user deployment, however, reusable KV blocks introduce a missing access-control question: semantic relevance alone cannot determine whether a memory block is authorized for the current physical user. We propose Bio-MemArt, a biometric-aware KV-cache memory framework for multi-user LLM agents. Bio-MemArt attaches a normalized biometric template to each stored KV memory block, filters the shared memory pool with the current user's biometric probe, and then runs the original MemArt retrieval and KV reuse pipeline only inside the authorized candidate pool. This design preserves latent-space retrieval, direct cache reuse, and decoupled position encoding while adding physical-user access control to shared KV memory. We evaluate Bio-MemArt under Owner and Non-owner query conditions on long-term dialogue QA with face and palmprint benchmarks. Across face benchmarks, the average owner and non-owner biometric success rates are 95.71% and 0.86%; across palmprint benchmarks, they are 97.60% and 2.00%. In the efficiency study, average prefill tokens drop from 18,781.96 under full-context prompting to 28.57 with Bio-MemArt, showing that biometric gating preserves the low-token operating regime of KV-cache memory.

</details>


### [62] [AgentGrad: Intervention-guided Prompt Optimization for Multi Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08572)
*Jaewon Chu,Jinwoo Seo,Jaewon Cho,Jeehye Na,Yunyang Xiong,Youngdae Kim,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentGrad是一个面向多智能体系统的提示词优化框架，通过顺序干预定位失败相关的目标智能体，并以语义文本梯度抽象替代杂乱梯度聚合，在五个基准上取得SOTA性能并将优化时间平均加速2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统性能高度依赖各智能体的提示词设计，而现有文本梯度方法的两个关键阶段存在缺陷：梯度提取阶段未验证修改目标提示词能否真正解决失败，也缺乏对相应智能体中间输出的代理级监督；梯度聚合阶段随机分组拼接个体梯度，常混合无关失败模式导致提示词泛化能力差。

Method: AgentGrad包含两个核心组件：(1) 顺序干预（Sequential Intervention）——对每次失败逐一修改单个智能体的行为以定位能解决该失败的目标智能体，并用其修改后的输出作为代理级监督以提取细粒度梯度；(2) 语义文本梯度抽象（Semantic Textual Gradient Abstraction）——对语义相似的梯度进行聚类以防止无关失败模式混合，并将每个聚类抽象为捕获共享修正模式的广义梯度。

Result: AgentGrad在五个多智能体系统基准上取得最先进性能，相比次快基线将墙钟优化时间平均降低2.5倍。

Conclusion: 通过顺序干预增强梯度提取的准确性、以语义聚类与抽象改善梯度聚合的质量，AgentGrad有效解决了现有文本梯度方法在两阶段的核心缺陷，实现了更优的性能与更高的优化效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) achieve strong performance by employing specialized multiple agents, yet their performance depends on the prompt design of each agent. For MAS prompt optimization, textual gradient methods that guide prompt updates using natural-language feedback have emerged as a leading paradigm. In this paper, we identify limitations in two stages of existing textual gradient approaches: gradient extraction and gradient aggregation. In gradient extraction, previous works select a target prompt without verifying whether modifying it resolves the failure, and derive gradients without agent-level supervision over the corresponding agent's intermediate output. In gradient aggregation, individual gradients are randomly grouped and concatenated, often mixing unrelated failure modes and producing prompts that fail to generalize. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{AgentGrad}, a prompt optimization framework for multi-agent systems based on sequential intervention and semantic textual gradient abstraction. For each failure, sequential intervention modifies the behavior of one agent at a time to identify the target agent whose modification resolves the failure. The modified output of the target agent then serves as agent-level supervision for extracting a fine-grained gradient. Semantic textual gradient abstraction clusters semantically similar gradients to prevent mixing unrelated failure modes, and abstracts each cluster into a generalized gradient that captures the shared corrective pattern. Experimental results show that AgentGrad achieves state-of-the-art performance across five MAS benchmarks and reduces wall-clock optimization time by $2.5\times$ on average compared to the next-fastest baseline.

</details>


### [63] [A Three-Tier Persona Vector for Controllable User Simulation in Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08592)
*Rahul Khedar,Eshita,Sneha Teja Sree Reddy Thondapu,Mayank Malhotra,Arup Kumar Das,Jitesh Chandra Mishra,Arun Menon,Avinash Karn,Mouli V*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三层人物画像向量模型（23个操作化维度），用于生成多样化、真实的用户输入以评估工具增强型LLM代理，并通过64,698轮多轮对话实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架使用扁平的角色描述（如"你是一个愤怒的顾客"），导致无论底层场景如何变化，生成的对话几乎相同，缺乏多样性和真实性，无法准确评估工具增强型LLM代理的性能。

Method: 提出三层次人物画像向量：6个分类人口统计维度（管辖地、年龄、渠道、设备、语言熟练度、可用时间）、12个连续行为特质（耐心、自信、数字素养等）以高斯噪声围绕策展基础向量采样、5个随场景上下文变化的连续情绪状态（挫败感、焦虑、信任、自信、压力）。另设4级查询复杂度叠加层控制话语表述从直接到故意模糊。

Result: 在64,698轮多轮对话（8个命名画像、3个生产语料库）中验证：(i) 跨画像代理目标达成率出现15.8个百分点的差异，证实特质向量产生可测量的不同行为；(ii) 同一画像在不同场景中表现不同，验证场景反应式情绪状态设计；(iii) 领域特定项目显示预订流程合规性上约有15-20个百分点的画像敏感性差距；(iv) 七条规则描述的特质相关性产生可审计的共现模式。

Conclusion: 该人物画像模型已被完整规范以供复现，能够忠实地重现真实世界的难度分布，为工具增强型LLM代理的评估提供了比传统扁平角色描述更有效的方法。

Abstract: Evaluating tool-augmented LLM agents requires diverse, realistic user inputs yet most evaluation frameworks use flat role descriptions ("you are an angry customer") that produce near-identical conversations regardless of the underlying scenario. In this paper, we propose a three-tier persona vector with 23 operationalized dimensions: 6 categorical demographics (jurisdiction, age, channel, device, language proficiency, time availability), 12 continuous behavioral traits (patience, assertiveness, digital literacy, etc.) sampled with Gaussian noise around curated profile base vectors, and 5 continuous emotional states (frustration, anxiety, trust, confidence, stress) that shift in response to scenario context. Orthogonal to the persona, a 4-level query-complexity overlay controls utterance phrasing from direct to deliberately vague. We evaluate the persona model inside a synthetic data generation pipeline across 64,698 multi-turn conversations spanning 8 named profiles and 3 production corpora. Key findings: (i) a 15.8 percentage-point spread in agent goal-achievement across personas confirms trait vectors produce measurably different user behavior; (ii) the same persona behaves differently across scenarios due to scenario-reactive emotional state shifts, validating the scenario-reactive design; (iii) domain-specific projects show persona sensitivity on booking-flow compliance (~15-20 percentage points gap between tier-aware and pressure-test personas), demonstrating the model faithfully reproduces real-world difficulty distributions; (iv) seven rule-described trait correlations produce auditable co-occurrence patterns without requiring learned covariance matrices. The persona model is fully specified for reproduction.

</details>


### [64] [Graph-Based Personalized Memory for LLM Agents: Representation, Evolution, Retrieval, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08599)
*Dac Duy Anh Nguyen,Zhangchi Qiu,Shigeng Chen,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了基于图的个性化记忆在LLM智能体中的应用，提出以生命周期为导向的视角，将现有研究围绕记忆表示、记忆演化、记忆检索与记忆评估四个维度进行组织，旨在阐明图结构记忆如何支持自适应、可控且以用户为中心的长期个性化智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正从单次会话工具转向需要跨任务、跨场景长期适应用户的个性化助手，记忆成为个性化的核心需求。然而，现有的个性化智能体研究与通用图记忆框架研究彼此割裂，缺乏对整体设计空间的系统性理解，难以厘清基于图的记忆如何构建、连接、修订与检索以支持个性化决策。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，提出一个生命周期导向的分析框架，将现有研究围绕四个核心阶段进行组织：(1)记忆表示——如何用图结构（显式关系、时间上下文、证据链接）建模用户信息；(2)记忆演化——记忆如何随时间追加、修订与更新；(3)记忆检索——如何从记忆图中提取相关信息支持个性化决策；(4)记忆评估——当前评估实践与指标。此外还比较了关键设计选择，讨论了现有评估实践与开放性挑战。

Result: 整理并比较了图基个性化记忆系统中的关键设计选择，梳理了当前基于图的个性化记忆在记忆表示、演化、检索与评估四个维度的研究现状，识别了评估实践方面的一致性与不足，并概括了构建可靠长期个性化智能体所面临的关键开放性挑战。

Conclusion: 基于图的个性化记忆为LLM智能体提供了一种结构化建模用户偏好、目标、约束、关系与历史经验的方式，能够支持长期自适应、可控且以用户为中心的智能体。但该领域仍存在研究碎片化、评估标准不统一等问题，未来需要在记忆演化的可靠性、跨场景泛化能力以及个性化评估方法等方面继续探索。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are evolving from single-session tools toward long-term personal assistants that must adapt to individual users across tasks, contexts, and interactions. This shift makes memory a core requirement for personalization, since user preferences, goals, constraints, relationships, and past experiences are accumulated gradually and often change over time. Graph-based personalized memory provides a structured way to model such user information through explicit relations, temporal context, and evidence links. Such representations can model not only what an agent remembers about a user but also how memories are connected, revised, and retrieved to support personalized decisions. However, existing work remains fragmented across personalized agents and generic graph memory frameworks, making it difficult to understand the design space as a whole. This survey develops a lifecycle-oriented view of graph-based personalized memory for LLM agents. We organize existing studies around memory representation, memory evolution, memory retrieval, and memory evaluation. We further compare key design choices, discuss current evaluation practices, and open challenges in building reliable long-term personalized agents. This survey aims to clarify how graph-based memory can support adaptive, controllable, and user-centric LLM agents.

</details>


### [65] [It's All in the Way You Say It: The Role of Information Representation in LLM-Based Glycemic-Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08772)
*Andrea Apicella,Pasquale Arpaia,Matteo Orefice,Andrea Pollastro,Roberto Prevete*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于提示的通用大语言模型（LLMs）在1型糖尿病患者餐后高血糖和低血糖预测中的表现，使用OhioT1DM数据集，在30/60/90分钟预测视野下进行零样本和少样本推理，并与传统患者特定监督模型及专门适配的Gluco-LLM架构进行对比。结果显示：传统监督模型在高血糖预测上表现最强，而特定提示配置的LLM在低血糖预测上全面占优；生理信息的文本表征方式对提示推理效果影响显著，但增加上下文信息并未带来系统性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLMs可能用于生理时间序列预测，但其效果不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于生理信息在推理时的表征和呈现方式。作者旨在系统探究提示工程中信息表征策略对通用LLMs在血糖事件（高/低血糖）预测中的影响，并明确其与专用方法的性能差距与互补性。

Method: 基于OhioT1DM数据集，选取多个开源权重的通用LLMs，在零样本与少样本推理模式下执行提示式分类/预测任务。研究系统变化两个维度：(1) 生理信息的文本表征形式（从仅血糖观测到衍生描述符，再到叠加胰岛素、进餐、碳水化合物和体力活动等上下文变量）；(2) 暴露给模型的信息量。在30、60、90分钟三个预测视野上评估，并与传统患者特定监督模型及Gluco-LLM（专为血糖时间序列预测适配的语言模型架构）进行基准对比。

Result: 实验结果呈现明显的任务依赖性：传统监督模型在高血糖预测上表现最优；最佳配置的提示式LLMs在全部预测视野上均改善低血糖预测性能。信息表征方式显著影响提示推理的有效性，而额外提供上下文信息并未导致系统性性能提升。

Conclusion: 生理信息的文本表征方式是基于提示的LLM血糖事件预测方法中的核心设计因素，研究成果为血糖预测中LLM应用的提示设计与信息呈现策略提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being investigated for physiological time-series prediction, yet their effectiveness may depend not only on the model itself, but also on how physiological information is represented and presented at inference time. This study investigates prompt-based general-purpose LLMs for postprandial hyperglycemia and hypoglycemia prediction in individuals with type 1 diabetes. Using the OhioT1DM dataset, we evaluate multiple open-weight LLMs under zero-shot and few-shot inference across prediction horizons of 30, 60, and 90 minutes. The analysis varies both the textual representation of the available physiological information and the amount of information exposed to the model, ranging from glucose observations alone to derived descriptors and additional contextual variables related to insulin, meals, carbohydrates, and physical activity. Performance is compared with conventional patient-specific supervised models and with Gluco-LLM, a language-model-based architecture explicitly adapted to glucose time-series forecasting. Results show a marked task-dependent behavior. Conventional supervised models achieve the strongest performance for hyperglycemia prediction, whereas the best observed prompt-based LLM configurations improve performance for hypoglycemia across all investigated horizons. The effectiveness of prompt-based inference is also strongly influenced by how physiological information is represented, while providing additional contextual information does not lead to a systematic improvement. Overall, these findings highlight physiological information representation as a central design factor in prompt-based LLM approaches to glycemic-event prediction.

</details>


### [66] [Closing the Consistency Gap: Self-Evolving Agents That Learn to Stay on Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08832)
*Evelyn Duesterwald,Benjamin Elder,Lilian Ngweta,Shashanka Ubaru,Malgorzata Zimon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自进化智能体框架，通过识别轨迹中不稳定、低一致性的步骤并将其转化为情景记忆来缩小LLM智能体在生产环境中的"一致性差距"（consistency gap）。在AppWorld基准上使用ReAct/GPT-4.1时，该框架将五次运行全部成功的任务比例提升了16个百分点（同任务评估）和13个百分点（相似任务泛化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体平均准确率看似不错，但在生产环境中可靠性不足——同一任务执行多次结果不稳定。论文观察到ReAct智能体在AppWorld上单次通过率平均为77%，但五次全过的概率仅53%，存在24个百分点的"一致性差距"。作者认为解决这一差距是可信AI智能体部署的前提条件，而这一问题此前虽被观察到但未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出一个自进化智能体框架，核心包含两个组件：(1) Consistency Analyzer——定位轨迹中哪些步骤可能在多次执行中翻转，以及翻转的原因；(2) Guideline Generator——将诊断结果转化为针对性的指导准则，提交到记忆库中，并在未来执行相似任务时注入到智能体的执行过程中，从而将不稳定的步骤固化为指导性记忆。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上使用ReAct/GPT-4.1配置，该框架将同任务评估中五次运行全部成功的任务比例提升了16个百分点，在相似任务泛化评估中提升了13个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过自进化式地将低一致性步骤转化为情景记忆，可以有效缩小LLM智能体的一致性与可靠性差距，为AI智能体的可信部署提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents can be accurate on average yet unreliable in production, a discrepancy that has been observed but remains largely unaddressed. When given the same task five times, a ReAct agent on the AppWorld benchmark using GPT-4.1 succeeds in all five runs only 53% of the time, even though its per-run pass rate averages 77%. We call this 24-point shortfall the consistency gap, and we argue that addressing it is a precondition for trustworthy AI agent deployment. We present a self-evolving agent framework that reduces this gap by identifying unstable, low-consistency steps in agent trajectories and converting them into episodic memory the agent can draw on in future runs. At its core is a Consistency Analyzer that pinpoints where and why a trajectory is likely to flip across executions, and a Guideline Generator that converts the diagnosis into targeted guidelines, committed to memory and injected into future agent executions on similar tasks. On AppWorld with ReAct/GPT-4.1, our framework raises the fraction of tasks that succeed in all five runs by +16 points on same-task evaluation and +13 points on similar-task generalization.

</details>


### [67] [PlannerForge: LLM Agents for Scenario-Based Testing of Motion Planners in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08965)
*Yuan Gao,Sebastian Müller,Mattia Piccinini,Marc Kaufeld,Yuchen Zhang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Qunying Song,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlannerForge是一个统一的LLM智能体框架，覆盖自动驾驶场景测试全流程，包括从场景生成到ADS评估的各个阶段，并新增ADS增强与基准测试两个模块。实验表明其在多项任务上表现优异，开源模型可与商业API媲美，且能显著提升规划器成功率并降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全性验证至关重要，基于场景的测试是验证自动驾驶系统（ADSs）的系统性方法，但现有流程是一个碎片化的模块化流水线，各环节（场景生成、检索、修改、ADS执行和结果分析）由独立工具完成且交互极少。尽管LLM智能体在ADS子系统（感知、规划、控制）中展现了潜力，但尚未有工作用统一的LLM智能体框架覆盖完整的场景测试流水线。

Method: 提出PlannerForge框架，用LLM智能体统一扩展从场景生成到ADS评估的所有测试阶段，并新增两个LLM增强阶段：ADS增强（Enhancement）和ADS基准测试（Benchmarking）。使用10个现成LLM在5种提示条件下评估全部任务（生成、选择、修改、模块路由、规划器测试和增强），并进行端到端流水线链接实验及成本调优实验。

Result: 各项任务最佳得分在0.88至1.00之间；开源20-35B模型在多数任务上可与商业API匹敌，如Qwen3.6:35B在5项任务中的3项匹配商业API。端到端链接保留83%（商业）/78%（开源）的种子查询。在自然语言生成上以193比144个可执行场景（共200个）超越Scenario Factory 2.0，实现92-96%的属性还原率；在排名1选择上以92.0% vs 67.5%超越BM25；在物理有效编辑上以≥94% vs 31%超越From-Words-to-Collisions。在N=400时，成本调优将规划器成功率从50.4%提升至70.2%，碰撞率从19.0%降至8.4%，且无需特定领域微调。

Conclusion: PlannerForge验证了统一LLM智能体框架覆盖自动驾驶场景测试全流程的可行性，证明开源模型在多数任务上可作为商业API的替代方案，同时通过成本感知的调优策略能够在不进行领域微调的情况下显著改善自动驾驶规划器的安全性和成功率。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of autonomous driving is a critical challenge. Scenario-based testing is a systematic process used to validate Autonomous Driving Systems (ADSs), but it remains a fragmented modular pipeline in which scenario generation, retrieval, modification, ADS execution, and results analysis are performed by separate tools with little interaction. Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise across ADS sub-systems such as perception, planning, and control. However, no prior work covers the whole scenario-based testing pipeline for ADSs with a unified LLM-agent framework. We present PlannerForge, an LLM-agent framework that extends all scenario-based testing stages (from Scenario Generation to ADS Assessment) and adds two further LLM-enhanced stages: ADS Enhancement and ADS Benchmarking. We evaluate PlannerForge with 10 off-the-shelf LLMs across all tasks (Generation, Selection, Modification, Module Routing, Planner Testing, and Enhancement) under 5 prompt conditions. Best-per-task scores range from 0.88 to 1.00, and open-source 20-35B backends match commercial APIs on most tasks. Open-source models such as Qwen3.6:35B match commercial APIs on three of the five tasks. Chaining the modules end-to-end retains 83% / 78% of seed queries (commercial / open). It outperforms Scenario Factory 2.0 (Finkeldei et al., 2025) on natural-language generation (193 vs. 144 executable of 200) and realises 92-96% of requested city, road and vehicle attributes. It outperforms BM25 (Robertson and Zaragoza, 2009) at rank 1 selection (92.0% vs. 67.5%) and From-Words-to-Collisions (Gao et al., 2025) on physically valid edits (>=94% vs. 31%). At N=400, cost-tuning lifts planner success from 50.4% to 70.2% and cuts collisions from 19.0% to 8.4%, without domain-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [68] [Deposon: An Auditable, Conservation-Guaranteed, Game-Theoretically Tested Scattering Layer over LLM Reasoning Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09001)
*Qihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Deposon散射层，为多步LLM推理的概念分解图构建机器可复核的"账本"，通过三通道散射（透射/反射/不可逆耗散）实现能量审计精确到机器epsilon；论文诚实地报告了所有三类证据层级，多数实验为阴性或关闭/削弱的结论，仅一致性层面的证据存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步LLM推理缺乏机器可复核的账本机制——被丢弃的推理路径没有可审计的记录。这导致推理过程无法被独立验证或事后审计，需要一种能绑定推理节点并提供能量守恒式审计记录的结构。

Method: 提出Deposon散射层：(1) 将LLM生成的概念分解图的每个节点绑定到双参数Deposon状态；(2) 路径经历三通道散射——透射(T)、反射(R)、不可逆耗散(A)，满足T+R+A=1；(3) 在合成陷阱基准上测路径过滤增益，在真实基准上对比六个关键词规则过滤器；(4) 使用凸组合测试融合增益；(5) 将反向动力学建模为图上的势博弈，量化经验协调比(ECR)；(6) 通过预注册的kill协议验证三个动力学等价命题。

Result: 最大每路径能量审计偏差为2.2E-16（机器epsilon）。合成陷阱上统一方法达100% vs 诱饵捕获基线7%/10%。真实基准上与六关键词规则过滤器无显著差异（GSM8K 0.87≥0.85, McNemar p=0.5；StrategyQA 0.899=0.899）。融合为阴性结果：凸组合从未改善（physics 0.484→0.452），lambda=2的增益是反场伪影。三个动力学等价命题(P1a/P1b/T-P1c)在kill协议下被证伪，势博弈声明降级为近似（循环图中位数残差0.669），仅一致性级证据存活。

Conclusion: Deposon层的差异化价值仅在于机器可验证性，而非推理性能提升；其融合增益必须是非线性的；在动力学层面只有一致性证据存活，其余声明均被诚实地报告为阴性、关闭或被削弱的结果。

Abstract: Multi-step LLM reasoning lacks a machine-recheckable ledger: discarded reasoning paths leave no auditable record. We propose the Deposon scattering layer, which binds each node of an LLM-generated concept-decomposition graph to a two-parameter Deposon state; paths undergo three-channel scattering -- transmission, reflection, irreversible dissipation -- obeying T+R+A=1 for arbitrary parameters, with a maximum per-path energy-audit deviation of 2.2E-16 (machine epsilon). We report all three evidence tiers honestly. On synthetic trap benchmarks the path-filtering gain is closed (pre-registered): unified reaches 100% versus a decoy-capture baseline at 7%/10%. On real benchmarks the layer is indistinguishable from a trivial six-keyword rule filter (GSM8K 0.87 >= 0.85, McNemar p=0.5; StrategyQA 0.899 = 0.899); no difference is detected here, so we sharpen the claim to "the differential value lies solely in machine verifiability." Fusion yields a second negative result: convex combinations with a semantic prior never improve (physics 0.484 -> 0.452), and the apparent lambda=2 gain is an anti-field artifact; any fusion gain must be nonlinear. Modeling the reverse dynamics as a potential game on the graph, we evidence an auditable scalar's monotonicity and near-gradientness and quantify the empirical coordination ratio (ECR). The three formalized dynamical-equivalence propositions (P1a/P1b/T-P1c) are falsified under the pre-registered kill protocol, and the potential-game claim is downgraded to approximate (cyclic-graph median residual 0.669): only consistency-level evidence survives at the dynamical level. Code: github.com/zeroandcat/Deposon.

</details>


### [69] [Answer-Distribution Trajectories: A Stochastic-Dynamics View of LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09030)
*Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Davide Murari,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Pietro Liò,George Montañez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"答案分布轨迹"（answer-distribution trajectories）方法，基于随机动力学思想，跟踪大语言模型在思维链推理过程中对完整答案分布的变化，作为端点准确率和熵曲线更精细的表示，用以区分推理成功与失败的不同动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链推理评估主要依赖端点准确率，忽略了模型到达答案所经历的路径。熵曲线虽能追踪推理过程中不确定性的演化，但无法揭示哪些相互竞争的假说导致了该不确定性。因此需要一个能同时捕捉推理路径细节与竞争假说信息的更精细表示。

Method: 引入"答案分布轨迹"这一受随机动力学启发的表示方法，记录推理每一步中模型对候选答案的完整预测分布。基于该表示，定义了一个"动力学推理画像"（dynamical reasoning profile），从探索（exploration）、修订（revision）、运动（motion）和承诺（commitment）四个维度刻画推理过程的动态特征，从而区分推理成功与失败的不同动力学机制。

Result: 在16个开放权重语言模型和4个推理基准上的实验表明：相同端点和相似熵曲线的轨迹可能呈现截然不同的推理动力学；这些动力学在模型间、任务间以及同一模型内部均存在显著差异；不同优化目标倾向于不同的动力学画像；训练和推理选择会系统性地重塑这些动力学画像。

Conclusion: 答案分布轨迹为分析和评估大语言模型推理的动态过程提供了一个丰富的框架，弥补了端点准确率和熵分析无法揭示推理路径与竞争假说信息的不足。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning provides a structured computation between a model's input and final answer. Yet it is often evaluated through endpoint accuracy, which ignores the path taken to reach that answer. An emerging line of work addresses this limitation using entropy profiles, which track how uncertainty evolves over the reasoning process but do not reveal which competing hypotheses account for that uncertainty. We introduce answer-distribution trajectories, a stochastic-dynamics-inspired representation that tracks the model's full predictive distribution over answers as reasoning unfolds. As a strictly finer representation than endpoint and entropy summaries, answer-distribution trajectories enable us to characterize a trace through a dynamical reasoning profile spanning exploration, revision, motion, and commitment, and to distinguish different dynamical mechanisms of reasoning success and failure. Across sixteen open-weight language models and four reasoning benchmarks, we show that traces with the same endpoint and similar entropy profiles can exhibit substantially different reasoning dynamics. We further find substantial variation in these dynamics both within and across models and tasks, with different objectives favoring different dynamical profiles. Additionally, we show that training and inference choices systematically reshape these profiles. Our results suggest that answer-distribution trajectories provide a rich framework for analysing and evaluating the dynamics of LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [MeClear: Cooperative Game-Theoretic Attribution and Risk-Aware Memory Clearance for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09115)
*Boyu Yang,Jiazheng Sun,Zilong Lu,Zhi Qiu,Xin Peng,Jun Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MeClear是一个任务条件的记忆清除框架，通过合作归因识别具有负面下游效用的记忆并选择性抑制，在十种长对话记忆池上实现85.9%的目标召回率和82.3%的整体任务恢复率，较Leave One Out基线提升25.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体依赖外部记忆系统保存用户偏好和任务知识，但传统检索机制只优化语义兼容性而非下游效用，经常将过时、误导或冲突的证据引入活动上下文，影响智能体性能。

Method: MeClear通过合作归因（cooperative attribution）识别具有负面下游效用的记忆，结合Leave One Out筛除与采样合作Shapley归因来跨交互证据分配效用，解决单次移除评估无法处理的冗余冲突遮蔽问题。利用归因排序，MeClear在嵌套过滤上执行查询范围的极简清除策略，在不清除持久记忆库的前提下验证清除后的上下文任务恢复。

Result: 在十个长对话记忆池上的综合实验评估中，MeClear实现85.9%的目标召回率和82.3%的整体任务恢复率，较Leave One Out (LOO)基线提升25.5个百分点。

Conclusion: MeClear证明了将下游任务效用作为记忆管理的优化目标比单纯语义匹配更为有效，合作归因方法能解决记忆中冗余冲突遮蔽的问题，为长时程LLM智能体的记忆系统提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Long horizon Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on external memory systems to preserve user preferences and task knowledge across extended interactions. Conventional retrieval mechanisms optimize semantic compatibility rather than downstream utility, frequently introducing outdated, misleading, or conflicting evidence into the active context. We present MeClear, a task conditioned memory clearance framework that identifies memories featuring negative downstream utility through cooperative attribution and selectively suppresses them from agent execution. MeClear combines Leave One Out screening with sampled cooperative Shapley attribution to distribute utility across interacting evidence, effectively resolving redundant conflict masking where single removal evaluations fail. Utilizing attribution rankings, MeClear executes a query scoped minimal clearance strategy over a nested filtration, verifying task recovery on the cleared context without permanently altering the persistent memory bank. Comprehensive experimental evaluations across ten long dialogue memory pools demonstrate that MeClear achieves a target recall of 85.9% and an overall task recovery rate of 82.3%, representing a 25.5 percentage point improvement over Leave One Out (LOO) baselines.

</details>


### [71] [Procedural Graphs: Self-Evolving Execution Structures for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09153)
*Yuxing Lu,Yicheng Chen,Shanchan Wu,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Procedural Graph框架，将智能体的程序性知识组织为（步骤，关系，步骤）三元组图结构，在每一步决策时通过指导模型将局部子图转化为情境化引导来偏置求解器行为，并通过LLM提炼器对比失败与成功轨迹实现图的自我演化，持续提升智能体长期任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为智能体进行长程规划和外部工具调用时，多数采用对累积历史的无约束生成来选择动作，隐含了程序性知识（做什么、按什么顺序做、在什么条件下做）而未显式建模。随着轨迹变长，智能体会丢失目标、乱序调用工具、重复无效动作。作者希望显式组织程序性知识以解决这些问题。

Method: Procedural Graph框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 图结构：将程序性知识组织为(步骤,关系,步骤)三元组，类比知识图谱组织事实性知识(实体,关系,实体)；(2) 决策引导：每个决策步骤定位智能体的活跃节点，由指导模型将周围子图翻译为即时的情境性引导，偏置而非强制求解器的下一步动作；(3) 自我演化：一个LLM提炼器对比失败与成功轨迹，编辑图拓扑和属性，仅保留能维持或提升验证集性能的编辑，同时保留被拒绝的编辑以抑制重复。

Result: 从最小骨架出发，循环构建的图可匹敌或超越手工设计的图；该方法还能修复有缺陷的专家先验。在多个数据集、任务类型和LLM上，Procedural Graph相较基于记忆的基线取得了一致性改进，自我演化进一步提升了性能而无需人工工程。

Conclusion: 将程序性知识以图结构显式组织和自我演化，能够有效提升LLM智能体在长程任务中的规划与工具调用能力，证明了程序性知识显式建模与自动提炼的价值。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents that plan over long horizons and act through external tools. Most agents select actions through unconstrained generation over an accumulating history, leaving implicit the procedural knowledge of what to do, in what order, and under which conditions. As trajectories lengthen, agents can lose track of their objectives, invoke tools out of order, and repeat unproductive actions. We introduce the Procedural Graph: just as a knowledge graph organizes factual knowledge into (entity, relation, entity) triplets for what-is questions, a Procedural Graph organizes procedural knowledge into (procedure, relation, procedure) triplets for what-to-do questions. At each decision step, the framework localizes the agent's active node, and a guidance model translates the surrounding subgraph into step-level situational guidance that biases the solver's next action without dictating it. The graph is self-evolving: an LLM refiner contrasts failed trajectories with successful ones and edits the graph's topology and attributes, committing edits that preserve or improve held-out validation performance while retaining rejected ones to discourage repetition. Starting from a minimal skeleton, the loop builds graphs that match or surpass hand-designed ones. It can also repair a flawed expert prior. Across multiple datasets, task types, and LLMs, the Procedural Graph delivers consistent gains over memory-based baselines, and self-evolution further improves performance without manual engineering.

</details>

### [72] [Differentiable Solvation Shell Model for Rational Electrolyte Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05671)
*Hancheng Zhao,Hongyi Lin,Celia Kelly,Venkatasubramanian Viswanathan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Ising模型的平均场建模框架，用于预测锂离子电池电解液中Li+溶剂化壳层组成，模型以供体数（DN）、受体数（AN）、摩尔比和分子大小为输入，可通过梯度优化直接从MD溶剂化结构参数化。在LHCE体系上实现壳层组成10.7% RMSE和R2=0.87的精度，并展现出对其他电解质体系的良好泛化能力，同时以极低计算成本复现了MD结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管局部高浓度电解质（LHCE）等锂金属电池电解质设计前景广阔，但对分子性质如何定量决定Li+溶剂化壳层组成仍缺乏量化理解，阻碍了电解质的理性设计。

Method: 作者建立了一个基于Ising模型的平均场框架，以供体数（DN）、受体数（AN）、摩尔比和分子大小为输入特征预测溶剂化壳层组成。由于该框架端到端可微，可通过梯度下降直接从MD模拟获得的溶剂化结构数据中拟合模型参数。

Result: 模型在LHCE体系上实现溶剂化壳层组成10.7% RMSE和R2=0.87，自由溶剂比RMSE为2.3%；能复现真实LHCE电解质的溶剂化趋势，且对LHCE之外的其他电解质体系表现出良好泛化性。交互项分析表明DN主导Li+溶剂化能量学。在未参与训练的LiTFSI/tetraglyme (G4)体系中，模型识别出氟苯（FB）为有前景的稀释剂，并预测了有利于阴离子衍生SEI和高氧化稳定性的盐浓度窗口，该预测得到高保真MD验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个可解释的、可微分的预测Li+溶剂化壳层组成的框架，为DN经验性设计规则提供了热力学基础，并具有扩展到其他电解质类别的潜力。

Abstract: Tailoring Li+ solvation structures has emerged as a promising design principle across multiple Li metal battery (LMB) electrolytes, with localized high concentration electrolytes (LHCE) emerging as a leading candidate. However, rational design of these electrolytes remains limited by a poor quantitative understanding of how molecular properties govern Li+ solvation shell composition. Here we introduce a mean field modeling framework, built on an Ising model, that predicts solvation shell composition from donor number (DN), acceptor number (AN), molar ratio, and molecular size. This framework is end-to-end differentiable, which enables parameterization directly from molecular dynamics (MD) solvation structures via gradient-based optimization. Focusing on LHCE, the model achieves 10.7% RMSE and R2=0.87 for shell composition, 2.3% RMSE on free solvent ratio, and further reproduces solvation trends in real LHCE electrolytes, at a fraction of MD's cost. Our model also shows good generalizability on other electrolyte systems in addition to LHCE. Analysis of its interaction terms shows that DN dominates Li+ solvation energetics, providing a thermodynamic basis for empirical DN-based design rules. We further demonstrate the model's design utility on the LiTFSI/tetraglyme (G4) system absent from training. The model identifies fluorobenzene (FB) as a promising diluent and predicts a salt-concentration window with anion-rich solvation shells and low free solvent, favorable for stable anion-derived SEIs and high oxidative stability, respectively. This prediction is validated by higher-fidelity MD. This work establishes an interpretable differentiable framework for predicting Li+ solvation shell composition, with potential to extend to other electrolyte classes.

</details>


### [73] [cuSOAP: a GPU-accelerated Generator of Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions Descriptor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05709)
*Hongyu Yan,Yuqing Xia,Yong Wei,Minghan Chen,Hanning Chen*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出cuSOAP，一个基于PyTorch的GPU加速SOAP描述符及解析导数生成器，通过融合CUDA/Triton内核消除大规模中间张量，在NVIDIA GB200节点上相比CPU参考实现DScribe快13倍至227倍，支持至百万原子水团簇的近线性扩展，使大规模凝聚相模拟中SOAP的实时评估成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOAP描述符是分子机器学习中最广泛采用的原子环境表示之一，但其计算成本（尤其是大径向和角向基组下及其导数）是SOAP类原子间势的主要瓶颈，制约了复杂多物种凝聚相环境的大规模模拟。

Method: 基于PyTorch构建cuSOAP，对GTO和高斯多项式径向基采用闭式表达式或求积格式计算投影系数及其笛卡尔梯度；通过融合CUDA和Triton内核避免朴素张量公式的多GB中间变量；作为DScribe的drop-in替代品，保持构造器签名、特征排序及输出一致性（误差约10^-6），直接接受ASE Atoms对象。

Result: 在NVIDIA Grace-Blackwell（GB200）节点上，单GPU对1000分子水团簇的完整描述符+Jacobian任务比DScribe快13倍至227倍（覆盖整个(n_max,l_max)超参数图），加速比随l_max增大而增长；仅描述符生成呈t∝n^1.34的近线性扩展，百万原子水团簇单GPU耗时19.5秒，四GPU共享内存多进程驱动降至5.7秒，并行效率约90%且无饱和迹象。

Conclusion: cuSOAP的出色性能使大规模凝聚相模拟中SOAP描述的实时（on-the-fly）评估成为现实，为SOAP基原子间势在复杂体系中的实际应用扫清了计算障碍。

Abstract: The Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions (SOAP) descriptor is one of the most widely adopted representations of atomic environments in molecular machine learning, but the cost of evaluating it and its derivatives remains a principal bottleneck of SOAP-based interatomic potentials, particularly at the large radial and angular basis sizes demanded by complex, multi-species condensed-phase environments. We present cuSOAP, a GPU-accelerated generator of atom-wise SOAP vectors and their analytic derivatives. Built on PyTorch, cuSOAP evaluates closed-form expressions or quadratures for the projection coefficients and their Cartesian gradients for Gaussian-type-orbital and polynomial radial bases, through fused CUDA and Triton kernels that eliminate the multi-gigabyte intermediates of a naive tensor formulation. The package is a drop-in replacement for the CPU-based reference DScribe, reproducing its constructor signature, feature ordering, and output to within ${\sim}10^{-6}$, and accepts structures directly as Atomistic Simulation Environment (ASE) Atoms objects. On an NVIDIA Grace--Blackwell (GB200) node, a single Blackwell GPU generates the full descriptor-plus-Jacobian workload for a 1000-molecule water cluster up to two orders of magnitude faster than DScribe, i.e., from $13\times$ to $227\times$ across the entire $(n_{\max}, l_{\max})$ hyperparameter map, with the speedup growing with the angular band limit $l_{\max}$. Descriptor-only generation scales as $t \propto n^{1.34}$, close to linear, up to a million-atom water cluster, which is processed in 19.5s on one GPU and, through a shared-memory multiprocessing driver, in 5.7s on the four GPUs of the node, demonstrating $\sim$90% parallel efficiency with no sign of saturation as devices are added. These results bring on-the-fly SOAP evaluation for large-scale condensed-phase simulation within reach.

</details>


### [74] [Performance of a minimally empirical local-hybrid density functional for molecular chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06162)
*Erin R. Johnson,Kyle R. Bryenton*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个极简经验性的局域杂化泛函LHnz，通过依赖关联长度和交换-关联空穴归一化的局域混合分数，仅用三个经验参数即在GMTKN55基准上超越了所有现有全局杂化泛函，且无大的离群值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离域化误差被认为是密度泛函理论中最重大的挑战之一。局域杂化泛函是减小该误差最有前景的途径，但现有精度好的局域杂化泛函往往高度经验化，依赖复杂的函数形式（如范围分离、校准函数、幂级数展开甚至神经网络）。作者希望探索一个极简的、避免这些复杂性的局域杂化泛函形式能达到的精度极限。

Method: 提出了LHnz泛函：使用无色散交换，局域混合分数依赖于关联长度和有效交换-关联空穴归一化。通过仅使用三个经验参数来平衡描述交换-关联效应。

Result: 在GMTKN55分子热化学基准测试中，LHnz以仅三个经验参数超越了所有现有全局杂化泛函，并且没有大的离群值。

Conclusion: 极简的、低经验性的局域杂化泛函形式也能达到甚至超越复杂的全局杂化泛函精度，表明局域混合分数策略的有效性及其在克服离域化误差方面的潜力。

Abstract: Delocalisation error has been argued to be the greatest outstanding challenge in density-functional theory (DFT). One of the most promising routes to minimise this error is development of local hybrid functionals, in which the fraction of exact-exchange mixing is position dependent. However, existing local hybrids capable of good thermochemical accuracy are often highly empirical, and tend to have complicated functional forms that involve some combination of range separation, calibration functions, power-series expansions, or even neural networks. In this work, we explore the limits of what can be achieved with a minimally empirical local hybrid functional form that avoids such complexities. The ``LHnz'' functional is proposed, which uses dispersionless exchange and a local mixing fraction dependent on the correlation length and effective exchange--correlation hole normalisations. With only three empirical parameters, LHnz is shown to outperform all existing global hybrid functionals for the GMTKN55 molecular-thermochemistry benchmark with no large outliers.

</details>


### [75] [Tensor-based Approximation of Molecular Kinetics: Generator Learning, Reaction Coordinates and Incremental Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06446)
*Minakshi Verma,Peter Benner,Feliks Nüske*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量的方法，通过张量近似分子动力学模拟的无穷小生成元，来分析长时间尺度的动力学——亚稳态和转变时间尺度。该方法从低能生成元本征值的变分近似出发，使用张量积基离散本征值问题，推导了张量算子（TT格式）的解析表达式及高效求解算法，并处理了与反应坐标相关的状态依赖扩散场情形，支持逐步增加反应坐标数量。在快速折叠蛋白的MD模拟和TICA坐标上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学模拟中长时问尺度的动力学分析（如亚稳态和转变时间尺度）是极具挑战性的问题。传统方法在处理高维反应坐标空间时面临维数灾难，而现有变分方法对低能本征值的近似在计算效率和可扩展性方面存在不足，缺乏对反应坐标逐步增加的灵活支持。

Method: 该方法：(1) 从低能生成元本征值的变分近似出发构建特征值问题；(2) 使用张量积基（tensor-product basis）对特征值问题进行离散化；(3) 推导出张量算子（tensor train, TT）格式下的解析表达式与相应的高效线性求解算法；(4) 处理状态依赖扩散场（state-dependent diffusion field）的一般情形，该情形在反应坐标计算中具有实际相关性；(5) 支持逐步增加反应坐标数量，无需从头重新求解变分问题。

Result: 在快速折叠蛋白的MD模拟和TICA（时间独立成分分析）坐标作为反应坐标的实验中，所提出的方法成功识别出了长时间尺度转变和亚稳态，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于张量近似的方法能够在处理长时间尺度动力学分析时有效克服维数灾难问题，为分子模拟中的亚稳态识别和转变时间尺度估计提供了一种高效且可扩展的计算框架。

Abstract: We present an approach to analyze long-timescale kinetics of molecular dynamics simulations - meta-stable states and transition timescales - by a tensor-based approximation of the infinitesimal generator. We start from the variational approximation of low-lying generator eigenvalues, and discretize the associated eigenvalue problem using a tensor-product basis. We derive analytical expressions for the resulting tensor operators in tensor train (TT) format, and provide efficient algorithms to solve the corresponding linear problems. We also treat the case of a state-dependent diffusion field, which is relevant when working in reaction coordinates. We show how the number of reaction coordinates can be gradually increased without having to solve the variational problem from scratch. Using MD simulations of fast-folding proteins and TICA coordinates as reaction coordinates, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method at identifying long-timescale transitions and meta-stable states.

</details>

Motivation: 传统机器学习逆设计方法依赖大规模训练数据集，且难以泛化到训练分布之外的条件，限制了材料发现的范围和效率。

Method: 将图神经网络分子动力学模拟器与可微逆设计框架结合，采用短时动力学初始化与基于物理信息的仿真细化，在模拟过程中实现超越训练数据范围的目标优化。

Result: 在无序弹性网络上，仅用泊松比0.1-0.4的非助柔系统训练，即可设计出泊松比低至-0.3的强助柔网络；能生成训练范围外的应力-应变响应和训练数据中不存在的局部缺陷；跨尺寸泛化良好，可优化多达5000节点的网络。

Conclusion: 该可微逆设计框架有效解决了传统结构-性质机器学习模型泛化能力不足的问题，为材料加速发现提供了新途径。

Abstract: Machine-learning-based inverse design can accelerate the discovery of materials with targeted properties, but conventional structure--property models often require large training datasets and generalize poorly beyond their training distribution. Here, we present a differentiable inverse design framework based on a graph neural network molecular dynamics simulator. By combining a short dynamical initialization with physics-based refinement during simulation, the framework enables optimization well beyond the conditions represented in the training data. Using disordered elastic networks, we show that a simulator trained only on non-auxetic systems with Poisson's ratios between 0.1 and 0.4 can design strongly auxetic networks with values as low as -0.3. The framework also produces stress--strain responses outside the range observed during training, creates localized mechanical defects absent from the training data, and generalizes across system size, enabling optimization of networks tested up to 5000 nodes despite being trained only on networks with fewer than 200 nodes.

</details>


### [77] [First-order Degenerate Symmetry-Adapted Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07215)
*Dominik Cieśliński,Tijs Karman,Anthony Scemama,Michał Hapka,Piotr S. Żuchowski*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了对称自适应微扰理论的一阶简并形式（dSAPT），通过弱对称强迫量化简并或近简并势能面区域中电子配置的混合，将其分解为静电和交换（泡利排斥）贡献，并在多个模型系统（H₂–F、H₂–NO、水及苯激基缔合物）中验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简并电子态控制着激基缔合物的光物理性质以及锥形交叉附近的分子动力学。在势能面简并或近简并的区域，分子间相互作用会混合相应的电子配置，而现有的理论框架缺乏对此类混合进行定量分解的工具。

Method: 作者发展了对称自适应微扰理论的一阶简并形式（dSAPT），采用弱对称强迫方案。比较了两种简并消解方式：在对一阶本征问题对角化之前或之后施加反对称性。单分子波函数使用CASSCF或CIS方法描述，理论上将配置混合分解为静电贡献和交换（泡利排斥）贡献。

Result: 在H₂–F(²P)和H₂–NO(²Π)两个空间简并模型系统中，dSAPT揭示了交换效应对配置混合的显著贡献在中间分子间距离已经出现。对H₂–NO(²Π)复合物，二阶色散能对定性地再现绝热混合角的角度依赖性至关重要。对水和苯激基缔合物，泡利排斥在短分子间距离控制激发的离域化。

Conclusion: dSAPT提供了一种新的定量工具，能够将简并/近简并区域的电子配置混合分解为物理上可解释的静电和交换贡献，并揭示了交换效应（泡利排斥）在空间简并体系的配置混合中扮演的关键角色，特别是对激基缔合物的激发离域化行为。

Abstract: Degenerate electronic states govern the photophysics of excimers and the molecular dynamics near conical intersections. In regions where potential energy surfaces are degenerate or nearly degenerate, intermolecular interactions mix the corresponding electronic configurations. We introduce a first-order degenerate formulation of symmetry-adapted perturbation theory (dSAPT) with weak symmetry forcing that quantifies the mixing in terms of electrostatic and exchange (Pauli repulsion) contributions. We compare two alternative ways of resolving the degeneracy, in which antisymmetry is enforced either after or before diagonalization of the first-order eigenproblem. Using CASSCF monomer wave functions, we apply dSAPT to two model spatially degenerate systems, H$_2$--F(${}^2P$) and H$_2$--NO(${}^2Π$), and show that exchange effects contribute significantly to configuration mixing already at intermediate intermolecular separations. For the H$_2$--NO(${}^2Π$) complex, we further demonstrate that second-order dispersion energy is essential for qualitatively reproducing the angular dependence of the diabatic mixing angle. Finally, using water and benzene excimers with monomers described either by CASSCF or CIS wave functions, we show that Pauli repulsion controls delocalization of the excitation at short intermolecular separations.

</details>


### [78] [Low-rank approximation of Moment Tensor Potential enables reducing training set size without loss of accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07372)
*Anna Bondarenko,Nikita Rybin,Maxim Rakhuba,Ivan S. Novikov*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于张量列车（TT）分解实现了力矩张量势（MTP）的低秩近似，即张量因子化MTP（TFMTP）。通过MaxVol算法对四组分熔盐FLiNaK和五组分MoNbTaWV随机合金进行主动训练，发现在1.5倍压缩下，TFMTP比原始MTP拟合所需的构型配置少两倍，且精度相当。两种模型预测的物理性质（密度、粘度、弹性常数、体积模量）差异可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTP作为机器学习原子间势在材料模拟中表现优异，但其高维张量参数存储和训练成本较高。作者旨在通过低秩近似降低MTP的计算与存储开销，同时减少主动训练所需的构型数量，提高训练效率。

Method: 采用张量列车（TT）分解对MTP的张量参数进行低秩压缩，实现张量因子化MTP（TFMTP）。在分子动力学模拟（FLiNaK熔盐）和几何优化（MoNbTaWV合金）过程中，通过基于MaxVol的算法同时对MTP与TFMTP进行主动训练。在1.5倍压缩条件下，对比两者拟合所需的构型数量及精度；随后用两种模型分别预测FLiNaK在600-1200 K下的密度和粘度，以及MoNbTaWV在零温下的弹性常数和体积模量。

Result: 在1.5倍压缩下，TFMTP达到与原MTP同等精度所需的训练构型数量仅为后者的一半（即减少两倍配置数）。对于FLiNaK的密度、粘度和MoNbTaWV的弹性常数、体积模量，两种模型的预测结果差异可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: TFMTP通过低秩张量化实现了MTP的模型压缩，在保持预测精度的同时显著提高了训练数据效率，是一种有效的MTP轻量化方案，适用于多组分复杂体系的机器学习势开发。

Abstract: In this study, we implement a low-rank approximation of Moment Tensor Potential (MTP) based on the tensor train (TT) decomposition. The implemented tensor-factorized MTP (TFMTP) and the original MTP model are actively trained via a MaxVol-based algorithm during molecular dynamics simulations of a four-component molten salt mixture, LiF-NaF-KF (FLiNaK), and geometry optimizations of a five-component equiatomic MoNbTaWV random alloy. We demonstrate that under a 1.5-fold compression, TFMTP requires two times fewer configurations for fitting than the original MTP model, while maintaining an indistinguishable level of accuracy. These actively trained MTP and TFMTP models are further used to evaluate the density and viscosity of FLiNaK at temperatures ranging from 600 to 1200 K, as well as the elastic constants and bulk modulus of the MoNbTaWV alloy at zero temperature. For both atomic systems, the differences in physical properties predicted by MTP and TFMTP are negligible.

</details>


### [79] [Simulation-Supervised Foundation Models for Retention Time Prediction in High-Performance Liquid Chromatography beyond Experimental Data Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07531)
*Stephen Wu,Yufeng Han,Yasuhiro Mito,Yoshiyuki Watabe,Yoshihiro Hayashi,Hikaru Takaya,Takuya Kubo,Ryo Yoshida*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了FUSE-RT，一个多任务基础模型，集成来自179种色谱方法的RT数据，并引入Sim2Real迁移学习，将大规模计算模拟数据（PolyOmics，涵盖约21,400个分子的39种性质）作为辅助监督迁移到实验RT预测中，实现对未知分子和方法的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效液相色谱保留时间（RT）预测面临挑战，因为实验训练数据仅覆盖有限的化学空间和方法空间，难以推广到多样化的分子和色谱方法。

Method: 开发FUSE-RT多任务基础模型：(1) 集成179种色谱方法的RT数据；(2) 引入Sim2Real迁移学习，将分子动力学和密度泛函理论计算生成的大规模模拟数据（PolyOmics中的39种分子性质）作为辅助监督迁移到实验RT预测；(3) 在分子、方法及联合分布偏移场景下评估泛化能力。

Result: 模拟数据监督显著提升超出实验分子域的迁移能力，尤其在覆盖缺口明显和少样本自适应场景下；RT预测误差随模拟数据规模系统性下降，遵循显著的幂律关系。

Conclusion: Sim2Real迁移是一种可扩展的策略，能够将RT预测扩展到实验色谱数据覆盖范围之外，为有限实验数据条件下的RT预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of high-performance liquid chromatography (HPLC) retention times (RTs) across diverse molecules and chromatographic methods remains challenging because experimental training data cover only a limited region of chemical and method spaces. Here, we develop FUSE-RT (Foundation model Unifying Simulation and Experimental supervision for Retention Time), a multitask foundation model that integrates RT data from 179 chromatographic methods and adapts to unseen molecules and methods using limited target-domain data. To extend transferability beyond experimental coverage, we introduce simulation-to-real (Sim2Real) transfer learning, in which molecular representations learned from large-scale computational data are transferred to experimental RT prediction. Specifically, we use PolyOmics, comprising 39 properties for approximately 21,400 molecules generated by molecular dynamics and density-functional theory calculations, as auxiliary supervision. We evaluate generalization under molecular, method, and joint molecular--method distribution shifts. Simulation-derived supervision substantially improves transfer beyond the experimental molecular domain, particularly under pronounced coverage gaps and few-shot adaptation. Moreover, RT-prediction error decreases systematically with increasing simulation-data size, following a significant power-law relationship. These results establish Sim2Real transfer as a scalable strategy for extending RT prediction beyond the finite coverage of experimental chromatographic data.

</details>


### [80] [Fixed-Dimensional Latent Flow for Generating Variable-Size 3D Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08333)
*Weichi Yao,Cameron Gruich,Bryan R. Goldsmith,Yixin Wang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出EF-TALFM，一种两阶段生成框架，完全依赖单一固定维度的分子级潜在表示来生成可变大小的3D分子。第一阶段通过自回归Transformer解码器解码潜在向量，在生成原子类型、坐标和化学信息状态的同时确定分子大小；第二阶段使用流匹配模型采样该潜在向量。该方法在PCQM4Mv2数据集上实现了89.4%的高质量分子比例，优于UAE-3D（75.6%）和FlowMol（69.8%），并在HOMO-LUMO间隙条件生成中将DFT验证的命中率提高了一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分子发现中，分子大小与组成、结构及其他目标属性相耦合，但现有的大多数3D生成器要求分子大小在生成前就被预先指定，这限制了生成的真实多样性和灵活性，无法支持开放式的分子设计。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段将分子编码为标准化的固定维度分子级潜在表示；第二阶段使用流匹配模型采样该潜在向量，随后由自回归Transformer解码器联合生成原子类型、坐标和化学信息状态（确定分子大小）。采用规范原子排序和刚体位姿对齐，使标准Transformer无需等变层即可工作。联合解码几何和化学状态实现确定性、化学引导的图恢复，无需稠密成对键解码器。支持无条件与属性条件流匹配，可选属性监督通过内部排序读出头实现。

Result: 在PCQM4Mv2上，EF-TALFM生成的可通过去重、训练集新颖性、净化及PoseBusters完整性检查的分子比例达到89.4%，显著高于UAE-3D的75.6%和FlowMol的69.8%；训练与采样吞吐量也更高。在10个目标HOMO-LUMO间隙上，内部排序头使DFT验证命中率（误差≤0.1eV内）翻倍，同时保持独特验证命中中97%的新颖性。

Conclusion: 实验证明固定维度的分子级生成加对称性解析的自回归实现，为开放式和属性定向的3D分子设计提供了一种实用的架构范式。

Abstract: In molecular discovery, molecule size is coupled to composition, structure, and other target properties. Yet most 3D generators require molecule size to be specified before generation. Here, we introduce Equivariant-Free Transformer-Autoencoded Latent Flow Matching, a two-stage generative framework that relies entirely on a single fixed-dimensional molecule-level latent representation to generate variable-size molecules. The second-stage flow matching model samples this latent vector, and an autoregressive Transformer decoder then determines molecule size while generating atom types, coordinates, and chemically informative states. Canonical atom ordering and rigid-pose alignment enable standard Transformers without equivariant layers, while joint decoding of molecular geometry and an enriched chemical state enables reliable, deterministic, chemistry-guided graph recovery without requiring a learned dense pairwise bond decoder. The same fixed-dimensional latent supports unconditional and property-conditioned flow matching, while optional property supervision adds an internal ranking readout, with no separate predictor or reference calculations. On PCQM4Mv2, EF-TALFM achieves the highest fraction of molecules that are unique, training-set novel, pass sanitization and PoseBusters sanity checks, 89.4\%, compared with 75.6\% for UAE-3D and 69.8\% for FlowMol. EF-TALFM also achieves higher measured computational throughput for training and sampling. Across ten target HOMO--LUMO gaps, internal ranking doubles the density functional theory (DFT)-verified hit rate within $0.1\,\mathrm{eV}$, while preserving 97\% novelty among unique verified hits. These results demonstrate that fixed-dimensional molecule-level generation followed by symmetry-resolved autoregressive realization provides a practical architecture for open-ended and property-directed 3D molecular design.

</details>


### [81] [TSBench: A physics-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLM understanding of chemical reaction mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08503)
*Xiaohu Xu,Tong Zhu*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了TSBench基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）代理通过结构编辑工具构建三维过渡态（TS）猜测的能力，并由自动化量子化学流程验证。在546次评估中，诊断驱动的修订使成功率从50.4%提升至66.8%，揭示了LLM在机理级化学任务中的强项与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学基准主要测试LLM的事实知识与基于文本的推理能力，而化学反应的真正理解需要将符号化的反应物-产物描述映射到原子重排的三维路径（即过渡态）。目前缺乏一个以物理为基础、能评估LLM对化学机理深层理解的基准。

Method: 作者设计TSBench基准：LLM代理使用结构编辑工具构建三维过渡态猜测，随后由自动化量子化学管道进行严格验证，给出基于物理学的通过/失败判定。对7个前沿LLM在78个基础反应上进行546次评估，并引入诊断驱动的修订机制（即根据失败诊断进行修正迭代）。

Result: 总体成功率在诊断驱动修订下从50.4%提升至66.8%；最优模型在最简单反应上接近90%成功率，但随着机理复杂性增加性能急剧下降。大多数失败尝试产生了局部合理的鞍点，但其反应路径通向错误的反应物-产物对。

Conclusion: LLM表现出局部几何直觉但缺乏对全局反应坐标的稳健把握。TSBench为机理敏感型任务（如合成规划与自主实验）中的LLM代理建立了机理性评价标尺，揭示了当前模型在化学机理理解上的关键短板。

Abstract: Understanding a chemical reaction requires mapping a symbolic reactant-product description onto the three-dimensional pathway through which atoms rearrange, yet chemistry benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) largely probe factual knowledge and text-based reasoning. Here we introduce TSBench, a benchmark in which an LLM agent uses structure-editing tools to construct three-dimensional transition-state (TS) guesses verified by an automated quantum-chemical pipeline, yielding a physics-grounded pass/fail verdict. Across 546 evaluations of seven frontier LLMs on 78 elementary reactions, the aggregate success rate rose from 50.4% to 66.8% under diagnosis-driven revision; the best models approached 90% on the simplest reactions, yet performance dropped sharply with mechanistic complexity. Most failed attempts produced locally plausible saddle points whose reaction paths led to the wrong reactant-product pair, revealing local geometric intuition without a robust grasp of the global reaction coordinate. TSBench establishes a mechanism-level yardstick for LLM agents in mechanism-sensitive tasks such as synthesis planning and autonomous experimentation.

</details>


### [82] [Competing Ring-Opening and Hofmann Elimination Pathways in Aqueous TEMPO Catholytes: A First-Principles Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08721)
*Line Mouaffac,Maiwenn Souetre,Guillaume Jeanmairet,Mathieu Salanne*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用从头算分子动力学结合增强采样方法，系统研究了五种TEMPO衍生物在水系液流电池中的降解机理，发现开环反应比Hofmann消除具有更低的活化自由能，是动力学上优先的降解途径，且不同官能化对开环能垒影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水系氧化还原液流电池中基于TEMPO衍生物的阴极电解液具有大规模储能潜力，但其氧化态（N-氧代铵态）的化学不稳定性严重限制了实际应用。理解降解机理是提高其稳定性的关键，但实验上难以直接解析原子层面的降解路径，因此需要理论模拟来揭示。

Method: 采用从头算分子动力学（AIMD）结合增强采样技术，对五种TEMPO衍生物提出两种降解机理（开环和Hofmann消除）进行系统研究，计算并比较各机理的活化自由能，以确定动力学上优先的降解路径。

Result: 所有研究的TEMPO衍生物中，开环反应的活化自由能均低于Hofmann消除，表明开环是动力学优先的降解途径。但开环能垒在不同分子间变化显著，说明不同官能化方式对其抗降解稳定性影响很大，计算结果与已有实验研究相吻合。

Conclusion: 研究提供了实验难以解析的降解路径的原子级图像，强调了分子结构在控制TEMPO衍生物在水系电解质中动力学稳定性方面的关键作用，为设计更稳定的TEMPO基液流电池阴极材料提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Aqueous redox-flow batteries based on TEMPO derivatives are promising for large-scale energy storage, but their practical use is limited by the chemical instability of the oxidized N -oxoammonium state. In this work, we investigate the degradation of five TEMPO derivatives using ab initio molecular dynamics combined with enhanced sampling. Two proposed degradation mechanisms, ring opening and Hofmann elimination, are examined and their corresponding activation free energies are compared. For all derivatives considered, ring opening exhibits a lower activation free energy than Hofmann elimination, identifying it as the kinetically preferred degradation pathway. The magnitude of the ring-opening barrier, however, varies significantly between molecules, showing that different functionalizations strongly influence its stability toward degradation. The predicted preference for ring opening is consistent with available experimental studies, which have identified or inferred ring-opening degradation for several TEMPO-based catholytes. These results provide an atomistic picture of degradation pathways that are difficult to resolve experimentally and highlight the importance of molecular structure in controlling the kinetic stability of TEMPO derivatives in aqueous electrolytes.

</details>


### [83] [Balanced and broadly-normed meta-generalized gradient approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09034)
*Yanyong Wang,Chandra Shahi,John P. Perdew,Adrienn Ruzsinszky*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过在r²SCAN型meta-GGA泛函中引入以自旋非极化成键体系为范数的局部插值函数修改，构建出bn-r²SCAN泛函，实现了带隙预测接近超非局域LAK meta-GGA精度、晶格常数接近r²SCAN精度的平衡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半局域密度泛函近似系统性低估带隙，而meta-GGA虽然理论上能通过动能密度依赖部分克服此局限，但原子化能、反应势垒高度、晶格常数和带隙对meta-GGA大参数空间的不同敏感性使得难以同时兼顾各项性能。因此需要探索合适的规范（norms）来指导泛函构建，以实现性能平衡。

Method: 探索了指导r²SCAN型泛函构建的合适规范空间，这些规范通过连接单电子极限和均匀电子气极限的插值函数来定义。引入对强光滑插值函数的局部修改，考察其对泛函性能的影响，并将自旋非极化的成键体系纳入规范集合中，最终构建出bn-r²SCAN meta-GGA泛函。

Result: bn-r²SCAN meta-GGA泛函实现了性能平衡：带隙预测精度接近超非局域LAK meta-GGA，而晶格常数精确度接近r²SCAN。

Conclusion: 通过在规范空间中纳入自旋非极化成键体系并对插值函数进行局部调整，可以在meta-GGA框架内同时改善带隙和晶格常数等相互竞争的性质，实现更均衡的整体性能。

Abstract: Conventional semi-local density functional approximations for the exchange-correlation energy systematically underestimate fundamental band gaps. Advanced meta-generalized gradient approximations (meta-GGAs) can partially overcome this limitation through their orbital dependence via the kinetic energy density. In principle, the flexible meta-GGA form should allow for balanced performance across diverse properties. However, atomization energies, reaction barrier heights, and lattice constants, as well as band gaps, exhibit different sensitivities to the large parameter space of meta-GGA functionals. In this work, we explore the space of appropriate norms that guide the r$^2$SCAN-type functional construction through the interpolation function connecting two fundamental paradigms: the one-electron limit and the uniform electron gas limit. We introduce local modifications to a strongly-smoothed interpolation function and investigate their impact on functional performance. To further probe the limits of the meta-GGA framework, we include spin-unpolarized bonded systems among the set of norms. The resulting bonded-norm bn-r$^2$SCAN meta-GGA achieves balance: band-gap predictions approaching the accuracy of the ultra-nonlocal LAK meta-GGA and lattice constants of nearly r$^2$SCAN accuracy.

</details>

### [84] [Bridging simulation length scales with cellular automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05696)
*John J. Karnes,Esteban D. Gadea,Shakkira Erimban,Ignacio J. Bombau,Valeria Molinero*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于元胞自动机（CA）的方法，通过从高保真模拟（如分子动力学MD）的构型中学习局部规则，生成任意规模的晶格初始构型，用于多尺度模拟中衔接细粒度和粗粒度模型（如从MD到动力学蒙特卡洛kMC）。以水合阴离子交换膜（AEM）为验证案例，生成的构型在kMC模拟中与MD基准结果一致，并能扩展到更大的晶格规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度模拟需要耦合不同时空尺度的物理模型，但粗粒度模型无法从任意堆积自组装出物理上有意义的结构，必须从更高保真度的模拟中继承初始构型。因此，粗粒度模型可访问的长度尺度受限于可负担平衡的最大细粒度构型，而不是由粗粒度方法本身决定。需要一个能生成任意大规模晶格初始构型的方法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用元胞自动机（CA）方法生成晶格起始构型。具体流程：1）从高保真模拟（MD）产生的构型中提取训练数据；2）通过逻辑回归从这些训练集中学习短程局部的CA演化规则；3）反复应用这些CA规则以产生涌现的长程有序结构；4）生成新构型并在介观kMC模拟中与MD基准进行比较验证；5）展示生成更大规模晶格的能力。

Result: 以水合阴离子交换膜（AEM）为原型验证，生成的构型在介观kMC模拟中的性能与MD推导的"ground truth"基准结果一致；且证明了能够生成更大的晶格，其kMC行为与较小的CA基准构型保持一致。

Conclusion: CA方法成功弥合了MD到kMC之间的尺度鸿沟，使得粗粒度模型可以不再受限于细粒度模拟可平衡的最大尺寸，从而访问更长的长度和时间尺度。短程局部规则产生长程有序结构这一特性天然契合了从细粒度到粗粒度模型的映射逻辑。

Abstract: Multiscale simulation requires coupling physics models that operate at different characteristic length and time scales, because no single method spans the range needed for most problems of interest. Moving to a coarser-grained representation unlocks longer length and time scales, but it discards the microscopic interactions that build morphology. A fine-grained model can be initialized from an arbitrary packing and left to self-assemble into a physically meaningful structure; a lower-resolution model cannot, and must inherit its starting configuration from a higher-fidelity simulation. The length scales accessible to the coarse-grained model are therefore set not by the coarse-grained method itself, but by the largest fine-grained configuration that can be affordably equilibrated. A representative example is the scale-up from particle-based molecular dynamics (MD) to a lattice-based representation such as kinetic Monte Carlo (kMC). In this work, we present a cellular automata (CA) approach for generating arbitrarily large lattice starting configurations. CA is a natural fit for this task: short-ranged local rules drive the evolution of a lattice, and their repeated application gives rise to emergent long-range order, thematically mirroring how short-ranged interactions in MD produce self-assembled morphology. We use a configuration from a higher-fidelity simulation as a training set and learn the CA rules from it via logistic regression. As a proof of principle, we develop these rules for a hydrated anion exchange membrane (AEM), generate new starting configurations, and benchmark their performance in mesoscale kMC simulations against an MD-derived "ground truth." We then demonstrate the ability to generate substantially larger lattices and show that their behavior in kMC is consistent with that of the smaller CA benchmark configurations.

</details>


### [85] [FPBench: Application-Oriented Error Decomposition for Foundation Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05714)
*Kiyan Amirian,Ramanuja Srinivasan Saravanan,Felix Adams,Charles E Schwarz,Yifei Mo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: FPBench是一个面向应用场景的基础势能（FP）基准测试平台，对多项最先进FP在力预测、能量排序和离子迁移三类基本计算任务上进行评估，发现平均力与能量误差无法可靠预测试任务表现，并提出面向应用的误差分解指标以精确定位FP误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础势能（FP）的平均能量和力误差指标常显示接近DFT精度，但在实际计算任务中表现却不一致。现有评估方法缺乏对应用场景可靠性的考量，需要一个面向应用的基准来揭示FP在真实计算任务中的实际表现。

Method: 构建FPBench基准，在三种基本任务（原子模拟的力预测、替代位和空位排序的能量排名、离子/空位迁移）上评估多个最先进的FP。引入面向应用的误差分解指标，包括高精度原子和大力误差原子比例、远离平衡构型、相对相稳定性与凸包一致性、离子迁移路径误差等，以按物理关键量和构型解析性能。

Result: 基准测试表明，平均力与能量误差往往无法预测试任务表现，不同FP在实际可靠性上存在显著差异，凸显了应用导向评估的必要性。误差分解指标能够定位FP在特定计算任务中误差产生的环节。

Conclusion: FPBench提供开放的基准、评估代码和公开排行榜，为严谨的FP评估和开发提供支撑，通过应用导向的误差分解为模型开发提供有针对性的指导。

Abstract: Foundation potentials (FPs) have emerged as a new basis for atomistic modeling. While their evaluation using average energy and force errors often indicates near-DFT accuracy, their performance in practical computational studies remains inconsistent. Here, we present FPBench, an application-oriented benchmark that evaluates FPs on representative computational tasks. Multiple state-of-the-art FPs are assessed on three fundamental tasks: force prediction for atomistic simulations, energy ranking for substitutional and vacancy orderings, and ion/vacancy migration. Benchmarking these FPs shows that average force and energy errors often fail to predict task performance, revealing substantial differences in practical reliability. FPBench introduces application-oriented error decomposition through metrics that resolve performance according to the physically consequential quantities and configurations governing computational tasks, including the fractions of highly accurate and large-force-error atoms, far-from-equilibrium atoms, relative phase-stability and convex-hull agreement, and along-path errors in ion migration. These error-decomposition metrics identify where FP errors arise within specific computational tasks, providing targeted guidance for model development. FPBench provides an open benchmark, evaluation code, and a public leaderboard for rigorous FP assessment and development.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文填补了笛卡尔形式下不可约张量分解通用构造的空白，提供了仅依赖秩和对称性、可复用的显式分解/重构算子，并扩展了笛卡尔谐函数与ICT耦合算子的应用。该工作对等变机器学习、材料科学中各向异性分析等领域具有重要价值，通过natto包为研究者提供了直接可用的工具。

Abstract: Molecular and material properties, from the polarizability to the elastic constants, are described by tensors. Their behavior under rotations is made explicit when a tensor is decomposed into irreducible parts that transform independently. In Cartesian form these parts are the symmetric and traceless irreducible Cartesian tensors (ICTs), whose decomposition and coupling underlie selection rules, orientational averages, and the use of symmetry. The decomposition is a textbook result at rank two, but higher rank and the intrinsic symmetry of physical tensors make it nontrivial. What has been lacking, unlike in the well-established spherical formalism, is a general construction for a given intrinsic symmetry, together with reusable operators that extract the ICTs and rebuild the original tensor exactly. Here, we develop such a construction and obtain these operators explicitly. These operators depend on rank and symmetry alone, and therefore each need only be built once and then applied to any tensor of that class. Building on them, we further obtain the Cartesian harmonics of a vector and the operators that couple two ICTs into a third, both central to equivariant machine learning. The construction is demonstrated on the elastic tensor, in both its second-order form of rank four and its third-order form of rank six. The ICTs of the rank-4 tensor also define a rotation- and scale-invariant measure of anisotropy, which we evaluate across the first-principles elastic tensors of crystalline materials from the Materials Project. The construction is implemented in the open-source package natto, which produces the operators in both exact symbolic and numerical form.

Method: 本文是一篇综述文章，采用多层面叙述结构：首先介绍理解交变磁体所需的基础概念（对称性、轨道特征、电子结构等）；其次系统梳理已鉴定出的交变磁性材料家族，着重分析配位环境和结构-性能关系；接着介绍适用于表征交变磁性材料的实验方法；随后探讨利用从头算方法结合化学约束（如对称性和成键）进行材料发现的计算策略；最后讨论交变磁体在技术上的可能应用以及与其他有序态（如铁电性和超导性）的耦合。

Result: 本文指出交变磁性已在多种材料家族中被鉴定，配位环境与结构-性能关系是促进交变磁序的关键因素；从头算材料发现结合化学约束是寻找新型交变磁体的有效途径；交变磁体在技术应用中展现出广阔前景，包括与铁电和超导等现有有序态的耦合。

Conclusion: 作者总结指出，交变磁性研究仍面临若干挑战，但以化学为基础的设计准则将在推动该领域进一步发展中发挥重要作用，从基础概念到前沿进展的全面梳理为该领域提供了重要的参考框架。

Abstract: Magnetism has been a central theme of research in chemistry, physics, and materials science, with chemical composition and bonding playing key roles in determining magnetic behavior. Altermagnets are a newly identified class of magnetic materials that combine features of conventional ferromagnets and antiferromagnets, arising from specific symmetry and electronic structure motifs. In this review, we present a chemistry-driven viewpoint on altermagnetism, highlighting how crystal chemistry, bonding, and electronic structure enable this unconventional magnetic order. We begin by introducing the fundamental concepts required to understand altermagnets, with an emphasis on symmetry considerations, orbital character, and electronic structure signatures. We then survey the diverse material families in which altermagnetism has been identified, drawing attention to coordination environments and structure-property relationships that favor altermagnetic order. We subsequently present experimental approaches which are useful for the characterization of altermagnetic materials. We examine ab initio materials discovery as a promising strategy for identifying new altermagnets, emphasizing how chemical constraints, such as symmetry and bonding, can guide computational searches. Other than their intrinsic importance, altermagnets provide interesting possibilities for technology. For this reason, we highlight possible applications that may be enabled through altermagnetic materials, along with their coupling with existing orders such as ferroelectricity and superconductivity. In conclusion, we point out some challenges and prospects, where chemically-based design guidelines can play an important role towards advancing altermagnetism research. In summary, this review offers an account of recent developments in altermagnetism, from basic concepts to the current state-of-the-art.

</details>

High-resolution scanning transmission electron microscopy revealed the extended core structures consistent with coupled synchro-Shockley partial dislocations bounded by extrinsic stacking faults in C15 NbCr2 and their involvement in twinning. These results highlight the critical role of coupled synchro-shear slip in enabling phase transformations between Laves polytypes and in governing twinning behavior, providing new atomistic insight into the kinetic nature of plasticity in topologically close-packed intermetallic phases.

Here we use spin-polarized density-functional theory with a Hubbard correction (DFT+U) to determine the atomistic thermodynamics underlying this preferential localization. A systematic set of composition-matched bulk/grain-boundary DFT+U supercells provides a common correlated-electron description across boundary chemistries. We compare B, Zr, Dy, and ZrB2-like local configurations in bulk and grain-boundary environments of Nd2Fe14B. Excess B is strongly stabilized at the boundary, while Zr also shows an independent thermodynamic preference for the interfacial region. When Zr and B are combined in a ZrB2-like configuration, the boundary preference is retained, indicating that the interface remains favorable as Zr-B coordination develops. These results establish a thermodynamic pathway for the co-localization of Zr and B prior to ZrB2 formation. Magnetic-state analysis further shows that all compared structures remain within the same high-moment Fe-sublattice regime, and boundary-localized defects generally perturb the normalized magnetization less than their matrix counterparts. The calculations therefore provide a first-principles explanation for why Zr-B chemistry concentrates at intergranular regions and how such boundary-localized states can support the microstructural stability required for high-coercivity recycled Nd-Fe-B magnets.
