Abstract: Water dissociation on metal oxide surfaces is a key elementary step in heterogeneous catalysis, photocatalysis, and radiation chemistry, yet its mechanistic details on rare-earth (RE) sesquioxides remain poorly understood. Here, we investigate water dissociation on the (110) surfaces of three cubic bixbyite oxides, Sc$_2$O$_3$, Y$_2$O$_3$, and Lu$_2$O$_3$, using molecular dynamics combining ab initio calculations with on-the-fly machine-learning force field acceleration. By sampling 25 independent trajectories per material, we obtain an unbiased picture of the reaction landscape inaccessible to conventional static calculations. Two distinct dissociation pathways are identified: a conventional proximal mechanism with a small but finite barrier of $\sim$0.1 eV, and a previously unreported distal mechanism that is effectively barrierless and energetically preferred at both the adsorption and dissociation stages. The low barriers are consistent with the periodic array of inherently undercoordinated RE$^{3+}$ sites in the bixbyite lattice, suggesting that ordered intrinsic coordination defects play a role analogous to stochastic oxygen vacancies in conventional oxides.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统探索新型NbMn6Ge6系列化合物的磁性基态、电子结构与成键特性，评估其作为磁性材料（特别是用于拓扑磁性或自旋动力学研究）的潜力，并为实验合成和表征提供理论指导。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）计算方法，对三种预测稳定的化合物（NbMn6Ge6、NbMn6Ge5、NbMn6Ge4）进行系统计算。具体包括：磁性基态构型确定（对比反铁磁与铁磁构型）、磁矩分析（评估Mn原子磁矩的局域化程度）、磁各向异性能计算、电荷密度差（CDD）与电子局域化函数（ELF）分析以考察成键特征，以及费米能级附近的电子结构（能带）分析以评估拓扑磁性特征。

Result: 三种化合物中，结构稳定的NbMn6Ge6呈现共线反铁磁基态，而亚稳的NbMn6Ge5和NbMn6Ge4为铁磁基态。Mn磁矩近似局域化。磁各向异性方面：AFM NbMn6Ge6和FM NbMn6Ge5为单轴各向异性，FM NbMn6Ge4为面内各向异性。CDD与ELF分析揭示了Mn-Ge Kagome网络内外的电荷重分布与成键。费米能级附近电子结构不具备拓扑磁性研究的特征。

Conclusion: NbMn6Ge6-x系列化合物中，结构化稳定性与磁性基态之间存在关联（稳定相为反铁磁，亚稳相为铁磁）。这些材料由于局部磁矩和特定磁各向异性行为，可能适合用广义自旋哈密顿量描述其自旋动力学，但费米能级附近缺乏适合拓扑磁性的电子特征。最终结论需要通过实验合成、结构表征和磁性测量来验证理论预测。

Abstract: In this work, we systematically investigate the magnetic ground states, electronic structures, and bonding characteristics of the computationally predicted stable NbMn6Ge6, NbMn6Ge5, and NbMn6Ge4 using first-principles calculations. Our results show that structurally stable NbMn6Ge6 has a collinear antiferromagnetic configuration, while the metastable rhombohedral NbMn6Ge5 and NbMn6Ge4 favor ferromagnetic ground states. Magnetic moments on Mn atoms are nearly localized, suggesting the applicability of a generalized spin Hamiltonian. Magnetic anisotropy in AFM NbMn6Ge6 and FM NbMn6Ge5 has uniaxial behavior, while FM NbMn6Ge4 has in-plane anisotropy. Charge density difference and electron localization function analyses further show charge redistribution and bonding features within the Mn-Ge Kagome network and between adjacent structural layers. Electronic structures near the Fermi level show no features suitable for topological magnetism studies. Experimental synthesis, structural characterization, and magnetic measurements are required to verify our predictions.

Method: 该框架将DFT计算的二元混合焓与亚规则溶液模型相结合来构建相图，无需拟合高阶相互作用即可跨组分空间高效扩展。模型纳入了依赖晶格构型的能量学以及随温度变化的元素相变信息，以增强预测精度。

Abstract: Multiple principal element alloys (MPEAs) exhibit complex phase equilibria involving multinary solid solutions and intermetallics, which makes it challenging to predict their temperature-composition phase diagrams. Their vast compositional space makes first principles methods prohibitively expensive, while CALPHAD is limited by scarce experimental data. Here, we present a computationally efficient framework to predict the solvus phase boundaries, and hence, phase fields, in refractory MPEAs composed of Cr, Hf, Mo, Nb, Ta, Ti, V, W, and Zr. The approach combines DFT calculated binary mixing enthalpies with sub regular solution models to construct phase diagrams without fitting higher order interactions, enabling efficient scaling across composition space. Validation against 36 binary and 15 ternary phase diagrams demonstrates good agreement, with both experimental results and CALPHAD calculations. We find that the prediction accuracy is enhanced by incorporating lattice dependent energetics through sub regular solution models and including temperature-dependent elemental phase transitions. The framework captures miscibility gaps, solid solution stability, and intermetallic formation, with predicted miscible temperatures typically within 300 K of experimental values. Overall, this work establishes a scalable, first principles based route for highthroughput prediction of phase diagrams in refractory MPEAs. A publicly accessible web interface has also been developed to allow interactive exploration of the predicted phase diagrams, available at https://raptor.engr.wustl.edu.

Conclusion: 该研究确立了共轭聚合物中光学退相干弱热缩放的普遍性，并强调其实验呈现必须结合检测可观测量的物理背景进行解读，为理解共轭聚合物的激子退相干机制提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Conjugated polymer aggregates exhibit optical decoherence in the presence of strong vibronic coupling and substantial diversity in chemical structure, solid-state organization, and excitonic character. Here, we use coherence-detected and population-detected two-dimensional electronic spectroscopies to examine the temperature dependence of the homogeneous optical linewidth across a series of semiconducting polymers. Despite substantial differences in molecular architecture and solid-state organization, all polymers studied exhibit remarkably weak thermal linewidth scaling over the measured temperature range. Comparison between complementary detection modalities further shows that, while this weak temperature dependence is obust, the absolute homogeneous linewidth depends on the measured observable. This behavior reflects the different ways in which coherence- and population-detected measurements project population relaxation and pure dephasing onto the spectroscopic response. These results establish the weak thermal scaling of optical decoherence across a diverse series of conjugated polymers and show that its experimental manifestation must be interpreted in the context of the detection observable.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态电解质中快速离子输运的微观起源尚未被完全理解，缺乏微观认识导致下一代固态电池开发主要依赖经验。现有测量要么只覆盖低频输运响应，要么只涉及高频束缚极化，而中间介观频率区间正是离子输运涌现的关键区域，此前缺乏系统研究。

Result: 发现存在从束缚电流主导的电导到短程色散离子输运产生的电导之间的交叉转变。与P-O^- -Ag^+基团的键弯曲运动相关的束缚极化在所有组分中都存在，但单独不足以产生离子输运。只有当该极化嵌入足够软的AgPO_3玻璃基质并伴随高载流子密度时，输运才会涌现。

Abstract: Fast ionic transport is a defining feature of many solid electrolytes, yet its microscopic origin is not fully understood. In the absence of microscopic insights, the development of next-generation solid-state batteries remains largely empirical. Most existing measurements access either the low-frequency transport response or the high-frequency bound polarization, yet the intermediate mesoscopic frequency regime is where ionic transport emerges. By varying the $\mathrm{AgI}$ concentration ($x$) and performing time-domain terahertz spectroscopy (TDTS) in a prototypical glassy electrolyte $\left(\mathrm{AgI}\right)_{x}\left(\mathrm{AgPO_3}\right)_{(1-x)}$, we reveal this intermediate frequency regime and identify a crossover from bound-current-dominated conduction to conductivity arising from short-range dispersive ionic transport. We find that bound polarization associated with the bond-bending motion of the $\mathrm{P{-}O^- -Ag^+}$ motif is present across compositions but is insufficient to produce ionic transport on its own. Transport emerges only when this polarization is embedded in a sufficiently soft $\mathrm{AgPO_3}$ glassy matrix and accompanied by a high carrier density. These ingredients together take the system from a vibrationally bound response to short-range dispersive motion.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖晶石硫族化合物是研究金属态、超导态和压力诱导绝缘态之间相互竞争与交织的理想平台，但此前对这类化合物的压力研究范围有限，对高压相结构的原子尺度细节及其与输运性质演化的关联尚不清楚。

Method: 结合同步辐射粉末X射线衍射与电电阻率测量，对三种尖晶石化合物在超出先前探索范围的压力区间进行系统研究；利用密度泛函理论松弛结构模型进行约束轮廓拟合以解析高压相结构；通过交流磁化率测量确认超导性。

Result: 三种化合物在高压下均出现密切相关的单斜超胞结构。CuIr2Se4和CuRh2S4的高压相与Phase-IV型键失配（bond-disproportionated）结构兼容，而CuRh2Se4仅确定了相容的单斜晶胞但未能解析原子尺度有序图案。三种化合物的绝缘化转变压力演化特征各异：CuIr2Se4在窄压力区间内急剧转变，Rh基化合物则在更宽压力范围内渐变，与各自结构转变高度对应。此外，CuIr2Se4在常温常压下表现出此前未被报道的体超导电性，零电阻在0.29 K实现，交流抗磁响应与近乎完全的超导屏蔽一致。

Conclusion: 高压单斜相的形成与绝缘输运行为的演化之间存在密切关联；过渡金属与硫族元素的替换可在相近的结构框架内有效调节特征压力尺度和与超导性的竞争关系。

Abstract: Spinel chalcogenides provide a platform for investigating the interplay among metallic, superconducting, and pressure-induced insulating states. Here, we combine synchrotron powder X-ray diffraction and electrical-resistivity measurements to investigate the pressure evolution of CuIr2Se4, CuRh2S4, and CuRh2Se4 over pressure ranges extending beyond those previously explored. High-pressure diffraction reveals closely related monoclinic supercells in all three compounds. For CuIr2Se4 and CuRh2S4, constrained profile fits based on structural models relaxed using density functional theory are compatible with Phase-IV-type bond-disproportionated structures, whereas the data for CuRh2Se4 establish a compatible monoclinic unit cell without resolving its atomic-scale ordering pattern. Insulating-like transport develops abruptly over a narrow pressure range in CuIr2Se4 but more gradually over broader pressure ranges in the Rh-based compounds, in close correspondence with their respective structural transformations. We also establish previously unreported bulk superconductivity in CuIr2Se4 at ambient pressure: zero resistance is attained at 0.29 K, and the accompanying ac diamagnetic response is consistent with nearly complete superconducting shielding. These results establish a close relationship between the formation of the high-pressure monoclinic phases and the evolution toward insulating transport, and demonstrate that transition-metal and chalcogen substitutions tune the characteristic pressure scales and the competition with superconductivity within a closely related structural framework.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZrO2纳米颗粒在实际应用中尺寸达数纳米，但传统DFTB方法在处理氧化物纳米结构时存在几何优化稳健性不足的问题，且缺乏对CO吸附过程中不同配位环境（体相面、尖端、边缘）的准确描述。因此需要开发一种实用策略来扩展DFTB在复杂氧化物纳米结构中的适用性。

Method: 开发了一种混合DFTB-FF框架：带结构计算依赖现有Slater-Koster框架，几何优化与吸附性质通过引入经典长程原子间势（替代传统斥力势）来控制精度；同时引入配位依赖的Zr-C势，以区分体相面位点与欠配位尖端/边缘位点的不同局域化学环境。

Result: 该混合DFTB-FF方法显著提高了几何优化的稳健性。计算表明：终止化学计量比可工程设计本征、p型或n型电子结构，进而调控ZrO2纳米颗粒的吸附活性。化学计量纳米颗粒不活化C-O键，而富Zr（n型）纳米颗粒中低配位位点发生化学吸附，伴随电荷向CO反键LUMO衍生轨道转移，导致C-O键活化。

Conclusion: 化学计量比控制的电子结构与纳米结构化引入的低配位表面位点共同决定了ZrO2纳米颗粒的吸附强度与反应活性，为通过终止工程调控氧化物纳米催化剂的催化性能提供了理论依据和计算方法学策略。

Abstract: We present a combined density functional theory (DFT) and density functional tight binding (DFTB) study of zirconia (ZrO2) nanoparticles of experimentally relevant sizes of several nanometers and their interactions with the CO molecule. A hybrid DFTB - Force Field (DFTB-FF) framework is developed, whereby band structure calculations rely on an existing Slater-Koster framework, while the accuracy of structural optimization and adsorption properties is controlled by the introduction of classical long-range interatomic potentials into DFTB instead of the traditional repulsive potentials. Additionally, coordination-dependent Zr-C potentials are introduced to account for the distinct local chemical environments of bulk-like facet sites and under-coordinated tip and edge sites, thereby improving the description of CO adsorption. This hybrid DFTB-FF approach substantially improves the robustness of geometry optimization and provides a practical strategy for extending the applicability of DFTB to complex oxide nanostructures. The calculations reveal that termination stoichiometry can be used to engineer intrinsic, p-type, or n-type electronic structures and thereby tune the adsorption activity of zirconia nanoparticles. While stoichiometric nanoparticles do not activate the C-O bond, low-coordinated sites in Zr-rich (n-type) nanoparticles exhibit chemisorption accompanied by charge donation into a CO antibonding LUMO-derived orbital, resulting in C-O bond activation. These results demonstrate that stoichiometry-controlled electronic structure and under-coordinated surface sites introduced by nanostructuring play a key role in governing the adsorption strength and reactivity of zirconia nanoparticles.

</details>


### [97] [Magneto-spin-orbit graphene: Chern topology and photoinduced Hall response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06858)
*Artem V. Tarasov,Alexander V. Eryzhenkov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算和有效四带模型，研究了四种面外磁化Gr/(Au,Pt)/(Co,Mn)异质结构在(2x2)公度超胞和(9x9)莫尔超胞中的磁-自旋轨道近邻效应。研究发现公度胞高估电荷转移和谷不对称性，莫尔平均显著减弱这些效应但保留可观的自旋劈裂；Pt间隔层比Au提供更强的近邻相互作用，Co替换为Mn会以间隔层依赖方式改变有效交换相互作用平衡。Gr/Pt/Co在所有超胞中均表现出C=2的有效铁磁Chern相，而其余三种组成的莫尔模型为亚铁磁且拓扑平庸(C=0)，但仍表现出螺旋度依赖的光致霍尔响应，Gr/Au/Co中的错配位错重构可显著增强该响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁-自旋轨道石墨烯由于近邻相互作用可以承载Chern相，但在真实异质结构中这些相依赖于衬底化学性质、局部堆叠配准，并可能被埋藏界面重构所改变。研究这些效应需要特别处理异质结构中常见的长程莫尔图案，而此前缺乏系统考虑莫尔尺度堆叠和界面缺陷影响的全面研究。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算和有效四带模型，在公度(2x2)超胞和包含Co基界面错配位错的莫尔(9x9)超胞中，对四种面外磁化Gr/(Au,Pt)/(Co,Mn)异质结构进行系统研究。

Result: 公度胞高估电荷转移并夸大谷不对称性，莫尔平均显著降低两者而保留较大的自旋劈裂。Pt间隔层比Au导致更强的近邻相互作用，Mn替代Co以间隔层依赖方式改变有效交换平衡。Co基结构中的错配位错具有选择性，可调控Gr/Au/Co的Dirac能隙或Gr/Pt/Co的自旋劈裂。Gr/Pt/Co在所有超胞中均呈现C=2的有效铁磁Chern相；其余三种组成的莫尔模型为亚铁磁且拓扑平庸(C=0)，但表现出螺旋度依赖的光致霍尔响应，且在Gr/Au/Co中该响应被错配位错重构强烈增强。

Conclusion: 莫尔尺度堆叠控制有效Chern相和霍尔电导，而埋藏界面缺陷在不改变拓扑性的前提下调控光致霍尔响应。这揭示了在实际异质结构中，长程莫尔图案和界面重构对拓扑性质的协同调控作用，为理解和设计石墨烯基拓扑器件提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Magneto-spin-orbit graphene can host Chern phases due to proximity interactions, but in realistic heterostructures these depend on substrate chemistry, local stacking registry and may be altered by buried-interface reconstruction. Investigation of these effects requires special efforts to take into account the long-range moire patterns that often arise for these heterostructures. We address this question using first-principles calculations and effective four-band models for four out-of-plane magnetized Gr/(Au, Pt)/(Co, Mn) heterostructures in commensurate (2 x 2) and moire (9 x 9) supercells where the latter include misfit dislocations for the Co-based interfaces. The commensurate cells overestimate charge transfer and exaggerate valley asymmetry, whereas moire averaging strongly reduces both effects while preserving sizable spin splittings. The Pt spacer mediates stronger proximity interactions than Au, while replacing Co by Mn substantially changes the balance of the effective exchange interactions in a spacer-dependent manner. Introduction of misfit dislocations into the Co-based structures is selective and tunes the Dirac gap in Gr/Au/Co or spin splittings in Gr/Pt/Co. Graphene in Gr/Pt/Co exhibits effective ferromagnetic Chern phase with C = 2 in all studied supercells. By contrast, the moire models of the other three compositions are ferrimagnetic and topologically trivial, with C = 0. Nevertheless, they exhibit helicity-dependent photoinduced Hall responses, which are strongly enhanced by misfit-dislocation reconstruction in Gr/Au/Co. This work demonstrates that moire-scale stacking controls the effective Chern phase and Hall conductivity, whereas buried-interface defects tune the photoinduced Hall response without changing the topology.

</details>


### [98] [Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory with Coulomb Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08983)
*Asbjørn Bækgaard Lauritsen,Mathieu Lewin,Jakob Oldenburg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次在连续域中为含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）提供了完全严格的数学证明：给定含时密度，证明存在唯一（至多相差一个含时常数）的外部势能使相应的薛定谔方程复现该密度，且该势能可通过迭代方案获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）在物理和化学中被广泛使用，但其数学基础——即Runge-Gross定理的严格性——长期缺少完整的理论证明。本文旨在填补这一空白，为TDDFT提供严格的数学依据。

Method: 证明涵盖库仑相互作用，不依赖时间的泰勒展开，而是要求密度和势能在空间上具有解析性（与扩展原子核相容）。通过构建迭代方案来显式构造外部势能，并证明其唯一性（差一个含时常数）。

Result: 证明了在连续域中，任意给定含时密度均可由某个外部势能（在含时常数意义下唯一）通过薛定谔方程再现，且该势能可通过迭代法显式构造。证明适用于库仑相互作用体系，包括扩展原子核情形。

Conclusion: 该工作为TDDFT提供了第一个完全严谨的连续域数学证明，确立了一一对应关系（密度→势能），同时避免了时间泰勒展开的限制，为TDDFT的理论基础和应用合理性提供了坚实保障。

Abstract: We provide the first fully rigorous justification of time-dependent density functional theory in the continuum: Given a time-dependent density, we prove that there exists an external potential, unique up to a time-dependent constant, such that the corresponding Schrödinger equation reproduces the given density. This potential can be obtained with an iteration scheme. Our argument covers Coulomb interactions and does not need any Taylor expansion in time. It instead requires analyticity in space for the density and potential, which is compatible with extended nuclei.

</details>


### [99] [Human-agent discovery of reconfigurable in-plane ferroelectric superdomain control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06887)
*Yu Liu,Boris Slautin,Ching-Che Lin,Jaegyu Kim,Lane W. Martin,Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARC框架，让编码代理与人类操作员共享一台显微镜、一个笔记本和两个持久化记忆文件（FINDINGS.md存储分级结论，PITFALLS.md记录失败模式），用于扫描探针显微镜的自主实验研究。该框架成功应用于重构(111)取向PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3薄膜的面内超畴方向，实验中发现了空间极性交替而非晶格与层状周期精确匹配决定方向选择，并能通过栅格扫描结合掩蔽脉冲晶格将"UTK"打印进超畴取向。文章还总结了智能体实验的实践要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化等自动化实验要求实验前定义好可观测变量、可用操作和目标，但在许多探索性实验中，描述样本的变量需从数据中提取、操作在实验过程中不断涌现、仪器预算有限难以通过试错学习，因此需要一种新的框架使代理能够在有限的实验中自适应地提取变量、涌现操作并进行探索式实验。

Method: SPARC框架包含三大核心组件：1) 编码代理与人类操作员共享显微镜和笔记本；2) FINDINGS.md文件存储有关实验的分级结论；3) PITFALLS.md记录已学到的分析和仪器失败模式。框架通过代理重分析早期人工测量数据，开发出具有交替极性的固定偏压脉冲定向晶格以重构超畴方向；在后续代理控制的实验中，PITFALLS.md条目被编译为在写入前验证设计有效性的检查。

Result: 实验表明空间极性交替（而非晶格与层状周期精确匹配）决定了方向选择。将栅格扫描与掩蔽脉冲晶格结合可成功将"UTK"字样打印进超畴取向。该活动还识别出智能体实验的实践要求，包括对仪器执行的物理验证、存储发现有效性的条件、在仪器数据上验证新观测量的必要性以及鲁棒的控制协议。

Conclusion: SPARC框架通过学习人类先验知识并转化为可验证的失败模式检查，实现了在极低预算下有效的探索性扫描探针实验。研究揭示了铁电薄膜超畴重构的新物理机制（极性交替而非周期匹配），并就智能体自主实验的鲁棒性、可验证性和安全性提出了关键实践要求，为未来将代理发现嵌入仪器控制回路提供指导。

Abstract: Automated experimentation is most effective when the observables, available actions, and objective are defined before the experiment starts, as is the case for Bayesian optimization. However, in many exploratory experiments, the variables that describe the sample must be extracted from the data, new operations emerge during the experiments, and the instrument budget is too small to learn the problem by trials. Here we introduce the Scanning Probe Agentic Research Cycle (SPARC) framework, in which a coding agent and a human operator share one microscope, one notebook, and two persistent memory files. FINDINGS.md stores graded conclusions about the experiment, whereas PITFALLS.md records learned failure modes of analysis and instrument. We apply SPARC to reconfigure the in-plane superdomain direction of a (111)-oriented PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3 film. In an operator-supervised campaign, the agent reanalyzed earlier manual measurements and developed an oriented lattice of stationary bias pulses with alternating polarity to reconfigure the superdomain direction. In a subsequent agent-controlled campaign, PITFALLS.md entries were compiled into checks that validate a design before any write. The experiments showed that spatial polarity alternation, instead of the exact matching between the lattice and lamellar periods, determines directional selection. Combining a raster scan with a masked pulse lattice printed the letters UTK into the superdomain orientation. The campaign also identified practical requirements for agentic experimentation where physical verification of instrument execution, the conditions under which stored findings remain valid, validation of new observables on instrument data, and robust control protocols.

</details>


### [100] [Scalable machine learning framework for multiphase identification from powder X-ray diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06908)
*Xinyang Tong,Ethan Jin,Jiahan Xu,Aditya Rao,Pengcen Jiang,Nathan J. Szymanski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GALAXI是一种基于独立一对一全部（one-versus-all）二元分类器的X射线衍射（XRD）自动相识别方法，通过预训练分类器缩小搜索空间，再结合Rietveld精修确定最优相组合，在实验数据上取得0.935的micro-F1分数，优于经典搜索匹配和先前深度学习方法，并支持扩展到大型参考库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XRD是合成后晶体相鉴定的主要工具，但自动相识别仍具挑战性，尤其对具有重叠峰和实验伪影的多相样品。现有深度学习方法大多将相识别视为单一封闭集分类问题，要求单个共享模型区分所有候选相，这限制了方法的扩展性和稳健性。

Method: GALAXI将相识别解耦为独立的one-versus-all二元分类任务，每个分类器专门识别单一相。首先利用预训练分类器将搜索空间缩小为少量合理候选相，随后通过Rietveld精修评估相组合方案，找到最能解释完整衍射图谱的相组合。这种模块化架构允许在不重训练已有模型的情况下扩展到大型参考库。

Result: 在一组精选实验图谱上，GALAXI的micro-F1得分为0.935，优于经典搜索匹配和先前深度学习方法。该方法对常见实验伪影（低杂质相分数、小晶粒尺寸、峰位偏移、样品位移和织构）保持稳健，并能在固态反应的时间分辨原位XRD数据上表现良好。此外，已为Crystallography Open Database中的64,594种结构训练了分类器并通过公共网络界面部署。

Conclusion: GALAXI通过将XRD相识别从封闭集分类问题重构为模块化的一对一分类加Rietveld精修的两阶段框架，显著提升了多相样品识别的准确性和稳健性，同时凭借其模块化设计实现了向大规模参考库的灵活扩展，为XRD自动相识别提供了一个可部署的系统性解决方案。

Abstract: X-ray diffraction (XRD) is the primary tool for identifying crystalline phases following synthesis, but automated phase identification remains challenging, particularly for multiphase samples with overlapping peaks and experimental artifacts. While deep-learning methods have been proposed to improve upon classical search-match algorithms, most formulate phase identification as a single closed-set classification problem, requiring one shared model to discriminate among all candidate phases. Here we introduce GALAXI, which instead decouples the identification task into independent one-versus-all binary classifiers that each specialize in recognizing a single phase. These pre-trained classifiers first narrow the search space to a small set of plausible phases, which are then evaluated through Rietveld refinement to identify the combination of phases that best explains the full diffraction pattern. On a curated set of experimental patterns, GALAXI identifies the correct phases with a micro-F1 score of 0.935, outperforming classical search-match and prior deep-learning models. The method remains robust to common experimental artifacts, including low impurity phase fractions, small crystallite size, peak shifts, sample displacement, and texture, and performs well when applied to time-resolved in-situ XRD data from solid-state reactions. Moreover, because the phase-specific models are independent, GALAXI can expand to large reference libraries without retraining existing models. This modular architecture enables us to train classifiers for 64,594 structures from the Crystallography Open Database and deploy them through a public web interface at https://galaxi-xrd.com.

</details>


### [101] [Metastable polar order and phase competition in Carpy-Galy LaTaO4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06943)
*Yongjin Shin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过第一性原理计算，揭示了Ca-doped LaTaO4中从反极性单斜P21/c相到极性正交Cmc21相的相变路径中，亚稳极性单斜P21相作为公度中间体的作用，并展示了其通过对称性联系及Gamma_1+弛豫耦合稳定化机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LaTaO4中反极性-极性竞争背后的相变机制，特别是非公度调制相与已知公度多晶型之间的结构关系，以及寻找连接极性与反极性态的对称性途径。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（密度泛函理论），进行全谱系结构对称性分析，计算模式分辨能量曲面，分析有序参量耦合，并通过晶格度量与中子衍射数据对比验证公度态与非公度调制的对应关系，还进行了组分依赖的能量学分析。

Result: 发现P21相是P21/c与Cmc21的共同子群，提供对称性连接的相变路径；Gamma_1+等对称弛豫与主有序参量的耦合稳定了P21极小值；P21/c→P21转变存在有限能垒；计算的公度态与实验IC-o调制的S2+主导位移特征一致；组分调控可改变极-反极性竞争平衡。

Conclusion: P21可作为IC-o非公度调制相的有效公度结构参考，为理解LaTaO4中极性-反极性竞争及通过化学掺杂调控该竞争提供了理论基础和可行路径。

Abstract: Carpy-Galy LaTaO4 undergoes a structural sequence linking the antipolar monoclinic P21/c ground state and the polar orthorhombic Cmc21 phase through an incommensurately modulated regime. First-principles calculations identify a metastable polar monoclinic P21 phase as a commensurate intermediate in the phase-transition landscape. As a common subgroup of P21/c and Cmc21, P21 provides a symmetry-connected route between the established polymorphs. Mode-resolved energy surfaces for these symmetry-breaking pathways show that coupling between the respective primary order parameters and an isosymmetric Gamma_1+ relaxation stabilizes the P21 minimum along both pathways. Consequently, the P21/c -> P21 transformation has a finite energy barrier, while a restricted soft-mode model retains competing P21 and Cmc21 basins over an illustrative range of harmonic stiffness. Lattice-metric and neutron-diffraction comparisons indicate a shared S_2+-dominated displacement character between the calculated commensurate state and the experimental IC-o modulation, supporting the use of P21 as a commensurate structural reference for IC-o. Finally, composition-dependent energetics suggest chemical routes for tuning polar-antipolar competition.

</details>


### [102] [Excitons probe intrinsic flat band Mottness in a van der Waals heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07101)
*Xinyue Huang,Xintong Tan,Haowei Chen,Yingzhou Huang,Yushen Zhou,Yuchen Gao,Zhijie Ma,Chengxin Xiao,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jianpeng Liu,Zuxin Chen,Youguo Shi,Wang Yao,Yu Ye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过将单层WSe₂与范德华Mott绝缘体Nb₃Cl₈直接接触，结合栅极可调光谱与第一性原理计算，研究了激子与内在平带Mott系统的耦合，揭示了关联重构的Mott能隙、Rydberg激子向层间极化子分支的多阶段演化，以及磁场下场致谷选择性激子耦合，将激子传感与激子-极化子物理拓展至内在平带Mott材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子能够灵敏探测邻近二维材料中的电子关联，但其与内在平带Mott系统的耦合此前尚未被充分探索，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 将栅极可调光学光谱与第一性原理计算相结合，研究与范德华Mott绝缘体Nb₃Cl₈直接接触的单层WSe₂体系，并通过外加垂直磁场引入自旋极化态以探测谷选择性耦合。

Result: 1) WSe₂激子共振的栅极演化揭示了Nb₃Cl₈中关联重构的Mott能隙，该能隙在单粒子能带图像中不存在；2) 电子掺杂区中WSe₂ 2s Rydberg激子经历多阶段演化，形成层间吸引与排斥极化子分支；3) 外磁场下Nb₃Cl₈的自旋极化态诱导谷选择性激子耦合，显著增强WSe₂激子发射的圆偏振度。

Conclusion: 这些结果将激子传感与激子-极化子物理从传统体系拓展至内在平带Mott材料，为探测和工程化关联驱动的界面准粒子提供了新的光学途径。

Abstract: Excitons provide a sensitive optical probe of electronic correlations in nearby two-dimensional materials, yet their coupling to intrinsic flat-band Mott systems remains largely unexplored. Here we combine gate-tunable optical spectroscopy with first-principles calculations to study monolayer WSe$_2$ in direct contact with the van der Waals Mott insulator Nb$_3$Cl$_8$. The gate evolution of WSe$_2$ excitonic resonances reveals signatures of a correlation-reconstructed Mott gap in Nb$_3$Cl$_8$ that is absent from the single-particle band picture. In the electron-doped regime, the WSe$_2$ 2s Rydberg exciton undergoes a multistage evolution and develops into interlayer attractive and repulsive polaron branches, showing that a Rydberg exciton can be dressed by strongly correlated flat-band electrons in an adjacent Mott layer. Under an out-of-plane magnetic field, spin-polarized Nb$_3$Cl$_8$ states further induce valley-selective exciton coupling, producing a strongly enhanced circular polarization of the WSe$_2$ exciton emission. These results extend exciton-based sensing and exciton-polaron physics to intrinsic flat-band Mott materials, providing an optical route to probe and engineer correlation-driven interfacial quasiparticles.

</details>


### [103] [Atomistic origin and strain control of the finite-temperature dielectric response in BaTiO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07219)
*Ryotaro Sahashi,Po-Yen Chen,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过电场耦合分子动力学和机器学习力场，揭示BaTiO3介电响应的局域原子起源：介电常数与Ti-O偏心位移的角度重分布相关，而非其大小或平均极性角，且温度和双轴应变遵循统一的取向描述符关系，提供了软模行为的实空间对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BaTiO3（BTO）的介电响应随温度剧烈变化，但其局域原子层面的起源仍不清楚。

Method: 采用电场耦合的机器学习力场分子动力学模拟。

Result: 介电常数追踪的是电场诱导下局域Ti-O偏心位移的角度重分布，而非其幅度或平均极角；温度和双轴应变虽以不同方式改变局域结构，但二者的介电响应均遵循同一取向描述符的共同关系。

Conclusion: 该发现为软模行为提供了实空间对应，即介电响应的局域原子机制可通过取向序参数统一描述。

Abstract: The dielectric response of BaTiO3 (BTO) varies strongly with temperature, yet its local atomic origin remains unclear. Using electric-field-coupled molecular dynamics with a machine-learning force field, we show that the permittivity tracks the field-induced angular redistribution of local Ti-O off-centering rather than its magnitude or mean polar angle. Temperature and biaxial strain modify the local structure differently, but both responses follow a common relation with the same orientational descriptor, providing a real-space counterpart to soft-mode behavior.

</details>


### [104] [Benchmarking Universal Machine Learning Force Fields for Crystal Structure Prediction of High-Energy Molecular Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07477)
*Musiha Mahfuza Mukta,Osman Goni Ridwan,Romain Perriot,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了通用机器学习原子间势（UMLIPs）在含能分子晶体结构弛豫中的可靠性问题，提出并用GAFF经典力场预弛豫作为UMLIP弛豫的起始几何假设，对比了MACE、MACE-OFF和UMA三模型，发现GAFF预弛豫可系统减少失败弛豫并降低计算成本，其中MACE-OFF在势能面平滑度、结构保真度和应力收敛间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（UMLIPs）为基于几何弛豫和能量排序筛选分子晶体提供了快速途径，但其在化学多样化的含能材料上的可靠性尚不明确，尤其是UMLIPs是否过度敏感而破坏分子连接性（即在弛豫周期性晶体时断裂化学键）的问题尚未澄清。

Method: 在大型高能分子晶体数据库上检验假设：经典力场（GAFF）预弛豫可为后续UMLIP弛豫提供更合适的起始几何。采用三个模型（MACE、MACE-OFF、UMA）结合GAFF进行对比测试，并进行失效分析和鲁棒性分析。

Result: 直接使用MACE-OFF和UMA显示出极高的弛豫成功率并最接近参考几何，但少数罕见情况仍存在失效。GAFF预弛豫可系统减少失效弛豫数量且计算成本更低。MACE-OFF在势能面平滑度、结构保真度和应力收敛之间展现出更优的权衡，是自动化结构优化的可靠选择。

Conclusion: GAFF预弛豫是一种有效的策略来提升UMLIPs在含能分子晶体弛豫中的鲁棒性；MACE-OFF模型在该应用场景中综合表现最佳，可作为自动化结构优化的可靠基础。

Abstract: Recent developments of universal machine learning interatomic potentials (UMLIPs) offer a fast route for screening molecular crystals based on geometry relaxation and energy ranking, but their reliability across chemically diverse energetic materials remains elusive. In particular, it is unclear whether or not these UMLIPs are over-sensitive to break the desired molecular connectivity for relaxing the periodic crystals. Herein we tested the hypothesis that classical force-field pre-relaxation can provide a more suitable starting geometry for subsequent UMLIP relaxation on a large database of high energy molecular crystals. Three models (MACE, MACE-OFF and UMA) in conjunction with the General Amber Force Field (GAFF) were applied to test this hypothesis. Among them, direct MACE-OFF and UMA showed very high relaxation success and preserved the reference geometries most closely, but they still exhibit failures for some rare cases. Using GAFF pre-relaxation can systematically reduce the number of failed relaxations with lower computational costs. Our comparative failure and robustness analyses revealed distinct trade-offs among the evaluated models. Among them, MACE-OFF achieves a better compromise between potential energy surface smoothness, structural fidelity, and stress convergence, serving as a good choice to provide a reliable foundation for automated structural optimization.

</details>


### [105] [Switchable Altermagnetism in a Layered van der Waals Metal-Organic Framework Driven by Spin-Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07548)
*Diego López-Alcalá,Alberto M. Ruiz,Andrei Shumilin,José J. Baldoví*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过自旋交叉（spin-crossover）机制实现了对交变磁性（altermagnetism）的动态可编程开关控制。基于密度泛函计算，作者在层状范德华MOF材料MnX₂(tdz)₂（X=Cl, Br）中发现了交变磁性基态，并证明静水压力可诱导高自旋到低自旋的转变，通过改变磁性基态的对称性来抑制/开启非相对论自旋劈裂，为可重构自旋电子器件提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性的动力学控制是将其自旋依赖功能转化为实际自旋电子器件的关键需求，但目前有效的开关机制仍未被充分探索，亟需一种通用、可编程的交变磁性切换方案。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算，研究了层状范德华MOF材料MnX₂(tdz)₂（X=Cl, Br）的交变磁性性质；通过静水压力模拟高自旋到低自旋转变，分析自旋态变化对Mn d轨道占据、磁交换网络及磁性基态对称性的影响。

Result: 发现MnX₂(tdz)₂具有交变磁性基态，由各向异性层间交换相互作用稳定，展现出特征性的d波动量空间自旋劈裂及自旋劈流传输响应。静水压力下高自旋到低自旋转变可重新配置Mn d轨道占据，改变磁交换网络，稳定一个对称性不同的反铁磁基态，从而抑制非相对论自旋劈裂。

Conclusion: 自旋交叉开关交变磁性并非通过直接改变电子结构，而是通过改变磁性基态的对称性来实现。分子自旋交叉交变磁体为外部可重构的自旋电子器件提供了新平台。

Abstract: Dynamical control of altermagnetism is a key requirement for translating its unique spin-dependent functionalities into practical spintronic devices, yet effective switching mechanisms remain largely unexplored. Here, we open an unprecedented, versatile and programmable route based on spin-crossover to switch altermagnetism on demand in molecular materials. Using density functional calculations, we demonstrate an altermagnetic ground state in the layered van der Waals MOFs MnX$_2$(tdz)$_2$ (X = Cl, Br; tdz = thiadiazole), stabilized by anisotropic interlayer exchange interactions, which gives rise to a characteristic d-wave momentum-space spin splitting and an associated spin-splitter transport response. Our findings reveal that under hydrostatic pressure, a high-spin to low-spin transition reconfigures the Mn d-orbital occupation, thus modifying the magnetic exchange network, and stabilizing a different antiferromagnetic ground state whose symmetry suppresses the nonrelativistic spin splitting. Crucially, spin-crossover switches altermagnetism not by directly altering the electronic structure, but by changing the symmetry of the magnetic ground state. These results establish molecular spin-crossover altermagnets as a platform for externally reconfigurable spintronic devices.

</details>


### [106] [Temperature-Dependent nonlinear optics from first-principles: Second-Harmonic Generation in few-layers MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07796)
*Anna Romani,Claudio Attaccalite,Myrta Grüning*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种第一性原理实时方法，用于研究固体在有限温度下的非线性响应，并将该方法应用于单层和三层MoS₂的二次谐波产生（SHG），揭示了SHG的非平凡温度依赖性及其微观物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体的非线性光学响应在有限温度下如何演化是一个重要的基础物理问题，但现有的第一性原理方法大多局限于零温或静态近似。实验上观察到单层MoS₂的SHG强度随温度升高而增加，这与直觉相悖，缺乏理论解释，需要一种能够自洽处理温度效应（包括准粒子重整化和退相）的实时计算方法。

Method: 该方法在第一性原理实时框架下求解非线性响应：通过DFPT计算电子-声子矩阵元，进而从电子自能的Fan和Debye-Waller项获得准粒子能量的温度重整化及其寿命（用于引入退相项）；电子激发在独立粒子近似水平处理。将温度效应以准粒子能量修正和寿命相关的退相项纳入实时演化方程，然后应用于单层和三层MoS₂的SHG计算。

Result: 计算发现MoS₂的SHG具有非平凡的温度依赖性，源于强晶体动量依赖的准粒子重整化。声子模式分析表明：单层MoS₂中声学模式（面内）的耦合主导整体晶体动量依赖性，而在三层MoS₂中剪切模式起主导作用。该结果能够合理解释实验中观察到的单层MoS₂在特定激光能量下SHG强度随温度升高而增强的现象。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种可在有限温度下研究固体非线性光学响应的第一性原理实时方法，揭示了MoS₂中SHG温度依赖性背后的微观机制——准粒子能量重整化的晶体动量异质性与特定声子模式的耦合。该工作为理解温度效应对非线性光学过程的影响提供了新的理论工具，并为实验观察提供了物理解释。

Abstract: We present a first-principles real-time approach to study non-linear response of solids at finite temperature. Finite temperature effects are included as renormalization of the quasiparticle energies and a dephasing term proportional to the quasiparticle lifetimes. We evaluate electron-phonon matrix elements from Density-Functional Perturbation Theory for lattice dynamics and then calculate quasiparticles renormalization and lifetime from the Fan and Debye-Waller terms of the electron self-energy. Electron excitations are treated at the independent particle level of approximation. We apply the approach to the second-harmonic generation (SHG) in monolayer and trilayer MoS$_2$. We observe a nontrivial temperature-dependence of the SHG due to a strong crystal-momentum dependent quasiparticle renormalization. From the phonon-mode analysis we find that the coupling with acoustic and shear modes determines the overall crystal-momentum dependence respectively in monolayer and trilayer MoS$_2$. The nontrivial temperature-dependence of the SHG can help rationalize the increase of SHG intensity with increasing temperature observed in monolayer MoS$_2$ at a given laser energy [Adv. Optical Mater. 8, 2000441 (2020)].

</details>


### [107] [Atomistic modeling of molecular beam epitaxy growth of SrTiO3 and Sr2TiO4 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08114)
*Guangfu Luo,Dane Morgan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本论文通过第一性原理计算系统研究了SrTiO3和Sr2TiO4薄膜在SrTiO3衬底上的分子束外延（MBE）生长过程，揭示了三种影响生长机制的重要现象：氧空位在表面扩散过程中的动态诱导与加速作用、TiSr缺陷促进SrO岛形成的机制，以及吸附Ti原子/TiO2分子插入SrO双层导致意外生长序列的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBE技术以其原子层级的生长控制能力而著称，但意外的生长机制可能危及这一精确性。理解钙钛矿SrTiO3及Ruddlesden-Popper Sr2TiO4薄膜MBE生长过程中的原子级机理，对于实现更精确的生长控制至关重要。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，系统考察了气相中潜在分子物种及其在薄膜表面和层边缘上的反应与扩散动力学，全面研究了MBE生长SrTiO3和Sr2TiO4薄膜的原子尺度过程。

Result: 发现了三种关键生长机制：（1）氧空位可在无缺陷衬底的表面扩散过程中被动态诱导并显著加速扩散过程；（2）尽管SrO层预期以单层生长模式生长，但TiSr缺陷的存在可能促进SrO岛的形成；（3）吸附在SrO双层上的Ti原子和TiO2分子可插入SrO双层内部，导致意外的生长序列。

Conclusion: 这些发现对金属氧化物薄膜的MBE生长具有更广泛的意义，为改善其生长过程的精确控制提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Molecular beam epitaxy (MBE) is renowned for its potential for atomic layer control, but unexpected growth mechanisms can potentially compromise this level of precision. In this study, we employ first-principles calculations to investigate the atomistic processes governing the MBE growth of perovskite SrTiO3 and Ruddlesden-Popper Sr2TiO4 films on a SrTiO3 substrate. We systematically explore the potential molecular species in the gas phase and their reactions and diffusion dynamics on the film surfaces and layer edges. Our analyses uncover three mechanisms of importance for understanding this type of growth. First, oxygen vacancies can be dynamically induced during surface diffusion on defect-free substrate and noticeably accelerate the diffusion processes. Second, while the SrO layer is expected to grow in a single-layer growth mode, the presence of potential TiSr defects may promote the formation of SrO islands. Lastly, adsorbed Ti atoms and TiO2 molecules on the SrO bilayer can insert into SrO bilayers, resulting in an unexpected growth sequence. These findings may have broader implications for the MBE growth of metal oxide films and provide guidance for achieving improved control over their growth processes.

</details>


### [108] [Pressure Evolution of Atomic Volume Systematics in Transition Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08197)
*Masaaki Geshi,Yuichi Akahama*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论计算，研究了过渡金属在高达400 GPa极端压力下原子体积对原子序数著名的抛物线依赖关系的演变。研究发现，高压下环境压力的抛物线趋势转变为特征性的立方样行为，这归因于bcc过渡金属更高的可压缩性及其伴随的总能量较大增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解过渡金属在极端压缩条件下的体积-原子序数关系如何偏离已知的环境压力抛物线规律，从而揭示高压下的新物理行为。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，在最高400 GPa的压力范围内系统研究过渡金属的原子体积随原子序数的变化。

Result: 发现环境压力下过渡金属原子体积对原子序数的抛物线趋势在高压下转变为特征性的立方样行为；该演变与bcc结构过渡金属具有更高的可压缩性以及相应总能量的较大增幅有关。

Conclusion: 过渡金属在极端压缩下的体积-原子序数关系从抛物线演变为立方样行为，相关发现与已有的实验观测和第一性原理计算结果进行了讨论和对照。

Abstract: We investigated the evolution of the well-known parabolic dependence of atomic volume on atomic number in transition metals under extreme compression at pressures up to 400 GPa using density functional theory calculations. Our results reveal that the ambient-pressure parabolic trend transforms into a characteristic cubic-like behavior at high pressures. This evolution is attributed to the higher compressibility of bcc transition metals associated with comparatively large increases in the total energy. The present findings are discussed in relation to previous experimental observations and first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [109] [Light-facilitated ferroelectric switching in wurtzite crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08270)
*Ricardo Jiménez-Sánchez,Fernando Salazar,Miguel Cruz-Irisson,Riccardo Rurali,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算提出一种无外场的策略来降低纤锌矿铁电体的矫顽场：利用高于带隙的光激发产生载流子，大幅降低极性纤锌矿相与非极性六方中间相之间的能量差，从而显著降低铁电翻转的能量壁垒。以LaN为代表材料，光致载流子通过部分金属化屏蔽偶极-偶极相互作用，使非极性结构更受青睐，该机制在岩盐多晶型中也得到验证，为更快、低电压、高能效的非易失性存储器开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤锌矿铁电体兼具大剩余极化和CMOS兼容性，是下一代非易失性存储器的领先平台，但其内在的大矫顽场源于极性相与介导翻转的非极性六方相之间的高能垒，严重阻碍了实际部署。现有策略多依赖外加电场，作者希望探索一种无外场的、非侵入性的新途径来降低这一能垒。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，以LaN为代表性纤锌矿铁电体进行研究。具体方案是施加高于带隙的电子光激发，通过光诱导载流子来改变六方中间相与纤锌矿基态之间的能量差，从而影响铁电翻转能垒。作者还分析了该机制背后的物理起源——光致部分金属化对偶极-偶极相互作用的屏蔽效应，并在岩盐多晶型中验证了机制的普适性。

Result: 研究发现，光激发产生的载流子能显著降低六方中间相与纤锌矿基态之间的能量差异，从而急剧减小铁电翻转的能垒。该效应的物理机制在于光致部分金属化屏蔽了稳定铁电序的偶极-偶极相互作用，从而有利于竞争性的非极性结构。机制在岩盐多晶型中同样成立，证明了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了光作为一种强大且非侵入性的手段来控制纤锌矿铁电体的铁电切换，为开发更快、更低电压、更节能的非易失性存储技术提供了新的可行性路径。

Abstract: Wurtzite ferroelectrics combine large remanent polarization with full CMOS compatibility, positioning them as a leading platform for next-generation non-volatile memory. Their practical deployment, however, is hindered by an intrinsically large coercive field, rooted in the high energy barrier separating the polar wurtzite phase from the nonpolar hexagonal phase that mediates polarization switching. Here, using first-principles calculations, we propose an alternative, field-free strategy for lowering this barrier: above-bandgap electronic photoexcitation. Taking LaN as a representative wurtzite ferroelectric, we show that light induced carriers dramatically reduce the energy difference between the hexagonal intermediate phase and the wurtzite ground state, sharply reducing the energy barrier to ferroelectric switching. This effect originates from a photoinduced partial metallization of the polar phase, which screens the dipole-dipole interactions that stabilize ferroelectric order and thereby favors the competing nonpolar structure. The robustness of this mechanism is further confirmed for the rocksalt polymorph. Our results establish light as a powerful, non-invasive route to controlling ferroelectric switching in wurtzites, opening a path toward faster, lower-voltage, and more energy-efficient non-volatile memory technologies.

</details>


### [110] [Flexoelectric Polarization in Wrinkled Janus Transition-Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08295)
*Stefan Velja,Surender Kumar,Domenico Corona,Caterina Cocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过密度泛函理论（DFT）与连续弹性模型相结合，系统研究了褶皱Janus MoSSe/MoSeS单层的电子和挠曲电响应。研究发现全局面外极化随褶皱投影纵横比呈线性变化，并据此建立了一个包含有效二维挠曲电系数的物理几何模型，可精确预测DFT极化结果而无需高阶非线性参数。原子和轨道分辨电荷密度分析揭示了微观机制：中央Mo 4d轨道作为线性挠曲电核心，而局部曲率驱动S 3p与Se 4p亚层之间的化学不对称电荷转移。研究结果为利用Janus材料挠曲电性设计柔性纳米电子学和光电器件提供了几何设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面外褶皱引起的应变梯度工程是调控二维材料电子和机电性能的有效途径，但对Janus MoSSe/MoSeS这类化学不对称单层在褶皱条件下的挠曲电响应及其微观物理机制尚缺乏系统理解，需要建立能够定量预测极化行为且无需复杂非线性参数的物理模型。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算耦合连续弹性线模型（elastica modeling），系统扫描不同褶皱尺寸和5%-20%的压缩应变范围。通过极化-纵横比线性关系的发现构建嵌入有效二维挠曲电系数的几何模型，并结合原子与轨道分辨的电荷密度分析揭示微观电荷转移机制。

Result: 全局面外极化在投影纵横比参数化下呈线性关系；所建立的含有效二维挠曲电系数的几何模型无需高阶非线性参数即可准确预测DFT极化结果。微观分析表明中央Mo 4d轨道为稳健的线性挠曲电核心，局部曲率则驱动S 3p到Se 4p亚层间的化学不对称连续电荷转移。

Conclusion: 揭示了Janus褶皱单层的挠曲电线性响应规律及其电子结构起源，确立了清晰的几何设计规则，为基于Janus材料的柔性纳米电子学和光电器件中挠曲电性的工程应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Strain-gradient engineering via out-of-plane wrinkling offers a powerful route to tune electronic and electromechanical properties in two-dimensional (2D) materials. Here, we systematically investigate the electronic and flexoelectric response of wrinkled Janus MoSSe/MoSeS monolayers using density functional theory (DFT) calculations coupled with continuum elastica modeling. Exploring varying wrinkle sizes and compressive strain levels ($5\%-20\%$), we show that the global out-of-plane polarization follows a linear behavior when parameterized by the projected aspect ratio of the nanowrinkles. On this basis, we develop a physically grounded geometric model incorporating an effective 2D flexoelectric coefficient, which accurately predicts DFT polarizations without requiring higher-order nonlinear parameters. Atom- and orbital-resolved charge density analyses reveal the microscopic origin of this behavior: while the central Mo $4d$-orbital manifold acts as a robust, linear flexoelectric core, local curvature drives continuous, chemically asymmetric charge transfer between the S $3p$ and Se $4p$ sublayer manifolds. Our findings establish clear geometric design rules for harnessing Janus-based flexoelectricity for flexible nanoelectronics and optoelectronics.

</details>


### [111] [Davydov Splitting Without a Davydov Pair and Highly Mobile Singlet Excitons in Perylene Red Microcrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08352)
*Chris Rehhagen,Tolibjon Abdurakhmonov,Magnus Frank,Oliver Kühn,Stefan Lochbrunner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文综合实验与理论方法研究了苝红微晶（单胞含八个分子）的激子性质。实验仅观察到名义上八个Davydov跃迁中的两个，表观分裂为610 cm⁻¹，与基于DFT参数化的Frenkel-Holstein激子模型吻合。对称性分析表明这两个跃迁并不属于同一Davydov对，因此该分裂不能直接反映单胞内库仑耦合。复杂的局域激发混合产生J型主导跃迁带，有利于激子转移。时间相关单光子计数和超快瞬态吸收光谱表明动力学由高迁移率单重Frenkel激子主导，实验提取的激子迁移率与Kinetic Monte Carlo模拟结果高度一致，支持非相干跳跃输运的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有机分子晶体中激子的性质（包括能带结构、Davydov分裂与激子迁移）对揭示其光物理过程至关重要。苝红微晶作为具有八个分子单胞的体系，其复杂的激子结构尚不清晰，现有对Davydov分裂的传统解释可能不适用于此类高复杂度单胞。

Method: 采用实验和理论相结合的方法：实验方面使用稳态光谱、时间相关单光子计数（TCSPC）和超快瞬态吸收光谱表征激子动态；理论方面基于密度泛函理论（DFT）参数化Frenkel-Holstein激子模型，并结合对称性分析对特征态进行归类，辅以Kinetic Monte Carlo模拟对比激子迁移率。

Result: 实验仅观测到八个名义Davydov跃迁中的两个，表观分裂610 cm⁻¹与理论模型合理吻合；对称性分析表明两观测跃迁并非同一Davydov对，分裂不代表单胞内库仑耦合。体系呈现J型主导能带，激子动力学由高迁移率单重Frenkel激子主导；实验提取的激子迁移率与Kinetic Monte Carlo模拟结果高度一致，支持非相干跳跃输运机制。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了大单胞有机分子晶体中激子光谱分裂的复杂性，指出传统Davydov分裂解释在多元胞体系中的局限性，并通过实验与模拟的相互印证确立了非相干跳跃输运为主导的激子迁移机制。

Abstract: We investigate the excitonic properties of perylene red microcrystals, whose unit cell contain eight molecules, using both experimental and theoretical methods. Only two of the nominal eight Davydov transitions are experimentally observed, with an apparent splitting of 610 cm$^{-1}$ that is reasonably reproduced by a Frenkel-Holstein exciton model parametrized from density functional theory. A symmetry-based analysis of the eigenstates reveals that the two observed transitions do not belong to the same Davydov pair, so the splitting does not directly report on the Coulomb coupling within the unit cell. The complex mixture of local excitations produces a J-like band for the dominant transitions, favouring exciton transfer through increased spectral overlap. Consistent with this picture, time-correlated single-photon counting of the emission and ultrafast transient absorption spectroscopy show that the dynamics is dominated by highly mobile singlet Frenkel excitons. The exciton mobility extracted from experiment agrees very well with that obtained from Kinetic Monte Carlo simulations, supporting a picture of incoherent hopping transport.

</details>


### [112] [The absence of a central metal ion destabilizes phthalocyanine on In$_2$O$_3$(111)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08742)
*Viktoria Waidbacher,Sarah Tobisch,Faith J. Lewis,Moritz Eder,Michael Schmid,Gareth Parkinson,Ulrike Diebold,Margareta Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用低温STM、nc-AFM和STS系统研究了金属无酞菁（H₂Pc）在In₂O₃(111)表面的吸附行为，发现其吸附位点与CuPc和CoPc一致，但热稳定性显著更差（~50°C即开始分解），无法形成有序单层膜，揭示了中心金属离子在稳定大环结构中的关键锚定作用及对前沿轨道特性的调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属酞菁（MPc）在有机光电器件界面和单原子催化（SAC）中具有重要应用。理解MPc在透明电极氧化物基底（如In₂O₃/ITO）上的吸附和成膜行为，对于界面电荷注入/传输调控和催化活性的理解至关重要。特别是需要厘清中心金属离子在吸附构型、热稳定性和单层组织中的具体作用。

Method: 采用低温扫描隧道显微镜（LT-STM）、非接触原子力显微镜（nc-AFM）和扫描隧道谱（STS）对H₂Pc在In₂O₃(111)上的吸附进行了原子级表征，并通过温和退火测试热稳定性；将结果与已报道的CuPc和CoPc在相同基底上的行为进行系统对比。

Result: (1) H₂Pc与CuPc、CoPc在In₂O₃(111)上吸附于相同的位点和构型；(2) 温和退火过程中H₂Pc在约50°C即开始分解，远低于CoPc和CuPc形成有序单层所需的温度，无法组装成扩展有序单层；(3) 化学计量比表面上未观察到自金属化现象；(4) 在还原表面上In⁰吸附原子存在时出现新的H₂Pc相关特征，但仅靠成像无法鉴别。

Conclusion: 中心金属离子在金属酞菁/In₂O₃(111)体系中扮演双重角色：一是作为结构锚定点稳定大环抗表面分解，二是通过改变前沿轨道特征决定第二吸附构型是否被占据。这为理解MPc在氧化物表面的界面行为及其催化性能提供了分子级别的认识。

Abstract: Metal phthalocyanines (MPc) are a versatile molecular platform for applications ranging from organic optoelectronic devices to single-atom catalysis (SAC). Their adsorption and layer formation on the prototypical transparent electrode substrates of organic optoelectronic devices is directly relevant for charge injection and transport across the organic-oxide interface. Moreover, the well-defined M-N$_4$ coordination of the metal cation defines their activity as SACs for (electro-)catalysis. Here, the adsorption of the metal-free phthalocyanine (H$_2$Pc) is characterized on In$_2$O$_3$(111) using low-temperature STM, nc-AFM, and STS. In$_2$O$_3$ is not only a model system of indium tin oxide (ITO) but also an active catalytic material for CO$_2$ reduction. H$_2$Pc adsorbs in the same site and configuration reported for copper phthalocyanine [J. Mater. Chem. C 13, 17650-17661 (2025)] and for the majority of cobalt phthalocyanine [Surf. Sci. 722, 122065 (2022)]. Despite this shared preference in adsorption site, H$_2$Pc cannot be organized into extended and ordered monolayer structures by gentle annealing: the molecule starts to decompose at $\approx$50$^\circ$C, well below the temperature used to grow monolayers of CoPc and CuPc. Self-metalation is not observed on stoichiometric In$_2$O$_3$(111). On the reduced surface where In$^0$ adatoms are present, new H$_2$Pc-related features appear but cannot be identified by imaging only. The comparison of H$_2$Pc with CoPc and CuPc identifies distinct roles of the central metal ion in the metal-Pc/In$_2$O$_3$(111) systems: it acts as a structural anchor that stabilizes the macrocycle against decomposition on the surface, and it modifies the frontier-orbital character in ways that determine whether a second adsorption configuration is populated.

</details>


### [113] [Controlling Hydrogen Isotope Retention at Helium Cavities through Radiation-Induced Segregation in Fusion Steels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08878)
*Lihao Shi,Logan N. Clowers,Qing Peng,Gary S. Was,Fei Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过三离子辐照实验和密度泛函理论计算，揭示了辐照诱导的溶质偏聚（Cr和Ta向空腔表面富集）显著增强氢同位素在面向等离子体材料中的滞留效应，并从电子结构层面阐明了其微观机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚变堆中氘-氚燃料循环中，氢同位素在面向等离子体材料和结构材料中的滞留是核心材料挑战，直接关系到氚库存控制和燃料经济性。理解并调控氢同位素的滞留机制对聚变能源的可行性至关重要。

Method: 采用三离子辐照实验（研究Fe-Cr-W减活化铁素体-马氏体钢F82H中Cr和Ta向空腔表面的偏聚行为），结合第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算系统分析Cr和Ta偏聚对氢原子在空腔处结合能和迁移势垒的影响，并通过电子态分析（d带中心位置和1s轨道杂化）揭示其物理本质。

Result: 三离子辐照下F82H钢中Cr和Ta显著偏聚在空腔表面。DFT计算表明，偏聚的溶质元素显著增强氢在空腔处的稳定性：一方面增强氢的结合能，另一方面提高氢的迁移势垒，从而导致氢滞留量显著增加。机制上，Ta促进强的H 1s与金属d轨道杂化，而Cr将表面d带移至更有利于成键的构型。

Conclusion: 首次建立了辐照诱导偏聚与高温氢同位素滞留之间的原子级关联，提出通过合金化学组分调控可控制聚变环境中氚库存的设计思路，为面向等离子体材料的优化提供了新的调控维度。

Abstract: Hydrogen isotope retention in plasma facing and structural alloys is a central materials challenge for deuterium-tritium fusion. Motivated by ion beam irradiation experiments and first principles calculations, we identify an irradiation-enabled mechanism whereby solute segregation to defect sinks enhances hydrogen isotope trapping. Triple-ion irradiation of reduced activation ferritic-martensitic steel F82H reveals pronounced segregation of Cr and Ta to cavity surfaces. Density functional theory shows that these segregants markedly increase H stability at cavities by strengthening binding energies and increasing migration barriers, leading to substantially higher attention. The effect originates from solute-tuned electronic structure: Ta promotes strong H 1s-metal d orbital hybridization, whereas Cr shifts the surface d-band toward a more favorable bonding configuration. These findings provide an atomistic link between irradiation-induced segregation and elevated-temperature hydrogen isotope retention and alloy chemistry routes to control tritium inventory in fusion environments.

</details>


### [114] [Zirconium Carbide as a High-Temperature Benchmark for the Beyond Quasi-Harmonic Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08913)
*Christopher M. Stanley*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以岩盐结构ZrC为高温基准材料，检验了Beyond Quasi-Harmonic (BQH)方法计算摩尔热容的准确性。BQH曲线较Cv(T)和准谐近似有显著改进，加上电子热容修正后与全非谐理论基准在约1200 K内高度一致，表明BQH方法能完整捕获普通准谐计算缺失的重要非谐声子贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔热容Cp(T)是材料科学中的核心热力学量，但当非谐振动效应变得重要时，从第一性原理精确计算仍具挑战性。本研究旨在验证BQH方法能否准确捕捉非谐振动对热容的贡献。

Method: 以岩盐型ZrC作为高温基准，应用BQH方法——一种基于预备超胞的第一性原理方法，直接从密度泛函理论能量评估中提取非谐振动能量。将BQH计算的摩尔热容与CALPHAD参考值、全非谐热力学积分计算、Cv(T)曲线及准谐计算进行比较。此外，由于ZrC具有电子导电性，还从费米能级态密度估算电子热容修正并叠加到振动BQH结果上。

Result: BQH曲线相较Cv(T)和准谐近似有大幅改进；加入电子热容修正后，计算的摩尔热容与全非谐理论基准在约1200 K内高度吻合。

Conclusion: BQH方法能够完整捕捉普通准谐计算中被遗漏的重要非谐声子贡献，是一种可靠的高温热容第一性原理计算方法。

Abstract: Molar heat capacity at constant pressure, C_p (T), is a central thermodynamic quantity in materials science, but it remains difficult to calculate accurately from first principles when anharmonic vibrational effects become important. In this work, rocksalt ZrC is used as a high-temperature benchmark for the Beyond Quasi-Harmonic (BQH) method, a prepared-supercell first-principles approach that extracts anharmonic vibrational energy directly from density-functional-theory energy evaluations. The calculated BQH molar heat capacity is compared with CALPHAD reference values, a fully anharmonic thermodynamic-integration calculation, a calculated C_v (T) curve from the present work, and a quasi-harmonic calculation. The BQH curve gives a substantial improvement beyond C_v (T) and beyond the quasi-harmonic approximation. Because ZrC is electronically conductive, an electronic heat-capacity correction was also estimated from the density of states at the Fermi level and added to the vibrational BQH result. This correction brings the calculated molar heat capacity into close agreement with the fully anharmonic theoretical benchmark through approximately 1200 K. These results show that the BQH method fully captures important anharmonic phonon contributions missing from ordinary quasi-harmonic calculations.

</details>


### [115] [Edge-Dominated Twist Mechanics at van der Waals Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08931)
*Yifan Shao,Zhaoheng Zhang,Hao Li,Oded Hod,Michael Urbakh,Quanshui Zheng,Xiang Gao,Deli Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的实验框架探测了同质和异质范德华界面的扭转力学及其滑动行为，发现两种界面的峰值扭矩均近似线性标度于接触面积，且摩擦力相差超三个数量级时相应扭矩仅相差约二十倍。大规模原子模拟揭示扭转运动由边缘主导的屈服机制控制，与平移滑动的体相应力传递机制截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转在调控范德华界面物理性质中起关键作用，但其力学机制仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用统一的实验框架同时探测同质和异质范德华界面的扭转力学与滑动行为，并结合大规模原子模拟。

Result: 发现峰值扭矩与接触面积几乎呈线性标度，与线弹性和刚性模型的预测不符；滑动摩擦相差超三个数量级，而扭矩遵循相同的线性标度且仅相差约二十倍；模拟揭示扭转屈服由边缘主导，弹性重构将有效承载区移至边缘。

Conclusion: 扭转与平移运动因加载几何的本质不同而遵循截然不同的力学机制（边缘驱动的屈服vs体相传质），二者无法相互预测，这对可动态重构的微纳机电设备设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Despite the pivotal role of twist in modulating physical properties at van der Waals (vdW) interfaces, the mechanics governing torsional response remain poorly understood. Here, we probe twist mechanics at homo- and heterogeneous vdW interfaces, together with their sliding behaviors within a unified experimental framework. For both systems, the peak torque scales nearly linearly with contact area, in contrast to predictions from linear elastic and rigid models. Remarkably, while the sliding friction of the two interfaces diverges by over three orders of magnitude owing to different scaling laws, the corresponding torque follows the same linear scaling and differs by only about twenty-fold. Large-scale atomistic simulations reveal an edge-dominated yielding mechanism for torsional motion, wherein elastic reconstruction shifts the effective load-bearing region toward the edges, eliminating torque from the contact interior. This mechanism contrasts with the bulk-mediated stress transmission governing translational sliding, a distinction rooted in the different loading geometries inherent to the two motion modes, where torsional loading necessitates perimeter actuation, whereas sliding enables center-driven loading. This symmetry-imposed divergence demonstrates that translational and torsional properties cannot be predicted from one another at vdW interfaces, providing critical insights for the design of dynamically reconfigurable micro- and nanoelectromechanical devices.

</details>


### [116] [Water, vacancies, and competing exchange interactions in Prussian blue analogues: a neutron diffraction study of field and dehydration-driven magnetic transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08976)
*N. S. Dhami,C. V. Colin,V. Nassif,O. Fabelo,T. Nait,A. Bleuzen,A. Bordage,V. Balédent*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过中子衍射系统研究了多种普鲁士蓝类似物（PBA）的结构与磁性质随温度和磁场的变化。发现所有双金属化合物在低散射角存在与关联空位及间隙水相关的宽弥散特征；CoFe和MnFe在脱水后经历从正到负热膨胀的连续交叉，而NiFe保持结构稳固。低温下除MnFe采用部分受阻磁结构(k=(1,0,0))外，其余化合物均为共线铁磁态(k=(0,0,0))。MnFe在Bc=1.3 T磁场或脱水条件下均可驱动自旋重取向至共线态，这与Mn同亚晶格上几何受阻的反铁磁Mn-Mn相互作用和反铁磁Mn-Fe耦合的近补偿有关。该结果解决了Fe K边XMCD响应的长期歧义，并确立水含量为调控该族材料结构和磁稳定性的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决普鲁士蓝类似物中Fe K边XMCD响应的长期解释歧义，并阐明水含量（间隙水/空位关联）对这类材料结构稳定性（热膨胀行为）和磁序（特别是MnFe的异常磁结构）的控制作用，为其作为电池电极等实际应用提供基础理解。

Method: 采用中子衍射技术对多组PBA体系（包括A4[Fe(CN)6]2.7的Co/Mn/Ni变体、Rb2Ni4框架及Mn4[Cr(CN)6]2.7）进行变温（2-450 K）和加场中子衍射实验；搭配四元（阳离子稳定的Rb2NiFe）框架进行对比识别内在弥散特征；结合最小海森堡模型理论计算来解释磁结构转变机制。

Result: 1) 所有Fm-3m族双金属PBA在低散射角均表现出宽弥散特征，经对照阳离子稳定的Rb2NiFe框架确认为关联空位及间隙水的内在特征信号；2) CoFe和MnFe在脱水过程中发生从正到负热膨胀的连续交叉，NiFe在450 K内保持结构稳定；3) 低温下多数化合物呈共线亚铁磁序(k=(0,0,0))，唯独MnFe采用部分受阻的k=(1,0,0)磁结构；4) MnFe在1.3 T中等磁场或脱水条件下可转变为共线亚铁磁态；5) 海森堡模型表明该转变源于Mn-Fe反铁磁耦合与面心立方Mn亚晶格上几何受阻的Mn-Mn反铁磁作用的近补偿，使MnFe处于磁补偿点附近。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了水/空位内容是控制PBA材料体系结构和磁稳定性（热膨胀、磁序类型及磁场诱导相变）的核心参数，将MnFe的独特磁行为归因于磁性交换的近补偿，从而解决了其XMCD响应的长期歧义，对这类材料在电池电极等领域的实际应用具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: We report a neutron diffraction study of the structural and magnetic properties of a family of ferro- and ferrimagnetic Prussian blue analogues (PBAs), $A_4$[Fe(CN)$_6$]$_{2.7}$ ($A$ = Co, Mn, Ni), Rb$_2$Ni$_4$[Fe(CN)$_6$]$_{3.3}$, and Mn$_4$[Cr(CN)$_6$]$_{2.7}$, as a function of temperature (2--450~K) and applied magnetic field. All bimetallic compounds of the Fm$\overline{3}$m family exhibit a broad diffuse feature at low scattering angle, which we identify, through comparison with the cation-stabilized Rb$_2$NiFe framework, as an intrinsic signature of correlated vacancies and their associated interstitial water. High-temperature diffraction reveals a continuous crossover from positive to negative thermal expansion in CoFe and MnFe upon dehydration, while NiFe remains structurally robust up to 450~K. At low temperature, all compounds order in a collinear ferrimagnetic state with propagation vector $\mathbf{k}=(0,0,0)$, except MnFe, which adopts a partially frustrated magnetic structure with $\mathbf{k}=(1,0,0)$. A moderate magnetic field of $B_c = 1.3$~T drives a spin reorientation in MnFe toward the collinear $\mathbf{k}=(0,0,0)$ ferrimagnetic state common to the other compounds; the same transition is independently induced by dehydration. A minimal Heisenberg model shows that this transition results from a near-compensation between antiferromagnetic Mn--Fe coupling and a geometrically frustrated antiferromagnetic Mn--Mn interaction on the face-centered-cubic Mn sublattice, placing MnFe in the vicinity of a magnetic compensation point. These results resolve a longstanding ambiguity in the interpretation of the Fe $K$-edge XMCD response of MnFe-based PBAs, and establish water content as a key parameter controlling both the structural and magnetic stability of this family of materials, with direct relevance to their use as battery electrodes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [LLM-Aided Design for Manufacturing: A Multi-Agent System for Intent-Preserving Redesign of CAD for Improved Manufacturability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05559)
*Kojo Welbeck,Xiangyu Shi,Zahra Sadeghi,Qi Zhu,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（LLM）的自主DFM（面向制造的设计）再设计方法。给定工程师的CAD模型，系统返回一个更易于制造且不丧失设计意图的变体。系统通过两个耦合的智能体子系统实现——DFM Reviewer逐个提出制造改进建议，CAD Modifier通过编译验证和视觉检查执行每个编辑，迭代地积累已验证的修改，无需微调即可处理复杂零件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单次生成DFM再设计对于复杂CAD零件可靠性不足，因为随着零件复杂度增加，CAD保真度会下降。因此需要一种能够逐步执行并验证每个设计转换的方法，既能提升可制造性又能保持原始设计意图，避免单次生成导致的精度损失。

Method: 提出DFM-Redesign流水线，包含两个由预训练多模态LLM驱动的耦合智能体子系统：(1) DFM Reviewer——审阅当前设计并逐个提出保持意图的可制造性改进建议；(2) CAD Modifier——将每个建议执行为对零件CadQuery程序的编辑，并通过编译验证和多视角渲染视觉检查形成闭环，若编辑未通过则重新生成或重新指示，直到被接受或放弃。迭代审阅与验证修改可逐步积累编辑，产生比单次生成更可靠的复杂零件。

Result: 在包含46个零件的基准测试中（以与参考几何体的chamfer距离为评分），CAD Modifier生成的目标零件平均精度优于使用相同工具的链式思维单智能体方法。消融实验分离了视觉审阅循环和编辑前设计状态字幕化各自的贡献。一个由32个链式转换构建的离心泵壳体展示了该方法通过复合验证编辑可达到的复杂度上限。

Conclusion: 该工作是一个初步报告：完整的审阅-再设计循环评估（包括可制造性增益和操作化的意图保持度量）仍在进行中。该方法展示了无需微调、通过迭代验证编辑来提升DFM重设计可靠性和复杂度的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce autonomous, intent-preserving Design for Manufacturing (DFM) redesign of CAD parts: given an engineer's CAD model, the method returns a variant that is easier to manufacture without losing its design intent. Generating such a redesign in a single shot is unreliable, since CAD fidelity degrades as parts grow complex; we instead produce it as a sequence of individually verified design transitions. Our DFM-Redesign pipeline realizes this with two coupled agent subsystems driven by a pretrained multimodal LLM: a DFM Reviewer that inspects the current design and proposes one intent-preserving manufacturability improvement at a time, and a CAD Modifier that executes each proposal as an edit to the part's CadQuery program. The CAD Modifier closes a verification loop, compiling every candidate edit and visually checking it against the intended change from multi-view renderings, then re-generating or re-instructing until the edit is accepted or abandoned. Iterating review and verified modification compounds edits into parts more complex than one-shot generators reliably produce, preserves the original intent at each step, and requires no fine-tuning. On a 46-part benchmark scored by chamfer distance to reference geometries, the CAD Modifier reproduces target parts more accurately on average than chain-of-thought single agents given the same tools, and ablations isolate the contributions of the visual review loop and of captioning the design state before each edit. A centrifugal pump casing built from 32 chained transitions illustrates the complexity reachable by compounding verified edits. This is a preliminary report: evaluation of the full review-and-redesign loop, including manufacturability gain and an operational measure of intent preservation, is ongoing.

</details>


### [118] [Risk-Aware Generative Inpainting for Optimized Design Editing of EV Battery Cooling Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08387)
*Leekyo Jeong,Yoon Koo Lee,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出一种风险感知的生成式编辑框架，用于电动车电池包冷却通道的局部设计优化。该框架通过离线编辑结果估算每个候选编辑位置的效果分布，而非仅依赖单一期望改进值，从而在选择编辑位置时显式考虑生成式inpainting的随机性。用户可在推理时按风险偏好切换策略（追求更高期望改进或更高一致性），无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的图像修复（inpainting）支持冷却通道的局部设计修改，但其随机性导致同一区域多次编辑结果不同。这引发一个根本问题：当每次修改的结果是随机的时，应如何选择编辑位置？现有方法仅用单一期望改进值来评分候选位置，忽略了结果的分布信息。

Method: 提出风险感知生成式编辑框架：(1) 利用CFD训练的代理模型评估离线编辑结果，估计每个候选位置的效果分布；(2) 根据选定的风险水平对位置排序，可强调更高期望改进或更高一致性；(3) 单个训练模型在推理时支持不同编辑偏好，无需重新训练；(4) 通过独立的CFD验证和七种掩码配置的配对研究评估策略。

Result: 与随机编辑相比，所提策略在大多数掩码配置中改善了冷却通道目标，且该优势在独立CFD验证下依然成立。变化风险水平展现了平均改进与运行间一致性之间的稳定权衡。学习到的分布对排序有效，但不应视为校准的概率分布。

Conclusion: 通过风险感知的位置选择，可以将随机生成式编辑从不确定性来源转化为可控的设计决策。有效编辑不仅取决于如何修改设计，还取决于在哪里修改以及可接受的结果变异性大小。

Abstract: Cooling-channel layouts for electric-vehicle battery packs must deliver temperature uniformity and low pressure drop while maintaining a single continuous channel. In late-stage design, local modification is a practical way to improve performance while retaining established global features. Diffusion-based inpainting supports such local edits, but its stochastic nature produces different outcomes even for the same region. This raises a fundamental question: how should edit locations be selected when each modification's outcome is stochastic? We propose a risk-aware generative editing framework that selects edit locations by accounting for this variability. Rather than scoring each candidate location by a single expected improvement, the method estimates a distribution of outcomes from offline edit results evaluated with a CFD-trained surrogate, and ranks locations by a chosen risk level. A single trained model therefore supports different editing preferences at inference time, emphasizing either higher expected improvement or greater consistency, without retraining. The policy is evaluated against random editing in a held-out paired study across seven mask configurations. It improves the cooling-channel objective over random editing in most configurations, and the advantage holds under independent CFD verification of the edited designs. Varying the risk level reveals a consistent trade-off between mean improvement and run-to-run consistency, while the learned distribution is useful for ranking locations but should not be read as a calibrated probability distribution. Together, these results show that stochastic generative editing can be converted from a source of variability into a controllable design decision through risk-aware location selection: effective editing depends not only on how a design is modified, but also on where and how much outcome variability is acceptable.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Robustness of LLM-Generated SystemVerilog Assertions to Semantics-Preserving RTL Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05658)
*FNU Aditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语义保持RTL变换的受控嬗变评估方法，系统度量LLM在SystemVerilog断言生成中的鲁棒性。结果显示在对原始RTL正确的行为中，有9.7%-27.0%在语义不变变换后变得错误，证明点准确率无法反映模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成SVA的评估仅在单一语法表示上报告正确率，无法揭示当同一RTL行为以不同方式书写时模型的输出是否稳定。点准确率掩盖了模型在语义等价变换下可能出现的显著不稳定性，因此需要一种能衡量LLM在断言生成任务中鲁棒性的评估方法。

Method: 基于VERT数据集构建质量过滤的条件控制池和分层40程序评估集（含295个赋值行为），选用Qwen2.5-Coder-7B和DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite两个开放代码模型，以相同评估提示和贪心解码进行实验。研究三种语义保持变换：操作数重排、确定性标识符重命名和冗余括号化。在基线和变换后准确率之外，度量条件鲁棒性、不变性失败和任意翻转率，并在RTL程序层面使用10,000样本聚类自助法计算置信区间。

Result: 在所有六个模型-变换条件下，原始RTL上正确行为的9.7%-27.0%在语义保持变换后变得不正确。例如，在标识符重命名下DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite准确率从53.9%提升至63.7%，但同时有19.5%原本正确的行为失败。对30个抽样正确到错误转变的人工审查识别出路径谓词丢失、分支极性错误、布尔结构破坏和输出契约违背等错误类型。

Conclusion: 点准确率不足以表征LLM在断言生成中的可靠性，需要面向鲁棒性的评估方法来支持AI辅助硬件验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for automating SystemVerilog Assertion (SVA) generation, yet most evaluations report correctness on a single syntactic representation of an input. Such point accuracy does not reveal whether a model's correct output is stable when the same RTL behavior is written differently. This paper presents a controlled metamorphic evaluation of LLM-based SVA generation under semantics-preserving RTL transformations. Starting from the VERT dataset, we construct a quality-filtered conditional-control pool and a stratified 40-program evaluation set containing 295 assignment behaviors. We evaluate two open code models, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite, with an identical evaluation prompt and greedy decoding. Three transformations are studied: operand reordering, deterministic identifier renaming, and redundant parenthesization. Beyond baseline and transformed accuracy, we measure conditional robustness, invariance failure, and any-flip rate, with 10,000-sample clustered bootstrap intervals at the RTL-program level. Across all six model-transformation conditions, 9.7%-27.0% of behaviors that were correct on the original RTL become incorrect after a semantics-preserving transformation. Aggregate accuracy can therefore hide substantial instability: under identifier renaming, DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite improves from 53.9% to 63.7% accuracy while 19.5% of its originally correct behaviors fail. Manual review of 30 sampled correct-to-wrong transitions identifies dropped path predicates, branch-polarity errors, Boolean-structure corruption, and output-contract violations. The results show that point accuracy alone is insufficient for characterizing LLM reliability in assertion generation and motivate robustness-aware evaluation for AI-assisted hardware verification.

</details>


### [120] [GraphNOSE: A Graph Transformer in Olfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05694)
*Mrityunjay Sharma,Sarabeshwar Balaji,Valentina Parma,Ritesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphNOSE是一个基于图transformer的开源框架，通过SMILES字符串预测单分子和二元混合物的多标签气味描述符，参数比标准GNN少6倍，AUROC平均提升4.52%，OOD数据上达到84%的AUROC，超越当前最优模型Open-POM（81%），并利用XAI方法识别驱动气味预测的分子子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从分子结构预测气味质量是化学信息学中的开放问题。线性模型在将分子特征与气味描述符关联时表现良好，但在外推到新型化学支架、极端分子量或复杂气味混合物时往往失效，因此需要更具泛化能力和可解释性的模型。

Method: 引入GraphNOSE框架：一种开源的图transformer架构，从SMILES字符串预测单分子和二元混合物的多标签气味描述符。集成位置编码和结构编码于transformer-based图架构中，并应用XAI方法识别驱动气味预测的子结构和分子特征。

Result: GraphNOSE参数比标准GNN少6倍，显著优于线性模型、分子语言模型嵌入、分子指纹和基线GNN，平均AUROC边际增益4.52%（p<0.01）。OOD化合物上达到84%的AUROC，超过当前SOTA Open-POM（81%，p<0.001），并识别出线性模型失效的条件。XAI分析结果与化学直觉一致。

Conclusion: GraphNOSE是一个可扩展且可解释的嗅觉预测架构，能泛化到当前感知数据库中代表性不足的结构上不同的化合物，为嗅觉预测领域建立了新的基准。

Abstract: Predicting olfactory qualities from molecular structure is an open problem in chemoinformatics. Although linear models can link molecular features to odor descriptors, they often fail when extrapolating to novel chemical scaffolds, extreme molecular weights, or complex odor mixtures. To address this, we introduce GraphNOSE, an open-source graph transformer framework that predicts multi-label odor descriptors from simplified molecular-input line-entry system (SMILES) strings for single molecules and binary mixtures. By integrating positional and structural encodings within a transformer-based graph architecture, GraphNOSE achieves strong performance with six times fewer parameters than standard graph neural network (GNN) baseline while consistently outperforming linear models, molecular language model embeddings, molecular fingerprints, and baseline GNNs by an average area under the ROC curve (AUROC) margin of 4.52% (p < 0.01). GraphNOSE achieves an AUROC of 84% on out-of-distribution compounds (OODs). This exceeds the current state-of-the-art GNN for OOD in olfaction (Open-POM: 81%, p < 0.001), and identifies conditions under which linear models empirically fail. Finally, we apply XAI (explainable AI) methods to identify which substructures and molecular features drive odor predictions, yielding insights consistent with chemical intuition and grounded in the model's learned representations. Together, these results establish GraphNOSE as a scalable and interpretable architecture for olfactory prediction that generalizes to structurally distinct compounds underrepresented in current perceptual databases.

</details>


### [121] [A Multi-Source Ensemble Approach to Candidate Generation for Alternative Vacation Rental Property Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05748)
*Syed Mohammed Arshad Zaidi,Eric Rincon,Shayan Hassantabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了度假租赁市场中备选房源推荐（alternative property recommendations）的候选生成（CG）方法，在大规模平台（200万+活跃房源）上对比了协同过滤、浅层嵌入和图神经网络三类方法。核心发现是：将基于物品的协同过滤与GNN检索相结合的混合架构在Recall@300上比最强基线提升14.8%；GNN嵌入单独比浅层Hotel2Vec嵌入在Recall上相对提升48-68%。研究还考察了CG阶段的收益如何传导到下游排序阶段，发现更强的候选池能提升最终排序质量，但CG与排序器训练之间的耦合使得该效应的归因复杂化，存在"召回-转化差距"（recall-conversion gap）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度假租赁市场中的备选房源推荐对于帮助用户发现相关选项至关重要，但候选生成面临异构库存、地理约束、可用性快速变化和长尾房源分布等独特挑战。现有研究缺乏对这一场景下候选生成方法的系统评估，因此需要全面比较不同CG方法的性能并探究其在下游排序阶段的影响。

Method: 该研究在一个超过200万活跃房源的度假租赁平台上，系统比较了三类候选生成方法：协同过滤（item-based CF）、浅层嵌入（Hotel2Vec）和图神经网络（GNN）方法。最终提出一种混合架构，将基于物品的协同过滤与GNN检索相结合，利用两类方法的互补优势：CF擅长为交互历史丰富的房源提供早期召回，GNN则能发现多样化、非显而易见的备选方案并更有效地应对冷启动场景。研究中还对比了GNN嵌入与浅层Hotel2Vec嵌入的单独表现，并分析了CG阶段收益对下游两阶段推荐系统中排序阶段的影响。

Result: 实验结果（200万+活跃房源）表明：混合架构（CF+GNN）在Recall@300上比最强基线提升了14.8%；GNN嵌入单独比浅层Hotel2Vec嵌入有48-68%的相对Recall提升（跨不同K值），这为其纳入集成提供了依据。此外，更强的候选池能带来更高的下游排序质量，但CG与排序器训练之间的耦合使得该效果的干净归因变得困难，揭示了"召回-转化差距"。

Conclusion: 该研究为度假租赁领域的两阶段推荐系统提供了重要的实际洞见：混合CF+GNN的候选生成架构能有效提升召回性能，且这种增益能传导到下游排序中。但研究者提醒实践者注意CG与排序器训练之间耦合带来的归因困难，理解"recall-conversion gap"对于在两阶段推荐系统中部署新的检索方法至关重要。

Abstract: Alternative property recommendations play a critical role in vacation rental marketplaces, helping users discover relevant options when viewing a specific listing. However, generating high-quality candidate alternatives presents unique challenges: heterogeneous inventory, geographic constraints, rapid availability changes, and long-tail property distributions. We present a comprehensive study of candidate generation (CG) approaches for vacation rental alternatives, comparing collaborative filtering, shallow embeddings, and graph neural network (GNN) methods.
  Our experiments on a large-scale vacation rental platform (over 2M active properties) show that a hybrid architecture combining item-based collaborative filtering with GNN-based retrieval improves Recall@300 by 14.8% over the strongest baseline, by leveraging the complementary strengths of the two sources: collaborative filtering excels at early recall for properties with rich interaction history, while GNNs discover diverse, non-obvious alternatives and handle cold-start scenarios more effectively. As a component result, GNN-based embeddings alone substantially outperform shallow Hotel2Vec embeddings (48-68% relative recall improvement across K), motivating their inclusion in the ensemble.
  Crucially, we examine how CG-stage gains carry through to the downstream ranking stage, and find that a stronger candidate pool yields higher downstream ranking quality, though attributing this effect cleanly is complicated by the coupling between candidate generation and ranker training. This recall-conversion gap is an important consideration for practitioners deploying new retrieval methods in two-stage recommendation systems.

</details>


### [122] [Data Scout: Targeted Web Crawling for Domain-Specific Pretraining Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05766)
*Chirag Garg,Eelaaf Zahid,Farhan Ahmed,Jay Pankaj Gala,Eric Butler,Heiko Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Data Scout提出了一种颠覆性的领域特定预训练语料构建方法：不再从大规模网页存档中过滤语料，而是通过LLM将主题扩展为分类树和数千条搜索查询，引导定向网络爬虫获取种子URL，并按子域名分组后利用用户提供的分类器（探针）进行样本审查，以决定是否收编整个子域名。该方法基于相关性在子域名层面存在清晰边界的观察（数学领域同一子域内的页面相关性是兄弟子域的21倍）。使用FineMath分类器作为探针时，爬取页面中21.9%为高质量数学内容，是过滤CommonCrawl（0.31%）的70倍；且63.2%的页面在CommonCrawl中完全缺失但同样有效。在1.9B token上的持续预训练实验中，Llama-3.2-3B在GSM8k上达到了与FineMath语料相当的性能。由于探针是唯一依赖领域定制的组件，Data Scout原则上可适用于任何拥有此类分类器的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于大型网页存档（如CommonCrawl）过滤方法在热门领域有效，但对专业领域失效——相关内容稀疏且往往不在流行度驱动的爬虫覆盖范围内。Data Scout反转了这一范式：先定向爬取再筛选，而非存档后过滤。

Method: Data Scout的工作流程包括：1) LLM将根主题扩展为分类树和数千条搜索查询；2) 收集返回URL（种子）并按子域名分组；3) 使用用户提供的分类器（探针）对每组的少量样本进行筛选，据此决定是否接受该子域名。其核心洞察是相关性在子域名级别具有锐利边界（可达21倍差距）。

Result: 在数学领域实验中：以FineMath分类器为探针，21.9%的爬取页面为高质量数学内容（相比CommonCrawl过滤的0.31%提升70倍）；63.2%的页面不在CommonCrawl中但同样有用；Llama-3.2-3B在1.9B Data Scout token上持续预训练后，GSM8k性能与FineMath语料相当。

Conclusion: Data Scout证明定向爬取策略在专业领域语料构建中不仅效率更高（浪费更少爬取努力），还能获取CommonCrawl缺失的独有价值内容且训练效果不打折扣。由于探针是唯一的领域特定组件，该方法具有良好的通用性，可推广至任何具备相应分类器的领域。

Abstract: The dominant approach to building domain-specific pretraining corpora is to filter large web archives such as CommonCrawl. This works well for popular domains but breaks down for specialized ones, where relevant content is sparse and often beyond the reach of popularity-driven crawlers. We present Data Scout, which inverts this: instead of filtering an archive, it directs a targeted crawl. An LLM expands a root topic into a taxonomy and thousands of search queries; the returned URLs (seeds) are grouped by subdomain and screened with a user-supplied classifier (the probe), admitting each subdomain on the basis of a small sample. This works because relevance has a sharp boundary at the subdomain level: in mathematics, a page is 21x more likely to be relevant than one on a sibling subdomain. With the FineMath classifier as the probe, 21.9% of crawled pages are high-quality math content, 70x the 0.31% rate from filtering a comparable web sample, so the crawl wastes far less effort. But the payoff is not just efficiency: 63.2% of these pages are missing from CommonCrawl altogether, yet just as useful for training. Continued pretraining of Llama-3.2-3B on 1.9B Data Scout tokens matches FineMath corpus on GSM8k. Because the probe is the only domain-specific component, Data Scout can in principle apply to any domain with such a classifier.

</details>


### [123] [Online Learning with LLM Experts from Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05820)
*Wang Wei,Soumyabrata Pal,Koyel Mukherjee,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Branislav Kveton,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线场景中、有限反馈条件下，如何自适应地将提示词（prompts）路由到不同的大语言模型（LLM）专家以最大化响应质量。作者将其形式化为一个多臂老虎机（bandit）问题，在K个动作（专家）和d个特征（提示词）上运行T轮。在完全信息设定下达到Õ(dT/√m)的遗憾界，在bandit设定下达到Õ(dT√(K/m))的遗憾界，其中m是反馈预算。实验表明能从有限反馈中高效学习高质量的LLM路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，面临多个不同能力和特点的LLM（专家），需要根据输入提示词自动选择最合适的模型来获得最佳响应质量。然而在线场景中反馈（如人工评分或偏好）是稀缺且昂贵的，因此需要高效的算法来在有限反馈预算下学习并优化路由策略。

Method: 将提示词路由问题形式化为带特征的多臂老虎机（contextual bandit）问题，其中K个专家对应K个臂，d维特征向量编码提示词。作者在完全信息（full-information）和bandit两种反馈设置下分别设计算法，通过策略性地选择哪些轮次去观察奖励来在反馈预算m的限制下最小化累积遗憾。

Result: 在完全信息设置下达到Õ(dT/√m)的遗憾上界；在bandit设置下达到Õ(dT√(K/m))的遗憾上界。实验验证了所提方法能在有限反馈下高效学习跨多种LLM的高质量路由策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法能够在给定有限反馈预算m下取得次线性遗憾界，证明了在在线、稀疏反馈条件下学习LLM自适应路由策略的可行性和高效性，为生产环境中的多模型路由提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: We study adaptive routing of prompts to large language model (LLM) experts to maximize response quality in an online setting with limited feedback. We formulate it as a bandit problem with $K$ actions that represent experts and $d$ features that encode prompts, over a horizon of $T$ rounds. We propose algorithms that strategically select and observe rewards to minimize regret. In the full-information setting, we achieve a regret of $\tilde{O}(d T / \sqrt{m})$, while in the bandit setting we achieve $\tilde{O}(d T \sqrt{K / m})$, where $m \ll T$ is a budget on feedback. Our experiments show that we efficiently learn high-quality routing strategies across diverse LLMs from limited feedback.

</details>


### [124] [Rethinking the Evaluation of Efficiency Methods for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05933)
*Jiamu Zhang,Lingxi Zhang,Pengjun Lu,Qiyue Zhang,Yu-Neng Chuang,Zhengchen Li,Shuai Xu,Vipin Chaudhary,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有多智能体系统（MAS）效率提升方法的评价可能存在高估，并通过引入受控且MAS密集型诊断基准来验证这一观点，发现许多报告的性能提升依赖于特定设置，可能源于结构坍缩、禁用工具路径或随机剪枝已能保持精度等情况，而非真正的MAS效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来MAS效率优化方法（如剪枝智能体、移除通信边、搜索紧凑结构）自称能降低成本，但作者认为现有评估存在缺陷：报告的性能提升是在方法特定的提示词和起始拓扑下测得的，难以归因于结构性改动；且许多成功案例出现在非MAS密集的任务中，单一智能体或随机剪枝系统本身就能保持强性能。因此需要对已有方法进行更严格和公平的检验。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控且MAS密集的诊断基准，在共享的骨干模型、智能体注册表及运行时条件下，系统性地评估代表性MAS效率方法。实验设计包含对拓扑结构、规模、深度和工具使用的受控变化，以便在公平环境下比较不同方法的真实效能增益，并识别其报告中性能提升的本质来源。

Result: 分析表明，许多方法报告的性能提升是设置依赖的，可能来源于结构性坍缩（如系统退化为单一智能体）、禁用了关键工具通路，或是起始系统本身性能已经很强以至于随机剪枝就能保持精度，而非反映方法对MAS效率的稳健改进。

Conclusion: 现有MAS效率方法在真实MAS密集型任务下的增益被高估，亟需建立更严格、受控且能真实反映MAS需求复杂性的评估基准，以推动该领域更可靠地发展。

Abstract: Efficiency is increasingly important for Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS), as larger models and more agents introduce substantial execution costs. Recent methods aim to make MAS cheaper by pruning agents, removing communication edges, or searching for compact structures. However, we argue that existing evaluations may overestimate their true ability to improve MAS efficiency. Reported gains are often measured under method-specific prompts and starting topologies, making them difficult to attribute to the proposed structural changes. Moreover, many reported successes appear in non-MAS-demanding settings, where a single agent or a randomly pruned system can already preserve strong performance. To study these issues, we introduce a controlled and MAS-demanding diagnostic benchmark for representative MAS efficiency methods. We evaluate methods under a shared backbone model, agent registry, and runtime, across controlled variations in topology, scale, depth, and tool use. Our analysis shows that many reported gains are setup-dependent and may arise from structural collapse, disabled tool pathways, or starting systems where random pruning already preserves accuracy, rather than robust improvements in MAS efficiency.

</details>


### [125] [Data Quality Rule Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06053)
*Anna-Christina Glock,Thomas Hütter,Johannes Fürnkranz,Wolfram Wöß,Christine Dominka-Kiss,Lisa Ehrlinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDQeR，一种基于大型语言模型（LLM）的自动化数据质量（DQ）规则生成方法，通过生成-过滤框架自动生成数据验证规则，解决手动维护规则集困难的问题。实验表明该方法能在多种数据集和错误类型上生成有效且紧凑的规则集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在客户和员工数据验证中依赖基于规则的数据质量工具，但手动维护全面的规则集非常困难，领域专家经常遗漏关键规则（例如患者记录中错误的药物单位可能危及生命，地址缺少门牌号会导致投递失败）。因此，自动生成数据质量规则以弥补规则缺口是一个亟待解决的实践问题。

Method: 论文形式化了一个通用的生成-过滤框架，并提出LeDQeR方法。具体分为两步：(1) 利用大型语言模型（LLM）从观测到的脏数据元组中，根据给定基于规则的数据质量工具语法生成候选规则；(2) 应用四种过滤技术确保生成的规则满足：(i)可执行性、(ii)正确性、(iii)泛化性，并避免(iv)冗余。

Result: 广泛的实验评估表明，LeDQeR能够在各种数据集和错误类型上产生有效且紧凑的规则集。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动化数据质量规则生成是可行的，生成-过滤框架能有效保障生成规则的质量（可执行、正确、可泛化且不冗余），为解决企业实践中规则维护难的问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: The validation of data, such as customer and employee data, is an important task in many organizations. Errors in data can have severe consequences. For example, a wrong drug unit in a patient record can lead to life-threatening medication errors, and a missing street number in an address to failed deliveries. Companies often employ rule-based enterprise data quality (DQ) tools, which allow domain experts to specify rules to validate the data over time. While rule-based DQ tools are computationally efficient and provide explainable reports, maintaining a comprehensive rule set manually is challenging, as domain experts often overlook essential rules, especially in complex domains and large data volumes. Hence, closing these gaps remains an open problem in practice. In this paper, we address the challenge of automated DQ rule generation. For this, we formalize a generalizable generate-filter framework and introduce LeDQeR, an LLM-based DQ rule generation approach.
