First, a large language model (LLM) generates candidates rules from an observed dirty data tuple for a given rule-based DQ tool syntax. Second, we apply four filter techniques that ensure the (i) executability, (ii) correctness, and (iii) generalizability, and avoid (iv) redundancy of the generated rules. An extensive experimental evaluation suggests that LeDQeR is able to produce effective and compact rule sets for various datasets and error types.

</details>


### [126] [FedSubMuon: Communication-Efficient Federated LLM Fine-Tuning via Structured Subspace Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06073)
*Shaolong Chen,Youming Tao,Shuzhen Chen,Falko Dressler,Qingqing Ye,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedSubMuon及其扩展FedSubMuon-GT，一种通信高效的联邦Muon微调方法。该方法在共享结构化子空间内优化紧凑系数矩阵，保持Muon对单一矩阵值可训练对象的矩阵感知更新几何，同时将客户端上传量降至紧凑系数矩阵级别。实验表明FedSubMuon-GT在五组数据集-模型配对中四项取得最佳精度，FedSubMuon在所有匹配通信预算下性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调将大语言模型适配到分散的客户端数据，但跨设备训练中高通信成本限制了其可扩展性。现有联邦Muon方法虽展现了矩阵感知优化的优势，但仍需传输全层大小的更新和优化器状态。直接将Muon应用于LoRA因子虽能降低通信，但会改变优化对象并削弱Muon的矩阵感知更新几何。

Method: FedSubMuon在共享结构化子空间内优化紧凑系数矩阵，使Muon保持作用于单一矩阵值可训练对象，同时将客户端上传量缩减为紧凑系数矩阵。FedSubMuon-GT作为精度导向的扩展，使用投影梯度来使追踪的子空间基朝向任务相关梯度方向自适应调整。

Result: 在指令微调和数学推理实验中，FedSubMuon-GT在五组数据集-模型配对中的四组取得最佳整体精度，FedSubMuon在所有匹配通信预算下性能最优。在Dolly-15K数据集上，最接近的通信基线在Llama-1B和Qwen-4B上分别需要5.5倍和1.4倍的总通信量。

Conclusion: FedSubMuon系列方法成功将Muon的矩阵感知优化优势与结构化子空间通信压缩策略结合，在保持精度的同时显著降低联邦微调的通信开销，验证了矩阵感知优化器与通信高效的联邦训练可以兼得。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning adapts large language models (LLMs) to decentralized client data, but its scalability in cross-device training is often limited by the high communication cost. Muon is an optimizer that improves optimization performance by orthogonalizing momentum for matrix-valued parameters. Existing federated Muon methods demonstrate the benefit of matrix-aware optimization in federated learning, but still require transmitting full layer-size updates and optimizer state. A natural way to reduce communication is to directly apply Muon to LoRA factors, but this changes the optimized object and weakens Muon's matrix-aware update geometry. We propose FedSubMuon, a communication-efficient federated Muon fine-tuning method that optimizes compact coefficient matrices within shared structured subspaces. This design keeps Muon on a single matrix-valued trainable object, while reducing the client upload to compact coefficient matrices. We further introduce FedSubMuon-GT, an accuracy-oriented extension that uses projected gradients to adapt tracked subspace bases toward task-relevant gradient directions. Experiments on instruction tuning and mathematical reasoning show that FedSubMuon-GT achieves the best overall accuracy on four of five dataset-model pairs, while FedSubMuon performs best under all matched communication budgets. On Dolly-15K, the closest communication baseline requires 5.5 times and 1.4 times more total communication on Llama-1B and Qwen-4B, respectively.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Retraining-Free MoE Compression: A Cost-Normalized Study of Post-Compression Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06076)
*Sieun Hyeon,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究挑战了"免重训MoE压缩后的checkpoint即最终产物"的传统观点，提出压缩后的MoE checkpoint应被视为"压缩初始化"，只需极小的后压缩调整阶段即可显著恢复性能。实验基于两个MoE LLM骨干、四种剪枝/合并方法、三个专家保留比例和28个基准，在仅用3,000个C4样本和单个训练周期的情况下，全参数微调平均恢复了原始到压缩性能差距的37.3%，且LM微调比token级KD更具成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免重训MoE压缩方法（如专家剪枝或合并）虽能降低部署内存，但通常将压缩后的checkpoint视为最终产物，忽视了通过极小规模的后续调整来恢复压缩损失的可能性。作者认为这一视角是不完整的，需要探究压缩后checkpoint作为初始化进行一次微小的后压缩调整阶段的价值。

Method: 在系统化的实验框架下，横跨两个MoE LLM骨干、四种剪枝/合并方法、三个专家保留比例和28个基准，在匹配的小数据预算和实测GPU成本下，对比了LM微调与基于teacher的蒸馏两种后压缩调整策略，并评估了不同参数范围（如全参数vs部分参数）的成本-恢复权衡。

Result: 仅使用3,000个C4样本和一个训练周期的调整，全参数微调平均恢复了原始到压缩性能差距的37.3%。LM微调比标准token级知识蒸馏更具成本效益，且在测试的调整范围中，全参数调整在成本与性能恢复之间提供了最强的权衡。

Conclusion: 免重训式MoE压缩应搭配小规模的后压缩调整阶段，以恢复压缩过程中损失的相当一部分性能。这为MoE模型压缩的实践提出了新的范式：将压缩产物视为初始化而非最终checkpoint。

Abstract: Retraining-free MoE compression reduces deployment memory by pruning or merging experts, but often treats the compressed checkpoint as the final artifact. We argue that this view is incomplete: compressed MoE checkpoints are better understood as compressed initializations that benefit from a tiny post-compression adjustment stage. Across two MoE LLM backbones, four pruning/merging methods, three expert-retention ratios, and 28 benchmarks, we compare LM fine-tuning and teacher-based KD under matched small-data budgets and measured GPU costs. Using only 3,000 C4 examples and a single epoch of adjustment, Full FT recovers 37.3% of the original-to-compressed performance gap on average. Moreover, LM fine-tuning is more cost-effective than standard token-level KD, and full-parameter adjustment gives the strongest cost--recovery trade-off among the tested scopes. These results suggest that retraining-free compression should be paired with small post-compression adjustment to recover a substantial portion of the performance lost during compression.

</details>


### [128] [Connectome-to-Function: Conditional Generative Latent Representations for Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06093)
*Zhuolin Yu,Xingyu Liu,Yuanhao Jia,Yunhang Xiao,Hairuo Xue,Feihan Sun,Guozhang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个条件生成潜变量框架（conditional generative latent framework），用于将连接组图（connectome graphs）编码为紧凑的结构空间，并利用节点级条件引导重建与生成。该框架能以高达0.910的平均边重建AUC重建观测连接，并生成新的候选连接组，实现图结构与计算行为的统一分析。通过将连接组衍生图作为递归计算基底，该潜空间在储层计算实验中捕获了功能变异（交叉验证R²最高约0.87）。可解释性分析揭示了任务特异性结构机制：记忆性能与交互式递归连接相关，而预测与分类与递归网络的谱性质更相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组作为大脑回路的图级结构映射，为理解神经回路如何支持功能与计算提供了结构基础，但将连接组结构与计算对应起来仍然困难，因为这些图是高维、稀疏且对局部结构变异敏感的。现有方法依赖手工设计的结构描述符或任务特异性预测器，限制了以生成性和功能有意义的方式表示连接组的能力。

Method: 提出一个条件生成潜变量框架：将连接组图编码为紧凑的结构空间，同时利用可用的节点级条件引导重建和生成过程。从该潜空间中，模型能够重建观测到的连接性，并生成新的候选连接组。后续使用连接组衍生图作为储层计算（reservoir computing）的递归计算基底来评估潜空间的功能捕获能力，并进行可解释性分析。

Result: 模型在边重建任务上达到最高0.910的平均AUC；在储层计算实验中，学习到的潜空间捕获功能变异的交叉验证R²最高约0.87。可解释性分析显示：记忆性能与交互式递归连接（reciprocal recurrent connectivity）相关，而预测和分类任务与递归网络的谱性质（spectral properties）更相关。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种AI-for-science方法，将神经连接与计算联系起来，为研究不同结构机制如何塑造计算能力提供了生成性和可解释性的基础。研究发现表明，结构机制（如递归互连与谱性质）在不同计算任务中存在任务特异性分工。

Abstract: Connectomes, graph-level maps of neurons and their synaptic connections, provide a structural basis for understanding how brain circuits support function and computation. However, mapping connectome structure to computation remains difficult because these graphs are high-dimensional, sparse, and sensitive to local structural variation. Existing approaches often depend on hand-crafted structural descriptors or task-specific predictors, which limits their ability to represent connectomes in a form that is both generative and functionally meaningful. We propose a conditional generative latent framework that encodes connectome graphs into a compact structural space while using available node-level conditions to guide reconstruction and generation. From this space, the model can reconstruct observed connectivity with a mean edge-reconstruction AUC up to 0.910 and generate new candidate connectomes, enabling a unified analysis of graph structure and computational behavior. Using connectome-derived graphs as recurrent computational substrates, we found that the learned latent space captures functional variation across reservoir-computing experiments, with cross-validated $R^2$ values up to approximately 0.87. Interpretability analysis further revealed task-specific structural mechanisms: in our examples, memory performance is associated with reciprocal recurrent connectivity, whereas prediction and classification are more strongly associated with spectral properties of the recurrent network. These findings suggest an AI-for-science approach to linking neural connectivity to computation and provide a generative and interpretable basis for studying how distinct structural mechanisms shape computational capacity.

</details>


### [129] [Linear Algebra Foundations of Efficient Attention: A Phase Reversal in Rank Collapse Under SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06341)
*Anjaneya Teja Sarma Kalvakolanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了十四篇关于线性代数技术（矩阵秩、SVD、特征分解）在基于Transformer的基础模型研究中的应用的同行评审工作，聚焦自注意力矩阵输出秩的推导与性质、利用该现象的压缩方法、以及低秩KV缓存投影及其与线性注意力和状态空间模型的半可分离矩阵对偶性。论文报告了一个原始发现：使用SVD压缩注意力投影对网络秩坍塌的影响是双面的——在初始化时强烈抑制秩坍塌，但在预训练模型上加速秩坍塌（在GPT-2 124M、GPT-2 Medium 355M和Pythia-160M上验证），且通过对照因果分解证明，该行为主要由SVD选择压缩子空间的方式驱动，而非其算子范数缩减。这一发现对校准感知压缩观点提供了修正与解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到该文献中一个开放问题：所讨论的压缩方法与网络自然秩坍塌现象之间的相互作用尚未被充分理解。他们被这一动机驱动，希望系统性地整合现有工作并揭示压缩方法对秩坍塌的因果影响机制。

Method: 作者统一分析了十四篇独立工作，覆盖三个主题：自注意力矩阵输出秩的推导与性质、利用秩坍塌现象的压缩方法、低秩KV缓存投影与半可分离矩阵理论。在此基础上，他们设计了对照因果分解实验，在GPT-2 124M、GPT-2 Medium 355M和Pythia-160M上，使用SVD压缩注意力投影，通过分离子空间选择效应与算子范数缩减效应来解释对秩坍塌的影响，并使用四种秩估计方法进行一致性验证。

Result: SVD压缩在初始化时强烈抑制秩坍塌，但在预训练权重上加速秩坍塌。因果分解显示，SVD选择压缩子空间的方式（而非算子范数缩减）是主要驱动因素：在初始化时解释了约76%的效应，在预训练权重上解释了约83%。在不同压缩比下均未出现明显的对象混叠伪影，四种秩估计方法结果一致。

Conclusion: 该发现对现有的校准感知压缩观点提供了重要修正：SVD压缩对秩坍塌的双向影响并非由算子范数缩减主导，而是由SVD的子空间选择机制主导。这解释了为何校准感知压缩方法优于朴素SVD截断，为未来设计秩坍塌感知的压缩策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Linear algebra provides the framework of concepts (matrix rank, singular value decomposition (SVD), and eigendecomposition) that modern artificial intelligence employs to encode, compress, and propagate information through neural networks. This paper unifies fourteen separate peer-reviewed works analyzing the usage of these techniques in the context of transformer-based foundation model research, focusing on three areas of the topic: derivations and properties of self-attention matrices' output rank, compression methods that purposefully utilize this phenomenon, and the low-rank key-value (KV) cache projection and its semiseparable-matrix duality to linear attention and state-space structured models. We were motivated to conduct this work after observing an open problem in this literature: the interplay of the mentioned compression methods with natural rank collapse of the network. With this paper, we report an original finding that using SVD compression of attention projections actually has the opposite effect on the rank collapse of the network: while it strongly suppresses it at initialization, it accelerates on pretrained models (for GPT-2 124M, GPT-2 Medium 355M, and Pythia-160M) with minimal risk of object aliasing artifacts appearing (verified on all compression ratios) and is consistent across four rank estimation methods. A controlled causal decomposition of the effect in both settings showed that the reason for this behavior can be explained by the choice of the subspace SVD makes when compressing the matrix better than the reduction of the operator norm it achieves, explaining roughly 76% of the effect at initialization and 83% on the pretrained weights, providing a refinement to the calibration-aware compression viewpoint and an explanation of why it outperformed naive SVD truncation.

</details>


### [130] [Robust Conformal Consensus: Multi-Agent LLM-as-a-Judge Interval Evaluation with Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06367)
*Lihui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体LLM-as-a-Judge评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架，通过基于保形预测为多个LLM评委生成的评分构建预测区间，从而获得更稳定可靠的不确定性估计，并在实验中验证了该方法的覆盖保证和评估稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式虽已成为评估自然语言生成的主流方法，但其不确定性尚未得到充分探索，限制了在实际应用中的可靠性。现有保形预测方法通常仅应用于单一LLM评委，忽略了不同LLM评估器所带来的变异性，无法充分刻画评估过程中的不确定性。

Method: 提出一个多智能体LLM-as-a-Judge评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架。具体地，为来自多个LLM的评分分别构建保形预测区间（conformal prediction intervals），并通过综合不同LLM评委产生的区间来获得更稳定和可靠的不确定性估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能够产生具有覆盖保证的有效预测区间，且基于区间的多评委聚合策略能够带来更稳定的评估结果。

Conclusion: 通过将保形预测扩展到多LLM评委场景并整合各评委的不确定性区间，可以有效提升LLM-as-a-Judge评估的可靠性与稳定性，为实际应用中的置信评估提供了新方案。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has emerged as a promising paradigm for evaluating natural language generation. However, the uncertainty associated with such evaluations remains largely unexplored, which limits their reliability in real-world applications. Although conformal prediction offers a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, existing approaches typically apply it to a single LLM judge, overlooking the variability introduced by using different LLM evaluators. In this work, we propose a robust uncertainty estimation framework for multi-agent LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our approach constructs conformal prediction intervals for LLM-based scores from multiple LLMs. By considering intervals from different LLM judges, we obtain more stable and reliable uncertainty estimates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces valid prediction intervals with coverage guarantees, and that interval-based aggregation across multiple judges leads to more stable evaluation outcomes.

</details>


### [131] [Online Surrogate Repair: Decoupling High-Fidelity Feedback from Search Length in Closed-Loop Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07655)
*Xiaotang Feng,Philip Torr,Bruno Andreis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线代理修复（OSR）算法，在闭环AI科学家系统中用稀疏高保真评估来持续更新代理模型，而主体搜索主要依赖廉价的代理反馈，从而在高保真反馈频率与搜索持续时间之间建立新的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环AI科学家可以以极低的边际计算成本生成候选设计，但可靠的反馈（湿实验合成、表征或高保真计算）代价高昂。现有方案要么依赖昂贵的实验室自动化，要么用固定代理模型替代新实验，但后者积累的模型误差会被优化过程放大。

Method: OSR算法采用闭环框架：代理模型在整个较长的智能体搜索过程中通过稀疏的高保真评估不断更新。具体而言，一个采集规则（acquisition rule）从智能体累积的候选设计中选出需要高保真评估的样本，得到的标签用于更新后续回合中的代理模型。文中对比了Q90-UCB和期望改进（EI）等采集策略，并与固定代理、每回合高保真反馈等控制条件进行比较。

Result: 在受控合成环境中，单纯改进全局代理拟合并不一定降低最大遗憾值，而Q90-UCB和EI通过将评估导向决定优化器决策的关键区域，显著降低遗憾。在MADE基准上，每回合都接收高保真反馈的控制组需要6.36–7.23倍的oracle查询才能匹配在线EI（在两种LLM编排器下），在非LLM的Chemeleon+MLIP工作流下则需10.27倍。

Conclusion: 在线代理修复引入了一种介于固定代理操作和每回合高保真反馈之间的第三种反馈模式，实现了高保真评估频率与智能体搜索持续时间的解耦。该方法在显著减少昂贵oracle查询的同时保持优化性能，为闭环AI科学发现提供了更高效的资源配置方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop AI scientists can generate candidate designs at low marginal computational cost, whereas reliable feedback may require wet-lab synthesis, characterization, or high-fidelity computation. Addressing this imbalance through custom laboratory automation remains infrastructure-intensive and costly, while replacing new experiments with a fixed surrogate leaves persistent model errors that can be amplified by optimization. We propose \emph{online surrogate repair} (OSR), a closed-loop algorithm that uses sparse high-fidelity evaluations to update the surrogate throughout a longer agent search conducted primarily with inexpensive surrogate feedback. An acquisition rule selects which designs from the agent's accumulated proposals receive high-fidelity evaluation, and the resulting labels update the surrogate used in subsequent episodes. Across controlled synthetic environments, we demonstrate that improving global surrogate fit does not necessarily reduce maximum regret, whereas Q90-UCB and expected improvement (EI) substantially reduce regret by directing evaluations toward regions that determine the optimizer's decisions. On MADE, controls receiving high-fidelity feedback after every episode require $6.36$--$7.23\times$ more oracle queries to match Online EI under two LLM orchestrators and $10.27\times$ more under the non-LLM Chemeleon+MLIP workflow. Online surrogate repair introduces a novel third feedback regime between fixed-surrogate operation and high-fidelity feedback after every episode, separating the frequency of high-fidelity evaluation from the duration of the agent's search.

</details>


### [132] [Structural Entropy-Driven Graph Diffusion Generation for One-Shot Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06499)
*Shutong Zheng,Lele Fu,Sheng Huang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Chuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIRE是一种单次联邦图学习方法，通过一阶度分布结构熵实现拓扑感知的客户端差异化权重分配，并结合服务器端图扩散模型生成伪图来训练全局GNN，在非IID和图扰动场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单次联邦图学习（one-shot FGL）中，服务器基于压缩信息估计客户端贡献时，传统基于数据量的加权只能反映客户端数据的规模，却忽视了图数据的连通性组织结构（拓扑信息），导致对异构客户端数据差异的建模不充分。

Method: (1) 提出SPIRE框架，采用一阶度分布结构熵作为度质量分布的紧凑描述符，据此推导结构化的客户端权重，引入超越数据量的拓扑感知归纳偏置；(2) 在服务器端使用图扩散模型，以加权的客户端原型为条件生成伪图，同时捕捉语义和结构信息，无需额外的客户端训练；(3) 通过不相交并集融合（disjoint union fusion）组装生成的伪图，用于训练全局图神经网络。

Result: 在七个真实世界图数据集上的大量实验表明，SPIRE在一致性和单次FGL方法上均表现更优，尤其在高度异构（non-IID）和图扰动设置下提升幅度显著。

Conclusion: SPIRE通过结构熵驱动的客户端区分和图扩散伪图生成，有效解决了单次联邦图学习中对拓扑信息利用不足的问题，为异构联邦图场景提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: One-shot federated graph learning (FGL) requires the server to estimate client contributions from highly compressed information, yet conventional volume-based weighting captures the amount of client data while overlooking how its connectivity is organized. In this paper, we propose SPIRE, a Structural Entropy-Driven Graph Diffusion Generation method that introduces topology-aware client differentiation into one-shot FGL. Specifically, we employ first-order degree-distribution structural entropy as a compact descriptor of degree-mass dispersion and use it to derive structural client weights, providing an inductive bias that accounts for differences in graph topology beyond data volume. On the generation side, a graph diffusion model on the server synthesizes pseudographs conditioned on the weighted client prototypes, capturing both semantic and structural information without requiring additional client-side training. The generated pseudographs are then assembled via disjoint union fusion to train a global graph neural network. Extensive experiments on seven real-world graph datasets demonstrate that SPIRE consistently outperforms conventional and one-shot FGL methods, with particularly strong gains under highly heterogeneous (non-IID) and graph-perturbed settings.

</details>


### [133] [Hidden in Plain Sight: The Overlooked Significance of Canonical Elements for Extreme LLM Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06557)
*Hyeondo Jang,Kwanhee Lee,Dongyeop Lee,Namhoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过逐步稀疏化框架、二阶显著性评分和与稀疏度协同的训练策略，证明预训练大语言模型即使在99%的极端非结构化稀疏度下仍能保持强大性能，在LLaMA-2和Qwen-3模型族上超越现有最先进方法，例如LLaMA-2-7B在95%和99%稀疏度下分别达到13.48和19.67的WikiText-2困惑度，并实现3.23倍解码加速与6.21倍内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前普遍认为LLM在激进稀疏化下表现脆弱，通常只能在中等稀疏度下维持可靠性能；尽管近期研究表明LLM对高稀疏比预想更具韧性，但问题仍被视作设计挑战。作者旨在挑战非结构化后训练剪枝的感知极限，重新审视此前在LLM规模下未充分探索的基础剪枝策略。

Method: 提出渐进式稀疏化框架，包含两个关键要素：采用二阶敏感度(saliency)评分指导剪枝，以及将持续训练与稀疏度递进相协调，使得模型在不同稀疏度阶段逐步适应并保持性能。该方法在非结构化后训练剪枝设置下将稀疏度推向99%。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和Qwen-3模型族上，该方法在高达99%稀疏度下均改进了困惑度和下游任务准确率，超越当前SOTA及代表性基线。LLaMA-2-7B在95%与99%稀疏度下分别获得13.48和19.67的WikiText-2困惑度；95%稀疏度下实现3.23倍解码加速和6.21倍内存节省。

Conclusion: 实验表明LLM可以在极端稀疏度下被推向极值同时保持强大性能，突破了以往认为的稀疏度上限，为在该稀疏区间进一步改进稀疏模型提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often considered fragile under aggressive sparsification, and maintaining reliable performance typically requires sticking to moderate sparsity levels. However, recent studies suggest that LLMs are more resilient to high sparsity than previously thought, reframing the problem as a design challenge rather than a fundamental limitation. In this work, we challenge the perceived limits of unstructured post-training LLM pruning by revisiting elementary pruning strategies that have remained relatively underexplored at this scale. Through a progressive sparsification framework with second-order saliency and continued training coordinated with sparsity progression, we show that pretrained LLMs can retain strong performance far beyond commonly studied sparsity regimes. Across LLaMA-2 and Qwen-3 model families, our approach improves perplexity and downstream accuracy up to 99\% sparsity, surpassing both the current state-of-the-art and representative baselines. Precisely, on LLaMA-2-7B, our approach achieves WikiText-2 perplexities of 13.48 and 19.67 at 95\% and 99\% sparsity, respectively, while delivering 3.23$\times$ decoding speedup and 6.21$\times$ memory savings at 95\% sparsity. Taken together, our results show that LLMs can be pushed into extreme sparsity while retaining strong performance, providing a foundation for further improving sparse models in this regime.

</details>


### [134] [Assessing Covariate-Informed Grid Load Forecasting with a Time-Series Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06656)
*Varsha Pendyala,Yiwei Fu,Weizhong Yan,Nurali Virani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了Amazon开发的多通道时间序列基础模型Chronos-2在真实电网负荷预测中的表现。基于ISO New England和ENTSO-E两个真实数据集，将其与任务特定的深度学习模型进行对比，发现Chronos-2在任务特定微调后能取得优秀的短时预测性能，但零样本精度落后于任务特定模型，且误差随预测步长增加而快速增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统日益复杂，负荷预测难度增加。虽然时间序列基础模型在零样本单变量负荷预测上表现良好，但真实场景通常涉及多目标变量和外部变量，且此前对Chronos-2在负荷预测中的应用研究有限，其相对任务特定模型的表现及自适应行为尚未被充分理解。

Method: 选择Chronos-2作为代表性多通道TSFM（支持单变量、多变量和协变量预测），在ISO New England和ENTSO-E两个真实公用事业数据集上，将其与广泛使用的任务特定深度学习模型进行基准比较，系统考察零样本和任务特定微调两种设置下的预测性能。

Result: Chronos-2在任务特定微调下获得了显著收益并展现出强大的短时预测性能，但其零样本预测精度不如任务特定模型，且随着预测步长增加，其预测误差增长更快。

Conclusion: 研究详细刻画了Chronos-2等TSFM在电网负荷预测中的优势与局限性，为如何将预训练TSFM有效适配于运营级负荷预测应用提供了实践见解。

Abstract: Modern power systems are growing increasingly complex as they integrate diverse generation sources to meet rising demand, making accurate load forecasting challenging. Recent advances in time-series foundation models (TSFMs) resulted in promising performance in zero-shot univariate load forecasting tasks. However, real-world load forecasting often involves multiple target variables and requires the integration of exogenous variables, raising important questions about the utility of TSFMs in realistic settings. In this study, we position Chronos-2, a recently developed model by Amazon, as a representative multi-channel TSFM that supports univariate, multivariate, and covariate-informed forecasting, and conduct a systematic investigation of how such models can be used for real-world load forecasting. While prior work has evaluated Chronos-2 on a limited number of energy-related tasks in a zero-shot setting, its performance relative to established task-specific deep learning models and its behavior when adapted using task-specific historical data remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we evaluate Chronos-2 on two real-world utility datasets, ISO New England and ENTSO-E, and benchmark it against widely used task-specific deep learning models. Our results show that Chronos-2 benefits substantially from task-specific fine-tuning and achieves strong short-horizon forecasting performance, but its zero-shot accuracy lags behind task-specific models and its forecasting error grows more rapidly with increasing forecast steps. Overall, this study provides a detailed characterization of the strengths and limitations of TSFMs such as Chronos-2 in grid load forecasting and offers practical insights into how a pretrained TSFM can be effectively adapted for operational load forecasting applications.

</details>


### [135] [Towards Unified Multimodal Graph Foundation Model: A Bridge-Router-Adapter Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06668)
*Sirui Zhang,Yubing Zhou,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Shumeng Li,Wang Luo,Yinlin Zhu,Yujin Gao,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BRAIN统一模型，解决多模态图基础模型中跨范围上下文纠缠和范围无关模态路由两大问题，通过范围条件桥、分层路由器和残差适配器实现范围-模态组合感知的图上下文建模，在九数据集四任务族上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图基础模型存在两个根本局限：(1) 跨范围上下文纠缠——将不同图范围的上下文合并为统一表示，模糊了多模态构建过程中各范围间的区别；(2) 范围无关的模态路由——在固定图范围内路由模态，忽视了模态相关性随邻域范围变化的事实。

Method: BRAIN由三个核心组件构成：(1) 范围条件桥（scope-conditioned Bridge），结合从局部到全局邻域范围的结构信息与不同的模态组合；(2) 分层路由器（hierarchical Router），评估范围与任务之间的相关性，并在每个范围内分别选择组合，使模态效用随图范围变化；(3) 轻量级残差适配器（residual Adapter），将路由后的嵌入专门化用于下游预测。训练采用多图预训练加任务特定适配的策略。

Result: 在九个数据集和四个任务族上的实验表明，BRAIN在节点分类和链接预测任务上相对最强基线最高提升4.73%，在四个图到文本和两个图到图像指标上平均相对提升14.72%。

Conclusion: BRAIN通过将邻域范围与模态组合联合建模，有效解耦了不同范围的上下文并允许模态路由随图范围动态适应，验证了范围和模态组合感知的图上下文建模在多模态图基础学习中的广泛有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal graphs couple node attributes in different modalities, such as text and images, with relational structure, enabling topological structure and cross-modality attributes to be modeled jointly. Multimodal graph foundation models seek unified representations from such data that transfer across different graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing methods exhibit two fundamental limitations. (1) Cross-Scope Context Entanglement. They merge scope-specific graph contexts into a unified representation, obscuring their distinctions during multimodal construction. (2) Scope-Ignorant Modality Routing. They route modalities within a fixed graph scope, overlooking how modality relevance varies across neighborhood ranges. To address these challenges, we propose BRAIN, a unified model that focuses on graph context that combines neighborhood scope with modality composition. BRAIN comprises a scope-conditioned Bridge that combines structural information spanning local-to-global neighborhood scopes with different modality compositions; a hierarchical Router that estimates the relevance between the scope and the task, and selects compositions separately within each scope, allowing modality utility to vary with graph range; and a lightweight residual Adapter that further specializes the routed embedding for downstream prediction. BRAIN is trained through multi-graph pretraining followed by task-specific adaptation. Experiments across nine datasets and four task families demonstrate its broad effectiveness, improving node-classification and link-prediction performance by up to 4.73% relative to the strongest baseline, while achieving an average relative improvement of 14.72% across four graph-to-text and two graph-to-image metrics.

</details>


### [136] [Data Efficient Sample Selection for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06670)
*V Venktesh,Cem levi,Avishek Anand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DearICL是一种新的框架，将ICL示范示例选择建模为子集排序问题，结合非线性代理与可微排序目标及gap-index bandit算法，实现实例级别的子集选择，相比线性bandit基线提升8.08-15.9%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL示例选择方法大多进行静态的任务级子集选择（离线一次性选定），未建模ICL样本与下游LLM性能之间的复杂关系，难以泛化到未见查询。需要一种能动态、实例级建模示例与性能关系的选择框架。

Method: DearICL将示例选择建模为子集排序问题。使用非线性surrogate模型，结合可微排序目标（differentiable sorting objective）融入gap-index bandit算法中。gap-index机制能对"好臂"与"边缘臂"（borderline arms）进行细粒度区分，通过充分采样边缘臂作为辅助目标训练非线性surrogate，支持实例级子集排序。

Result: 在开源LLM的示例选择基准上，DearICL相比强线性bandit基线获得8.08-15.9%的准确率提升，且具有较低的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过将ICL示例选择转化为子集排序问题并引入gap-index bandit与非线性可微排序代理，DearICL有效动态适应未见查询，优于现有线性bandit方法，为ICL数据高效选择提供了新思路。

Abstract: The In-context learning (ICL) paradigm aids large language models (LLMs) to adapt to new tasks without need for fine-tuning. However, selecting an optimal combination of demonstration examples from a large pool of example subsets is a challenging problem. Existing approaches for selection do not model the complex relationship between ICL samples and downstream LLM performance. They typically perform static task-level selection, choosing subsets once offline, which can fail to generalize to unseen queries. We introduce DearICL (Data Efficient Algorithm for Ranking) ICL samples, a new framework that models demonstration example selection as a subset ranking problem. DearICL employs a non-linear surrogate employing a differentiable sorting objective within a gap-index bandit algorithm. The gap-index based approach enables fine-grained separation of good arms and borderline arms, which is used as an auxiliary objective to train the non-linear surrogate through sufficient sampling of borderline arms, supporting instance-level subset ranking. On exemplar selection benchmarks with open-source LLMs, DearICL achieves 8.08-15.9% accuracy gains over strong linear bandit baselines, with low sample complexity. Code and data: https://github.com/VenkteshV/DearICL.

</details>


### [137] [DrugReason: Dynamic Multi-View Reasoning over Knowledge Graph and Language Evidence for Drug Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06779)
*Zijie Liu,Hongxuan Li,Zhen Tan,Jinhao Duan,Baixiang Huang,Zunpeng Liu,Kai Shu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DrugReason是一个多视角推理框架，通过整合知识图谱（KG）的实体关系推理与大语言模型（LLM）生成的机制性推理，并采用条件路由机制将不同推理路径分配到专门专家模块，同时利用跨专家蒸馏目标实现知识共享，从而在药物重定位任务中提供可解释且可靠的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物重定位方法存在两大局限：基于知识图谱的方法受限于已观测的图结构，无法捕获未知关系；基于大语言模型的方法虽然能生成灵活的机制性理由，但缺乏事实支撑，容易产生幻觉。单独使用任一方法都难以可靠地预测药物-疾病对之间的真实治疗关系，因此需要一种能够结合两者优势的推理框架。

Method: DrugReason框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 多视角推理，同时利用KG的结构化多跳推理和LLM的机制性推断；(2) 自适应条件路由，根据查询上下文将不同推理路径动态分配到专门的专家模块；(3) 跨专家蒸馏目标，在保持专家专业化能力的同时实现知识共享。

Result: 在PharmaDB、DDInter和DrugBank三个数据集上的实验表明，DrugReason相比强单视角推理基线在平均性能上有所提升，并且与基于图的方法相比取得了有竞争力或更优的结果，同时提供基于路由的可解释预测。

Conclusion: DrugReason通过将知识图谱的扎实推理与大语言模型的机制性推断有机结合，有效解决了单一视角方法的局限性，验证了多视角融合推理在药物重定位任务中的有效性和可解释性优势。

Abstract: Drug repurposing aims to identify new therapeutic uses for existing compounds and, compared with de novo drug discovery, offers a faster and more cost-effective path to clinical translation. However, the space of candidate drug-disease pairs is enormous and their underlying relationships often depend on complex multi-hop biological mechanisms, making it difficult to reliably predict which pairs represent true therapeutic relationships. Existing approaches tackle this from two directions: knowledge graph-based methods organize curated biomedical evidence into structured relational networks for grounded multi-hop reasoning, while LLM-based methods leverage pretrained knowledge to generate flexible mechanistic rationales. Yet neither is sufficient alone - KGs are confined to observed graph structure while LLMs lack factual grounding and risk hallucination. To address this gap, we propose DrugReason, a multi-view reasoning framework that integrates grounded KG reasoning with LLM-generated mechanistic inference for drug repurposing. DrugReason adaptively routes diverse reasoning paths to specialized experts conditioned on the query context, while a cross-expert distillation objective enables knowledge sharing without sacrificing expert specialization. Experiments on PharmaDB, DDInter, and DrugBank show that DrugReason improves average performance over strong single-view reasoning baselines and achieves competitive or superior results compared with graph-based alternatives, while providing interpretable routing-based predictions.

</details>


### [138] [When Retain Constraints Conflict: Mitigating Forget-Retain Interference in Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06786)
*Zijie Liu,Jinhao Duan,Bingqi Shang,Xinming An,Sijia Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对表格数据的模型遗忘方法CAU（Conflict-Aware Unlearning），解决了序列化表格数据中由于共享属性槽导致的"遗忘-保留重叠"问题，通过放宽与遗忘集最冲突的保留行约束来减少干扰，在临床和非医疗表格任务上更接近重训练基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘在表格数据上的行为尚未被充分探索，而表格预测在高风险领域广泛应用且正越来越多地通过记录序列化和模式感知提示适配到大语言模型。作者识别出一个关键挑战：在序列化表格数据中，记录共享固定的列名/值槽位、相似的属性范围和共同的输出空间，导致遗忘行附近可能存在依赖相同高信号属性的保留行，使得保留目标与遗忘更新目标相互对立。

Method: 提出Conflict-Aware Unlearning (CAU)，一种模式感知的遗忘方法，通过放宽与遗忘集冲突最大的保留行上的保真约束来减少遗忘-保留间的干扰。在样本级和特征级遗忘场景下，对临床和非医疗表格任务进行评估。

Result: CAU在样本级和特征级遗忘任务上均更接近重训练oracle的效果，同时保持了预测效用和保留区域行为。

Conclusion: 可靠的表格大语言模型遗忘不仅取决于遗忘目标的设定，还取决于保留约束的构建方式，即需要意识到模式诱导的遗忘-保留重叠并相应调整约束。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of designated training data while preserving model utility, but its behavior on tabular data remains underexplored. This gap is important because tabular prediction is widely used in high-stakes domains and is increasingly adapted to language models through record serialization and schema-aware prompting. We identify a key challenge that distinguishes tabular unlearning from unlearning in free-form text or other modalities: schema-induced forget-retain overlap. In serialized tabular data, records share fixed column-name/value slots, similar attribute ranges, and common output spaces. Consequently, a forget row may have nearby retain rows that rely on the same high-signal attributes, causing retain preservation to oppose the update required for forgetting. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose Conflict-Aware Unlearning (CAU), a schema-aware approach that reduces forget-retain interference by relaxing preservation constraints on retained rows that most conflict with the forget set. Across sample-level and feature-level unlearning on clinical and non-medical tabular tasks, CAU more closely matches a retraining oracle while maintaining predictive utility and retain-region behavior. Our results show that reliable tabular LLM unlearning depends not only on the forgetting objective, but also on how retain constraints are constructed.

</details>


### [139] [Train Smarter, Not Harder: Switching Signal-Guided Training in Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06806)
*Nagham Omar,Maya Rozenshtein,Evgeny Mishlyakov,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HybridAL，一种自适应的主动学习训练调度策略，通过监控在线稳定化信号，在模型轨迹稳定后从完全重训练切换到微调，在保持宏F1不劣于基线的同时节省多达49%的重训练时间，并恢复重训练的大部分校准优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习中，每轮是否从零重训练或从上一检查点微调（训练策略）是一个被忽视的决策变量。作者发现该选择具有可利用的结构：早期轮次重训练能显著重塑标注分布，而模型轨迹稳定后微调更安全，因此需要一个自适应的切换机制来平衡效率和性能。

Method: 提出HybridAL，一种自适应训练调度策略。它通过监控一个在线稳定化信号来判断模型何时从重训练切换到微调。论文使用两种互补信号：光谱指数变化Δα（基于权重的信号）和精度变化ΔAcc（基于验证集的信号），它们分别覆盖了时间-校准权衡的不同位置。混合调度仅在持续稳定后才进行切换。

Result: 在三个编码器主干和六个文本分类任务（每个任务五种随机种子）上，HybridAL的端点宏F1在0.010的边际内不低于重训练和微调基线，节省高达49%的重训练时间，并以负对数似然（NLL）衡量的校准优势恢复了重训练的大部分优势。与预提交轮次切换的调度相比，HybridAL在中度额外成本下获得更低的NLL，表明轨迹依赖的切换比固定早期切换提供更强的时间-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 轨迹依赖的自适应切换策略（HybridAL）相比固定早期切换具有更好的时间-校准权衡，能够在几乎不损失分类性能的前提下大幅节省训练时间，同时保留重训练的校准优势，为主动学习中的训练策略选择提供了新的视角和实用方案。

Abstract: Training strategy, namely whether to retrain from scratch or fine-tune from the previous checkpoint, is an overlooked decision variable in active learning. We show that this choice has exploitable structure: retraining is most useful in early rounds, when each batch can substantially reshape the labeled distribution, while fine-tuning becomes safer once the model trajectory stabilizes. We propose HybridAL, an adaptive training schedule that monitors an online stabilization signal and switches from retraining to fine-tuning after sustained stabilization. Two complementary signals, spectral exponent change $Δα$ (weight-based) and accuracy change $Δ$Acc (validation-based), span different points on the time-calibration trade-off. Across three encoder backbones and six text-classification tasks (five seeds each), HybridAL keeps endpoint macro-F1 non-inferior to retraining and fine-tuning at a 0.010 margin, saves up to 49% of retraining time, and recovers a substantial fraction of retraining's calibration advantage as measured by negative log-likelihood (NLL). Compared with schedules that switch at a pre-committed round, HybridAL obtains lower NLL at moderate additional cost, showing that trajectory-dependent switching provides a stronger time-calibration trade-off than fixed early switching.

</details>


### [140] [Constrained Bayesian Optimization for Hierarchical Federated Learning in IoT Networks for Plant Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06830)
*Athanasios Papanikolaou,Athanasios Tziouvaras,Apostolos Xenakis,Periklis Chatzimisios,Shameem A. Puthiya Parambath,George Floros,Enrica Zereik,Ivan Petrovic,Fabio Bonsignorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于约束贝叶斯优化的框架，用于高效配置资源受限IoT环境中的分层联邦学习（HFL）部署，在智慧农业的植物病害分类任务上，仅探索11.11%的搜索空间即可找到接近穷举搜索最优解（平均最优性差距仅0.056%）的配置方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的IoT环境中部署HFL需要仔细配置以平衡预测性能、能耗和执行时间，特别是在智慧农业中，分布式IoT设备需在有限计算和通信资源下支持植物病害分类。现有方法缺乏系统性配置优化手段。

Method: 提出一个约束贝叶斯优化框架，联合探索深度学习骨干网络架构、聚合策略和通信轮数，联邦规模根据农业部署的空间覆盖需求确定；采用加权目标函数捕捉能耗、执行时间和预测性能之间的用户定义权衡，通过显式约束确保满足部署特定的资源和精度要求。

Result: 在基于IoT的植物病害分类任务上进行评估，涵盖多种深度学习架构、联邦聚合策略和通信轮数设置。在30次独立优化运行中，该方法仅探索11.11%的搜索空间，持续识别出与穷举搜索最优解差距在1%以内的方案，平均最优性差距仅为0.056%。

Conclusion: 该约束贝叶斯优化框架能够高效地在资源受限IoT环境中为HFL部署找到接近最优的配置，在保证性能的同时大幅减少搜索开销，对智慧农业等实际应用场景具有实用性。

Abstract: The deployment of Hierarchical Federated Learning (HFL) in resource-constrained Internet of Things (IoT) environments requires careful configuration to balance predictive performance with energy consumption and execution time. This challenge is particularly relevant to smart agriculture, where distributed IoT devices can support automated plant disease classification while operating under limited computational and communication resources. This paper presents a constrained Bayesian Optimization framework for the efficient configuration of HFL deployments. The proposed approach jointly explores the deep learning backbone architecture, aggregation strategy, and number of communication rounds, while the federation size is determined according to the spatial coverage requirements of the agricultural deployment. A weighted objective function captures user-defined trade-offs among energy consumption, execution time, and predictive performance, while explicit constraints ensure compliance with deployment-specific resource and accuracy requirements. The framework is evaluated on an IoT-based plant disease classification task considering multiple deep learning architectures, federated aggregation strategies, and communication-round settings. Experimental results across 30 independent optimization runs show that the proposed approach explores only 11.11% of the search space, while consistently identifying solutions within 1% of the exhaustive-search optimum, with a mean optimality gap of only 0.056%.

</details>


### [141] [HealthLoopQA: A Context-Aware Question Answering Benchmark for Interpreting Wearable Monitoring Data in Diabetes Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06976)
*Yuchen Niu,Yanan Ma,Srinivasan Nandakumar,Maolin Chen,Viktor Schlegel,Kexin Wei,Ling Cheng,Anna Bird,Anil Anthony Bharath,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HealthLoopQA是一个用于评估大语言模型在连续糖尿病监测数据上进行长时序推理能力的综合诊断基准，包含127个任务、超过1500个QA实例，并引入故障注入模拟测试台来评估安全感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗可穿戴设备问答基准主要聚焦短期分类或统计摘要，忽略了真实部署中固有的长期模式、治疗与行为背景以及潜在系统故障，无法全面评估LLM在多样化监测任务中的推理能力和分析严谨性。

Method: 构建包含11种原子推理能力的分类体系，基于30天视野的连续血糖监测数据设计127个任务和1500多个QA实例，涵盖流程挖掘、异常检测和预测；并补充故障注入式仿真测试台模拟设备故障和网络物理攻击，生成生理上合理的危险场景。

Result: 对当前最先进的LLM在提示工程和智能体框架下的评估显示，它们在复杂时间模式挖掘上存在严重局限；同时识别出长上下文提示下\"上下文惰性\"（In-context Laziness）的普遍现象。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在长视野医疗推理中部署面临的关键开放挑战，强调亟需解决长上下文下的惰性推理与复杂时序模式挖掘能力不足等问题。

Abstract: As medical wearables become integrated into daily chronic disease care, effectively interpreting longitudinal monitoring data is essential for patients and clinicians to understand health trends, detect safety-critical events, and make informed decisions. While large language models (LLMs) show promise for transforming this streaming physiological data into personalized health insights, evaluating their reasoning capability and analytical rigor in diverse monitoring tasks remains a fundamental challenge. Existing medical wearable question answering (QA) benchmarks primarily assess short-horizon classification or statistical summaries, largely ignoring the long-term patterns, therapeutic and behavioural contexts, and potential system failures inherent in real-world deployments. To address this, we introduce HealthLoopQA, a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over continuous diabetes monitoring data. Grounded in a novel taxonomy of eleven atomic reasoning abilities, HealthLoopQA comprises 127 tasks and over 1,500 QA instances spanning process mining, anomaly detection, and prediction over 30-day horizons. To systematically evaluate safety awareness, we complement real-world datasets with a fault-injected simulation testbed modeling diverse device malfunctions and cyber-physical attacks to generate physiologically plausible hazard scenarios. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs across prompting and agentic frameworks reveals severe limitations in complex temporal pattern mining. Furthermore, we identify a broader phenomenon of In-context Laziness under long-context prompting, highlighting critical open challenges in deploying LLMs for rigorous long-horizon medical reasoning.

</details>


### [142] [Efficient Learning and Symmetry Discovery under Exact Invariances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07031)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Behrooz Tahmasebi,Patrick Jaillet,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了群不变性学习中精确不变性学习的计算难题，提出了统一适用于有限和无限群的多项式时间算法，并解决了未知对称性（对称性发现）场景，可多项式时间内恢复底层对称性并达到最优样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群不变性学习是科学和几何学习问题的核心，但其计算基础尚不清楚。此前研究仅在有限且已知群的情况下能多项式时间内强制精确不变性，而无限群和未知对称性的情况仍悬而未决。

Method: 针对已知对称性场景，提出统一适用于有限和无限群的首个多项式时间精确不变性学习算法，其运行时间与数据维度和样本量成多项式关系且与群无关。针对未知对称性场景，聚焦有限群的子群格，利用随机Cayley图和扩张图（expander）理论工具，设计算法从数据中识别精确对称性并用于学习。

Result: 已知对称性场景下，算法运行时间与数据维度及样本量多项式相关、与群无关，并具有强泛化保证。未知对称性场景下，算法在有限维特征空间回归问题上可证明地恢复底层对称性，样本复杂度与已知对称性情形下的极小极大最优相匹配，运行时间为数据和样本量的多项式时间。

Conclusion: 本文从计算角度解释了不变/等变方法在几何机器学习中取得经验成功的原理，部分回答了文献中的开放问题。所引入的随机Cayley图与扩张图工具可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Learning with group invariances is central to many scientific and geometric learning problems, yet its computational foundations remain poorly understood. Even for classical supervised regression settings, it has been unclear whether one can efficiently compute a regression function that is exactly invariant to a given group action. Recent work showed that exact invariance can be enforced in polynomial time when the underlying group is finite and known, but left open the cases of infinite groups and unknown symmetries. In this paper, we resolve both challenges. First, we present the first polynomial-time algorithm for learning with exact group invariances that applies uniformly to finite and infinite groups. The runtime is polynomial in the data dimension and sample size, and independent of the group, while achieving strong generalization guarantees. This provides a computational explanation for the empirical success of invariant and equivariant methods in geometric machine learning and partially answers a recent open question in the literature. Second, we study learning in the symmetry discovery setting, where the invariance group is unknown. Focusing on the subgroup lattice of a finite group, we show that exact symmetries can be identified from data and exploited for learning in polynomial time. For regression over finite-dimensional feature spaces, our algorithm provably recovers the underlying symmetry, matches the minimax-optimal sample complexity of the known-symmetry setting, and runs in time polynomial in the data dimension and sample size. Our analysis relies on tools from random Cayley graphs and expander theory, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [143] [AI and TCAD for Inverse Design and Defect Discovery: From Simple Machine Learning to LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07046)
*Hiu Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用TCAD（技术计算机辅助设计）仿真数据驱动机器学习（ML），实现仿真增强型ML在半导体器件设计与缺陷发现中的应用。通过自编码器、噪声工程等手段，无需深厚领域专长即可训练出达到器件工程师水平的模型，并可无缝迁移到实验数据。论文展示了多个应用案例（如PiN二极管厚度变化逆向工程、Ga2O3肖特基二极管参数提取、FinFET IV/CV预测等），并展望了利用大语言模型（LLMs）和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）实现自动化器件设计和缺陷发现的愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在半导体器件设计和缺陷发现领域的应用仍受限，主要原因是数据稀缺和维度灾难。作者旨在利用TCAD仿真生成精确数据来弥补实验数据的不足，从而将机器学习引入半导体器件工程，使缺乏领域专长的人也能构建达到器件工程师水平的模型。

Method: 采用TCAD仿真生成精确的合成数据作为ML训练集（仿真增强型ML）；使用自编码器架构学习潜在物理特征；引入噪声工程增强模型对实验数据迁移的鲁棒性；通过多个实际器件案例验证方法有效性，包括仅用无损电气数据进行逆向工程、TCAD参数自动校准等；最后探讨LLM/MLLM在TCAD命令文件生成和自动化器件设计中的应用。

Result: 证明了在最少领域专长条件下，利用TCAD数据训练的机器学习模型可以达到与器件工程师相当的性能；自编码器和噪声工程能有效学习潜在物理并迁移到实验数据；成功实现了PiN二极管层厚变化、Ga2O3肖特基二极管掺杂与阳极功函数变化、逆变器晶体管接触电阻的逆向工程，以及FinFET IV/CV预测、晶体管图像到IV曲线映射和Ga2O3 TCAD参数自动校准等任务。

Conclusion: TCAD增强型机器学习是克服半导体数据稀缺问题的有效途径，可以显著降低器件建模和缺陷发现的门槛。但要充分释放AI在自动化器件设计和缺陷发现中的潜力，需要进一步发展LLM和MLLM技术，使其能够处理TCAD命令生成、多模态数据融合等更为复杂的任务。

Abstract: AI has revolutionized various engineering domains, but its impact on semiconductor device design and defect discovery is still limited, due to limited data and the curse of dimensionality. In this paper, we will discuss our work on using the Technology Computer-Aided-Design (TCAD) to generate precise data needed for machine learning (ML) to enable simulation-augmented ML. We demonstrate that with minimal domain expertise, it is possible to create a machine that performs as well as a device engineer on a specific task. We will show that auto-encoder-based machine learning models and noise engineering applied to TCAD data are effective at learning latent physics, and that the models can be seamlessly applied to experimental data. We will demonstrate how to build a device-engineer-level model step by step through various examples, including using only non-destructive electrical data to inverse-engineer the PiN diode layer thickness variations, the Ga2O3 Schottky diode doping and anode workfunction variations, and the transistor contact resistance in an inverter. Examples also include the generation of a FinFET IV/CV prediction model, the mapping between transistor images and IV curves, and the automatic calibration of TCAD parameters for a Ga2O3 Schottky diode, which can only be handled well by experienced TCAD engineers. Finally, to fully realize the potential of AI, large language models (LLMs) and multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) are believed to be necessary. We will discuss the application of LLMs to TCAD command file creation and our vision for MLLMs in automated device design and defect discovery.

</details>


### [144] [The Oversight Gap: What LLM Safety Monitors Miss, and Why It Is Not Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07162)
*Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用TV距离（总变差距离）将2-安全超属性的二值不可判定性转化为可量化的分级检测边界，定义了监督差距（oversight gap）。在闭式TV的泄漏族上测试了九个LLM监控器，发现它们在信息与程序的配合上有系统性短板：单迹监控在TV增长时捕获信号不足；缺乏比较程序导致性能远低于执行第二趟运行的规则；缩小投影虽能跟踪k-重放曲线但会遗漏离通道泄漏。最终指出超属性基准的构念效度应机械化证明而非由模型审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨租户非干扰、沙袋效应、评估意识等安全属性是2-安全超属性，需要两条执行迹同时见证，因此单个迹无法判定它们，呈现出二值不可判定性。作者希望将这种二元不可能性转化为可测量的梯度，从而量化LLM监控器的检测能力边界与缺陷来源。

Method: 用总变差距离TV(P0,P1)推导单迹监控器平衡精度的紧界1/2+1/2·TV(P0,P1)，将不可判定转换为分级检测边界，定义监督差距为监控器与边界的差距。在闭式TV的泄漏族上测试九个LLM监控器，并通过2×2因子实验分离"信息"（是否有第二条运行）与"程序"（是否有比较规则）的作用；另在非确定性下研究k-重放曲线与投影前缘。

Result: 九个LLM监控器在TV=0时最优，但TV增长时仅捕获少量信号；TV=1时20行成员检查代码得100%，而LLM平均仅60.9%。命名检查目标可关闭61%的监督差距且不影响TV=0对照。2×2因子实验显示"想象的第二趟运行"止于偶然（50.4%），执行的真实第二趟运行达90.0%，存储预言机无比较程序仅68.2%。窄投影遗漏98.6%离通道泄漏，宽投影对75.7%干净流量误报；可达精度随1/(qm)衰减。

Conclusion: 信息（具有两条运行的迹）与程序（可执行的比较规则）分别必要且均不能被能力替代。LLM审计自身基准存在方向性偏差（p=2.7×10^-5），使三项发现失效；超属性基准的构念效度必须机械化证明，而非交由模型审计。

Abstract: Several properties safety monitors are asked to certify, among them cross-tenant noninterference, sandbagging and evaluation awareness, are 2-safety hyperproperties, witnessed only by two executions. The standard consequence is a binary impossibility: one trace cannot decide them. We replace the binary with a measurement. A tight bound puts the balanced accuracy of any single-trace monitor at $\tfrac12+\tfrac12\,TV(P_0,P_1)$, turning undecidability into a graded detectability frontier and defining an oversight gap: a monitor's shortfall below it. On a leak family with closed-form $TV$, nine LLM monitors are optimal at $TV=0$ but capture little signal as $TV$ grows; at $TV=1$, where a 20-line membership check scores $100\%$, they average $60.9\%$. That shortfall is mostly not capability: naming what to check closes $61\%$ of it while leaving the $TV=0$ control at chance. The same split runs through a $2{\times}2$ factorial: an imagined second run leaves monitors at chance ($50.4\%$) while the same rule on an executed second run reaches $90.0\%$, and a stored oracle without a comparison procedure yields only $68.2\%$. Information and procedure are each necessary and neither is capability. Under nondeterminism, replay tracks a closed-form $k$-replay curve only under the right projection, and a projection frontier shows the resulting dilemma is forced: narrow misses $98.6\%$ of off-channel leaks, broad flags $75.7\%$ of clean traffic, and attainable accuracy decays like $1/(qm)$ in the benign-variation rate and the channel count. Finally, two frontier LLM judges certified an earlier version of our own benchmark as sound while a sign test found a directional bias ($p=2.7\times10^{-5}$) that invalidated three of our findings. Construction validity for hyperproperty benchmarks should be proved mechanically, not audited by models.

</details>


### [145] [Dense Structural Compression of Transformers via Gauge-Correct Channel Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07264)
*Jed A. Duersch,Naïm Es-Sebbani,Nathanaël Haas,Zied Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GaugeLasso方法，在训练过程中自适应压缩Transformer的结构复杂度，通过通道级惩罚将张量切片驱至零并物理移除，在保持稠密张量和GPU吞吐的同时大幅降低推理能耗。在多项式长除法、字符级语言建模和掩码自编码任务上验证了显著的计算压缩效果和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer推理的每token能耗主要由稠密矩阵乘法的FMA运算和内存流量驱动，这决定了部署成本和碳足迹。现有方法要么在推理时进行后验剪枝（受限于已训练的结构，无法发现更高效架构），要么无法在保持稠密张量以获得高GPU吞吐的同时减少计算。需要一种从第一性原理出发的训练期结构性压缩方法。

Method: 作者提出GaugeLasso方法：1) 对操作符各通道分量施加加性对称group-lasso惩罚，而非直接惩罚通道范数（后者被gauge自由度破坏稳定性）；2) 通过平衡分析，在网络收敛到gauge平衡时恢复乘积范数的单调函数属性；3) 基于均衡分析进行逐通道校准，正确地抑制推理效用每单位计算性能不佳的通道，驱动整个张量切片归零并物理移除，保持密度与网络功能。

Result: 在多项式长除法（模F31）上实现148-255倍计算压缩且保持完美精度；在字符级语言建模上，压缩后模型在相同FMA下超越手工设计基线；在掩码自编码任务中揭示过配置和饱和的轴，指导更优的第二轮设计；训练过程单调加速；后验剪枝无法达到相同结构，表明持续压力对发现高效模型至关重要。

Conclusion: 训练期持续的结构性压力（而非后验剪枝）是发现高效Transformer结构的关键。发现的架构在统计任务上可通过重训练恢复基线质量，但在精确算法任务（如长除法）上重训练失败，暗示算法任务中某些压缩后的结构对训练路径具有独特依赖性。

Abstract: Inference energy per token drives the cost and carbon footprint of deployed transformers. It is dominated by dense matrix products that incur fused multiply-accumulate (FMA) operations and memory traffic. To reduce these computations while retaining dense tensors for high GPU throughput, we develop a methodology from first principles to adapt structural complexity during training to maximize inference utility per unit compute. Channel penalties drive entire tensor slices to zero to enable physical removal while preserving density and the network function.
  The natural approach, penalizing the norm of operator components acting through each channel, is provably destabilized by gauge freedom. We resolve this pathology with GaugeLasso: additive symmetric group-lasso penalties that recover a monotone function of product-norms when the network converges to gauge balance. Our equilibrium analysis enables per-channel calibration to correctly suppress slices that under-perform in inference utility per unit compute.
  Under adaptive pressure, the network reorganizes into depth-dependent structural profiles that can be far smaller than the architecture required to learn the task. On polynomial long division over $\mathbb{F}_{31}$, compute compresses from 148 to 255 times with perfect accuracy. On character-level language modeling, compressed models outperform the hand-designed baseline at equal FMA. On masked autoencoding, a compression trial exposes which axes were over-provisioned and which saturated, guiding a better second design. Compaction also accelerates training monotonically as the model progresses. Post-hoc pruning with the same utility ranking cannot reach these structures, showing that sustained pressure is central to discovery of efficient models. Retraining a discovered architecture recovers baseline quality on our statistical tasks, but fails on our exact algorithmic task.

</details>


### [146] [TASTE: Throughput-Aware Batch Size Tuning for On-Device Edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07444)
*Avik Bhatnagar,Federico Nicolas Peccia,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的批大小（batch size）调优技术，用于在资源受限的边缘设备上最大化深度学习训练吞吐量，同时保持模型精度，并在标准监督学习和在线持续学习两种范式下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护AI的发展推动了模型适配与个性化向设备端学习转移，即在边缘硬件上使用本地数据直接微调深度学习模型。然而这需要在资源受限硬件上优化训练过程，在最大化吞吐量的同时保持预测精度，因此亟需有效的超参数调优策略。

Method: 提出一种高效的基于贝叶斯优化的批大小调优方法，用于最大化设备端硬件吞吐量；并结合梯度累积与线性学习率缩放策略。在标准监督学习（SL）与在线持续学习（CL）两种范式下评估该超参数对学习动态的影响。

Result: 实验表明，在各种边缘设备上存在吞吐量上限，超过该上限后增大批大小不再带来吞吐量增益。所提调优方法能识别最优批大小，在Raspberry Pi 4等平台上相比最大批大小实现高达2倍的训练吞吐量提升，且不损害模型精度。在持续学习范式中，最优批大小维持了稳定性-可塑性平衡，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，同时最大化边缘硬件计算效率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯优化调优批大小是边缘设备训练中一种高效且有效的策略，兼顾吞吐量优化与学习性能保持；最优批大小在持续学习中对维护稳定性-可塑性平衡同样至关重要。

Abstract: The rise of privacy-preserving artificial intelligence (AI) has shifted the focus of model adaptation and personalization towards on-device learning, where deep learning models are finetuned directly on edge hardware using local user data. However, this shift requires optimization of deep learning training on resource-constrained hardware to maximize throughput while maintaining predictive accuracy. This paper introduces a novel technique for on-device model training that incorporates an efficient Bayesian optimization-based batch size tuning approach to maximize hardware throughput. To evaluate the impact of this hyperparameter on the learning dynamics, we investigated two distinct paradigms: standard supervised learning (SL) and online continual learning (CL). Experimental results across various edge devices demonstrate a throughput ceiling, beyond which increasing the batch size yields no additional throughput gains. The proposed tuning approach identifies the optimal batch size, which, when combined with gradient accumulation and linear learning rate scaling, achieves up to a 2X increase in training throughput on platforms such as Raspberry Pi 4 compared to maximum batch sizes, without compromising model accuracy. Furthermore, in the CL paradigm, we demonstrate that optimal batch sizes maintain the stability-plasticity balance required for incremental learning, effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting while maximizing computational efficiency on edge-hardware.

</details>


### [147] [MpSub: A Momentum $p$-Dimensional Subspace Trust-Region Method for Derivative-Free Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07666)
*Yuyang Wang,Haoyu Yao,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动量p维子空间信赖域方法(MpSub)，一种无需学习率调参的零阶优化器，用于大规模语言模型的全参数微调。MpSub在每次迭代中于p维子空间内搜索：一个方向保留历史动量，其余方向随机采样探索，通过中心差分估计子空间梯度并采用自适应信赖域半径消除学习率。在OPT-125M和OPT-350M上的CommitmentBank实验中，MpSub在相同预设参数下达到与调参后的MeZO相当的精度，且无需任何学习率搜索。理论上证明了在光滑确定性目标下，有限差分误差有界、子空间捕获的梯度能量可量化，且梯度范数几乎必然收敛到零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调大型语言模型因反向传播存储激活和梯度导致内存开销巨大，零阶优化虽可通过损失评估估计更新方向来规避此问题，但现有零阶方法需要针对每个模型和任务调优敏感的学习率，这既耗时又缺乏鲁棒性。本文旨在设计一种无需手动调学习率的零阶优化方法。

Method: 提出动量p维子空间信赖域方法(MpSub)。核心包括：(1) 每轮迭代在p维子空间中搜索，其中一个方向是最近的已接受动量步方向，其余p-1个方向通过新鲜随机采样生成；(2) 用中心差分估计子空间梯度；(3) 基于线性信赖域模型计算试验步；(4) 信赖域半径根据预测损失减少与实际损失减少的吻合度自适应调整，从而消除学习率参数；(5) 大模型微调时，同一迭代内的评估共享一个minibatch，方向通过种子原地再生，仅使用前向传播。

Result: 在8,400次训练目标前向传播的匹配预算下，微调OPT-125M和OPT-350M于CommitmentBank数据集，MpSub在两个模型规模下均使用相同的预设参数，获得三种子平均测试准确率0.673和0.690，与调优后的MeZO(0.685)相当，且无需任何学习率搜索。理论上证明了在光滑确定性目标和非正交高斯方向下，有限差分误差有界、子空间梯度能量可量化，且在安全半径更新下梯度范数几乎必然收敛到零。

Conclusion: MpSub通过将动量维度与随机探索维度相结合的子空间搜索架构和自适应信赖域策略，成功实现了无需学习率调参的零阶LLM微调，在匹配前向传播预算下达到与最优调参的MeZO相当甚至更优的准确率，同时提供了严格的收敛性理论保证。

Abstract: Full-parameter fine-tuning of large language models has substantial memory costs because backpropagation stores activations and gradients. Zeroth-order optimization avoids this by estimating update directions from loss evaluations, but existing methods require tuning a sensitive learning rate for each model and task. We propose the momentum $p$-dimensional subspace trust-region method (MpSub). At each iteration, MpSub searches within a $p$-dimensional subspace: one direction preserves historical momentum from the most recent accepted step, while the remaining directions explore via fresh random sampling. The subspace gradient is estimated by central differences, a trial step is computed from a linear trust-region model, and the trust-region radius adapts according to the agreement between predicted and observed loss reduction, eliminating the learning rate. For LLM fine-tuning, evaluations within an iteration share a minibatch, and directions are regenerated in place from seeds, using forward passes alone. For smooth deterministic objectives under unorthogonalized Gaussian directions, we bound the finite-difference error, quantify gradient energy captured by the subspace, and prove that $\lim_{k\to\infty} \|\nabla f(x_k)\|_2 = 0$ almost surely under a safeguarded radius update. Under a matched budget of 8,400 training-objective forward passes, we fine-tune OPT-125M and OPT-350M on CommitmentBank. With the same preset parameters at both model sizes, MpSub attains mean test accuracies of 0.673 and 0.690 over three seeds, matching tuned MeZO (0.685) without any learning-rate search.

</details>


### [148] [Emergent Charging Coordination in Electric Delivery Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07689)
*Javier Vales-Alonso,Juan J. Alcaraz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种纯局部控制的电动配送车队中途充电协调范式：每辆车运行相同策略，仅依据自身时间预算和广播的站点占用率独立决策（何时、何地、充多少电），无需中央调度或预约机制。在20个真实OpenStreetMap城市的冻结场景中评估（其中4个用于训练、16个零样本未见城市），神经进化（NEAT）与策略梯度（PPO）智能体分别完成96.8%和98.6%的班次（对比Oracle的99.5%和朴素贪心规则的73%），并将排队等待从约45分钟降至2分钟以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动配送车队的中途充电决策相互耦合——多个车辆选择同一站点会导致排队，而现有解决方案（中央调度、预计算时刻表、预约机制）需要充电基础设施并不支持的复杂机制。本文旨在在不依赖这些重型机制的前提下，仅通过纯局部控制实现接近全局最优的协调效果。

Method: 采用同构多智能体强化学习框架：每辆车部署相同的策略网络，输入为自身时间预算（finish on time的安全约束）与广播的站点占用率，输出为充电决策（时间、地点、充电量）。训练方法对比了神经进化（NEAT）与近端策略优化（PPO），在4个城市训练后零样本迁移至全部20个城市（含16个未见城市）。同时在重度占用城市额外测试了基于车辆紧迫性的阈值启发式作为基线。

Result: NEAT与PPO分别在20个城市中完成96.8%和98.6%的班次，显著优于朴素贪心规则（最近站点/低电量触发）的73%；PPO控制器在需求或车辆特性偏离训练域时表现出更强的鲁棒性；仅依赖车辆紧迫性的阈值启发式在拥挤城市仅约80%；训练后智能体自发学会了部分充电与短时机会充电策略，并绕开繁忙站点，将排队等待从约45分钟降至2分钟以下。

Conclusion: 该局部控制范式通过平衡自身紧迫性与公共占用率信息，在最小实现成本（无需中央调度或基础设施改造）下达到接近全知Oracle（99.5%）的性能水平，验证了学习型自主协调作为电动运输中途充电分布式解决方案的可行性。

Abstract: In electric delivery fleets, mid-shift charging is non-trivial: each vehicle must decide when, where and how much to charge to finish on time with battery above a safety floor. The choices are coupled: queues build where too many vehicles pick the same station. Prior work resolves this coupling with central dispatching, precomputed schedules or reservations, machinery that charging infrastructure rarely supports. Instead, we use a family of learning agents under purely local control: every vehicle runs the same policy, deciding alone from its time budgets and broadcast station occupancies, leading to emergent coordination without central control or messaging. We validate this paradigm in simulation on real OpenStreetMap networks of twenty cities, each with a frozen scenario calibrated by an omniscient Oracle (99.5% of shifts completed on time), whereas a naive greedy rule (nearest station on low battery) completes just 73%. Agents trained with neuroevolution (NEAT) and policy gradients (PPO) on four cities and deployed zero-shot across all twenty, sixteen never seen in training, complete 96.8% and 98.6% of shifts, with the policy-gradient controllers proving more robust when demand or vehicle characteristics drift beyond the trained regime. In contrast, tuned threshold heuristics that read vehicle urgency alone fall short in contended cities (~80%). Through training, these learning agents rediscover partial charging and short opportunistic sessions, and route around busy stations, cutting per-session queue waits from about 45 minutes to under 2. In summary, this coordination paradigm balances local urgency against public occupancy, reaching near-Oracle performance at minimal implementation cost.

</details>


### [149] [Guiding Worker Self-Selection in Crowdsourcing Contests: An LLM-Augmented Algorithmic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07749)
*Nguyen Thach,Hau Chan,David Parkes,Karim Lakhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究众包平台如何通过竞赛推荐来优化工人自选择问题。作者提出GRAF框架（基于排序向量的贪心多项式时间算法）构建自选择结果，并进一步提出LLMScore框架，利用LLM驱动的进化搜索自动设计GRAF的评分算法。实验表明该方法在1000个合成实例中能获得高质量、接近最优的结果，同时保证低工人后悔度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包平台上工人策略性地选择参赛并投入精力，这种自选择可能导致重要比赛参与人数不足或投入过少，工人也可能对已选择的比赛感到后悔。平台需要一种方式来推荐比赛给工人，以同时优化平台效用和工人满意度。

Method: 提出SSTC两阶段模型（工人先选择竞赛再竞争）以及GRAF贪心框架，通过按评分向量对工人排序来构建自选择结果，在SSTC特例中保证零工人后悔和平台最优性。为解决工人异质性下排序设计困难，提出LLMScore：LLM驱动的进化框架，自动设计GRAF的评分算法，同时优化平台效用和工人满意度，并在精确计算不可行时评估工人后悔度。

Result: LLMScore仅在单一设定的小规模实例上训练，能够迁移到更大规模及结构不同的设定。跨四种设定共1000个合成实例的实验中，GRAF结合LLMScore始终获得高质量、常接近最优的结果，且工人后悔度低。

Conclusion: 该研究为众包平台竞赛推荐提供了可扩展、自动化且可解释（生成人类可读代码）的解决方案，同时惠及平台和工人双方。

Abstract: Crowdsourcing platforms coordinate large pools of online workers who strategically choose which contests to enter and how much effort to invest. This self-selection can leave important contests with too few participants or too little effort, while workers may regret entering contests that leave them worse off than available alternatives. We study how platforms can recommend contests to workers using self-selection in Tullock contests (SSTC), a two-stage model in which workers first choose contests and then compete within them. We introduce GRAF, a greedy polynomial-time framework that constructs self-selection outcomes by ordering workers according to a score vector, with guarantees of zero worker regret and platform optimality in special cases of SSTC. Because effective orderings are difficult to design under worker heterogeneity, we propose LLMScore, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that automatically designs GRAF's scoring algorithm. LLMScore addresses two challenges: jointly optimizing platform utility and worker satisfaction, and evaluating worker regret when exact computation is intractable. Trained only on small instances of one setting, it transfers to larger and structurally different settings; moreover, its output is human-readable code that platform operators can inspect and modify. Across 1,000 synthetic instances spanning four settings, GRAF with LLMScore consistently achieves high-quality, often near-optimal, outcomes with low worker regret, benefiting both platforms and workers.

</details>


### [150] [Decomposition-Guided Diffusion Language Models for Inertial Confinement Fusion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07756)
*Xiang Zhang,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Rahman Ejaz,Riccardo Betti,Dongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICF-DLM，据作者所知是首个基于语言模型（LM）的惯性约束聚变（ICF）预测器，用于从激光脉冲和靶设计参数直接推断512步中子速率波形。通过物理类型分解（产量、峰时、局部波形）结合双向去噪和物理驱动的PPO奖励，在ICFBench（5万模拟+232次实验）上将峰时误差从11.6降至9.2步，优于经典序列模型和LLM时间序列预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家点火装置（NIF）每次实验成本约一百万美元，准确且廉价的AI代理模型具有极高价值。ICF波形预测面临巨大挑战：时间稀疏性（皮秒级峰值位于纳秒窗口）、输入输出尺度不匹配（可用真实数据不足300发）、峰值对皮秒级时序高度敏感，这些条件极大地考验了标准时间序列预测器。

Method: 提出ICF-DLM，包含三个核心组件：(i) 物理类型化分解，将预测目标划分为产量Y_DT、峰值时间t_peak和局部波形w_local三部分；(ii) 双向去噪策略，延迟对峰值位置的承诺以减少误差积累；(iii) 物理驱动的PPO奖励机制，将度量结构重新注入数值token中。该模型由外生驱动，即直接从激光脉冲和靶设计参数推断波形，无需历史响应观测。

Result: 在ICFBench基准（5万模拟数据+232次实验数据）上，ICF-DLM相比匹配的自回归LLaMA-3-8B将峰值时间误差从11.6个步长降低到9.2个步长，同时优于经典序列模型和基于LLM的时间序列预测器。

Conclusion: ICF-DLM展示了语言模型在科学领域预测中的潜力，尤其是在低数据量、稀疏事件场景下。该方法框架不只限于ICF，还有望推广到其他数据稀缺且事件稀疏的科学领域。

Abstract: Inertial confinement fusion (ICF) is a leading pathway toward clean energy, but each shot at the National Ignition Facility costs on the order of one million dollars, making accurate AI surrogates a high-value target. We study exogenous-driven ICF waveform prediction, where a 512-step neutron-rate diagnostic must be inferred directly from a laser pulse and target design parameters, with no historical response observed. The regime stresses standard time-series predictors with temporal sparsity (picosecond peak in a nanosecond window), input-output scale mismatch (under 300 real shots), and peak sensitivity (picosecond timing). We propose ICF-DLM, to our knowledge the first LM-based ICF predictor, combining (i) a physics-typed decomposition into yield $Y_{DT}$, peak timing $t_{\mathrm{peak}}$, and local waveform $w_{\mathrm{local}}$; (ii) bidirectional denoising that defers commitment to peak location; and (iii) a physics-driven PPO reward re-injecting metric structure across numeric tokens. On ICFBench (50K simulations + 232 experimental shots), ICF-DLM cuts peak-timing error from 11.6 to 9.2 steps over a matched autoregressive LLaMA-3-8B and outperforms classical sequence models and LLM-based time-series predictors. Beyond ICF, the recipe shows potential to address science domains with low data and sparse events.

</details>


### [151] [Foundation Models for Generalizable Semantic and Goal-Oriented Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07853)
*Boliang Liu,Wint Yi Poe,Riccardo Trivisonno,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FMSGOC框架，利用视觉-语言基础模型先验进行语义和面向目标的通信，通过发送稀疏目标对齐锚点并使用预训练的扩散模型在接收端重建，在极低比特率（0.039 BPP）下实现高语义保真度和良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6G语义通信系统在极低比特率下性能严重退化，原因是过度拟合训练数据且试图压缩整个信号。系统缺乏对未见数据的泛化能力是关键技术弱点。

Method: FMSGOC框架将"发送什么"与"如何重建"解耦：发送端利用视觉-语言基础模型选择并传输稀疏的语义锚点，接收端使用针对掩码补全微调的预训练扩散模型重建图像。基础模型的广义先验用于缓解过拟合，比特集中在稀疏目标对齐锚点上以提升速率效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上达到0.039 BPP，语义余弦相似度0.87-0.90；在未见过的ImageNet数据上保持0.83-0.86的稳健性；感知相似度在CIFAR-10/ImageNet上分别为0.1278/0.1558，在更低比特率下优于强端到端基线。

Conclusion: 利用生成式基础模型先验可以在极低带宽条件下维持语义保真度和泛化能力，证明解耦内容选择与生成式重建是提升语义通信系统速率效率与鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Semantic and goal-oriented communication is increasingly studied for 6G, but generalization beyond seen data remains a key weakness under tight rate budgets. Many existing systems overfit their training data and degrade sharply at very low bit rates because they attempt to compress the entire signal. We introduce Foundation Model-Guided Semantic and Goal-Oriented Communication (FMSGOC), a framework that uses broad visual-linguistic Foundation Model priors to mitigate overfitting. It further improves rate efficiency by concentrating bits on sparse, goal-aligned anchors and relying on generative foundation-model priors to reconstruct the masked regions. By decoupling what to send from how to reconstruct, a vision-language foundation model selects and transmits a sparse set of semantic anchors, while a pretrained diffusion model, fine-tuned for masked completion, reconstructs the image at the receiver. In our experiments, FMSGOC reaches 0.039 bits per pixel (BPP), maintains high semantic fidelity (cosine similarity 0.87-0.90 on CIFAR-10), remains robust on previously unseen inputs (0.83-0.86 on ImageNet), and shows good perceptual similarity (0.1278/0.1558, CIFAR-10/ImageNet), outperforming strong end-to-end baselines at lower bit rates.

</details>


### [152] [Prevalence calibration as shortcut mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07922)
*Mohamed Amine Kina,Eike Petersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将捷径学习（shortcut learning）重新定义为校准问题，提出两种编码器无关的方法（in-processing正则化器和post-hoc患病率均衡重校准）来消除不同捷径组之间的校准差异，在两个胸部X光数据集（CheXpert和SIIM-ACR）上显著优于所有基线，并将错位组AUROC从0.23提升至0.73。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有捷径学习缓解策略大多试图学习捷径不变的表示，但其经验成功有限，且无法应用于使用冻结基础模型编码器的分类器。此外，捷径依赖导致的性能退化问题根源尚未被充分理解。

Method: 作者提出将捷径学习重框定为校准问题：无约束学习隐式地将每个捷径组校准到其训练集的疾病患病率，导致分类器在一个组过自信、在另一个组欠自信。基于此洞察，通过两个编码器无关方法实现组间患病率均衡校准：(1) in-processing正则化器；(2) post-hoc患病率均衡重校准步骤。

Result: 在CheXpert和SIIM-ACR的气胸基准上，涵盖微调CNN和冻结基础模型骨干，两种方法均大幅优于所有基线。post-hoc重校准将标准ERM训练的DenseNet的错位组AUROC从0.23提升至0.73，表明捷径依赖破坏的是分类头而非底层表示。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了两种新的最先进捷径缓解方法，并从更根本上将捷径学习与校准理论和算法公平性联系起来，启发了在新的方向上的研究。

Abstract: Shortcut learning denotes the widespread situation in which a classifier exploits spurious correlations rather than diagnostic features. Existing mitigation strategies mostly aim to learn shortcut-invariant representations; their empirical success is limited and they cannot be applied to classifiers using frozen foundation model encoders. We propose to reframe shortcut learning as fundamentally a calibration problem: unconstrained learning implicitly calibrates each shortcut group to its training set disease prevalence, rendering the resulting classifier necessarily over-confident in one group and under-confident in the other. Building on this insight, we prevalence-equalize calibration between shortcut groups through two encoder-agnostic methods, an in-processing regularizer and a post-hoc prevalence-equalized recalibration step. Across chest-drain-pneumothorax benchmarks on CheXpert and SIIM-ACR, spanning fine-tuned CNNs and frozen foundation-model backbones, both methods substantially outperform all baselines. Post-hoc recalibration of a standard ERM-trained DenseNet raises misaligned-group AUROC from 0.23 to 0.73, indicating that shortcut reliance degrades the classification head rather than the underlying representation. Besides two new state-of-the-art shortcut mitigation approaches, our findings more fundamentally connect shortcut learning to calibration theory and algorithmic fairness.

</details>


### [153] [MetaKV: Adaptive KV Cache Compression for Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07966)
*Michael Wang,Keith Li,Roozbeh Bostandoost*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaKV是一个自适应框架，根据每个输入提示词以及用户指定的延迟和峰值内存预算，为KV缓存压缩选择最佳配置，从而在准确性、推理延迟和峰值内存之间取得最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存压缩方法在准确性、推理延迟和峰值KV缓存内存利用之间做出不同的权衡，单一固定配置无法适用于不同提示词和资源约束场景。

Method: 使用轻量级预测模型为每个候选配置估计端到端延迟、峰值内存和正确答案概率，然后选择最能满足延迟-内存约束同时保持准确性的配置。

Result: 在数学、科学、常识推理和阅读理解四个数据集上，对来自KVQuant、H2O和RocketKV三种代表性KV缓存压缩方法的十个配置（加上未压缩FP16配置）进行评估。MetaKV持续优于最佳静态配置，约束成功率达到率（CSR）平均提升约0.07，最高提升0.135。

Conclusion: 研究表明将KV缓存压缩适配到具体提示词和延迟-内存约束条件是有益的，并展示了MetaKV框架在该领域的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Key--value (KV) cache compression is an effective way to reduce the memory overhead of large language model (LLM) inference, particularly for long-context workloads. However, existing compression methods make different trade-offs among accuracy, inference latency, and peak KV cache memory utilization, making a single fixed configuration unsuitable across different prompts and resource constraints. We introduce MetaKV, an adaptive framework that selects a KV cache compression configuration for each input prompt based on user-specified latency and peak memory budgets. MetaKV uses lightweight prediction models to estimate the end-to-end latency, peak memory, and probability of a correct response for each candidate configuration, and selects the configuration that best satisfies the latency-memory constraints while preserving accuracy. We evaluate MetaKV across ten configurations from three representative KV cache compression methods, KVQuant, H$_2$O, and RocketKV, together with an uncompressed FP16 configuration, on four datasets covering mathematics, science, commonsense reasoning, and reading comprehension. Across a wide range of latency and peak memory constraints, MetaKV consistently outperforms the best static configuration, improving constrained success rate (CSR), the fraction of prompts answered correctly while satisfying both constraints, by approximately 0.07 on average and up to 0.135. These results demonstrate the benefit of adapting KV cache compression to individual prompts and latency-memory constraints. Code is available at https://github.com/MichaelWang0505/MetaKV.git

</details>


### [154] [Automated Chest CT Protocol Selection via Large Language Model Derived Text Embeddings from Imaging Request Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07986)
*Zahra Hosseini,Mahan Pouromidi,Farzad Khalvati,Patrik Rogalla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）特征的决策支持系统，用于从自由文本临床指征中推荐胸部CT检查协议。使用Meta的LLaMA-3.1-70B微调模型嵌入临床文本，输入逻辑回归分类器预测18种CT协议标签。在28.5万份胸部CT请求的回顾性队列中，系统达到79%的总体准确率，与放射科医生的表现无显著差异，表明其可作为协议推荐工具的可行基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CT协议选择依赖人工操作，耗时且易产生不一致。已有的机器学习方法（基于关键词或词袋模型）缺乏上下文理解能力，且在罕见协议上表现不佳。需要一种能捕捉临床细微差别和措辞变化的方法，以提高协议选择的准确性和一致性。

Method: 回顾性研究，纳入285,123份胸部CT检查请求（2017-2024），按80/20拆分训练与测试集。临床文本经微调的LLaMA-3.1-70B模型嵌入，特征输入逻辑回归分类器，预测18类CT协议标签。以300例专家共识病例为基准，将模型与放射科医生的表现进行对比，并采用熵分析评估协议使用的一致性。

Result: 加权精确率0.84，加权F1值0.81，整体准确率79%。在300例专家共识独立病例中，LLM准确率80% vs 放射科医生83%，差异无统计学意义（p=0.263）。多数类别性能相当，LLM在某些具有挑战性的类别上超过放射科医生，熵分析显示协议使用更为均衡，降低了变异性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的推荐系统能够利用大规模自然语言文本的通用知识，从自由文本影像请求中准确分配胸部CT协议。该系统可作为协议推荐工具的可行基础，尤其适用于需要语言理解的输入场景。

Abstract: Purpose: Accurate CT protocol selection is critical for diagnostic quality and patient safety, yet the current process is manual, time-consuming, and prone to inconsistencies. Prior Machine Learning methods using keywords or bag-of-words lack contextual understanding and perform poorly on rare protocols. We propose a decision support system using large language model (LLM) features to recommend protocols from free-text clinical indications, capturing clinical nuance and phrasing variation for more consistent, efficient selection.
  Methods: In this REB-approved retrospective study, 285,123 chest CT imaging requests from a large academic medical center (2017-2024) were split into training (228,099, 80%) and held-out test (57,024, 20%) sets. Each request included procedure names, clinical indication, HIS comments, and the selected protocol. Clinical text was embedded using a fine-tuned LLM, Meta's LLaMA-3.1-70B; these features input a logistic regression classifier predicting 18 protocol labels (e.g., PE, LDCT).
  Results: The pipeline achieved a weighted precision of 0.84, weighted F1-score of 0.81, and overall accuracy of 79% across 18 CT protocols. On 300 independent cases with expert consensus, the LLM reached an overall accuracy of 80% versus 83% for radiologists, with no significant difference (p = 0.263). Performance was comparable across most classes, with the LLM exceeding radiologists for some challenging categories, and entropy analyses indicated more balanced protocol use, suggesting reduced variability.
  Conclusion: An LLM-based recommendation system can leverage general knowledge from a large natural-text corpus to accurately assign chest CT protocols from free-text imaging requests, and may serve as a viable foundation for protocol recommendation tools where inputs require language understanding.

</details>


### [155] [Risk-Conditioned Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08064)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出风险条件化RLHF（risk-conditioned RLHF），训练单一策略模型，在推理时通过连续风险控制接口调整风险厌恶程度，无需为不同风险水平重新训练或部署多个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Risk-Averse RLHF通过优化CVaR来解决LLM部署中罕见但严重的危害性生成问题，但该类方法针对固定的风险水平进行训练，无法在推理时动态调整所需的风险厌恶程度，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出risk-conditioned RLHF框架，训练一个带有连续风险控制接口的单一策略，该策略能够条件化于风险水平，使用户在推理时可以选择不同的风险厌恶程度，而无需重新训练或部署多个风险特定模型。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，单一风险条件化策略能够在推理时适应不同的风险水平，实现更灵活和风险感知的LLM部署。

Conclusion: risk-conditioned RLHF提供了一个统一的、可调节的风险控制框架，使单一模型即可覆盖不同风险偏好的部署需求，提升了RLHF在风险敏感场景下的实际可用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where rare but severe harmful generations can have significant consequences. Existing Risk-Averse RLHF addresses this issue by optimizing Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR), but it trains policies for fixed risk levels and therefore cannot adjust the desired degree of risk aversion at inference time. In this paper, we propose risk-conditioned RLHF, a framework that trains a single policy that provides a continuous risk-control interface, enabling users to select different degrees of risk aversion without retraining or deploying multiple risk-specific models. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that a single risk-conditioned policy can adapt to different risk levels at inference time, enabling more flexible and risk-aware LLM deployment.

</details>


### [156] [TC-Next: Zero-Shot Multimodal Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02085)
*Zhe Wang,Sijie Chen,Yiming Luo,Daehyun Kim,Chien-Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TropicalCycloneNext（TC-Next），一种多模态深度学习模型，利用基础模型的天气预测场和卫星红外影像，在6-24小时预见期内预测热带气旋路径和强度。在仅用GraphCast预报训练的模型在西北太平洋多个基础模型上显著优于传统规则型追踪器，并展示了良好的泛化能力和跨模型可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规则型热带气旋追踪器（如TempestExtremes）依赖于物理规则和启发式判断，在利用现代深度学习基础模型（如GraphCast、Pangu-Weather等）生成的高分辨率天气预测场时，无法充分挖掘其中的信息。作者旨在构建一个能够从基础模型预测场中学习并更准确地追踪和预测热带气旋的多模态深度学习方法。

Method: TC-Next是一个多模态深度学习模型，输入包括：1）基础模型（如GraphCast）预测的大气运动学和热力学场；2）GridSat红外卫星图像。模型仅在GraphCast的西北太平洋预报上训练，但设计上仅依赖通用的大气变量，因此可直接迁移到其他基础模型的预测场上（包括Pangu-Weather、IFS HRES及WeatherNext Cyclones的零样本场景），无需重新训练。

Result: 1）在GraphCast预测场上，TC-Next的路径误差比传统规则追踪器TempestExtremes降低15%-44%，强度误差降低3-6倍；2）无需重新训练即可迁移到Pangu-Weather和IFS HRES预测场，效果均优于TempestExtremes；3）在2025年西北太平洋季节的WeatherNext Cyclones预测场上零样本应用，在每个预见期均达到更低强度误差，路径误差更低或相当；4）消融实验表明多模态融合在路径追踪全过程和较长时间尺度的强度预测中均有增益。

Conclusion: TC-Next验证了多模态深度学习模型可以超越传统规则追踪器，并且仅基于通用大气变量进行训练即可跨不同基础模型泛化，无需针对每个模型重新训练，体现了良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值。多模态融合（卫星影像+天气场）是提升短临追踪与强度预测准确性的有效策略。

Abstract: We present TropicalCycloneNext (TC-Next), a multimodal deep learning model that forecasts tropical cyclone track and intensity at $6$-$24$ h leads by leveraging a foundation model's forecast fields of atmospheric kinematic and thermodynamic fields and GridSat infrared satellite imagery. Trained only on GraphCast forecasts over the Western Pacific (WP), yet reliant only on generic atmospheric variables, TC-Next on GraphCast lowers track error by $15$-$44\%$ and intensity error by a factor of $3$-$6$ relative to a conventional, rule-based tracker, TempestExtremes; applied without retraining to the forecast fields of Pangu-Weather and IFS HRES, it stays ahead of TempestExtremes on both. Applied zero-shot to the generic weather fields of WeatherNext Cyclones on the 2025 WP season, TC-Next attains lower intensity error at every lead time, and lower or comparable track error, compared to that model's specialized direct tracker in a deterministic comparison. Our ablation studies show that our multimodal model is able to utilize the additional modality to improve performance in tracking errors at every lead time and in intensity prediction at longer lead times.

</details>


### [157] [IPM-FM: A Foundation Model with Consensus Feature Selection for Industrial Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08375)
*Liang Cao,Weide Liu,Yan Qin,Jun Cheng,Weisi Lin,Bhushan Gopaluni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出工业过程监控基础模型（IPM-FM），通过自监督预训练学习通用表征，再以少量带标签数据适配具体监控任务，并使用不确定感知预测头输出校准预测。在七年加氢处理装置柴油闪点软测量数据集上，IPM-FM取得RMSE 2.99、R² 0.50、95%预测区间覆盖率97%，分别比最强经典基线和从头序列基线降低8.3%和14.6%的RMSE，验证了统一预训练-适配框架在工业过程监控中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工业过程监控采用"一任务一模型"范式，标签效率低且易因运行漂移而性能退化。虽然基础模型已在语言、视觉和通用时间序列预测领域取得突破，但尚未被适配到工业过程监控，该场景面临安全关键决策和过程变量与实验室测量间非对称采样等独特挑战。

Method: IPM-FM采用三阶段框架：(1)通过自监督预训练从无标签工业过程数据学习通用表征，基于Informer骨干网络；(2)利用少量任务标注数据进行适配，集成多准则共识特征选择器与递归滞后特征回归头；(3)通过校准的蒙特卡洛Dropout不确定模块产生校准预测。

Result: 在七年加氢处理装置数据集上进行柴油闪点软测量，IPM-FM达到RMSE 2.99、R² 0.50、95%预测区间覆盖率97%，相较于最强经典基线和从零训练的序列基线，RMSE分别降低8.3%和14.6%。

Conclusion: IPM-FM验证了统一的预训练-适配基础模型框架在工业过程监控领域的可行性，为打破"一任务一模型"范式、提升标签效率和应对运行漂移提供了新路径。

Abstract: Industrial process monitoring is fundamental to the safety and economic performance of modern process plants. Current practice remains a one-task-one-model paradigm that is label-inefficient and prone to degradation under operating drift. Foundation models have reshaped language, vision, and generic time-series forecasting, but it has not been adapted to industrial process monitoring. This setting poses domain-specific challenges, including safety-critical decisions and asymmetric sampling between process variables and laboratory measurements. We propose the industrial process monitoring foundation model (IPM-FM). It first learns general-purpose representations from unlabeled industrial process data through self-supervised pretraining, then adapts to specific monitoring tasks using a small amount of task-labeled data, and finally produces calibrated predictions through an uncertainty-aware prediction head. IPM-FM integrates a self-supervised Informer backbone with a multi-criteria consensus feature selector, a recursive lag-feature regression head, and a calibrated Monte Carlo dropout uncertainty module. On a seven-year hydrotreater dataset for diesel flash-point soft sensing, IPM-FM attains an RMSE of 2.99, $R^2$ of 0.50, and 97\% coverage of its 95\% predictive interval, outperforming the strongest classical and from-scratch sequence baselines by 8.3\% and 14.6\% in RMSE respectively, supporting the viability of a unified pretraining--adaptation framework for industrial process monitoring.

</details>


### [158] [Equivariance Breaks the Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08381)
*Andrei Manolache,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Muon等矩阵结构优化器在等变网络上优于Adam的一个原因：等变线性层中每个irrep块共享通道混合矩阵，Adam按权重逐元素缩放学习率而忽略了irrep块的边界，导致同一层内不同块的谱步长不一致。作者提出对每个块单独归一化更新（不引入新超参数），结合调整Adam的动量系数，使Adam在多个数据集上达到与Muon相媲美的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关研究报道矩阵结构优化器Muon在等变架构上表现优于Adam，但缺乏理论解释。作者希望找出这一性能差异的根源，并探索用Adam实现类似性能的方法。
