Method: 首先识别等变线性层中梯度的结构：W_l的梯度是2l+1个外积贡献之和，秩至多为2l+1。Adam逐权重缩放存储的权重而忽略irrep块边界，导致学习率在不同块间产生不同的谱步长。作者提出对每个irrep块的更新分别归一化（不改变更新方向，仅改变尺度，保持Adam的动量估计不变），并结合独立调整Adam动量系数进行实验验证。

Result: 在受控SO(3)等变模型实验中，发现块归一化能解决随宽度增长的不匹配问题，而稠密对照组无此增长。在e3nn原子间势模型（rMD17和MD22数据集）上，块归一化和调整动量系数单独使用均能提升性能但不及Muon；两者结合后Adam在所有数据集上与Muon表现相当。

Conclusion: 块级步长控制和动量累积可以解释Muon相对Adam的大部分优势，通过块归一化和动量系数调整可使Adam达到与Muon竞争的性能，且无需引入新的超参数。

Abstract: Equivariant networks are commonly trained with Adam, yet recent work reports that matrix-structured optimizers such as Muon can perform better on these architectures without explaining why. We identify one source of this difference inside equivariant linear layers. Each irrep block learns a channel-mixing matrix $W_l$ shared across its $2l+1$ components, giving the expanded map $W_l \otimes I_{2l+1}$. For a single application of the layer, the gradient of $W_l$ sums $2l+1$ outer product contributions and has rank at most $2l+1$. Adam rescales stored weights individually without using the irrep boundaries, so one learning rate can produce different spectral step sizes across blocks within a layer. We address this mismatch by normalizing each block update separately, without introducing a new hyperparameter. This changes only the scale of the update, leaving Adam's moment estimates and its direction within each block unchanged. We evaluate the mechanism in a controlled $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-equivariant model with a matched dense control and in an e3nn interatomic potential model trained on rMD17 and MD22. The toy setup isolates a mismatch that grows with width while the dense control shows no corresponding growth. In the interatomic potential model, block normalization and tuning Adam's momentum coefficients independently improve performance, but neither alone matches Muon. Combined, they make Adam competitive with Muon on all datasets, indicating that blockwise step control and momentum accumulation account for much of Muon's advantage.

</details>


### [159] [Leveraging contextual events on structure-aware next activity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08622)
*Alessandro Mele,Claudia Diamantini,Domenico Potena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于实例图（Instance Graphs）的预测性流程监控方法，通过多种编码策略将上下文流程实例信息纳入图神经网络（GNN），以提升下一个活动预测任务的性能。在多个真实事件日志上的实验表明，整合上下文流程实例有助于提高预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性流程监控中的下一个活动预测任务受到广泛研究，但现有方法大多依赖事件日志属性或聚合度量来编码上下文信息，缺乏对不同上下文编码策略的系统性探索，且如何有效地在过程挖掘模型中融入上下文流程实例仍是一个挑战。

Method: 本文基于实例图（Instance Graphs）概念提出新方法：首先设计多种上下文流程实例编码策略，分别将其嵌入到图中；针对每种编码策略，生成一组前缀实例图（prefix-Instance Graphs），随后将其输入到图神经网络（GNN）中进行分类，以预测下一个活动。

Result: 在多个真实世界事件日志上进行评估，实验结果表明，将上下文流程实例信息纳入模型能够有效提升下一个活动预测的性能。

Conclusion: 上下文流程实例的显式编码对预测性流程监控中下一个活动预测任务具有正面影响，基于实例图与图神经网络的框架是一个有效的建模途径。

Abstract: Predictive process monitoring aims at forecasting various aspects of running processes. Among the different tasks, next activity prediction represents the most extensively investigated. However, only a limited number of existing approaches explicitly encode contextual information, i.e., the environmental conditions in which the process is executed, typically modeled through event log attributes or aggregated measures. In this paper, an approach based on the concept of Instance Graphs is introduced. To incorporate contextual process instances, several encoding strategies are proposed and evaluated by measuring their impact on prediction performance. For each encoding strategy, a set of prefix-Instance Graphs is generated and subsequently provided as input to a Graph Neural Network for the classification task. The proposed approach is evaluated on multiple real-world event logs, and the experimental results demonstrate that incorporating contextual process instances benefits prediction performance.

</details>


### [160] [MoEMB: Scaling Universal Multimodal Embeddings with Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08663)
*Xuanming Cui,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Wentao Bao,Aashu Singh,Zihao Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Jun Xiao,Ser-Nam Lim,Jianpeng Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOEMB，通过混合专家（MoE）沿专家轴扩展通用多模态嵌入（UME）模型，在保持单向量、非自回归编码的同时增长编码器容量。仅用3B激活参数，MOEMB在MMEB-V2和MRMR基准上超越TTE类方法（后者需要4倍以上的激活参数），并首次系统研究了基于MoE嵌入的自适应计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态嵌入模型面临扩展难题：传统方法或增大表示维度/检索开销，或将编码器扩展为重型多模态LLM。TTE等近期工作尝试通过推理token扩展，但嵌入模型难以直接扩展参数——增加参数会与对比学习所需的大训练批次产生权衡，且检索需在严格延迟下服务。此外，UME任务复杂度各异，统一扩展嵌入器会带来大量冗余计算。

Method: 提出MOEMB，通过混合专家机制沿专家轴扩展UME模型，在不改变单向量、非自回归编码范式的前提下扩大编码器容量。同时系统研究了MoE-based UME的设计空间与训练策略，并首次全面探索了自适应计算方法（涵盖基于训练和仅推理的策略），以进一步提升MoE嵌入模型的可扩展性与效率。

Result: 在公共MMEB-family数据训练的模型上，MOEMB在MMEB-V2和MRMR基准上均达到新SOTA：仅需3B激活参数即可超越TTE方法（其需要超过4倍的激活参数），且计算量大幅减少。自适应计算研究进一步提升了效率。

Conclusion: 专家扩展（expert scaling）是通用多模态嵌入的有效且高效方向，自适应计算可进一步提升基于MLLM的嵌入模型在大规模检索和推荐系统中的效率。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedding (UME) increasingly demands encoder's capacity for handling a broad range of tasks and modalities with increased complexity. Prior scaling methods either increase the representation size, retrieval effort, or scales the encoder into a heavy multimodal LLM. Recent works, such as Think-Then-Embed (TTE), explore scaling via reasoning tokens. However, embedding models are hard to scale up: increasing parameters directly tradeoffs for the large training batch size that contrastive learning needs, and retrieval has to be served under tight latency. Moreover, UME tasks are diverse in complexity, where scaling up embedders can bring significant redundant computation. In this work, we propose MOEMB, which instead scales UME along the expert axis through mixture-of-experts (MoE), growing encoder capacity while preserving single-vector, non-autoregressive encoding. Through a systematic study of the design space and training recipes for MoE-based UME, MoEMB sets a new state of the art on both MMEB-V2 and MRMR among models trained on public MMEB-family data: with only 3B active parameters, MoEMB surpasses TTE-based methods with >4x active parameters, using significantly less computes. To further improve the scalability and efficiency, we conduct the first comprehensive study of adaptive computation for MoE-based embedding, spanning diverse strategies across training-based and inference-only methods. Together, these results support expert scaling as an effective and efficient direction for UME, with adaptive computation further improving efficiency for MLLM-based embedding models towards large-scale retrieval and recommendation systems.

</details>


### [161] [Adaptive Anisotropic Attention for Axis-Structured Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08788)
*Mahir Jain,Parshva Runwal,Aditya Ray Mishra,Arvasu Kulkarni,Sandeep Singh,Siddharth Panwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性注意力（AAA），将注意力分解为时间轴和空间轴两条路径，通过门控机制为每个token学习两条路径的凸组合权重，用于处理低信噪比的结构化信号（如EEG）。基于此的AXON模型在六个EEG下游任务上优于密集注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自注意力在训练前将所有token对视为同等可能，这是一种各向同性的先验，与结构化信号不匹配。对于EEG这类低信噪比的结构化信号，依赖关系沿着电极和时间轴组织，均匀先验使每个token暴露于大量无关交互中，导致性能不佳。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性注意力（AAA），将注意力拆分为两条路径：时间路径（token只关注同一电极不同时刻的token）和空间路径（token只关注同一时间步其他电极的token）。一个轻量门控为每个token预测两条路径输出的凸组合（两个非负权重，和为1）。基于此构建AXON（AXis-factorized Operator Network）模型。

Result: 在六个EEG下游任务上，AXON在线性探测和全微调两种设置下均优于密集注意力的平衡准确率基线。消融实验表明：时间路径和空间路径都不可或缺，加权求和优于硬选单一路径；主要收益来自门控在网络的每一层学习不同的时间/空间注意力平衡。在受控音频语谱图实验中，轴因式分解的收益超越EEG领域。

Conclusion: 将注意力与结构化信号的自然轴对齐是一种有效的归纳偏置，可以改善低信噪比结构化信号上的自注意力模型性能。

Abstract: Dense self-attention treats all token pairs as equally plausible before learning, an interaction-isotropic prior that can be mismatched to structured signals. For structured, low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) signals such as EEG, dependencies are organized along the electrode and time axes, and this uniform prior exposes each token to many irrelevant interactions. We introduce Adaptive Anisotropic Attention (AAA), which splits attention into two paths: a temporal path, where each token attends to the tokens of its own electrode across time, and a spatial path, where it attends to the tokens of the other electrodes at the same time step. A small gate predicts, for every token, a convex combination of the two path outputs: two non-negative weights that sum to one. On six EEG downstream tasks, the resulting model, AXON (AXis-factorized Operator Network), improves mean balanced accuracy over a dense baseline under both linear probing and full fine-tuning. We show that both paths (temporal and spatial) are necessary and that the weighted sum beats a hard choice of one path; most of the benefit comes from the gate learning a different temporal/spatial balance at each layer of the network. Controlled audio spectrogram experiments show that axis factorization transfers beyond EEG. These results suggest that aligning attention with the natural axes of structured signals provides a useful inductive bias.

</details>


### [162] [Do Reasoning Representations Help Humans Evaluate LLM Outputs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09038)
*Jaewoo Lim,Sungbok Shin,Sanghyun Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控人类研究比较了六种推理表示格式作为人机交互界面的效果，发现用户偏好与表征质量存在错位：参与者更喜欢规划和分解式表示，但更简单的思维链模板在验证、信任和可解释性上表现更好，且受欢迎的表示会引发校准风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理表示常被用作大语言模型输出的解释，但现有评估以模型为中心（如答案准确性和忠实度），未能回答关键问题：这些表示是否真正帮助人类评估模型响应？本文试图以人为中心重新审视推理表示。

Method: 开展受控人类研究，评估六种推理格式在不同复杂度任务上的表现。通过基于网页的框架随机化任务领域、问题实例和表示顺序，收集用户对结构理解、错误检测与定位、信任校准的细粒度判断。

Result: 发现用户偏好与人类评估支持之间存在错配：参与者偏好规划和分解式表示，但更简单的思维链轨迹在验证、信任和可解释性方面更优。用户偏好的表示还引入校准风险：正确轨迹上误报更多，且信任度虽高但验证意愿低。

Conclusion: 推理表示作为人类面对界面的效用不能通过偏好间接衡量，设计者需注意受欢迎表示可能并不利于用户准确评估模型输出，应重新审视推理表示以人为中心的设计目标。

Abstract: Reasoning representations are increasingly used as explanations for large language model outputs. Yet they are typically evaluated with model-centric criteria, such as answer accuracy and faithfulness, leaving it unclear whether they help people evaluate model responses. In this work, we study reasoning representations as human-facing interfaces rather than proxies for model reasoning ability. We conduct a controlled human study of six reasoning formats across tasks of varying complexity, supported by a web-based framework that randomizes task domains, problem instances, and representation order. The study collects fine-grained judgments of structural understanding, error detection and localization, and trust calibration. Our study shows a mismatch between perceived preference and support for human evaluation. Participants prefer planning- and decomposition-based representations, but simpler chain-of-thought traces better support verification, trust, and interpretability. Preferred representations also introduce calibration risks, with more false alarms on correct traces and high trust despite low willingness to verify.

</details>


### [163] [Training-Free Task Vectors for LLM Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09054)
*Gabriel J. Perin,Lucas Boscaini,André Araujo,Nina S. T. Hirata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出训练无关任务向量（TFTVs），通过仅使用前向传播统计将激活引导向量映射为秩一权重空间编辑（EigenAlgorithm），无需微调即可实现任务向量的加减与组合，用于大语言模型行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务向量依赖微调模型与预训练模型的权重差，计算成本高，限制了训练后模型编辑的实用性。因此需要一种无需微调即可获得任务向量方向的方法。

Method: TFTVs方法仅利用前向传播统计信息，将激活引导向量映射为秩一权重空间编辑，得到类似任务向量的方向。该方法满足算术性质：支持通过加法实现学习、减法实现遗忘、以及多编辑的叠加组合。

Result: 在大语言模型行为控制任务上的实验表明，TFTVs能够一致地放大、抑制和组合目标行为，同时保持通用知识与问题求解能力。与传统编辑和引导基线相比，TFTVs在特征控制上更强，且在实用性保持上更好或相当。

Conclusion: TFTVs为训练后模型编辑开辟了无需微调的新路径，有望推动社区在训练无关模型控制方向的进一步探索。

Abstract: Task vectors enable post-training model editing by identifying semantically meaningful directions in weight space, typically computed as the difference between a fine-tuned model and its pretrained initialization. However, this reliance on fine-tuning makes discovering such directions costly and limits the practicality of post-training model editing. To address this limitation, we introduce Training-Free Task Vectors (TFTVs), a novel method to compute task-vector-like directions without requiring fine-tuning. Our method maps activation steering vectors to rank-one weight-space edits using only forward-pass statistics, while satisfying arithmetic properties that directly support learning via addition, forgetting via subtraction, and the composition of multiple edits. Empirically, we evaluate TFTVs on large language model behavioral control tasks and show that they consistently amplify, suppress, and compose target behaviors while preserving general knowledge and problem-solving skills. We also validate our method against other editing and steering baselines, experimentally demonstrating that TFTVs achieve stronger trait control with better or competitive utility preservation. We hope our work opens new directions for the community in post-training model editing and broader training-free model control. Code is available on the project website: tftv-llm.github.io.

</details>


### [164] [PlayTrain: An Efficient Reinforcement Learning Framework for LLM-Generated Adaptable JavaScript Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09059)
*Ryan Truong,Lance Ying,Samuel J. Gershman,Kazuki Irie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PlayTrain是一个将大语言模型(LLM)生成JavaScript游戏的能力与强化学习(RL)训练流程相结合的新型框架。它只需用户提供极简提示即可由LLM自动生成JS游戏，并通过高效管线在标准gym环境中运行和训练RL智能体。该框架能以每秒超过100万个智能体决策的速度端到端训练基于像素的智能体，并支持游戏克隆和修改（如Atari和ProcGen游戏），从而彻底改变了RL视频游戏环境的开发范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习视频游戏环境（VGEs）开发或修改过程繁重，需要大量手工编码，严重阻碍了RL研究中对多样化游戏环境的探索。尽管已有许多VGEs在推动RL进展中起到关键作用，但开发新环境或扩展特性的人力成本极高。作者旨在利用LLM在JavaScript代码生成方面的强大能力，构建一个自动化生成、修改和运行游戏环境的端到端RL框架，降低环境开发的准入门槛。

Method: PlayTrain框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用大语言模型（LLMs）从用户极简文字提示中稳健地生成JavaScript游戏代码；(2) 一个高效的JS游戏到标准gym环境的执行管线，支持在生成的游戏上直接训练RL智能体。由于JS格式天然便于用户在浏览器中游玩同一游戏进行人工演示或验证，PlayTrain通过统一JS文件同时服务于人类交互和智能体训练两个需求。

Result: 实验展示了多个用例：成功克隆了知名的Atari和ProcGen游戏为简单JS版本，并在此之上以单GPU节点每秒超过100万次智能体决策的速度端到端训练基于像素的智能体；同时支持对克隆游戏进行修改以产生新特性，如新颖的测试集、程序化生成逻辑或游戏动力学变化。

Conclusion: PlayTrain重新定义了RL视频游戏环境的开发流程——只需一个JS文件，通过LLM生成和修改即可完成。该框架开启了若干有前景的未来RL研究方向（如程序化内容生成、环境动态调整等），有望显著加速RL研究与实验速度。

Abstract: While many video-game environments (VGEs) have played crucial roles in advancing reinforcement learning (RL), developing novel VGEs or modifying existing ones to support new features, has been a laborious process requiring extensive hand-coding. Here we present PlayTrain, an RL framework that combines the abilities of large language models (LLMs) to robustly generate JavaScript (JS) games from a minimal human prompt, and an efficient pipeline that can run any JS game in a standard 'gym' environment. Not only are recent LLMs particularly good at writing JS code, but the JS format also allows users to easily play generated VGEs, while PlayTrain enables us to train RL agents on the exact same games. We demonstrate multiple use cases of PlayTrain, including cloning well-known Atari and ProcGen games in simple JS, where PlayTrain trains pixel-based agents end-to-end at over 1M agent-decisions per second on a single GPU node; and creating modified versions thereof (e.g., that support novel test sets, procedural generation logics, or game dynamics). Through PlayTrain, we reimagine RL VGE development: all we need is a single JS file, generated and modified through an LLM. We discuss promising future RL research directions that PlayTrain unlocks.

</details>


### [165] [When Does Scale-Invariant Optimization Become Unstable? An Exact Schedule Law with Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09116)
*Hasan Amin,Wei-Kai Chang,Rajiv Khanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了归一化网络中学习率调度与权重衰减通过参数范数形成离散时间反馈环路的精确定律，提出一个标量量统一刻画训练动力学，并在理论分析和多种神经网络上验证了该定律的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化使网络大部分结构具有尺度不变性，导致学习率调度与权重衰减之间通过参数范数产生隐性反馈，控制优化器的有效步长。理解这一相互作用对于训练动力学、优化器行为和调度设计至关重要。

Method: 推导了一个精确的离散时间定律，用单一标量捕获所有调度与衰减的强迫作用，同时范数增长产生几何自我淬灭效应，形成收缩/扩张主导的有效学习率边界。通过对完全可解的归一化回归模型进行精确分析（动力学降至二维），证明平衡点本质不稳定。进一步提出统一的同质优化器框架，揭示自适应方法在归一化下稳定化系统性偏弱的结构性二分法。

Result: 在多种动力学系统及MLP、CNN、GPT2等神经网络（覆盖MNIST、CIFAR、wikiText、OpenWebText数据集）上，预测规律以高精度成立，性能在预测边界处出现尖锐峰值。表明恒定学习率配合权重衰减无法稳定维持内部均衡，而是产生由离散时间Jacobian结构驱动的周期性行为。

Conclusion: 尺度不变优化的动力学可由单一标量量统治，该发现为训练动力学、优化器行为和调度设计提供了精确且可操作的视角，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Normalization renders large parts of neural networks effectively scale invariant, inducing a hidden feedback loop in which learning-rate schedules and weight decay interact through the parameter norm to control the effective step taken by the optimizer. We show that this interaction is governed by an exact discrete-time law: a single scalar quantity captures all schedule and decay forcing, while norm growth induces an opposing geometric self-quenching effect. This yields a sharp boundary that cleanly separates contraction- and expansion-dominated effective learning rate regimes. To understand the underlying mechanism, we provide exact analysis of a fully solved normalized regression model where the dynamics reduce to two dimensions and show that the balance point is intrinsically unstable, implying that constant learning rate with weight decay cannot stably maintain an interior equilibrium and instead produces recurrent behavior driven by discrete-time Jacobian structure. We further extend this perspective across optimizers through unified homogeneous-optimizer framework that reveals a structural dichotomy in self-quenching strength, providing a first-principles explanation for why adaptive methods exhibit systematically weaker stabilization under normalization.
  Across dynamical systems and neural networks (MLP, CNN, GPT2 / MNIST, CIFAR, wikiText, OpenWebText), the predicted law holds with high precision and enables direct control of training via the identified scalar, with performance peaking sharply at the predicted boundary. Together, these results isolate a single governing quantity for scale-invariant optimization, providing a precise and actionable lens on training dynamics, optimizer behavior, and schedule design in modern deep learning. Code is available in https://github.com/shasanamin/normalized-optimization-dynamics.
